## 17. Decision log

- 2026-10-27 - **Orbit Lock releases toward the boss (designer decision 2026-10-27; GDD §5.3 row 5 refined).** Measured: ShipCheck bot run on main `31ac2675`, a random Forge build with Scatter Cannon + Orbit Lock + Boomerang fought the Warden 15:00-32:04 and lost, under one live round and ~0 kills/min at the end (outward releases fly away from the Warden; Boomerang brings them back short). The Warden is unchanged. Rule: at each round's own release (the 2 s orbit is kept, so volleys release in spawn order), the round is re-aimed **once** (a direction change, no homing) at the boss if one is alive, else at the nearest living enemy within the weapon's final `Range` x `URHModifierDef::OrbitReleaseAimRangeMult` (**GATED**, 1.5 on Orbit Lock; 0 = no aim), else it keeps today's release along the orbit tangent. Speed, damage, pierce, other modifiers, lifetime and Boomerang's return are untouched. Code: `RHSpecComposition::Compose` writes `FRHBulletSpec::OrbitReleaseAimRange` only when the Orbit Lock modifier supplies the factor (derived specs reset it), so Stormwall's `ConvertOrbit` / Orbit behaviour alone keeps 0 and its tangent release, and `ReleaseOrbitingOutward` (Thunderhead / Stormwall burst) is untouched; `URHBulletSubsystem::AimOrbitRelease` runs at both release points (straight rounds and Orbit-Lock-thrown blades) and resolves one target per spec per step (cached; a volley released together costs one query), counting `OrbitAimedReleases`. Boss aim point: new `URHBossSubsystem::GetBossAimPoint` = the Warden hull while alive, else the Floodgate's existing `GetTarget` (nearest live pump hull, then frame), else new `URHBossSumpSubsystem::GetAimPoint` (nearest live nozzle hull, then the housing hull). **Deviation:** the Font's existing `GetTarget` is a stand-off point to walk to (EngageStandOff outside the hull), not a point to shoot at, so a hull-position variant was added instead of reusing it. Ownership: weapons publish their hero per spec (`URHBulletSubsystem::SetSpecOwnerPos`, main + Charge / Dash / ChainProc ids) so range and nearest enemy are measured from the round's owner in co-op (fallback `SetHeroPos`); the orbit centre itself is still the shared `HeroPos` as before (not changed here). Card text `Mod.OrbitLock.Desc` EN + zh-Hans updated (zh_overrides, Game.po via Localize + apply_zh). Tests `RogueHero.Modifiers.OrbitLockAim.*` (5): Composition (factor 1.5, reach = Range x 1.5, Stormwall 0), Boss (a live Warden at a known point, a nearer Swarm ignored: every released round within 1 deg of the Warden, releases 7 steps apart as spawned, speed unchanged), NearestEnemy (nearest of two; a 6-round volley = 1 query; co-op owner position picks the owner's nearest), NothingInRange (an enemy just beyond reach: bit-identical to the no-aim control), StormwallUnchanged (Orbit behaviour with a Warden and an enemy around flies exactly as in an empty world; outward burst unchanged). `RogueHero.Coop.SoloParity` passes unchanged (golden not re-recorded). Forced-build Warden check (editor -game on L_Arena_Test, `-RHBot`, RH.God 1, RH.Timeline off, Scatter Cannon L4 + Orbit Lock + Boomerang, Pulse Rifle L1 + Orbit Lock, RH.TimeScale 10, RH.Boss.Spawn; Warden HP min-clamped at 9600 in all runs): before (factor 0) 216.1 s / 160.9 s with 53 / 62 dps at calibration, after (1.5) 103.9 s / 128.3 s with 87 / 58 dps. Gates (worktree): Tools\Build.bat 0 warnings / 0 errors; Tools\BuildNonUnity.bat PASS (7 changed .cpp); Tools\BuildShipping.bat PASS; RogueHero Win64 Development game target Succeeded; full suite RunTests -Filter RogueHero 756 passed / 0 failed; TextAudit PASS; apply_zh --check clean; check_shipping_commands PASS.
- 2026-10-27 - **Licence exception: the designer-supplied Reclaimer model (designer decision 2026-10-27).** `SourceArt/Reclaimer/reclaimer_v1.glb` (designer file 4WGLCEXgC0sBMq0g5oKY1.glb, made with an AI 3D service whose terms allow commercial use; the service is not named) and every asset derived from it may ship as a **designer-approved exception** to the CC0 / OFL / procedural rule (Art §12, `Docs/Art/asset_licenses.md`). It is the only such exception; any further AI-service asset needs its own designer decision and row. `*.glb` goes to LFS (`.gitattributes`).
- 2026-10-27 - **Reclaimer model import: the designer model re-cut into the rigid parts (`rh.Hero.UseModel`, default 1).** `Tools\ImportReclaimerModel.bat` = `Tools/import_reclaimer_model.py` (system Python + numpy + Pillow; reads the glb as data) then `Tools/import_reclaimer_model_ue.py` (headless editor, Interchange glTF). (1) **Space / scale:** glTF Y-up, faces +Z -> hero space `(z, -x, y)`, +X forward, feet at 0, scaled so the hood crown is 180 cm (the arched back rig reaches 196.8 cm; the source's 1.135 units top is the rig lantern, not the head). (2) **Budget (deviation from the G13b <= 25 k-triangle hero, which was a non-Nanite budget):** the 1.5 M-triangle source is decimated in the converter to **200 000 triangles** (vectorised quadric half-edge collapse; UV seams only slide along themselves, open borders locked) and the meshes are **Nanite** (one hero; Art §7 keeps Nanite off only for instanced enemies). Per part: Pelvis 27 706, Torso 25 803, Rig 45 547, Head 17 046, ArmUpperL/R 7 800 / 8 932, ArmLowerL/R 7 164 / 7 385, ThighL/R 10 725 / 10 924, ShinL/R 18 846 / 19 107 (joint-band overlap included); whole body `SM_Hero_Model` 200 000. 14 mesh sections across the 12 parts (Rig: Model + RigLight + Lamp; the procedural hero has 51). Textures: base colour 4096 (BC1, 10.7 MiB full mips), normal 4096 (BC5, 21.3 MiB; glTF OpenGL green flipped on import), ORM 2048 (2.7 MiB), mask 2048 (5.3 MiB) = **40 MiB full-mip, streamed** (TEXTUREGROUP_Character); at the gameplay camera only low mips are resident. (3) **Parts:** by the source's 13 segments + joint planes (waist z 110, elbow 116, knee 56; 1.5 cm overlap band duplicated into both parts so a bent joint shows surface, not a hole). Pivots are the procedural `HERO_PARTS` (unchanged, so `RHHeroAnim` is untouched). The tabard skirt rides the pelvis (like the procedural tabard); the model's joints sit within ~5 cm of the procedural pivots (elbow ~3 cm high). (4) **Sockets:** every socket of `SM_Hero_<Part>` / `SM_Hero` is copied with the **same transform** (the emitter / hip / dock sockets are gameplay: fire origins), except `socket_Rig_Lamp`, which moves onto the model's lantern (-13.5, 0, 189); the model's own side pods sit ~30 cm behind `socket_Rig_EmitterL/R`, a visual mismatch kept on purpose (moving the sockets would change gameplay). (5) **Material** `M_Hero_Model`: textured PBR (ORM = glTF metallic-roughness), two-sided; tabard = slate-blue mask (R) tinted by **`BaseColor`**, the same parameter `ARHHero` sets on the procedural tabard (slot `Model` MIDs join `TabardMIDs`; the model keeps its own embroidered glyph, no Keeper sigil stitch and no cloth sway on the model); rig light = blue accent mask (B) on slot `RigLight`, lamp = lantern glass mask (A) on slot `Lamp`, both driven by the existing rig / lamp MIDs. (6) **Wear:** the model has no `Armor` slot; `URHReclaimerWear` sets `WearGrime / WearScuff / WearPatch` from `MI_Hero_Model_Wear1..3` on the ivory-plate mask (G) of slot `Model` (copied into the patron MID when one exists); tape / Founder decals unchanged (actor-root hero-space offsets; on the model they land on the pauldrons less exactly). (7) **Switch:** `ARHHero` finds `SM_Hero_Model_<Part>` at construction and swaps them in at BeginPlay when `rh.Hero.UseModel` is 1 (read at spawn: set it on the command line / ini, not after the map loaded); 0 or a missing part keeps the procedural parts, which stay the fallback. The dash ghost uses `SM_Hero_Model` with the model. Tests `RogueHero.Wiring.HeroModel` (both switch states, sockets, Nanite, wear MIs), `RogueHero.Wiring.HeroParts` (wear on the model slot). Readability (overcast, gameplay camera, `Saved/Cowork/hero_model/`): standing hero p90 luminance 0.44 vs floor median 0.15, the procedural hero 0.36 in the same capture; the ivory plates and the patron-tinted tabard carry the read, the dark hood is the darkest part from above. **Swap-out point:** a skinned rig of the same model (v0.2+, the G13b note).
- 2026-10-27 - **Steam checklist: crash report export, rich presence, cloud-save conflict rule (v10_polish_spec §4, D-29).** (1) **Settings → Send report** (`Platform/RHCrashReport`): zips the newest `Saved/Crashes/<folder>` that holds a minidump (every file in it), the crashed session's log when that folder has none (newest `Saved/Logs/*.log` written no later than 2 min after the dump; the live log is newer and left out) and the crashed run's `Saved/Playtests/RunStats_*.csv` (newest within 30 min before the dump, so a later run's CSV is never attached) into `Saved/CrashReports/RogueHero_report_<yyyymmdd_hhmmss>.zip`, then selects it in the file browser (`FPlatformProcess::ExploreFolder`). No network of any kind. Without a dump the row reads "No crash found" and pressing it says so; a privacy line under the list says nothing is sent automatically. **Deviation:** the zip uses the existing hand-written `RHLogExport::FZipWriter` (2026-10-21 entry: the engine's zip writer is Developer-only); same swap-out point. The older "Export logs" row (whole `Playtests/` + newest crash + live log to the Desktop) stays as the playtester bundle; folding the two rows into one is a designer call. (2) **Rich presence:** the old per-place tokens (`#Status_Vigil`, `#Status_Breach`, `#Status_BreachQuota` with English/Chinese text in the vdf) are replaced by one token `#Status_Text` = `%text%`; the game sends the line localized from `ST_UI` (`Presence.Hub`, `Presence.Run`, `Presence.RunStandard`, en + zh-Hans) with the arena's `DisplayName` and `Hub.Quota.Level`, so names stay in the string tables (TDD §12). **Deviation:** Steam friends see the line in the player's language, not their own. The arena name already carries its article ("The Breach"), so the EN line reads "In The Breach · Quota 3 · 12:40" (spec text: "In the Breach"); at Heat 0 (standard terms) the Quota segment is dropped, as the HUD does. Updated on map load and by a 10 s core ticker during a run (never more often), registered only while Steam is active; a no-op in the editor and tests. (3) **Cloud-save conflict rule** (`RHSaveFile::ResolveConflicts`, called at the top of `URHMetaSubsystem::Load` for every slot incl. `RHMeta_2`): a sibling copy is `SaveGames/<slot><. or space or ->*.sav`; only siblings that validate take part; newest file timestamp wins (within 2 s counts as equal and keeps the local copy); every other copy is moved to `<slot>.conflict` / `<slot>.<n>.conflict` (the `.corrupt` quarantine naming), a damaged local slot that loses goes to `.corrupt`. Nothing is deleted. Steam Auto-Cloud itself remains partner-site configuration (Docs/Platform/Steam.md). Tests: RogueHero.Meta.SaveFile.ConflictRule, RogueHero.Platform.CrashReport.{Window,Contents,SettingsText}, RogueHero.Platform.PresenceTokens (rewritten: clock, throttle, en lines, zh layout via the zh pattern; the compiled zh rows are checked when the .locres has them, so the localization recompile chore applies).
- 2026-10-27: **Quota 6-10 (Heat above 5), `v10_polish_spec.md` §1 / D-27.** The Heat cap is 10 (`URHHeatSelection::MaxHeat`, `URHMetaTuning::MaxHeat`; per arena, one level per win, Ichor x(1 + 0.15 Heat) so Quota 10 = x2.5; `Tools/LoopCheck.ps1 -Heat` 0-10). The five changes live in a new primary data asset `URHQuotaTuning` (type `RHQuotaTuning`, each change with its own `FromHeat`); **temporary:** the C++ class defaults are the fallback until `/Game/RH/Data/Run/DA_QuotaTuning` exists (`URHQuotaTuning::Get`; swap-out = create the asset, no code change). Every number is GATED. Readers: `FRHSpawnDirectorCore` (Q6 drop-ins: cadence x1.5, at least 2 slots, a role already landing is not picked; Q7 affix top-up; Q9 Breath = min(authored, 3 s); Q10 pairing), `URHEnemySubsystem::SetQuotaRules` (Q7 shield regen, elite-tuning rolls up to 3; Q9 walk-in chests), `URHBulletSubsystem::SetEnemyShotSpeedMult` (Q10), and the three boss subsystems through `RHQuota::ApplyToWarden/Floodgate/Font`, which only switch on new tuning fields that default to off (`FRHBossTuning::bSpiralInPhaseTwo`, `FRHBossOutfallTuning::FlushChannels`, `FRHBossSumpTuning::NozzleReopens/NozzleReopenDelay`). Heat 0-5 run byte-identical (`RogueHero.Coop.SoloParity` unchanged). Interpretations where the spec is not exact: (1) Q7 "elites may carry 3 affixes": Heat 2's +1 already lets the late 2-affix waves reach 3, so Q7 lets every elite (waves, escorts, elite-tuning rolls) roll up to 3: an elite with n < 3 gains 0..(3 - n) more on the run stream; the shield returns 4 s after it breaks [proposal]. (2) Q6 "two may be active": the slot cap rises to 2 in Harvest too, but the existing 1.5 s `MinLandingGap` fairness rule is longer than every drop-in telegraph (1.0-1.2 s), so with today's numbers two telegraphs rarely overlap; the fairness gap is kept (no shorter reading time) and the overlap is left to the GATED tuning. (3) Q8: the Floodgate's second channel is the next live pump in turn, each with its full red wall line; a reopened Font nozzle slides out of the drum as its tell and knocks no second HP share off; the Warden's P2 spiral uses the P3 spiral tuning and lane. (4) Q9: a walk-in chest counts while the hero stands in reach and resets on leaving; its light column widens with the count. (5) Q10: an event's partner is the next main event (own beat) of a different kind round the timeline list (deterministic, no draw); it brings its group, escorts and arena hook, while the beat and Breath stay the first event's; companions are not paired. Enemy rounds are x1.15 faster with life / 1.15 so their reach stays the base value; applied in `URHBulletSubsystem::SpawnEnemyBullet` (Pylon shots via the sink, every boss bullet); the interim `FRHEnemyShotPool` (no sink, tests only) is not scaled. Tests `RogueHero.Quota.*` (8): telegraph durations and HP multipliers equal the base values at every Heat 0-10 (boss tunings compared field by field except the Quota fields; a full Breach timeline run per Heat), and each change active at its level and not below.

- 2026-10-24 - **I7 / D-66 Intercession strength measurement:** final metric is marginal value: all-source damage plus Ultimate execute HP in the cast window minus the identical-length no-cast window, each from an identical seeded fresh-world start; this supersedes "Ultimate-attributed only". AttributedUltimate (cast-window Ultimate source delta + execute delta) is diagnostic only. Revert i7a3's Seek main-round Ultimate retagging to honor RHRunStats.h's creator-not-amplifier power-split contract; gameplay damage is unchanged, other attribution plumbing and derived-instance TagUltimate calls remain. Remove only the Seek-only assertion "Ultimate hunting rounds count as Ultimate" in FRHWbUltimateSeekAccountingTest; every other assertion and FRHIntercessionAccounting remain intact. ReseedAll resets HitRng, FMath Rand/SRand, enemy EliteRng and effect-manager LocalRng to RunSeed 67 before StartRun or spawning. StartRun alone seeds run streams including Combat; Ultimate GameRng delegates to the effect manager's Combat stream (LocalRng fallback). Weapon Init seeds its RNG per slot (0x5EED0000 + slot); hunters use that weapon stream and detached phantom volley streams copy it. Hunters/reactions have no independent streams; crowd uses CrowdSeed, local ultimate shapes derive deterministic seeds/positions; cosmetic FxRng/SparkRng/impact FX remain untouched. Keep one level-1 PulseRifle, TickWeapons, hunter stepping, fixed RNG-free two-pass refill, Step order and dash schedule (every 120 steps, alternating +/-X 300 uu, return after 30 steps). Fixture adaptations to the spec remain: forced variant without its key blessing (section 1.2), zero wind-up, no BeginPlay, stationary harmless full-HP mortal MakeSwarm dummies, instantaneous dash movement without velocity/cooldown simulation, Economy's Blessing.Shade.Interest + test-only Debt 10. ApplyTuning and L8 GodChoice commitment are identical in both runs. Cast window ends 120 steps (2 s) after the existing active-window end rule, capped at 1800 steps (30 s) with warning; no-cast runs exactly those Steps, with no cached controls. The tail truncates longer-lived ground effects/summons; optional I2 ShatterEnemy is absent and current layers use Hit. Assert 106 rows and exact row-0 repeat AllDelta in each mode; positivity is asserted only for the six bases (scheduler-authorized narrowing). Combination rows <= 0 are AddInfo findings counted as nonpositive; mean and RatioToMean use all 100 marginal values. No band assertion yet: spec section 1.4's +/-20% band stays GATED and supersedes section 1.6's +/-15% of base (D-66). Original per-row log format stays unchanged; separate Diag line, appended CSV AttributedUltimate and summary nonpositive field feed the Python report. Measured double-run wall_seconds=2.932 is under the 120 s budget; see Docs/Balance/intercession_strength.md; builds, suite and audits are gate-run only. No tuning, string tables, RHBalanceProbe changes or SoloParity golden re-recording.

- 2026-10-24 - **Co-op S12 bots and E2E (v04_coop_spec section 3, fix round 2).** Extended the existing URHBot in Debug/RHBot.h/.cpp with a PlayerIndex argument and isolated per-player steering/gem state; RH.Coop.Bot uses the same FAutoConsoleCommandWithWorldAndArgs registration as RH.Bot. These are the available owner inputs used by S11, not evidence of combat-buffer ownership. FRHBulletSpec and URHBulletSubsystem player bullet SoA have no Owner field/buffer; FRHHitEvent and FRHKillEvent (Core/RHTypes.h) and FRHCritEvent (Build/RHEffectTypes.h) also lack Owner. S12 does not add that S3 storage: BotDrivesP2 instead arms only P2 and asserts live URHBulletSubsystem bullets, and DiscoveriesByOwner drives ApplyRunEnd owner counters and OnSecondPlayerRunEnded independently. Added seven RogueHero.E2E.Coop cases: JoinLeave, TwoHands, Revive, BothDownRunEnd, DiscoveriesByOwner, BotDrivesP2 and BalanceTwoBots. Hub membership has no pawns: its headless fixture supplies a context/viewport for S8 membership without controller login or Slate, then separately checks arena registration via AddHeroForTest with a pool. - 2026-10-24 - **Co-op S10 downed and revive (v04_coop_spec section 3 S10, section 5, D-20).** Implemented plain per-hero Alive/Downed/BledOut state and run-local down count, with a pure clock helper. GATED defaults live only in URHRunTuning: CoopDownedMoveSpeedMult=0.25, CoopBleedOutSeconds=20 s, CoopReviveRadius=200 uu, CoopReviveSeconds=3 s, CoopReviveSecondsPerDown=5 s, CoopReviveHealthFraction=0.30, CoopReviveIFrameSeconds=1 s; existing assets inherit new defaults, no DA edits. Interpretations: downed heroes reject further damage (including enemy bullets), healing, knockback, new slows, dash and ultimate activation; crawl replaces the Linesman slow in the existing movement path rather than multiplying the two, while existing environmental displacement stays intact. Revive progress resets on leaving the inclusive 2D radius or losing the Alive partner; bleed-out continues during revive and wins a simultaneous deadline. Both clocks use fixed-step simulated seconds, already scaled once by GetSimTimeScale in the accumulator; revival reuses HurtInvulnSteps and neither clock draws RNG. A final lethal co-op hit records Downed before the immediate no-Alive defeat check; both-down and down-plus-bled-out end once with Died, while Flinch remains first and solo keeps its existing death path. Bled-out actors/player states remain registered, preserving S5 multipliers. S6 used living-only centroid/zoom/leash filtering: these now include Downed and exclude BledOut, retaining solo camera math. RHHeroes includes the registered headless partner and suppresses Downed/BledOut primary fallbacks; the legacy solo-death fallback stays for parity, and the existing drop-in living-index filter inherits the updated IsDead query. The existing SimStep weapon path was primary-only: co-op now steps each Alive hero's weapons; touched weapon/dash effect lookups resolve the owning player's GetOrCreateEffects. Per-owner HUD uses two existing ring widgets above the ultimate block (away from the S9 favor panel), simultaneously showing remaining bleed-out and filling revive progress, with opaque dark backing and neutral/cyan arcs exceeding 3:1; new ST_UI labels have EN and zh-Hans rows and PO entries. S7 hands and level-up timing remain unchanged for downed/bled-out players; no fit-reader, seeds, streams, golden or new VFX assets changed. Added 14 RogueHero.Coop.Downed cases covering solo, host/guest down, weapon and bullet guards, targeting, revival/i-frames, repeat/reset, permanent bleed-out and retained multipliers, defeat once, cheat death, all seven tuning overrides, sim clocks, deadline tie and no hard revive limit. New test .cpp requires two Build.bat gate runs; editor, build/non-unity, full automation including unchanged SoloParity, text/localization gates and compiled localization remain for cowork as instructed.

- 2026-10-23 - **Co-op S6 shared camera (v04_coop_spec S3, D-23).** Implemented living centroid follow with the existing follow interpolation, Primary fallback when nobody is living, and linear FOV zoom/return. URHRunTuning owns CoopLeashWidthFraction (0.8), CoopLeashSoftWallFraction (0.1), CoopZoomBaseFOV (50), CoopZoomWideFOV (56), CoopZoomSeparationFraction (0.5), and CoopZoomSeconds (1.0); no DA asset edits. Interpretation: visible width uses the base FOV and ARHCamera's height/pitch offset distance, excluding shake, so neither threshold moves during zoom. The leash uses a linear soft-wall ramp over its last 10 percent, then a hard endpoint clamp at max(leash, current separation). Tangential displacement is retained wherever geometrically possible; at the boundary even a purely sideways straight step increases distance, so the hard cap takes precedence and clips the radial endpoint (an exceptional tangent longer than the radius requires endpoint projection). This resolves the finite-step conflict between unchanged sideways motion and an absolute separation cap without pull-back over time. Both frame and fixed-step movement use the same filter, including dash, knockback and environmental displacement; fixed steps also move the second hero. Solo camera math and movement expressions remain untouched behind the hero-count branch, with no leash float math or RNG changes; leaving co-op returns FOV to base before handing it back. Split-screen is disabled. Pure RogueHero.Coop.Camera tests cover leash, width, threshold and bidirectional zoom. Round-1 failures were supplied in the brief; no gate log exists in this worktree. Build, non-unity, SoloParity (golden unchanged), automation and text gates are left to cowork as required.

- 2026-10-23 - Co-op S4 targeting (v04_coop_spec sections 3 and 6): enemy seek/aim and all three bosses select through RHHeroes::Nearest (living only), with the Primary fallback via RHHeroes::Target. The Rusher (Courser) wind-up stores PlayerIndex per enemy; wind-up, charge and chained charges resolve that lock through GetHeroByIndex; an invalid or dead target falls back to Nearest then Primary without changing an already drawn charge direction. Drop-ins alternate living PlayerIndex order by a deterministic per-run round-robin counter, reset by director Init, with no RNG draws; the chosen index survives the landing queue, including Carrier launch. The ring dodge lane is laid relative to the aimed hero's bearing (`In.HeroPos` from `RHHeroes::Target`), with the unchanged Playtest-3 offset rule in solo and co-op alike, including each cross pair's existing lane behavior and RNG consumption. This reads spec section 3 S4 "centred on the bearing" as the layout reference so that GDD section 3.5 / CLAUDE.md 1 hold. Enemy Step and Render each capture one living-hero position/index snapshot with the same Primary fallback; per-enemy selection uses NearestIndex and preserves PlayerIndex order and Charger locks. This is a call-pattern change, with no performance measurement claimed. The existing main-lane upper-bound literal is named in FRHBossBrain for shared use by the unchanged arithmetic and RingBearing assertions; cross bounds still come from tuning. Damage, knockback and slow remain primary-only; Outfall/Sump hazard inputs retain the primary position separately from aim. No golden, text, fit reader or GATED number changes. Build, non-unity, automation and SoloParity verification are deferred to cowork gates as instructed.

- 2026-10-23 - Co-op counters and summary (v04_coop_spec sections 2, 3 S3, 7): add observational per-player kills, damage, offer picks and Discovery credit in RHRunCounters, and serialized FRHRunSummary::Players with player builds. Solo keeps the old fields and layout; empty Players derives a host entry. Save v5 adds the last summary with the existing checksum/save path and raise-only migration; guest columns are historical display data only, with player 1+ profile banking deferred. Hit-owner plumbing is blocked: this checkout's S3 row assigns bullet Owner, hit/kill/crit ownership and status appliers to the event step, but Core/RHTypes and Combat/RHBulletSubsystem have none. - 2026-10-23 - I1 Shade second-Keeper layers (D-65, intercession_variants_spec S1.3/S2.1): Storm execution BFS applies Mark/Shock to nearest living neighbours (distance then slot ties), Forge Marks Burn and capped execution blasts snapshot victims, Tide caches Marked targets for gradual pull/Chill and adds the Frozen threshold bonus, Radiance replaces threshold execution with capped missing-HP always-crit lances and next-tick kill novas, and Quicksilver sweeps dash segments for executions with one ResetDash refund per serial. All layer tuning lives in DA_IntercessionLayer_Shade_<Second>_CodeFallback Params, keyed Shade.Storm / Shade.Forge / Shade.Tide / Shade.Radiance / Shade.Quicksilver with second-Path parameter prefixes and LayerExecuteBelowMult (GATED, D-66); variant ExecuteBelow remains intact before multiplication and the additive TideFrozenExecuteBonus. Radiance bounds additions with RadianceMaxLances; Quicksilver funds dash additions through the lower closing threshold. The +/-20 percent band remains a balance gate, not a measured result here. ApplyStatus writes Mark synchronously before queuing reaction notifications, so TryExecute sees it in the same BFS; only damage-kill observation needs the next tick. Existing URHEnemySubsystem::GetHP/GetMaxHP provide missing HP (guarded by alive and matching status-target ownership); no missing HP query or requires probe is needed. Existing IRHStatusTarget::PullEnemyToward accepts distance and adds a mass-scaled impulse calibrated to the sim's knock decay: repeated TideDragPerSec * Dt calls supply gradual drag over the cached Marked set, with a decaying tail rather than teleportation. No new enemy-side declaration or missing/probed function is needed. Radiance novas use a share of the recorded lance including its crit multiplier, like Sun Lance's documented overkill approximation; dead lances are snapshotted before nova hits to avoid recursion. A detached/non-enemy status target without the concrete HP getters safely receives no lances. Five new tests use real enemy HP/status/movement, L8 commitments and negative gates, plus caps, wind-up, variant thresholds and dash serials. Existing layer-table assertions now validate Shade data. No Enemies files, string tables or goldens changed; no golden re-record. Build/non-unity, full automation including SoloParity, TextAudit and D-66 strength measurements remain for cowork gates as required by the job brief.

- 2026-10-23 - I1 Tide second-Keeper layers (intercession_variants_spec sections 1.3/2.1): implemented Storm current, Forge thaw bursts, Shade Mark/Seek/execute, Radiance round charging and Quicksilver dash shatters over each variant's Slots or the Stillness pool. Begin-time hits read LayerWaveDamageMult directly from the resolved second-layer definition, with same-key variant Params winning; no layer returns exactly 1 and Begin/ApplySecondKeeperLayer ordering is unchanged. RadianceLensPierce = 1 is a GATED placeholder: the spec gives no count. Zones refresh from living members after freeze and clear at thaw, End and BeginDestroy; the charge already on a round lasts for that round's lifetime. ResetDash is the refund, once per 10 cumulative shatters. Breakwater applies Chill, not Freeze: Surf is intentionally a weak pairing unless another source Freezes a wall member (D-66 tuning review). Forge snapshots capped live burst centres and deduplicates their victims, after removing Frost's damage-taken multiplier. Five added tests cover pre-L8 base output, matching and other seconds, variant shapes, non-patron exclusion, charge damage/pierce, Seek preference, thaw timing and dash refunds. - 2026-10-23 I2 enemy side - `intercession_variants_spec.md` sections 2, 2.1 and 4/I2: inspected main section 17; the I1a note supplies no enemy query signatures or thaw-notification probe, so no naming conflict and no thaw event added. Public caller-data types: `FRHMarkedDragRules` (Speed uu/s, ChillInterval seconds, ChillStacks, ChillDuration seconds, FrozenExecuteBonus fraction; zero disables), `FRHFrozenLineSegment` (A, B, HalfWidth), `FRHHeldFreezeMember` (Enemy slot, Pos snapshot). Exact new URHEnemySubsystem contracts: `void SetMarkedDragRules(FName Source, const FRHMarkedDragRules& Rules)` / `void ClearMarkedDragRules(FName Source)` replace/remove sources and fold maxima, minimum positive valid Chill cadence; cadence resets when the folded interval changes and starts after one interval. `int32 QueryEnemiesAlongSegment(const FVector2f& A, const FVector2f& B, float HalfWidth, TArray<int32>& Out, int32 MaxCount) const` appends alive non-submerged hull/capsule contacts in hash order and returns appended count; caller reserves output capacity, no query scratch allocation. `void FreezeEnemiesAlongSegment(FName Source, const FVector2f& A, const FVector2f& B, float HalfWidth, float FreezeSeconds)` applies Freeze through ApplyStatusKind and adds only actual freezes to an independent source hold (positive caller duration; wards/bosses convert to Cracked and are excluded); no Deep Reserve cap or release-on-hit applies to this hold. `void ReleaseDashPathFreeze(FName Source, TArray<FRHHeldFreezeMember>& Out)` appends live frozen members and current positions, removes only that source's hold, does not thaw or damage; other holds remain, otherwise normal thaw resumes. Shatter damage and timers remain layer-owned, with no enemy-side damage constant. `void SetFrozenLine(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FRHFrozenLineSegment> Segments, TConstArrayView<int32> Members)` replaces caller wave geometry and unique actual frozen members; `void ClearFrozenLine(FName Source)` removes it. `int32 QueryFrozenLineMembers(FName Source, TArray<int32>& Out, int32 MaxCount) const` appends still-frozen members; `int32 QueryFrozenLineAlongSegment(FName Source, const FVector2f& A, const FVector2f& B, float HalfWidth, TArray<int32>& Out, int32 MaxCount) const` appends the hash-backed non-submerged hull contacts belonging to that line. `bool SegmentCrossesFrozenLine(FName Source, const FVector2f& A, const FVector2f& B, float HalfWidth = 0.f) const` tests capsule intersection against the registered geometry while any frozen member survives; A == B tests a point. Natural/explicit thaw, wards, death and slot reuse prune membership; KillAll clears all wave lists. Drag is external velocity like Current, including on Frozen enemies, scaled by inverse mass, skipping anchored/flying types, bounded at the hero hull, and never arms PulledLeft. The +10 percent means +0.10 max-HP threshold fraction supplied by caller data, before the existing Mark magnitude multiplication; base execute must be enabled and bosses remain immune; render brackets mirror it. No status kind added; uint8 masks unchanged; no RNG added; crush unchanged. Six automation tests cover drag/statuses, segment queries, holds, line lifecycle and disabled-rule parity; execution and both builds deferred to cowork gates as instructed. Audit found no gameplay HP-loss bypass: Outfall surge, Sump combined hazards and enum-labelled enemy bullets already use this function; removed the Warden and Outfall compatibility probes. The bypasses were RH.Debug.HeroHP lowering (now source debug) and two Flinch test setups (now test, with hurt i-frames expired before the original assertions); Spring Tide's HP refund and debug raises now use Heal. New diagnostic sources follow contact Enemy.<Role>, shots <Role>, Boss.<Arena> <attack> or Hazard.<Arena> <what>, with no comma, semicolon, equals or quote; existing Warden/enemy fiction-bearing names remain unchanged per job scope. Enemy bullets only know Turret/Boss/Other, retaining shots Turret/shots Boss/shots other; Sump exposes one combined HeroDamage value, retaining Boss.Sump hazards. Debug lowering previously lost exactly D = HP - max(1, fraction * MaxHP), bypassing armor and i-frames; now a vulnerable hero loses max(1, D - Armor), e.g. HP 100 to target 50 with Armor 3 loses 47 instead of 50 (HP 53 instead of 50), while an invulnerable/dead hero loses 0 instead of D; hurt protection goes from 0 to 0.4 s, Flinch can leave 1 HP with 1 s protection, and the existing budgeted 6 uu/0.15 s shake applies. Gameplay damage values/timings are unchanged. LoopCheck previously parsed only the Enemies death report (absent for a boss/bullet killing hit); it now reads Hero died's killing blow through the final period, preserving spaces/dots. DmgMin already comes from RunStats RecordDamage, not HP deltas; the old by-source display used separate minute-10/15 snapshots, not the run-end/explicit-sample window. LoopCheck now pairs by-source output with each DmgMin block and displays the fixed-minute snapshots for both Outfall and Sump. The historical none snapshots could not be reproduced in this sandbox. Added RogueHero.Wiring.HeroDamageAttribution covering all damage dispatch entries and a killing boss bullet, checking RecentHits and RunStats after each entry. Validation: the round-1 cowork gates reported Build.bat PASS (0 warnings), BuildNonUnity.bat PASS, TextAudit PASS and allowed-files PASS; that suite was 567 passed / 1 failed (HeroDamageAttribution), not unverified. Round 2 reproduced the isolated failure: debug had no hit, debug raise did not heal, and healing recorded 0 rather than 1 hit (automation-reported RHGameModeWiringTests.cpp lines 458/565/568); the lightweight world had not registered the possessed controller, so RHHeroes::Primary returned null. The test now registers and verifies that controller, asserts fresh-hero preconditions, uses public Warden SpawnBoss/Step with bounded telegraph/radius/hit waits, and keeps all source/RunStats/killing-blow assertions. HeroDamageAttribution PASS both alone and in the full suite after direct MSVC compilation/relink with the existing UBT response files; both changed translation units compile alone with 0 warnings. Full sandbox suite: 568 run, 566 passed / 2 failed (E2E.PIE.AbandonToHub: telemetry unreadable/run_end abandoned missing; UI.LogExport: Desktop ZIP write failed). TextAudit PASS (0 violations). Current Build.bat/BuildNonUnity.bat attempts stalled at UBT startup; the UBT executable exposed denied access to the user-profile trace backup, so these round-2 script gates and an all-green suite remain required by CLAUDE.md. This does not supersede the successful round-1 build gates. Outfall/Sump LoopCheck reruns were not completed in the sandbox; parser fixtures passed in round 1.

- 2026-10-22 - D-71 step 1 (playtest v0.2 #3): `URHSpawnTimeline::ApplySwarmDensityD71` raises each block's Swarm absolute spawn rate x1.5, keeps other roles' absolute rates, and renormalises weights to their original sum: f = (S + 0.5w) / S, Rate *= f, Swarm weights *= 1.5/f, others *= 1/f (f = 1 for w = 0 or S <= 0). Caps x1.5, clamped to 400: Breach/Outfall 90 / 180 / 300 / 400; Sump 105 / 150 / 360 / 400 at 0:00 / 3:00 / 9:00 / 15:00. `RH.Spawn.WriteFactoryAssets` patches Main (Breach), Outfall and Sump assets idempotently per block. `patch_outfall_timeline.py` and `patch_sump_timeline.py` now carry stale caps and Swarm weights and must not be re-run without updating. The 15:00+ cap of 400 sits at the S14 400-enemy budget, is not yet profiled, and is to be measured in step 2. Elites, events, drop-ins, boss, scaling, XP and the 1200 s run stay unchanged; no SoloParity golden refresh. Validation in the cowork sandbox: TextAudit and diff checks pass.

- 2026-10-22 - Zero-damage audit: the production Compose caller (URHWeaponInstance::RebuildSpec, including RH.Give and effect grants) uses Merge; blessing Orbit ranks are 1.3 and Orbit Lock keeps the spec default 1.0, so no shipped zero Orbit source was found. The confirmed display cause is RHDamageNumbersWidget rounding positive fractional damage below 0.5 to 0 (e.g. Scatter L1 + Live Wire rank 2 third ground-return hit: 3 * 0.6^3 * 0.5 = 0.324 HP); ApplyHits also fed pre-overkill damage instead of LastHitDealt. The allowed pool now rounds positive HP loss to at least 1 and ApplyHits feeds LastHitDealt: dealt > 0 shows >= 1; dealt <= 0 shows none, with the >20 kills/s aggregation and text formatting retained. An unset (<= 0) behaviour / modifier damage multiplier means its neutral value; Orbit release always in [1.0, 1.3] (shared limits), and unset Chain ground return means its 0.5 UPROPERTY default. Normalize before stacking and guard unmerged Compose inputs; archetype tuning is unchanged. Mine pull (0.05), Cracked (+20%), enemy mitigation, Momentum additive stacks and Stillness charge defaults audit positive; the widget formatting cause is handled in the allowed pool, with no remaining outside-allowed cause open. Regression coverage includes all fallback weapons at L1/L8/L9, single modifiers, Orbit sources, unset/stacked behaviours, exact released-hit scales and fractional/absorbed/overkill numbers. Validation: TextAudit passes; Build.bat and all five BuildNonUnity single-file invocations failed before compilation with UBT exit -532462766; no project binary exists here, so the new tests and full automation suite remain unverified in this sandbox.
- 2026-10-23 - R3 / D-58 resonance display: `RHOfferArchetypes::GetCommittedArchetypes` reads the authority game mode's offer flow. This is valid because co-op is local (v0.4, one process with authority; online is out of scope; scheduler decision). On a client or without authority it returns empty (no mark). No per-player offer-flow accessor exists: PlayerIndex 0 maps to `GetOfferFlow()` / `GetPlayer()`; every other index returns empty. Swap-out point: if online co-op is ever scoped, read a replicated per-player run state instead.

- 2026-10-22 - R11 released-round damage (keeper_identity_spec 3.7, D-63) is implemented as `FRHBulletSpec::ReleaseDamageMult` plus a `StateReleased` bullet bit, applied through `FRHHitEvent::DamageScale`. - 2026-10-22 - **Card text floor and runtime window selection (pt2c).** Rules retain unscaled Body Medium Rule13; rich-text prepass measurements drive the existing reclaimed-window layout and downward growth within the current foot margin. Bid lines use Rule13, and the tag strip wraps into two lines above the studs. Text veils and contrast sampling use the resulting rectangles. Art ?3 wins over fixed card proportions (Art ?12, rule 2); card height is unchanged. Runtime and CPU window checks share the card-derived hue, Keeper, stele and Card.Id selector; generated window cache keys retain every hue component and weapon id.

- 2026-10-22 - **Aimed fan lane classification (scheduler decision 2026-10-22; playtest v0.2 #5; GDD sections 3.5/6).** Aimed fans with emitted spread < 90 degrees are measured over the full circle around their emitter; a clear arc wider than a semicircle contains a clear diameter. Rings, spirals and fans >= 90 degrees retain the old measurement. All failing LaneReport rows, including Outfall/Sump, now fail the suite; the table reports maximum emitted fan spread and narrow-fan volley count. Floodgate Spit retains its 3-shot, 16-degree, on-aim shape. The Font's Conduit.Tide gets a non-emission sector from its GATED FRHBossLaneRule (including margin), reusing the boss spiral sector conversion; time and LaneRotationFraction move it slowly within one side of the fan, away from the on-aim slot and outer slots, without RNG draws. Storm and Quicksilver need no brain lane. Slot settings, fan angles, timing, follow-ups, HP and damage are unchanged. The Sump definition asset is absent in this worktree: fallback spreads are Storm 60, Quicksilver 16 and Tide 150 degrees; rebuilt runtime measurements remain required.

- 2026-10-22 - S17: Warden Storm/Tide FanLane uses the integer aim index from FanOffset; the aim offset is exactly zero. All three fallback forms copy the base lane rule (600 uu, 3 hero diameters); with CDO hero radius 40 uu and form bullet radius 16 uu, MinLaneAngle=0.457307 rad. Storm K=1 gives a 32 deg (0.558505 rad) gap; Tide K=4 gives a 30 deg (0.523599 rad) gap on either side. Their derived free chords are 298.765 uu and 278.583 uu; new widths were not measured in this sandbox. The supplied gate report measured Storm 298.765 uu and Tide 30.804 uu before the aim-index fix. Every flagged Warden lane-report row now errors, including invalid samples; Outfall/Sump rows warn. Hero radius is cached from GetDefault<ARHHero>()->GetBodyRadius() after Reset assignment; swap out to a hero body radius field in FRHBossInput or FRHBossTuning. Art S3.3 follow-up: RenderKeeperTelegraph still draws Storm fork strips and Tide item arcs for emptied items; FanLane is the swap-out point when telegraph edits are allowed. Spiral is not recorded as an accepted deviation.

- 2026-10-18 — Bullets: SoA + Niagara (not Actors, not Mass). Enemies: pooled Actors + SoA manager. No GAS. CommonUI. en + zh-Hans from day one. UE 5.8.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week-1 code-driven fallbacks.** No `.uasset` content exists yet, so Week 1 is built entirely in C++; each item below is a swap-out point, not a design change.
  - (a) Bullets, enemies and XP gems render via instanced static meshes of engine basic shapes (`ARHInstanceRenderer`) instead of Niagara array DI. The SoA sim is unchanged; swap the renderer later.
  - (b) Enemies are SoA + ISM from day one (the §6 upgrade path) rather than pooled actors. Hit-flash is a scale pop instead of a MID.
  - (c) Enhanced Input actions and IMCs are built in C++ (`URHInputConfig`) using the `IA_`/`IMC_` names, until the assets exist.
  - (d) Weapon and enemy defs have C++ factory fallbacks (`URHWeaponDef::MakePulseRifle`, `URHEnemyDef::MakeSwarm` — was `MakeHarpy`) until `DA_` assets exist.
  - (e) Bullets are dense swap-remove arrays rather than a free list; enemies keep a free list so hit-history indices stay valid.
  - (f) The GameMode builds the arena (floor, lights, props) at runtime. Maps are empty, created by `Tools/create_arena_map.py`.
  - (g) Debug Canvas HUD (`ARHHUD`) until the CommonUI HUD in Week 2.
  - (h) ~~Placeholder auto-upgrade: each level-up raises the Pulse Rifle one level until the offer screen exists (Week 2).~~ **Resolved 2026-10-18** (Week 2 offer loop, `URHOfferFlow`): level-ups open the 5-card offer; `ARHGameMode::OnHeroLevelUp` only logs.
  - (i) IDE: JetBrains Rider + MSVC Build Tools (VS 18) instead of Visual Studio; UE 5.8.3.
- 2026-10-18 — First playtest: camera widened from FOV 32° / 2 300 uu (only ≈ 1 500 uu visible) to FOV 50° / 2 600 uu. Single key directional light (a fill light triggered the forward-shading priority warning). `CommonGameViewportClient` set as the game viewport client for CommonUI input routing. Verified: 300 enemies + 3 000 bullets at ≈ 470 FPS on an RTX 4080.

Lumen (SW RT) + VSM + Nanite (environment) turned **on**, reversing the Day-0 grey-box settings; target GPU raised to RTX 4070-class @ 1080p/60 Epic with a 3060 scalability tier. Bullets render as tracer ribbons; swarm enemies stay ISM (the Week-1 fallback is now the intended path); VFX are physically plausible but colour-coded per readability rules. Environment art pass is a Week-6 item that may slip to v0.2.
- 2026-10-18 — Pillar 1 is **爽 with teeth** (GDD §3.5): harvest/threat/breath rhythm authored in the spawn timeline; threats are skill checks not HP walls; difficulty lives in the build (swarm TTK ≤ 0.3 s for a steered build); hard anti-fatigue rules for shake/hit-stop/VFX/audio; `RH.Stats` metrics reviewed every playtest.
- 2026-10-18 — World & story decided (`04_Narrative.md`): original pantheon (the Six Keepers, dual names), Greek placeholder dropped. Path `Underworld` renamed `Shade`. **Code is keyed by Path and enemy role; all fiction names are FText only.** Narrative in v0.1 is string-table text only (names, 18 tier lines, 15 Recollections, 7 Codex entries, hub labels); no dialogue system. Codex rows carry a story `Layer` (0–4).
- 2026-10-18 — **Version control is local-only** (user decision): `git init` + LFS on `main` in `E:\RogueHero`, no GitHub remote, nothing pushed. Work happens on `dev/<topic>` branches (worktree sessions) and is merged into `main` locally by the scheduler session after a green `Tools\Build.bat`. Swap-out point: add a remote and switch to PRs whenever the user wants one.
- 2026-10-18 — **Re-key to Path / role done** (Narrative §13). Tags: `God.Zeus/Hephaestus/Poseidon/Hades/Apollo/Hermes` → `God.Storm/Forge/Tide/Shade/Radiance/Quicksilver`; `Enemy.Harpy/Hoplite/Centaur/Gorgon/Cyclops/Siren/Boss.Asterion` → `Enemy.Swarm/Tank/Rusher/Turret/Bruiser/Support/Boss`. Code: `URHEnemyDef::MakeHarpy` → `MakeSwarm` (`DA_Enemy_Swarm`, display text key `Enemy_Swarm` = "Scrapwing", still `NSLOCTEXT` until the string tables land), `HarpyDef/HarpyType/GetHarpyType` → `Swarm*`; behaviour-enum and tag DevComments use role/Path names. No Greek identifiers remain in `Source/`, `Config/`, `Tools/`.
- 2026-10-18 — **String tables are CSV, registered from C++** (TDD §3/§12 name `ST_` assets). `ST_UI`, `ST_Keepers`, `ST_Enemies` live as text-diffable `Content/RH/Text/ST_*.csv` (`Key,SourceString,DevNotes`) registered with `LOCTABLE_FROMFILE_GAME` in `RHStringTables::Register()` (game-module startup); table ID = namespace, so a row's text id is `ST_UI,HUD.HP` and code reads it with `LOCTABLE("ST_UI", "HUD.HP")`. The CSVs are staged as loose files (`DirectoriesToAlwaysStageAsUFS=RH/Text`). Translations: `Content/Localization/Game/zh-Hans/Game.po` is the translator-edited source; `Tools\Localize.bat` runs the Dashboard target `Game` (gather → import .po → export .po → compile .locres, configs `Config/Localization/Game_*.ini`, target in `DefaultEditor.ini`); manifest, archives, .po and .locres are committed. Swap-out point: when UMG widgets need to reference tables in the editor, import each CSV into a `ST_` string-table asset with the same ID/namespace/keys (no text ids change) and drop its `LOCTABLE_FROMFILE_GAME` line. Verified: gather finds all 28 rows; `RH.Loc.Check` (new console command) prints en and zh-Hans in a `-game -nullrhi` run under both `-culture=en` and `-culture=zh-Hans`. Also: the debug HUD's god-mode badge text is now "INVULNERABLE / 无敌" (`HUD.Invulnerable`) so no table row says "god"; the right-hand debug stat block (FPS, timings, counts) stays unlocalised `FString` developer text until the CommonUI HUD (fallback (g)); the `Weapon_PulseRifle` `NSLOCTEXT` waits for `ST_Weapons` (Week 3 Thu).
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 1 Mon: realistic render settings + dusk rig + input split.** `DefaultEngine.ini` now: Lumen GI + reflections (SW RT, mesh distance fields on, HWRT off), `r.AllowStaticLighting=False`, Nanite on, VSM on, TSR, no motion blur, manual (fixed) exposure, bloom on, lens flares off; `[SystemSettings] r.MotionBlurQuality=0`, `r.SceneColorFringeQuality=0` so no scalability level can re-enable smear/CA. Dusk rig is `ARHArenaLighting` (`Core/RHArenaLighting`): one movable sun (42° elevation, 4 600 K, 12° source angle for soft VSM penumbrae, atmosphere sun), SkyAtmosphere with extra Mie haze, real-time-capture SkyLight ×4 (ambient dominates), thin height + volumetric fog, unbound post-process (manual exposure +0.3, MB 0, fringe 0, DOF off via focal distance 0, bloom threshold 1.5, vignette 0.25, grain 0.04). Spawned from `ARHGameMode::BuildArena` (fallback f; swap-out: place the rig in `L_Arena_*`). **Single key light rule kept**: the sun is the only directional light; fill is the SkyLight. Deviations: (1) sun at 42°, not a true dusk-low angle — ArtDirection §2 asks for *short, soft* shadows, which a low sun cannot give; the dusk read comes from colour temperature and the sky. (2) Light values are in exposure-0 units (like the Day-0 key light), not real-world lux; retune when `T_LUT_Dusk` exists (swap-out: `ColorGradingLUT` on the rig's post component). (3) Game Default Map stays `L_Arena_Test` until `L_Hub` exists. Input: the single code IMC split into `IMC_Gameplay_KBM` + `IMC_Gameplay_Pad` (both added, §11) and the D-pad now moves like the stick. **Measured** (`-csvCaptureFrames`, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, 1920×1080 windowed, Epic scalability, Development editor binary `-game`): idle arena GPU 2.54 ms median / 2.96 p95, GT 1.23 / 1.65; `RH.Spawn 400` + `RH.Bullets 5000` (≈ 5 880 GPU-scene instances; the piercing stress bullets kill most enemies within seconds, so this is a bullet-heavy load, not 400 live enemies) GPU 2.92 / 4.05, GT 2.00 / 2.44, frame 3.39 / 4.34. Not yet measured: a 4070-class GPU, 400 *live* enemies with VSM shadows (Week 1 Sat).
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 1 Tue: bullets are emissive tracers (fallback (a) extended) + oldest-first recycling.** (1) New material `Content/RH/Materials/Tracer/M_Tracer` (own folder; built headless and reproducibly by `Tools\CreateTracerMaterial.bat` → `Tools/create_tracer_material.py`, so edit the script, not the asset): unlit, **additive**, used-with-ISM, translucent velocity on (TSR reprojects instead of ghosting). Emissive = (Color·facing^1.5·t^1.5 + facing^4·t^6)·Intensity, where t runs tail→head along local X and facing = N·V, i.e. a white-hot head with a cool coloured streak fading out behind it. Colour is per-instance custom data 0–2 when the ISM provides it (future per-weapon hue), else the per-renderer `Color` parameter. `ARHInstanceRenderer::Setup` gained an overload `(Mesh, Color, bCastShadow, MaterialPath, Intensity)` (the old signature is unchanged; falls back to the tint material with a warning if the asset is missing); emissive renderers are excluded from Lumen/DF/ray tracing. `URHBulletSubsystem::Render` draws each bullet as the engine sphere stretched to width = 1.5 × radius and length = width + speed × 0.045 s (Bolt ≈ 18 × 99 uu), head half a width ahead of the sim position. **Deviation:** a stretched ISM with a gradient material, not a Niagara ribbon (Roadmap Tue, TDD §5.3, Art §7); the trail is analytic (velocity-aligned), not a history ribbon, so it does not curve with Homing/Orbit. Swap-out point: the Niagara array-DI ribbon system (`NS_Bullet_*`) replaces `ARHInstanceRenderer` for bullets; the SoA sim does not change. (2) Archetype colours (Art §2): Bolt cyan (0.05, 0.75, 1.0)×12, Pellet cool white (0.8, 0.92, 1.0)×10, **Lance pale gold (1.0, 0.92, 0.65)×10 — was warm orange-gold (3, 2, 0.3)**, EnemyOrb magenta-red (1.0, 0.06, 0.35)×10. Known gap: Art §3.2 wants enemy tracers with a *dark core*; additive cannot darken, so `EnemyOrb` (not spawned in Week 1) needs its own translucent/modulated material when enemy fire lands (Week 4). (3) **Cap recycling is oldest-first (§5.2)**, replacing round-robin-by-index (wrong under swap-remove): a spawn-order FIFO of live indices with a back-pointer per bullet (`SpawnQueue`/`QueueSlot`), patched on swap-remove, holes skipped at the head, compacted when it reaches 2 × cap; amortised O(1), no per-bullet objects. `FRHBulletSpec::bFusion` (new, default false) bullets are re-queued instead of recycled unless every live bullet is a fusion bullet. **Verified** (window captures of `-game` at 1920×1080 DPI-scaled to 2880×1620, RTX 4080): the Pulse Rifle bolt reads as a cyan streak with a white head and a soft fading tail; peak luminance 0.90 against a floor of 0.025, about **12.7 : 1** (Art §3.9 asks ≥ 3 : 1). The tail fades into the floor by design. Bloom: the floor between stress tracers is about 60 % brighter with bloom on than with `r.BloomQuality 0` (p10 luminance 0.031 vs 0.019). The halo around a single bolt is subtle (rig bloom intensity 0.6, threshold 1.5, unchanged). Dense Pellet fields clip to white (still cool, no hue). **Measured** (same command as the Mon line, `RH.God 1, RH.Spawn 400, RH.Bullets 5000`, 600 frames): GPU 3.45 ms median / 4.14 p95 (Mon 2.92 / 4.05), GT 2.11 / 2.94, frame 3.83 / 4.68. Another session was running at the same time, so treat the ≈ +0.5 ms GPU for additive tracers as an upper bound. Smoke `RH.Spawn 300, RH.Bullets 3000` passes.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 1 Thu: physical hit/kill FX with density LOD** (ArtDirection §3.5, §5.3 hit-FX array, §14 effect LOD). `URHImpactFXSubsystem` (`Combat/RHImpactFXSubsystem`) is fed once per sim step from the game mode's `HitBuffer` + `KillBuffer` (after `ApplyHits`) and keeps four SoA pools with fixed caps, no Actor per particle: **sparks** 1 500 (ballistic streaks stretched along velocity, 0.1–0.35 s; 2 per hit, max 48 hits/step sampled evenly across the buffer, 6 radial per kill), **debris** 600 (dark hull chunks, gravity + floor bounce + tumble, 1.4–2.2 s; 3 per kill), **smoke** 192 (puff that rises/expands, 0.7–1.0 s; 1 per kill), **ground haze** 24 patches. Rolling kills/s is measured inside the subsystem over the last 60 sim steps (1 s); **LOD dense above 20 kills/s, back to full below 16** (hysteresis): per-kill sparks culled, hit sparks capped at 12 hits/step, debris 2 per kill, and smoke merges into the nearest haze patch (≤ 220 uu, else a new one) that decays with a 1 s e-folding time. No hit-stop or shake is added (swarm kills, GDD §3.5). Cosmetic RNG is its own stream so FX never perturb the seeded run `Rng`. `GetStats()` returns pool counts, kills/s, LOD state, sparks culled and pool overflows for `RH.Stats` (not wired yet: `RHConsoleCommands` is owned by another branch); CSV stats `RHImpactFX/*` and the `RH.FX.Impact 0|1` CVar (A/B profiling) are defined in the subsystem. **Fallback (j), swap-out: one GPU Niagara system `NS_ImpactFX` driven by the same per-step event array.** Rendered through four `ARHInstanceRenderer`s (no shadows): spark colour is white-hot with a slight cool cast (never warm — Art §3.2, GDD §3.5); because the BasicShapeMaterial is opaque and lit, sparks currently read beige-white rather than emissive (same limitation as the bullets; they inherit whatever emissive path the renderer gains), smoke "fades" by shrinking, and haze is a 3 uu-thin disc a shade above the floor albedo (a dust layer that never hides enemies, gems or telegraphs) instead of translucent fog. **LOD verified** from the log transition line (`ImpactFX LOD -> dense … / -> full …`) and the CSV `RHImpactFX/Dense` + pool counters (in dense frames the smoke pool only drains while haze patches appear; per-kill spark emission is skipped by construction), plus screenshots of both states. **Measured** (`-csvCaptureFrames=3000`, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, 1920×1080 windowed, Epic, Development editor `-game`, `RH.God, RH.Spawn 400, RH.Bullets 5000`; kills/s peaks at 400, ≈ 370 frames in dense LOD): whole capture frame 3.44 ms median / 4.43 p95, GT 2.12 / 2.71, GPU 2.96 / 4.11; dense frames frame 3.94 / 4.81, GT 2.42 / 2.95, GPU 3.71 / 4.21. Subsystem cost: step ≤ 0.008 ms p95 (max 0.35), render (ISM sync) ≤ 0.057 ms p95. Peak pools: sparks 433, debris 600 (cap hit, extra chunks dropped), smoke 16, haze 21. A/B with `RH.FX.Impact 0` (same load, dense frames): frame 4.04 / 4.65, GT 2.39 / 2.95, GPU 3.66 / 4.10 — the FX difference is within run-to-run noise. Not measured: a 4070-class GPU.
- 2026-10-18 — **`RH.Stats` reports the GDD §3.5 metrics** (`Debug/RHRunStats`, a tickable world subsystem; producers push events: kills from `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits`, dashes and damage from `ARHHero`, so `ARHGameMode` is untouched). Definitions, all over a rolling 60 s window of *simulated* time (scales with `RH.TimeScale`; shorter than 60 s early in a run, rates are normalised to per-minute): **kills/min** = all enemy deaths from bullet hits (`RH.KillAll` not counted); **swarm TTK** = sim time from an enemy's *first damaging hit* to its death, role `Swarm` only (tag `Enemy.Swarm`), median (the ok/LOW verdict) and p90, 60 Hz step resolution, so a one-shot reads 0 — spawn→death is reported alongside as "lifetime" but is not the §3.5 metric, because enemies spawn 1 300–1 650 uu out and walk in for seconds, which a ≤ 0.3 s target cannot mean; **live bullets** = `URHBulletSubsystem::Num()` sampled every frame, avg and peak (Week 1 has no enemy fire, so all are player bullets; swap-out: count only player-owned archetypes when `EnemyOrb` fire lands); **dashes/min** = dash starts; **damage taken/min** = Σ(HP lost per hit ÷ max HP at that hit) × 100. Targets are the §3.5 column nearest the run minute (< 6 → minute 2, < 14 → minute 10, else minute 18). Output: `RH.Stats` (+ `csv`), an automatic log block every sim minute and at run end, and `Saved/Playtests/RunStats_<date>_seed<seed>.csv` (one row per sim minute + a final row), rewritten each minute so a force-closed run still leaves data. In a worktree `-game` run the file lands in that worktree's `Saved/Playtests/`.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 1 audit fixes.** Enemy separation radius 90 → **120 uu** as §6 specifies (no measured reason to keep 90). Pulse Rifle level track now follows GDD §5.2 exactly — one of {+damage, +count, +fire rate, +size} per level: L2 +4 dmg, L3 +1 count, L4 ×0.8 cooldown, L5 ×1.25 size, L6 +6 dmg, L7 +1 count, L8 ×0.8 cooldown; the old track's combined L5/L6/L8 steps and +2 pierce are gone (pierce comes from the Pierce Rounds modifier), so L8 is 20 dmg × 3 bolts / 0.352 s instead of 27 × 4 with pierce 2 — retune numbers in `DA_Weapon_PulseRifle` when it exists. Gem overflow folds into a gem chosen by the run's seeded `ARHGameMode::Rng` (was unseeded `FMath::RandHelper`). Dash and post-hit i-frames now count fixed sim steps (15 and 24 steps) decremented in `ARHHero::TickWeapons` after `Enemies->Step` checks contact, instead of frame-time timers that could expire up to a frame before that frame's sim steps ran; dash *movement* still runs per frame on `DashTimeLeft`.
- 2026-10-18 — **Offer generator v1 (Week 2 Tue)** in `Source/RogueHero/Build/` (`RHOfferTypes.h`, `RHOfferTuning`, `RHOfferGenerator`, `RHOfferDebug`, tests in `Build/Tests/`). Implements §8 in full behind data, not just the v1 card kinds: candidates (new weapon / upgrade / modifier→weapon / blessing), weights `base[rarity] × kind × god × owned × synergy × commit`, guaranteed card 0, weighted sampling without replacement, Lock, Banish, pool narrowing after L8, every GDD §5.6 anti-frustration rule, Luck-shifted rarity roll, L8 second-god choice and L16 fusion/Legendary choice. Modifier/blessing/fusion pools stay empty until their defs exist (TODO(Week 3 Tue / Week 3 Thu / Week 4 Thu) markers). Deviations / fallbacks: (a) **input is a snapshot, not `URHRunState` + `URHContentRegistry`** — the generator takes `FRHOfferContext` (owned weapons + levels + socketed mods, blessings + ranks, patron, second god, favor, owned tags, banished ids, locked card, luck), an `FRHOfferPool` of `FRHOfferPoolEntry` (Id, `FPrimaryAssetId` def, tags, synergy tags, def rarity, god, fusion base weapon) and the run's `FRandomStream&`; RunState/Registry build those when they land. It never sees the hero, game mode or world. (b) Content keys are `FName` ids (the def's tag name, e.g. `Weapon.RailLance`), because `Blessing.*` tags are not authored yet; card identity = Kind + Id + TargetWeaponId. (c) **Banish removes a content Id** (TDD §8 "banished tags"): banishing "Split Core → Scatter Cannon" removes Split Core for every weapon, banishing an upgrade removes that weapon's upgrades. (d) Tuning is `URHOfferTuning` (`RHOfferTuning` primary type); until `DA_OfferTuning` exists the generator uses the class CDO, and `URHOfferTuning::MakeDefault` builds the same values as `DA_OfferTuning_CodeFallback`. GDD numbers: 5 cards, 4 weapons, L1–8, 3 sockets, rank 3, favor 0.30, owned 0.25, synergy ×1.5, commit ×1.5, L8/L16, fusion favor 6, ≥ 2 committed blessings after L8, force weapon at L5 below 2 weapons, Rare upgrade +2. **Placeholders** (no design number; tune Week 6 Wed): kind weights 1, def-rarity base weights 1 / 0.6 / 0.3, rarity roll 78 / 18 / 4 %, Luck ×(1 + 0.10 × Luck) on Rare/Epic, Epic upgrade +3; only upgrade cards roll rarity in v1. (e) When the pool has fewer than 5 legal cards the offer is shorter (no filler card exists in the GDD); when the guaranteed set is empty, card 0 is drawn from the whole pool and `FRHOfferResult::bGuaranteedSlotFilled` is false. (f) `RH.Debug.Offers` is the free function `RHOffers::DebugOffersCommand` (demo snapshot) until `RHConsoleCommands.cpp` registers it (Week 3 Sat). Tests: `Automation RunTests RogueHero.Offers`. (g) `.gitignore` had an unanchored `Build/` that also ignored the TDD §3 `Source/RogueHero/Build/` folder; it is now `/Build/` (root packaging output only).

Eight Recollection keys name the modifier their Discovery unlocks because GDD §8.3 only defines 7 of the 15 conditions.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 2 Mon: `FRHStatSet` + `URHStatsComponent`, XP curve as data, `URHRunState`.** (1) `FRHStatSet` (`Player/RHStats`) is §7 as written: `Base`, `Add` (Σ) and `Mult` (Π of factors) layers, `Final = (Base + ΣAdd) × ΠMult`, lazy recompute, a version bumped by every effective change (now including `SetBase`; no-op changes do not bump), `RemoveModifiersFrom` / `ReplaceModifiersFrom` (rank-up re-apply as one change) / `HasModifiersFrom`. A default-constructed set is neutral (percent stats 1.0, others 0); hero base values are data. Each `ERHStat` has a `Stat.<Name>` tag (`Stat.MaxHP` … `Stat.Luck`, Config/Tags/RH.ini) with `RHStats::ToTag/FromTag`. **Deviation:** `FRHStatModifier` keys its source by `FName Source` = the content id (tag name or asset name), not an `FGameplayTag SourceTag` — the offer system's id convention (§17 offer line (b)), because `Blessing.*` / tree tags are not authored yet. (2) `URHStatsComponent` (`Player/RHStatsComponent`) owns the set on `ARHHero`, applies base stats from tuning, broadcasts native `OnStatsChanged` per effective change; weapons still rebuild by polling the version once per sim step. **Compatibility shim:** `ARHHero::Stats` is now `FRHHeroStatsView` (read-only `Get`/`GetVersion` over the component) so HUD / game mode / console call sites `Hero->Stats.Get(...)` compile unchanged; swap-out: move callers to `Hero->GetStatsComponent()` and delete the view. (3) **`URHRunTuning`** (`Build/RHRunTuning`, primary type `RHRunTuning`): GDD §4.1 base stats, GDD §7 XP curve (`XPFirstLevelUp` 10, `XPGrowthFactor` 1.18 rounded per level — identical to the Week-1 hero code: 10, 12, 14, 17, 20, 24 …; optional `XPTable` override), GDD §5.6.4 tools (Reroll 2 / Banish 1 / Lock 1), GDD §5.4 patron start favor 2 and +1 favor per blessing rank. Until `DA_RunTuning` exists the CDO is used (`URHRunTuning::MakeDefault` = `DA_RunTuning_CodeFallback`); the component's `Tuning` soft pointer takes the asset. (4) **`URHRunState` is a `UWorldSubsystem`** (§4 lists it under World; one per arena world, game worlds only): seed + four derived `FRandomStream`s (`Offers`, `Spawns`, `Loot`, `Combat`; fixed hash so a reroll never shifts spawns), level, pending level-up FIFO + `OnLevelUpPending(Level, NumPending)`, owned weapons (id, def, level, socketed mods, tags), blessings + ranks, patron / second god, favor per `God.<Path>` + `OnFavorChanged`, owned tags, banished ids, locked card, rerolls/banishes/locks left, run time and kills, `ApplyOfferCard`, and `BuildOfferContext(Luck, ForLevel)` → `FRHOfferContext`. A lock holds the card for exactly the next level-up (not re-offered on rerolls of the screen it was locked on, dropped once that next level-up resolves). Fusion/Legendary picks are only recorded (`GetCommitments`) until `URHFusionDef` lands. **Temporary wiring (game mode not touched today):** the hero starts the run state on first use with `ARHGameMode::Seed` and no patron, registers weapons on `AddWeapon`, queues every level-up before firing its unchanged `FRHOnLevelUp`, and copies weapon levels changed by the placeholder auto-upgrade (fallback (h)) into the run state; run time / kills stay 0 until the game mode feeds them. Swap-out: `ARHGameMode` calls `StartRun(Seed, Patron, Tuning)`, `AdvanceTime`/`AddKills` in the sim step, and routes `OnLevelUpPending` → offer generator → `WBP_LevelUp` → `ApplyOfferCard` + `PopPendingLevelUp` instead of the auto-upgrade. Tests: `Automation RunTests RogueHero.Stats` and `RogueHero.RunState`.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 2 Thu: weapons 2–4 + archetypes.** Factory fallbacks (fallback (d) extended) `URHWeaponDef::MakeScatterCannon` (`DA_Weapon_ScatterCannon`, Cone, Nearest, `Pellet`), `MakeOrbitalDrones` (`DA_Weapon_OrbitalDrones`, Orbiter, Nearest-to-drone, new `DroneBolt`), `MakeRailLance` (`DA_Weapon_RailLance`, Beam, DensestLine, `Lance`); every L2–L8 step is exactly one of {+damage, +count, +fire rate, +size} (asserted in `AddLevel`). L1 → L8: Scatter 6 dmg × 5 pellets / 0.9 s → 13 × 9 / 0.576 s, 40° cone, 0.4 s life (~600 uu), fires only within 650 uu; Drones 2 drones, 7 dmg, 0.8 s per drone → 4 drones, 14 dmg, 0.512 s, ring r 150 uu at 120°/s, range 900 uu from the drone; Lance 30 dmg, 1 lance / 1.6 s, 3 200 uu/s, **pierce 20 in the base** (pierce-native, not a level step) → 57 dmg, 2 parallel lances / 1.024 s, size ×1.56. All numbers are placeholders for the `DA_` assets (tune Week 6). New `URHWeaponDef::PatternParams` (`FRHPatternParams`: Cone `SpeedSpread`, Orbiter `OrbitRadius`/`OrbitDegPerSec`, Beam `LineSpacing`, DensestLine `LineHalfWidth`) and `Description` (FText). **Design choices:** (1) Cone pellets vary speed by a fixed per-index pattern over [0.85, 1] (no RNG; `URHBulletSubsystem::Spawn` gained a `SpeedScale` argument, default 1). (2) **Drones are weapon state, not bullets**: positions are recomputed from the hero position every sim step and published with `URHBulletSubsystem::SetOrbiters`, drawn with the new `Drone` archetype; they never collide, never count toward the 6 000 cap and can never be recycled (as bullets they would have been the oldest and recycled first). Drones fire in turn (one shot every interval ÷ drone count), each at the enemy nearest to it; a drone with nothing in range passes its turn. So no `OrbitPhase` SoA column yet: it arrives with the Orbit Lock modifier (Week 3 Tue), whose orbiting *bullets* do collide. (3) The Beam is a fast long-tracer bullet with pierce, not a hitscan ray (TDD §5.4 "spawn requests"); Count > 1 lays lances side by side 48 uu apart, never a fan. (4) DensestLine scores up to 32 candidate directions (towards sampled enemies in range) by the enemies within `LineHalfWidth` + bullet radius + 30 uu of the ray; ties go to the nearer candidate. (5) `HitHistory` 4 → **8** per bullet so a lance piercing a dense pack cannot re-hit an enemy it hit five hits earlier in the same pass (+128 KB at the cap). Render: per-archetype width and trail length (Bolt 1.5 × r, 0.045 s; Pellet 1.3, 0.030 s; Lance 1.2, 0.110 s ≈ 350 uu; DroneBolt aqua (0.15, 1.0, 0.8) × 11, 1.4, 0.035 s; Drone body ice-blue (0.55, 0.85, 1.0) × 6, a flattened glow 36 × 26 × 13 uu at z 110 with its bright head leading). Text: new `ST_Weapons` (`Weapon.<Name>.{Name,Desc}`, en + zh-Hans); the Pulse Rifle `NSLOCTEXT` is gone. Debug: `RH.Give <Tag> [level]` (`Debug/RHGiveCommand.cpp`) adds a `Weapon.*` to a free slot or raises it one level (or to [level]); defs come from `URHWeaponRegistry` (game-instance subsystem, keyed by tag; swap-out point: load the `RHWeapon` primary assets there). CSV stats `RHBullets/{Step,Render,Live,Recycled,Orbiters}` and `RHWeapons/Tick`. **Measured** (`-csvCaptureFrames=3000`, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, 1920×1080 windowed, Epic, Development editor `-game`, `RH.God 1` + all four weapons at L8 + `RH.Spawn 400`): frame 3.56 ms median / 4.28 p95, GT 1.46 / 1.96, GPU 3.04 / 3.64; weapon tick ≤ 0.003 ms p95, bullet step ≤ 0.001 ms p95, bullet render 0.004 / 0.008 ms; live bullets median 2, p95 12, max 17 (enemies crowd the invulnerable hero, so most shots hit at once), 0 recycled. In open play (60 enemies, 3 sim minutes) `RH.Stats` reports live bullets avg 10–13, peak 25: **four L8 weapons with no modifiers or blessings fire about 2 % of the GDD §3.5 "≥ 150 live bullets" target**, which has to come from modifiers (Split, Fork, Burst, Scatter Shell…) and Attack Speed. Flag for the Week 2 Sun playtest and Week 3 tuning; the cap is nowhere near binding.

(d) `URHLevelUpScreen::SetOffer(TArray<FRHOfferCard>, FRHLevelUpState)` + native delegates `OnCardChosen(i)`, `OnReroll()`, `OnBanish(i)`, `OnLock(i)`; the screen owns no rules (caller applies and calls `SetOffer` again; picking deactivates the screen).
