(e) Key prompts are text (`ST_UI Key.*`) until `CommonInputBaseControllerData` glyph assets exist; cards have a Keeper-coloured accent bar instead of an icon/render. (f) Card names resolve by content id: `ST_Weapons/ST_Modifiers/ST_Blessings "<Id>.Name"`, `ST_Fusions` as is, god choice → `ST_Keepers Keeper.<Path>.Honorific`; a missing row shows the raw id with a `LogRH` warning (`UI/RHCardText`) — swap to the def's FText once `DA_` defs exist. (g) Debug: `RH.Debug.LevelUpUI [level] [shot]` (`Debug/RHUIDebug.cpp`) shows fake cards with stubbed reroll/banish/lock; `shot` takes a UI-inclusive screenshot 1 s later (`HighResShot` renders the scene only and never contains UMG). (h) On opening, CommonUI re-centres the cursor; with a mouse that hovers (and so focuses) the middle card instead of card 1 — accepted, the pad path starts on card 1.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 1 Sat: stress tooling (`Debug/RHStressTest`, TDD §3/§14).** `URHStressTest` (tickable world subsystem) runs a reproducible stress scene: director off, hero invulnerable, run reseeded to 20261024 (`ARHGameMode::Reseed`), and every frame tops up to N live enemies (respawned at the spawn ring, ≤ 40/frame) and M bullets (≤ 1 500/frame; the hero's own bullets count towards M). It starts automatically on `L_StressTest` (400 + 5 000), with `RH.Stress [enemies] [bullets] [lethal] | off`, or with `-RHStress=N,M` on any map. Stress bullets are **non-lethal by default**: a second zero-damage stress spec (`RH.Bullets N nodmg`, `SpawnStressBullets(Count, bLethal)`) whose hits `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits` skips (`Damage <= 0`), so they still collide, are hashed and feed impact FX, but never kill. The hero's Pulse Rifle still kills, and those kills are replaced. Unattended profiling: `-RHStressProfile=<frames> [-RHStressWarmup=12] [-RHStressLabel=x] [-RHStressQuit]` turns on `stat unit` after the warm-up, records the raw `FStatUnitData` frame/GT/RT/GPU per frame, writes a CSV-profiler file `RHStress_<label>_<time>.csv`, logs median/p95/max and quits. `RH.Spawn N [Role|index]` takes a type (`Swarm` → `Enemy.Swarm`, via `URHEnemySubsystem::FindType`). Automated Week-1 checks: `RH.Test.Dash` (Enhanced Input injection of `IA_Move` + `IA_Dash` through a 9-enemy wall, god off, PASS if the hero crosses with overlaps and takes no damage) and `RH.Test.XP` (24 kills, gem ledger `ARHGameMode::XPDropped`/`XPCollected` must close, level-up required). Deviation: GT/GPU come from `FStatUnitData` because `GGameThreadTime`/`RHIGetGPUFrameCycles` need RenderCore/RHI in `RogueHero.Build.cs`, which this task did not own; swap-out: read them directly if those modules are added.
- 2026-10-18 — **Swarm shadow policy decided (TDD §16).** `FRHEnemyStats::bCastShadow` (new, default **false**); `MakeSwarm` = false. Elites and the boss set it true in their defs when they exist. Elite affixes are per-instance on a type's ISM, so a shadowed elite needs its own renderer (TODO with the affix work, Week 4). `RH.Enemy.Shadows -1|0|1` (data / all off / all VSM) is the runtime A/B switch, applied in `URHEnemySubsystem::Render` with `SetCastShadow`. Measured on the 4080 (below): swarm VSM on vs off is **within noise** (GPU median Δ ≤ 0.05 ms; `DrawCall/ShadowDepths` 13 → 16 confirms the toggle). Contact-shadow-only was not measured: UE's per-primitive contact-shadow flag requires *Cast Shadow*, so contact shadows without VSM are not available per primitive. Light-level contact shadows belong to `ARHArenaLighting`. Kept the §16 default (swarm none) because it costs nothing to keep and protects the 3060 tier. Turning swarm shadows on later is a data change if Art §3 or the playtest asks for grounding; re-measure on the 3060 tier first.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 1 Sat measurements** (`L_StressTest`, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, 1920×1080 windowed, `scalability 3` Epic, Lumen SW + VSM + Nanite, Development editor `-game`, 1 200 frames after 12 s warm-up, live counts held exactly, raw `stat unit`, median / p95 ms). **Done-when, 300 live Scrapwings + 3 000 bullets:** frame 4.81 / 6.43 (≈ 208 fps), GT 2.06 / 3.06, RT 4.80 / 6.38, GPU 4.26 / 5.27; with swarm VSM 4.69 / 5.40, GT 2.01 / 2.51, GPU 4.21 / 4.63. **Budget, 400 live enemies + 5 000 bullets** (3 runs, shadows off): frame 4.78–4.91 / 5.48–5.75, GT 2.22–2.26 / 2.77–2.87, RT 4.78–4.90 / 5.46–5.72, GPU 4.28–4.32 / 4.78–4.84; with swarm VSM (2 runs) frame 4.80–4.83 / 5.48–5.49, GT 2.23 / 2.78–2.81, GPU 4.29–4.31 / 4.77–4.81. Draw calls ≈ 100. Verdict: GT ≤ 8 ms and GPU ≤ 12 ms met with large headroom **on a 4080**; a 4070 (≈ 1.6–1.8× slower raster) would land near 7–8 ms GPU by estimate, **not measured**. The frame is render-thread bound (RT ≈ frame, `RenderThreadIdle` 0), probably from ≈ 6 000 ISM instance updates per frame. No budget is missed, so nothing was optimised (rule 9); it is an input to the Week 2 Niagara-migration risk (§16 row 1). Details and the Done-when verdict: `Docs/Playtests/2026-10-18_W1_automated.md`.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 2 loop closed: level-up → 5-card offer → pick → run continues** (`Build/RHOfferFlow`, `URHOfferFlow`; fallback (h) resolved). (1) **Ownership:** `URHOfferFlow` is a plain UObject owned by `ARHGameMode` (`GetOfferFlow()`), not a subsystem, so tests drive it on a bare `URHRunState` with no world/UI. The game mode now calls `URHRunState::StartRun(Seed, no patron, hero DA_RunTuning)` in its first sim-ready tick (before the starting weapon), feeds `AdvanceTime`/`AddKills` every sim step, and takes `-RHSeed=N` for reproducible runs. (2) **Flow:** `OnLevelUpPending` → (if idle) snapshot `BuildOfferContext(hero Luck)` → generator (L8 `GenerateSecondGodChoice` over the six `God.<Path>` tags, L16 `GenerateFusionChoice`, else `GenerateLevelUp`) → `URHLevelUpScreen` pushed on the Menu layer (pauses via the screen's ref-counted pause) → pick: `RunState.ApplyOfferCard` + hero (`NewWeapon` → `URHWeaponRegistry` def → `ARHHero::AddWeapon`; upgrade → instance `SetLevel`, clamped to L8 in both) → `PopPendingLevelUp` → next pending offer on the same screen, or close and resume. Multiple pending level-ups resolve oldest first; the game mode also stops stepping the sim the moment an offer opens mid-frame. Every draw uses RunState's `Offers` stream (same seed + same picks ⇒ same offers; test `RogueHero.OfferFlow.Determinism`). (3) **Tools (GDD §5.6.4):** Reroll spends a reroll and regenerates; **Banish replaces only that slot** (same guaranteed-slot rule for card 0; a dry pool shortens the hand), never a free reroll; Lock keeps the card for the next level-up; **unlocking a card locked on the same screen refunds the lock**, unlocking a carried card just drops it; one locked card at a time. **Deviation:** Reroll/Banish/Lock are disabled on the L8/L16 commitment screens (they are a choice, not an offer; the GDD does not say). An L16 screen with no fusion/Legendary content is skipped with a warning (content lands Week 4). (4) **Stubs / adapter points:** modifier and blessing cards are recorded in the run state but not applied to the hero yet (`URHOfferFlow::ApplyToHero`), and `URHOfferFlow::BuildPool` has marked adapter points for `Pool.Modifiers` / `Pool.Blessings` / fusions; `GetAllGods()` is a fixed list until the URHGodDef registry exists; patron stays empty until the hub. Until those land a hand has at most 4 cards (3 unowned weapons + 1 upgrade, or upgrades only), which TDD §17 offer line (e) allows. (5) **Unattended play:** `RH.Offer.AutoPick <0|1|first|random>` (global; `random` uses its own stream seeded from the run seed, so it never shifts offers), and **a running stress scene auto-picks `first` when it is Off**, so `L_StressTest` profiling runs unattended (card 0 = the guaranteed Pulse Rifle upgrade, about what fallback (h) did). `RH.Offer [status|pick i|reroll|banish i|lock i|shot]` (`shot` = UI-inclusive screenshot 1 s after an offer opens; `HighResShot` contains no UMG); `RH.Debug.Offers` is now registered. **Verified:** 30/30 `RogueHero` tests headless (8 new `RogueHero.OfferFlow.*`: Queue, Apply, Reroll, Banish, Lock, Determinism, AutoPick, Commitment); smoke with `RH.Offer.AutoPick random` PASS; smoke without auto-pick shows the L2 screen with the timer frozen at 00:24 (PASS); a real-time 5:00 run on `L_Arena_Test` (`AutoPick random`, `RH.God`, stationary hero) took **12 level-ups in 5:00** (L2 0:28 … L7 1:11, L8 god choice 3:30, L13 5:01), all 12 offers logged and picked, 4 weapons owned, swarm TTK median 0.167 s, live bullets avg 3 (LOW, same as Week 2 Thu). 12 is twice the Roadmap's "~6": the invulnerable hero stands in the swarm and collects every gem, but it is also an input for the Week 2 Sun playtest's XP-curve check. Pad-only play cannot be verified unattended.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 2 Sat: CommonUI HUD + view model, damage numbers.** (a) `URHHUDWidget` (`UI/RHHUDWidget`, the `WBP_HUD` stand-in, code-built per the Week-2 UI fallback; swap-out point: `WBP_HUD` reparented onto it with MVVM view bindings to the same fields) is hosted by `URHGameLayerScreen` on the Game layer: top-left HP bar (cool fill, warm only below 30 % — a danger signal), XP bar + level, dash charge pips + recharge bar, weapon rows (glyph tile = first letter of the name until icons exist, name + level / MAX, 3 modifier socket pips filled from `URHRunState`), top-centre timer + kills, bottom-left offer tools left (`World.Renegotiate/Hold/Refuse`). **Reserved, collapsed slots** for the ultimate bar and the six favor bars (Roadmap W3 Wed); socket pips become modifier icons in W3 Tue. (b) `URHHUDViewModel` is a real MVVM `UMVVMViewModelBase` with FieldNotify properties (`ModelViewViewModel` + `FieldNotification` added to `RogueHero.Build.cs`); the widget subscribes per field and only touches its tree on change. **Deviation from "not per-frame polling" (§11):** `ARHHero` / `ARHGameMode` / `URHRunState` have no change events (Player/, Core/RHGameMode, Build/ are other owners), so `Sample()` compares-and-copies their public state once per frame and broadcasts only on change (values quantised: whole seconds, 1/64 dash recharge); swap-out point: subscribe to hero/run-state events when they exist. Run time / kills read from `ARHGameMode` until RunState is wired as the source. (c) `ARHHUD` keeps only the developer stat block (top-right, **`RH.Debug.HUD 0/1`**, default on) and the run-over overlay; its Canvas vitals draw only when no CommonUI game layer exists, which retires fallback (g). (d) **Damage numbers = one Slate leaf widget** (`URHDamageNumbersWidget`, full-screen under the HUD) painting one outlined text element per live entry of `URHDamageNumbers` (`UI/RHDamageNumbers`, tickable world subsystem holding `FRHDamageNumberPool`: fixed 160 entries, oldest recycled, no allocation per hit, one projection setup per paint, text re-formatted only when a value changes). Cheapest of the §11 options: no Niagara text atlas to author, no widget/Actor per number. Rules (GDD §3.5): ≤ 20 kills/s → per-hit numbers, hits in the same 96 uu cell within 0.12 s add into one number, crits never merge and draw bigger and gold (GDD §9); > 20 kills/s (exit < 16, same hysteresis as the impact-FX LOD) → per-hit numbers stop and kills collect into one `×N` tally per 640 uu area (`ST_UI HUD.DamageTally`, cool accent) that counts up while kills land (0.6 s after the last, ≤ 1.5 s) then floats and fades; a tally of 1 is not drawn. `RH.DamageNumbers 0` turns them off. Feed API `AddHit(FVector2f Pos, float Damage, bool bCrit)`, `AddKill(FVector2f Pos)`; **not wired yet** — the hook belongs in `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits` (other owner), where damage and crit are known (`FRHHitEvent` carries neither). (e) Debug: `RH.Debug.DamageNumbers [kills/s] [seconds] [shot]` feeds synthetic hits/kills around the hero; `RH.Debug.HUDShot [delay]` takes a UI-inclusive screenshot. Tests `RogueHero.UI.DamageNumbers.*` (individual below 20/s, merge, aggregate above 20/s + hysteresis back, cap).
- 2026-10-18 — **W5 UI pulled forward: CJK font fallback, pad glyphs, Settings (Roadmap W5 Fri UI half + W5 Tue Settings).** (a) **Font: code-built composite font, not a `UFont` asset.** `RHUIStyle::Font(Typeface, Size)` returns an `FSlateFontInfo` on one static `FStandaloneCompositeFont`: default typeface = engine Roboto (Regular / Italic / Light / Medium / Bold / BoldItalic / Black), plus a **CJK sub-font with no culture filter** (CJK Unified + Ext A, compatibility ideographs, CJK symbols & punctuation, radicals, strokes, enclosed, compatibility forms, half/full-width forms) and the fallback typeface, both on the engine-bundled `Engine/Content/Slate/Fonts/DroidSansFallback.ttf`; every Latin typeface name is mapped in the sub-font too, so a Bold request resolves (CJK has one weight, so Bold CJK renders regular). Why: the engine default font maps zh-Hans **only in the editor** (`FLegacySlateFontInfoCache`, `GIsEditor`), so a packaged game had only the Regular-only fallback typeface; culture-free mapping also renders `中文` on the language toggle in en. Why code, not an editor-Python `UFont` asset: the UI is already code-built (Week-2 fallback), the asset would be an LFS binary that worktrees cannot load un-smudged, and the font files are referenced by path from engine content that is always staged (`BaseGame.ini EngineExtraStageFiles Content/Slate`). The run-over overlay and Canvas vitals in `ARHHUD` now draw with the same font (`FCanvasTextItem` + Slate font measure); the developer stat block keeps the engine font (ASCII). **Licences:** Roboto, Apache 2.0 (`Engine/Source/ThirdParty/Licenses/ROBOTO_License.txt`); DroidSansFallback, Apache 2.0, Android Open Source Project (`Engine/Content/Slate/Fonts/DroidSans.tps` → `AndroidOpenSourceProject_License.txt`); both may be redistributed in a shipped game; ship the two licence texts with the build's third-party notices. Swap-out point: when the art font (e.g. Noto Sans SC, OFL) is chosen, add its files under `Content/RH/UI/Fonts` and point `RHUIStyleFont::Build` at them, or author `Font_RH_UI` and return `FSlateFontInfo(FontObject, …)` from the same function; every UI call site already goes through it. Verified at 1280×720 with `RH.Debug.Language zh-Hans`: HUD (11 pt labels up to the 30 pt timer), level-up (40 pt title, 26 pt bold card names), pause and settings, no tofu. (b) **Pad glyphs: code-drawn, from the bindings.** `URHInputPrompt` / `URHKeyGlyph` (`UI/RHInputPrompt`) draw a rounded badge per key (circle for A/B/X/Y, pill for bumpers / triggers / Menu / View / D-pad, keycap for keyboard) with the designation from `ST_UI Glyph.*` (Xbox-style; the same in both cultures except Menu / View / Space) or the engine key name for letters. Keys are **resolved from `IMC_Menu`, then the gameplay contexts**, for the current device (`RHInputGlyphs::Resolve`), so prompts cannot drift from the bindings; Slate-owned keys (Accept = Enter / A, ← → / D-pad) are given as fixed pairs. Every prompt subscribes to `UCommonInputSubsystem::OnInputMethodChangedNative`, so they switch live on the last-used device. Badges are monochrome (cool / neutral player UI, Art §3); the Xbox face colours are not used because B is red. Used on: level-up tools (`[RT/R] Renegotiate ×n`, `[X/F] Hold`, `[Y/B] Refuse`) and footer (`[A/Enter] Choose`, `[LB][RB] / [A][D] Move`), pause (`[A/Enter] Select`, `[B/Esc] Resume`), settings (`[←][→] Change`, `[B/Esc] Back`). **Deviation from §11 "platform glyphs via `CommonInputBaseControllerData`"**: no glyph textures exist, and controller data needs brushes; only Xbox-style pads are labelled. Swap-out point: `T_Glyph_*` textures in `DA_ControllerData_Pad/KBM` (`CommonInputBaseControllerData`), then `URHKeyGlyph` draws the brush that `UCommonInputPlatformSettings` gives for the key instead of the badge (PlayStation names come with it). The old text prompts (`Key.*`, `*.Hint.*`, `LevelUp.Tool`) are removed from `ST_UI`. Menu screens now focus their desired target on activation for every device (CommonUI only routes it for gamepad), and buttons / rows show focus as a 2 px accent edge. (c) **Settings screen** `URHSettingsScreen` (`UI/RHSettingsScreen`, menu layer, opened from the pause screen's new *Settings* button; Back returns to pause): **Language** en ⇄ 中文 (endonyms, `ST_UI Settings.LanguageName.*`) switches the culture live (every `FText` re-resolves; the screen refreshes on `OnCultureChanged`), and **Master volume** 0–100 % in 10 % steps, applied with `FAudioDevice::SetTransientPrimaryVolume` (placeholder until sound classes exist, W6 Tue). Only what the docs list (§10: language, audio, bindings; no rebinding UI on the roadmap; accessibility is post-v0.1, GDD §11); no screen-shake option (GDD §3.5 is a budget, not a setting). Rows are `URHOptionButton`: Up / Down move, Left / Right (arrows, D-pad, stick) and LB / RB / A / D step, click / Accept steps forward, hover takes focus. **Persistence deviation from §10 (`URHSaveGame` holds settings):** settings are per machine, so they live in **GameUserSettings.ini**: language under the engine's own `[Internationalization] Culture` (written by `UKismetInternationalizationLibrary::SetCurrentCulture(…, true)`, read by the engine at startup; `-culture=` still overrides; not written in PIE), volume under `[/Script/RogueHero.RHUserSettings] MasterVolume` (`URHUserSettings`, `config=GameUserSettings`, applied in `ARHPlayerController::BeginPlay`). Swap-out point if the save must carry them (cloud saves): copy both into `URHSaveGame` on save and back on load (Meta owner). Note: `-game` runs from a worktree write GameUserSettings under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\UnrealEngine\5.8\Saved`, which all worktrees share, so the debug switch does not save. (d) Debug: `RH.Debug.PauseUI [shot]`, `RH.Debug.SettingsUI [shot]`, `RH.Debug.Language <en|zh-Hans> [save]`, `RH.Debug.InputType <pad|kbm>`, `RH.Debug.After <s> <cmd…>` (staged screenshots from one `-ExecCmds`). Tests `RogueHero.UI.Font.CJKFallback`, `RogueHero.UI.Glyphs.{ResolveFromMappings,LabelsAndShapes}`, `RogueHero.UI.Settings.{Values,LiveLanguageSwitch}`. Not verified: a physical pad (switching was driven by `RH.Debug.InputType`), persistence across a restart (the test path does not save), a packaged build.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 3 Mon + the flag half of Tue: `URHModifierDef`, spec composition, all 20 modifiers** (GDD §5.3, §5.1–5.4, §9). (1) **Data.** `URHModifierDef` (`Build/RHModifierDef`, primary type `RHModifier`): FText name/desc (`ST_Modifiers` `Mod.<Name>.{Name,Desc}`, en + zh-Hans), `Tag` (`Mod.*`, the content id), `SynergyTags`, `Rarity`, `bUnlockedByDefault` (1–8 true), `Flags` (bitmask over `ERHBulletFlagBit`, which mirrors `ERHBulletFlags` bit-for-bit; new bits `Fork`, `Mirror`, `Burst`, `ChainOnKill`, `Lifesteal`, internal `Zone`), multipliers (Damage, FireRate, Speed, Size, Lifetime, Range — multiply across sockets), adds (Knockback, Crit chance/mult, Lifesteal, StatusChance, Pierce, Ricochet, Split, Fork, Burst, Scatter, Wave, ChainVolleys — sum) and behaviour parameters (0 = unset; the socket that sets one wins, the largest if two do). Factory fallback (TDD §17 (d) extended): `URHModifierDef::MakeCodeFallbacks` builds all 20 as `DA_Mod_<Name>_CodeFallback`, registered in `URHWeaponRegistry` (`FindModifier`, `GetModifiers`). Sockets: `URHWeaponInstance::AddModifier` — 3 per weapon, each once per weapon (GDD §5.1/§5.3); no per-weapon socket restrictions (every modifier on every weapon). **Placeholders** (no GDD number; tune Week 6): all rarities Common, synergy tags, Split ±30° fan / child life ½, Ricochet range 500 uu / ≥ 0.4 s flight after a bounce, Homing 360°/s within 600 uu (retarget every 4 steps), Orbit ring 200 uu (eased in over 0.25 s, ≤ 216°/s), Boomerang turn at 50 % of life / caught within 60 uu, Heavy Slug knockback 600 uu/s (≈ 60 uu shove), Chain 1 volley per kill / ≤ 4 pending / ≥ 0.12 s apart, Burst 0.08 s between rounds, Spread Wave 5 bullets over 70°, 0.25 s, ×0.8 speed, ×1.3 size, 50 % dmg, pierce-all, Lingering Field 70 uu zone ticking every 0.25 s for 25 % dmg, Scatter Shell children 50 % dmg / 0.35 s. (2) **Composition** (§5.4) is the pure `RHSpecComposition::Compose(WeaponDef, Level, Mods, HeroStats, PatronStatus, DerivedIds)` → `FRHComposedWeapon` {level stats after mods, fire interval, main spec + 4 derived specs: Split / Zone / Scatter / Wave}; order base[level] → modifiers → hero stats (Damage%, Area%, ProjectileSpeed%, AttackSpeed% × FireRateMult, Crit). Each weapon instance owns 5 spec slots (main + derived, linked by id). **Derived bullets never spawn derived bullets** and lose Orbit/Boomerang; they keep Pierce, Ricochet, Homing, status and Lifeline (so Split "multiplies with Pierce": every pierce hit splits and the children pierce too). (3) **Behaviours** live in `URHBulletSubsystem` SoA (new columns `RicochetLeft`, `State` {orbiting, returning}, `Timer`, `Phase`, `Radius0`, `HomingTarget`); every behaviour spawn goes to a deferred list applied after the loop through the normal capped `Spawn`, so the 6 000 cap and oldest-first recycling hold. On a hit: Split children (inherit the hit history) and a Linger zone are deferred, then **Pierce → Ricochet → Boomerang turn-around → die**. **Interpretations:** (a) Ricochet also bounces at the *end of flight* when bounces are left, so the pierce-native Rail Lance (pierce 20) still ricochets. (b) **Scatter Shell / Spread Wave fire at the end of a bullet's flight — lifetime expiry *or* the hit that used it up** (children skip that enemy); not on leaving the arena or a caught Boomerang. GDD says "on expiry"; misses-only would make the "弹幕 generator" inert on auto-aimed weapons that rarely miss. (c) Orbit Lock freezes the bullet's lifetime while orbiting and releases tangentially with a full lifetime. (d) Boomerang: mid-life turn clears the hit history (the way back hits the same enemies again); a turn caused by a hit keeps it. (e) Lingering Field zones are stationary bullets (`Zone` flag) that hit everything inside once per tick, ignore hit history, count toward the cap, and draw as a flat cool disc on the floor (placeholder look; swap-out with the Niagara array DI). (f) Fork/Mirror/Burst wrap every shot of every pattern (`URHWeaponInstance::Emit`): each pellet, each lance, each drone shot; Mirror point-reflects through the muzzle origin; Burst keeps the cooldown (3 shots per volley, re-aimed). (g) Chain Trigger free volleys never touch the normal cooldown and stop when nothing is in range. (h) Lifeline heals `S.Damage × 3 %` per hit (crits and overkill are not visible to the bullet side), applied by the weapon through `ARHHero::Heal`. (i) Divine Conduit puts the patron's element on the spec (`StatusTag`, chance 100 %) via `RHSpecComposition::PatronStatusFallback` (Path → status from the §5.4 table; **swap-out: `URHGodDef`'s element status**); the enemy side applies it once the Week 3 Wed status system lands. **Interim hook:** `URHImpactFXSubsystem::Step` (which already receives the resolved hit + kill buffers every sim step) forwards them to `URHBulletSubsystem::ResolveHitOutcomes` (Chain Trigger kill credit per weapon slot, Lifeline heal) — **swap-out: `ARHGameMode::SimStep` calls `Bullets->ResolveHitOutcomes(HitBuffer, KillBuffer)` right after `Enemies->ApplyHits` and the forward in ImpactFX is deleted** (both at once, or kills count twice). (4) Debug: `RH.Give Mod.<Name> [Weapon.<Name>|all]` (unique prefix ok, `Mod.Split` → `Mod.SplitCore`; no weapon = first free socket; a named weapon not owned yet is waited for 5 s — the game mode adds the Pulse Rifle after `-ExecCmds` — then added); also records the socket in `URHRunState::AddWeaponModifier`. Tests `RogueHero.Modifiers.*` (11): fallbacks, composition base/each flag/stacking, sockets, sim on-hit/paths/expiry/wrappers, **every modifier × every weapon at L8** (fires, hits, shows its behaviour, arrays consistent), cap storm (6 000 reached, ~196 k recycles, spawn-queue invariants every step). **Measured** (RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, 1920×1080 windowed, Epic, Development editor `-game`, `RH.God 1` + all four weapons L8 + `RH.Spawn 400`; the user's own `-game` session was running on the same GPU; 3 600-frame `CsvProfile` after the spawn): **with 3 modifiers per weapon** (Rifle Split/Fork/Scatter, Scatter Cannon Split/Pierce/Burst, Drones Fork/Mirror/Burst, Lance Wave/Linger/Split) `RH.Stats` minute 1: **live bullets avg 74–95, peak 178–179** (two runs), kills/min 440, swarm TTK median 0 / p90 ≤ 0.12 s; frame 3.64 ms median / 4.43 p95, GT 1.54 / 1.88, GPU 2.87 / 3.37; bullet step ≤ 0.007 ms p95, bullet render 0.013 / 0.021 ms, weapon tick ≤ 0.004 ms p95. **Control without modifiers:** live bullets avg 8–9, peak 22–23. So modifiers are ≈ 10× the bullet volume, still below the GDD §3.5 "≥ 150 live" target on average (the peak passes); the rest has to come from Attack Speed blessings, more weapons (Ring Emitter / Spiral) and enemy density, not more modifier tuning. At the cap the sim stays cheap: the cap-storm test (6 000 bullets with Split/Pierce/Scatter/Linger/Wave + 500 enemies) runs ≈ 0.7 ms per step including 120 spawns per step. Build note: `Tools\Build.bat` refuses to build while any `UnrealEditor.exe` holds the engine-wide Live Coding mutex (UBT `HotReload.cs` keys it on the engine executable, so another checkout's running `-game` blocks it); iterated with the same UBT command plus `-NoHotReloadFromIDE`; the final `Tools\Build.bat` run is green with no warnings.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 3 Wed–Thu data/effect layer: `URHGodDef`, `URHBlessingDef`, `URHEffect` + `URHEffectManager`, favor tiers, 30 blessings.** (1) **Defs** (`Build/RHGodDef`, `Build/RHBlessingDef`, primary types `RHGod` / `RHBlessing`) are TDD §9 as written: god = `PathTag`, Honorific/Designation/Short/Path/Facet FText (rows of `ST_Keepers`; Facet empty until the Week 5 Contracts node), Colour (same values as `RHUIStyle::KeeperColor`), `ElementStatus`, `Tiers[3]{Threshold 3/6/9, EffectClass, TierLine = Keeper.<Path>.Tier<N>}`, `UltimateClass`, `Blessings[]`; blessing = `Tag` (`Blessing.<Path>.<Name>`, the run/offer id), `God`, `Kind` (Stat / Status / Signature), Rarity, `bUnlockedByDefault`, FText Name/Desc from `ST_Blessings`, `Ranks[3]{StatMods, StatusMods, Params, DescArgs}` (each rank holds its **total**; a rank-up replaces, never stacks), optional `EffectClass`, Tags, SynergyTags, `MakePoolEntry()` for `FRHOfferPool::Blessings`. **Fallback:** until `DA_` assets exist `RHGods::GetAll()` / `RHBlessings::GetAll()` serve rooted C++ factory defs `DA_God_<Path>_CodeFallback` / `DA_Blessing_<Path>_<Name>_CodeFallback`; swap-out = load the primary assets there (URHContentRegistry, Week 5) with the same ids. (2) **The 30 blessings are authored here** (GDD §5.4 gives only the template and "Ember Trail"): per Path #1–#2 stat (Common), #3 applies the element on hit (Rare, effect), #4 improves the element (Rare, **data only**: `FRHStatusMod{Status, MagnitudeMult, DurationAdd}` scales every `URHEffectManager::ApplyStatus`), #5 signature (Epic, effect); #1–#3 unlocked by default = the 18-blessing new-player pool. Storm: Overcharge, Static Focus, Live Wire, Ground Fault, Thunderclap · Forge: Tempered Plating, Bellows, Kindling, Banked Heat, Ember Trail · Tide: Deep Reserve, Wellspring, Undertow, Deep Cold, Cold Front · Shade: Interest, Long Arm, Lien, Foreclosure, Dividend · Radiance: Glint, Halo, Flashpoint, Afterimage, Corona · Quicksilver: Fleet, Light Step, Slipstream, Tailwind, Wayfare. **Deviation:** Quicksilver has no status (GDD §5.4), so its #3 is a third stat blessing (projectile speed) and #4 a dash buff (Tailwind). All numbers are placeholders (tune Week 6 Wed); names en + zh-Hans in `ST_Blessings` (Game.po). 12 effect classes `URHEffect_<Path>_<Name>` in `Build/RHEffect_Blessings` read every number from rank `Params`. (3) **`URHEffect`** (UObject, Abstract, Blueprintable): `OnActivated/Deactivated/RankChanged`, `OnHits(TConstArrayView<FRHHitEvent>)`, `OnKills(TConstArrayView<FRHKillEvent>)`, `OnCrit(TConstArrayView<FRHCritEvent>)`, `OnDashStart/End(FRHDashEvent)`, `OnLevelUp(int32)`, `OnTick(dt)`; subscriptions are declared in the constructor (`ERHEffectEvents`) and the manager calls each subscribed effect **once per event type per sim step** with the whole array (test: 5 000 hits + 20 kills = 3 virtual calls). `OnCrit` is batched like the others (TDD lists it singular). BP subclasses get Activated/Deactivated/LevelUp/Tick events. (4) **`URHEffectManager`** (`UWorldSubsystem`, game worlds): binds to the hero (`ARHHero::BeginPlay` → `BindHero`) and the run state; `URHRunState::OnBlessingChanged` → rank stat mods on `URHStatsComponent` (source = blessing id) + status mods + effect create/re-rank; `OnFavorTierChanged` → `Tiers[n].EffectClass` (null until Week 3 Fri/Sat: the crossing is logged); `OnRunStarted` → everything removed; `OnLevelUpPending` → queued level-up event. Services: `ApplyStatus[InRadius]`, `DamageEnemy` / `DamageInRadius` (× hero Damage), `HealHero`, `AddTimedModifier`, Combat RNG stream. Dashes are queued by the hero and delivered in the next `DispatchStep`. (5) **Status interface:** `IRHStatusTarget` (`Build/RHEffectTypes.h`: ApplyStatus / HasStatus / DamageEnemy / IsEnemyAlive / GetEnemyPos / FindNearestEnemy / QueryEnemies). **Temporary:** `FRHEnemyStatusAdapter` answers queries from `URHEnemySubsystem`'s public API and **stubs ApplyStatus / DamageEnemy** (counted, logged once) — so status and effect-damage blessings do nothing in game yet. Swap-out (enemy/status session): `URHEnemySubsystem` implements `IRHStatusTarget` (magnitudes: Burn dps, Chill stacks, Shock +dmg-taken fraction, Mark strength, Blind stun strength), effect-damage deaths join its next kill buffer, Freeze reads `URHEffectManager::GetStatusScale(Status.Freeze)` for Deep Cold, and it calls `URHEffectManager::Get(this)->SetStatusTarget(this)` in Initialize; then delete the adapter. (6) **Favor:** `URHRunState` gains `SetFavor`, `GetFavorTier`, `OnFavorTierChanged(God, Old, New)` (one broadcast per change, thresholds from the god def), `OnBlessingChanged` (fired before the favor it grants, so a tier crossed by a pick sees that blessing applied) and `OnRunStarted` (before the patron's starting favor). **Hook lines for the scheduler** (files I do not own): `ARHGameMode::SimStep`, after `ImpactFX->Step(...)`: `World->GetSubsystem<URHEffectManager>()->DispatchStep(Dt, HitBuffer, KillBuffer /*, CritBuffer*/);` · `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits` gains a `TArray<FRHCritEvent>& OutCrits` filled when `bCrit` (`{e, Pos[e], Dmg, S.WeaponSlot}`) and the game mode passes it to `DispatchStep` · `Debug/RHGiveCommand.cpp` `CmdGive`: before the Weapon branch, `if (Args[0].StartsWith(TEXT("Blessing."))) { RHGodCommands::GiveBlessing(World, Args[0], Args.Num() > 1 ? FCString::Atoi(*Args[1]) : 1); return; }` (`#include "Debug/RHGodCommands.h"`) · HUD: tier toast = `RunState->OnFavorTierChanged` → `LOCTABLE("ST_Keepers", "Keeper.<Path>.Tier<N>")`; offer pool: `for (const URHBlessingDef* D : RHBlessings::GetAll()) if (D->bUnlockedByDefault) Pool.Blessings.Add(D->MakePoolEntry());`. (7) Debug (`Debug/RHGodCommands.cpp`): `RH.Favor [Path] [N]`, `RH.GiveBlessing <Blessing.Path.Name> [ranks]` (`RH.Give Blessing.*` once hooked), `RH.Blessings`. Tags: 30 `Blessing.<Path>.<Name>` in `Config/Tags/RH.ini`. Tests: `RogueHero.Blessings.*` (Data, StatMods, AllApply), `RogueHero.Favor.*` (Thresholds, TierHook), `RogueHero.Effects.*` (BatchDispatch, StatusOnHit, Signatures) — 30/30 `RogueHero` pass headless; smoke `RH.Favor Storm 9, RH.GiveBlessing ×3, RH.Spawn 300, RH.Blessings, RH.Stats` SMOKE: PASS.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 4 Fri (ultimate part): `URHUltimateComponent` + six Intercessions.** (1) **Charge rule** = GDD §4.2 as written: a 60 s cooldown × the `Cooldown` stat (so the Contracts −20 % node is just a stat mod), time-based on the sim clock, **starts empty at run start** (interpretation: GDD gives no start state), resets on cast; kills/damage do not charge it. API `GetCharge01 / IsReady / OnReady / OnFired / TryActivate / Fire(Path) / FillCharge`. Patron from `URHRunState::GetPatron()` (no Patron = Q does nothing and logs). (2) **Flash lockout:** `URHUltimateComponent::RequestScreenFlash()` is the only path to a full-screen white flash and grants at most one per 60 s of sim time **independent of the cooldown** (a 50 % Cooldown Sun Lance still fires inside the lockout, without the overexposure). Only Radiance asks. The flash is physical: an unbound post-process (priority 10) raising the fixed exposure bias +2.5 EV (0.04 s rise, 0.66 s fall) with lens flare; the Quicksilver grade is a second post-process (saturation 0.55–0.6, slight silver-green gain). (3) **Ultimates** (`Build/Ultimates/RHUltimate_Paths`): `URHUltimate` (Abstract, : `URHEffect` so `URHGodDef::UltimateClass` can name it) with `Step(dt)` + `Draw(FRHUltDraw&)`; owned and stepped by the component, **not** by `URHEffectManager::DispatchStep` (a few seconds of self-contained sim; the manager is not dispatching in game yet). Class lookup: god def `UltimateClass` if set, else `RHUltimates::GetClassForPath_CodeFallback`. Every number is a `Param()` (placeholders, tune Week 6 Wed); radii scale with Area, damage with Damage. Storm *Thunderhead*: 6 s, 7 strikes/s across the visible area (60 % centred on a random on-screen enemy), 200 uu, 90 dmg + Shock 0.15/4 s; 2–3 return strokes in 0.2 s, ground flash, point light, 4 s scorch. Forge *Forge Nova*: fire front 1 600 uu/s to 1 500 uu, 120 dmg once per enemy + Burn 20 dps/4 s; burning-ground rings every 300 uu (patches 120 uu, 12 dmg + Burn every 0.5 s for 4 s). Tide *Tidal Wave*: 2 400 uu × 2 400 uu wall from just behind the hero along the densest strip (auto-aim, the player never aims), 1 800 uu/s, 70 dmg once + 2 200 uu/s push + 3 Chill stacks (= Freeze) 3 s, rime left behind. Shade *Closing*: `URHGemSubsystem::MagnetAll` at the cast (souls join when they exist), then 3 drain pulses (0.25 s, +0.45 s) of 45 dmg + Mark within 1 300 uu, heal 0.5 HP per enemy capped 8 HP per pulse; violet particulate drawn inward + contracting ring. Radiance *Sun Lance*: 2 s beam on the hero along the densest direction (fixed at cast), 3 200 × 260 uu, 32 dmg every 0.1 s (640 total) + Blind every 0.5 s, the one flash. Quicksilver *Time Dilation*: 4 s enemy time ×0.3 + dash reset. Names/descs `ST_Ultimates` (`Ult.<Path>.Name/Desc`, descs formatted from the ultimate's own Params), en + zh-Hans; Narrative §14 row decided. (4) **Damage route (temporary):** `IRHStatusTarget::DamageEnemy` is still stubbed in game, so each ultimate hit is a **pin**: a one-step `Zone` bullet (4 uu, 1.5 sim steps of life, `bFusion` so it is never recycled at the cap, speed 1 uu/s only to carry the push direction into `FRHHitEvent::Dir`, spec `Knockback` = push) spawned on the enemy through `URHBulletSubsystem::Spawn`; damage, kills, XP gems, hit FX and `RH.Stats` all flow through the normal pipeline. Pin specs are cached per (damage, push), ≤ 256. Swap-out: once `URHEnemySubsystem` implements `DamageEnemy` (deaths into its kill buffer) set `URHUltimateComponent::DamageRoute = StatusTarget` (the tests already use it) and delete the pin path. Statuses go through `URHEffectManager::ApplyStatus` (stubbed in game, recorded in tests). (5) **Rendering** = ISM fallback (a): `ARHUltimateFX` owns one `ARHInstanceRenderer` per `ERHUltLook` (M_Tracer emissive for bolts/flame/water/motes/beam; the lit tint material for scorch and rime, which are surfaces, not lights), a pool of 6 transient point lights (brightest requests win) and the two post-process components; swap-out = one `NS_Ult_<Path>` Niagara system per Path fed from the same `FRHUltDraw` arrays. Colours = Art §2 Keeper colours (Forge amber kept off enemy magenta). (6) **Attachment / time scale (temporary):** `URHUltimateSubsystem` (tickable world subsystem) adds the component to the player's `ARHHero` and calls `Init`, which binds `IA_Ultimate` itself. Quicksilver's slow is read by the game mode through `URHUltimateSubsystem::GetEnemyTimeScale(World)`; **until that call exists** the subsystem falls back to world time dilation 0.3 with the hero's `CustomTimeDilation` compensating (so player weapons slow too during the fallback). **Hook lines for the scheduler** (files I do not own): `ARHHero` ctor `Ultimate = CreateDefaultSubobject<URHUltimateComponent>(TEXT("Ultimate"));` (+ `UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<URHUltimateComponent> Ultimate;` in RHHero.h) and in `BeginPlay` after `BindHero`: `Ultimate->Init(this);` · `ARHHero::OnUltimate`: `if (URHUltimateComponent* U = FindComponentByClass<URHUltimateComponent>()) { U->TryActivate(); }` (then delete the `BindAction` block in `URHUltimateComponent::Init`) · `ARHHero::ResetDashCooldown() { DashCharges = MaxDashCharges; DashCooldownLeft = 0.f; }` and in `URHUltimateComponent::ResetDash` replace the log with `if (ARHHero* H = Hero.Get()) { H->ResetDashCooldown(); }` · `ARHGameMode::SimStep`: `Enemies->Step(Dt * URHUltimateSubsystem::GetEnemyTimeScale(World), Hero);` (`#include "Build/Ultimates/RHUltimateSubsystem.h"`; enemy-owned bullets get the same factor when they exist; player bullets and weapons keep `Dt` — "enemies/bullets at 30 %" read as enemy bullets) · `Build/RHGodDef.cpp` factory (optional, lookup already falls back): `Def->UltimateClass = RHUltimates::GetClassForPath_CodeFallback(Def->PathTag);` · HUD ultimate ring: `GetCharge01()`, `IsReady()`, `OnReady`, `OnFired`, `RHUltimates::GetName(Patron)`. **Enemy-side needs:** `DamageEnemy` with deaths in the kill buffer; an `IRHStatusTarget::PushEnemy(Enemy, Impulse)` for Tide on the status route; Shock / Burn / Chill (3 = Freeze) / Mark / Blind as specified in the 2026-10-18 effect-layer entry; boss refusal of Freeze/Blind. (7) Debug (`Debug/RHUltCommands.cpp`): `RH.Ult [Path]` (force-fire, default the Patron's), `RH.Ult.Charge`, `RH.Ult.Status`. Tests `RogueHero.Ultimates.*` (9: Registry incl. the zh-Hans locres rows, Charge, FlashLockout, one per Path on synthetic enemies: area, once-per-enemy, damage totals, statuses, heal cap, Damage scaling, time scale) — 84/84 `RogueHero` pass headless after merging main f034044 (adaptive unity off).
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 2 Fri: `URHSpawnDirector` + `URHSpawnTimeline`, Bulwark + Courser.** (a) **Director** (`Enemies/RHSpawnDirector`) is a `UTickableWorldSubsystem` whose real entry point is `Step(Dt, RunTime, HeroPos, Rng)`, called by `ARHGameMode` inside its fixed 60 Hz step before the enemy step (TDD §4 order, deterministic per seed); it only steps itself in the debug preview (`RH.Timeline preview`), which exists so it can be exercised before the game-mode handover. The timeline logic is a world-free `FRHSpawnDirectorCore` (tested directly). Its first tick registers `DA_Enemy_Tank` / `DA_Enemy_Rusher` into `URHEnemySubsystem` after the game mode's Swarm (one type per role tag, resolved via `FindType`), so `RH.Spawn N Tank|Rusher` works on every game map. (b) **Timeline** (`URHSpawnTimeline`, primary type `RHSpawn`): `Blocks[]{StartTime, Phase, Mix{EnemyDef, Weight}, Rate, Cap, HPMul, DmgMul}`, `EliteWaves[]{Time, Count, Mix, HPMul 5, MaxDuration, BreathSeconds}`, `Events[]{Time, Ring|Stampede, Enemy, Count, Distance, Duration, BreathSeconds}`, `BossTime`, per-minute scaling (`HPPerMinute` 0.12, `DmgPerMinute` 0.05, multiplied by the block's authored mults) and the off-screen ring (1 350–1 700 uu; was 1 300–1 650). A Breath auto-follows each wave/event (regular spawns paused, nothing banked); a wave's Threat ends when its elites are dead or after `MaxDuration`. Over the block cap regular spawns are **dropped, not queued**; waves and events ignore the cap. C++ fallback `URHSpawnTimeline::MakeDefault` (named `DA_SpawnTimeline_Main`, fallback (d) extended); the director loads `/Game/RH/Data/Spawn/DA_SpawnTimeline_Main` instead when it exists. Authored 0:00–5:00 as twelve 30 s blocks: Harvest throughout (GDD §3.5: nothing between threat beats is dangerous by itself), Scrapwings only at 1.6 → 2.4 /s cap 60; 1:30 + Bulwarks (12 → 20 %); 3:00 + Coursers (10 → 17 %), cap 120, 3.4 → 4.3 /s; 5:00 **elite wave** (2 elites, Tank/Rusher, 5× HP) in a Threat block (2.5 /s, Rusher 25 %), then a 6 s Breath, then Harvest at 4.5 /s until the 6:00+ rows land with their enemies. All numbers are placeholders for the `DA_` asset (tune at the W2 Sun playtest). **Elites are minimal**: per-instance flag, 5× HP, ×1.3 body, bigger status light, counted for the wave's end; no affixes, chest or own shadow yet (Week 4, see the shadow-policy line above). (c) **Bulwark** (`Enemy.Tank`, `ERHEnemyBehaviour::Shielded`, `MakeTank`): HP 90, speed 165, r 58, contact 12, XP 4; drives along its facing, which turns at most 70 °/s (a 180° turn takes ≈ 2.6 s, so circling or dashing past it reaches the flank). Shield: a hit whose **contact point** lies within ±60° of the facing takes **×0.5** (GDD §6 "−50 % from the front"); **piercing bullets (PierceCount > 0 or the Pierce flag) ignore the shield** — interpretation of GDD §6 "rewards pierce, orbit, flanking" so the skill check is "flank or pierce". `RH.Timeline status` reports the damage the shields absorbed. Look: dark squat box hull + a pale-steel plate across the frontal arc. (d) **Courser** (`Enemy.Rusher`, `Charger`, `MakeRusher`): HP 32, walk 240, r 40, contact 14, XP 3. State machine (`RHEnemy::StepCharge`): Approach → within 750 uu and cooldown over → **WindUp 1.0 s** standing still, direction locked at its start → **Charge** 1 500 uu/s for 0.65 s (≈ 975 uu, longer than the trigger range, no steering, no separation) → Recover 0.8 s still → Approach with a 2 s cooldown. Telegraph: a **red emissive line on the ground at exactly the charge length** (M_Tracer, red), widening 10 → 34 uu through the wind-up; its status light swells too. Look: long orange dart (stretched cone). (e) Every enemy now carries a **warm-magenta status light** (M_Tracer sphere at the front-top, ArtDirection §3.4), and a per-enemy facing used for rendering. All accents are three shared ISMs (`RH_Enemies_StatusLights/ShieldPlates/Telegraphs`), so the enemy SoA and the one-ISM-per-type rule stay; enemy contact damage now scales with the spawn-time damage mult. (f) Debug: `RH.Timeline [status|on|off|preview [on|off]|time <mm:ss>|wave]` (`Debug/RHEnemyCommands.cpp`; `RH.Director 0|1` keeps gating whichever spawner the game mode runs). Stress: `RH.Stress.Mix 1` / `-RHStressMix` holds a 60/20/20 Swarm/Tank/Rusher mix. `RH.ShotIn <s> [cmd]` runs a command after a delay (screenshots once spawns are on screen). Tests: `RogueHero.Spawn.{Timeline.Evaluate, Caps, Determinism, EliteWaveBreath, RingOffscreen, Events, Scaling}`, `RogueHero.Enemies.{ShieldArc, TurnRate, ChargeStateMachine}`. Telegraph emissive is kept at 3.5 (status lights 6): at 7 the full-width line bloomed to near-white and lost the red read. **Measured** (`L_StressTest`, `-RHStress=300,5000`, 900 frames after 12 s, RTX 4080, 1920×1080 windowed, Epic, Development editor `-game`, median / p95 ms): **300 mixed (180 Swarm / 60 Tank / 60 Rusher)** frame 3.97 / 4.73, GT 2.30 / 3.43, GPU 3.43 / 3.96; **300 Swarm** baseline frame 3.96 / 4.75, GT 2.24 / 3.04, GPU 3.41 / 3.96. The difference is within noise. `RH.Stats` sim ms for the mixed scene: enemy step 0.20–0.30 ms per frame (swarm only: 0.15–0.19), render push 0.47–0.82 ms (0.41–0.45), the extra being 300 status lights + 60 shield plates on three shared ISMs. **Handover pending:** `ARHGameMode` still runs the Week-1 spawner (`TickSpawning`) until the game-loop owner applies the change listed in the side-session note `w2-fri-spawn.md` (call `URHSpawnDirector::Step` from `SimStep`, route `SpawnAroundHero` through `SpawnRing`, delete `TickSpawning`).
- 2026-10-18 — **Adaptive unity off** (`bUseAdaptiveUnityBuild = false` in both targets). UBT compiled git-modified files outside the unity blob, so parallel worktree sessions built green while their file-local names (`Tag()`, `Dt`, `HitZ`, test `Flags`) collided only on `main` after a merge (three merge breaks on 2026-10-18). Every build is now full unity. Rule for all code: file-local helpers and constants get file-specific names. Cost: a one-file edit recompiles the module blob (~20 s on the dev machine).
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 5 meta core pulled forward as self-contained code (Roadmap W5 Mon, Wed data model, Thu systems; no UI).** New `Source/RogueHero/Meta/` + `Debug/RHMetaCommands.cpp`, tests `RogueHero.Meta.*` (7). (1) **`URHSaveGame`** (slot `RHMeta`, user 0; `-RHSaveSlot=<name>` redirects it) is versioned: `CurrentVersion = 2`, `Migrate()` steps one version at a time (v1 → v2: `bFirstWin` back-filled from `Discovery.Win` / `RunsWon`, lifetime stats re-keyed `Kills` → `Stat.Kills`); a save from a newer build loads read-only (never overwritten, purchases refused). Fields: Ichor + lifetime (int64), `UnlockedContent`, `Discoveries`, `TreeRanks`, `CodexSeen`, `bFirstWin`, run/win counts, `LifetimeStats`, `LastPatron`, `Culture`. **Deviation from §10:** unlocks are `TSet<FName>` content ids (the offer system's id convention, offer line (b)), not `FPrimaryAssetId`; tree ranks are keyed by node id. Tests save only to `RHMeta_Test` (deleted after) or to memory. (2) **`URHMetaSubsystem`** (GameInstance) loads on boot and autosaves after run end, mid-run discoveries and purchases; entry point `OnRunEnded(WorldContext, bWon)` (or `OnRunEnded(FRHRunSummary)`; a second call for the same run is ignored); delegates `OnDiscoveryUnlocked(def, bMidRun)` (the toast is this event only; UI later), `OnRunEndProcessed`, `OnMetaChanged`. Rules are pure functions in `RHMeta::` (`Meta/RHMetaLogic`) so tests need no game instance. (3) **Ichor** = `floor(10 × minutes + 0.1 × kills + 50 × elites + 500 × boss kills)` (GDD §8.1; minutes fractional; +1e-6 before the floor so float coefficients never drop a whole unit; a won run counts ≥ 1 boss kill). Display split (Narrative §7): `Reclaimed = round(Retained / (1 − share))`, `SixShare = Reclaimed − Retained`; **share 0.4 is a PLACEHOLDER** in `URHMetaTuning` (`DA_MetaTuning_CodeFallback` until the asset exists); `RHMeta::FormatSummaryLines` fills the four `ST_World` `Summary.*` lines. (4) **`URHContentRegistry`** (GameInstance) holds `FRHContentEntry` (id, kind, def id, tags, Keeper, blessing slot 1–5, fusion base weapon, `bUnlockedByDefault`); `BuildFallbackContent()` is the C++ fallback listing the GDD's 10 weapons (1–4 open), 20 modifiers (1–8 open), 30 blessings (slots 1–3 open; ids mirror the blessing session's `Blessing.<Path>.<Name>` tags) and 6 fusions (locked until branch node 5) — new-player pool 4 / 8 / 18 / 0 (tested). `RegisterEntry` replaces by id when DA_ defs exist. API for the offer flow (offer files untouched): `IsUnlocked(Id)`, `BuildOfferPool([filter])` → `FRHOfferPool` (the filter lets the caller drop weapons that have no `URHWeaponDef` yet). The unlock set = save unlocks ∪ tree unlocks resolved through the registry (blessing slot / fusion by Keeper), recomputed after every load / discovery / purchase. (5) **15 `URHDiscoveryDef`** (fallbacks `DA_Discovery_<Key>`): conditions are run-stat counter thresholds; counters are `FName`s with tag spelling (`RHCounters::*`), not `FGameplayTag`s, so nothing depends on tag registration. GDD rows: ReachL10 (`Stat.Run.Level` ≥ 10 → Flak Mortar), ScatterKills500 (`Stat.Kills.Weapon.ScatterCannon` ≥ 500 → Scatter Shell), Survive10 (`Stat.Run.Seconds` ≥ 600 → Arc Blade + Seeker Swarm), Elite3Mods (`Stat.Kills.Elite.Mods3` ≥ 1 → Chain Trigger), Freeze100 (`Stat.Status.Freeze` ≥ 100 → Ring Emitter), Crit500 (`Stat.Crit.Max` ≥ 500 → Crit Lens), Win (`Stat.Run.Won` ≥ 1, run end only → Spiral Projector + Gravity Mine + Divine Conduit). **Authored here** (GDD "one per locked item"; tune Week 6): Kills1000 (`Stat.Kills` ≥ 1 000 → Magnify), WeaponL8 (`Stat.Weapon.MaxLevel` ≥ 8 → Overclock), RailKills300 (Rail Lance kills ≥ 300 → Heavy Slug), Elites10 (`Stat.Kills.Elite` ≥ 10 → Burst Capacitor), TwoPaths6 (`Stat.Path.SecondFavor` ≥ 6 → Spread Wave), DroneKills300 (Orbital Drones kills ≥ 300 → Lingering Field), Dashes100 (`Stat.Dashes` ≥ 100 → Velocity Coil), Favor9 (`Stat.Path.MaxFavor` ≥ 9 → Lifeline). Every locked weapon / modifier is opened by exactly one Discovery (tested). All but Win are also checked mid-run (every `MidRunCheckInterval` = 1 s of world time, by `URHRunCounters`). Recollections link by ST_Codex key; `StoryLayer` is read from the CSV `Layer` column. **ST_Codex key fix:** `Codex.Recollection.Homing/Orbit/Boomerang` → `.Magnify/.Overclock/.HeavySlug` — Homing Gyro, Orbit Lock and Boomerang are GDD §5.3 starting modifiers and unlock nothing; text and layers unchanged, zh-Hans carried over, Narrative §13.6 updated. (6) **`URHRunCounters`** (tickable world subsystem): producers push `RecordKill(weaponId, bElite, bBoss, modCount)`, `RecordFreeze`, `RecordCrit`, `RecordDash`; time / level / kills / weapon levels / favor are read from `URHRunState` (and the larger of RunTime / HeroLevel from `URHRunStats::Evaluate`) by `BuildSummary`; counters reset when the run seed changes. (7) **Contracts data** (`URHTreeNodeDef`, fallbacks `DA_Tree_<Branch>_<Node>`, 45 nodes): trunk MaxHP (5 ranks, +10) is the root; XPGain (×1.05/rank, 3) and PickupRadius (×1.10/rank, 3) ← MaxHP 1; Reroll ← PickupRadius; Banish, Lock ← Reroll; StartWeapon (+1 level) and Luck (+1/rank, 3) ← XPGain; Armor (+1/rank, 3) ← MaxHP 3. Each Keeper branch is the GDD §8.2 chain Retainer (+1 Favor as Patron) → Intercession (ultimate −20 %) → Advance4 (blessing #4) → Advance5 (#5) → Reforging (fusion) → StandingAdvance (start-with-a-blessing option). Effects are data (`ERHTreeEffectKind`); `ComputeRunBonuses(patron)` sums them into `FRHMetaRunBonuses` for the run-start code. **Costs, ranks beyond the GDD's, magnitudes and prerequisites are PLACEHOLDERS** (trunk 60–300 per rank, branch 150 / 250 / 300 / 400 / 600 / 500; sized so three early runs buy ≥ 4 nodes, Roadmap W5 Done-when). Purchase fails on unknown node / max rank / missing prerequisite / Ichor / read-only save and spends nothing. (8) **`RH.Codex.ShowLayer <n>`** classifies every layered `ST_Codex` + `ST_Keepers` row (readable / reachable without a win / gated by the first win / orphan) from the Codex defs (6 Keeper entries = start, `Codex.Root.Title` = first win), Discovery recollections, tree `UnlockCodex` effects and in-run tier lines, then proves the bound on a fresh save and on the pre-win worst case (all 14 non-win discoveries + every contract): **max layer reachable before the first win = 2, Layer ≥ 3 rows reachable = 0; after the first win exactly `Codex.Root.Title` (L3) opens; no Layer 4.** Also `RH.Meta.Dump`, `RH.Meta.Ichor <n|+n|-n>`, `RH.Meta.Reset`. (9) Text: `ST_Discoveries` (32 rows) and `ST_Tree` (34 rows) CSVs, en + zh-Hans, registered in `RHStringTables::Register`; branch-node text is shared and formatted with the Keeper honorific. **Pending (scheduler / owners):** the game-mode hook (`Meta->OnRunEnded(this, bWon)` where the run ends), counter producers (enemy kill path, Tide freeze, crit, dash), applying `GetRunBonuses` at run start, building the offer pool from `URHContentRegistry`, and `PrimaryAssetTypesToScan` entries for `RHTree` / `RHDiscovery` / `RHCodex` / `RHMetaTuning` (DefaultGame.ini) when the DA_ assets are authored. Build note: UBT ran with `-NoHotReloadFromIDE` because another checkout's running game held the engine-wide Live Coding mutex (named after `UnrealEditor.exe`, so shared by all worktrees); safe because no running process had this worktree's binaries loaded.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 3 Wed enemy half: status system on enemies + status VFX + the enemy-side hooks** (GDD §5.4, TDD §6/§7, Art §3.6). (1) **SoA.** `FRHEnemyStatuses` (`Enemies/RHStatus`, a plain struct so the rules test without a world) holds TDD §6's `BurnTime/BurnDps`, `ChillStacks/ChillTime`, `FreezeTime`, `ShockTime/ShockMag`, `MarkTime/MarkMag`, `BlindTime/BlindMag` + a per-enemy bit mask; `URHEnemySubsystem` ticks it at the top of `Step` (fixed 60 Hz, before movement, so slows and DoT deaths are in this step's hash). **Rules (GDD numbers):** Burn = magnitude dps for 4 s, refresh keeps the stronger dps (no stacking), ticks every 0.5 s, the partial last tick is flushed so a full burn deals exactly dps × duration; Chill −20 % move speed per stack, 3 stacks = Freeze (stacks consumed, Chill refused while frozen); Shock = ×(1 + 0.15) damage taken on every source (hits, DoT, effect damage); Blind = 0.5 s stun (magnitude = strength: 1 halts, 0.5 = half speed); Mark executes at ≤ 15 % × magnitude of max HP **only when the rule is on** (`SetMarkExecuteThreshold(RHStatus::MarkExecuteFraction)` — the Shade tier-3 effect sets it; off by default because GDD §5.4 makes execution a tier-3 rule). Halted enemies (frozen / full Blind) do not move or turn and their behaviour timers pause (Courser wind-up); a slowed Courser's committed charge keeps its telegraphed length. **Placeholders (GDD silent; tune Week 6 Wed):** Freeze 1.5 s (+ `Status.Freeze` blessing DurationAdd = Deep Cold), Chill 3 s, Shock 3 s, Mark 5 s, bare Burn 5 dps — the defaults equal the blessing procs (Kindling R1, Undertow, Live Wire, Lien) and are what Divine Conduit bullets apply (× the owned status-blessing scale). **Decisions:** contact damage is unchanged by Freeze/Blind (a stopped hull is still solid hardware; keeps teeth, GDD §3.5); role `Boss` refuses Freeze and Blind (Chill caps at 2 stacks) and is never executed. (2) **`IRHStatusTarget` is implemented by `URHEnemySubsystem`** and registered with `URHEffectManager::SetStatusTarget(this)` at `PostInitialize` (and lazily in Step/ApplyHits); cleared at Deinitialize. `FRHEnemyStatusAdapter` (`Build/RHEffectTargets.*`) is now unused — **its owner can delete it**. Deaths outside the hit buffer (DoT, `DamageEnemy`, executions) queue and lead the next `ApplyHits` kill buffer (effect-damage deaths during `DispatchStep` therefore surface one step later). `GetKillStatusMask(killIndex)` gives the victim's status bits for the last ApplyHits' kills (hook for Forge T2 "burning enemies explode" and Tide T3 "frozen enemies shatter"). (3) **Bullet → status:** `ApplyHits` applies `S.StatusTag` after the hit's damage when the enemy survives, rolling `Rng.FRand() < StatusChance` on the sim stream only when 0 < chance < 1 (chance 0 never draws, so pre-status RNG sequences are unchanged; Divine Conduit's 1.0 never draws). (4) **Hooks done:** damage numbers (`AddHit` per resolved hit, `AddKill` per death; DoT feeds **one number per enemy per second of burning**, flushed at burn end/death, through the same pool so the >20 kills/s aggregation still applies); `URHRunCounters` `RecordKill(weapon Tag name, elite, boss, modifier count)` (weapon resolved from the hero's weapon slot; DoT/effect kills have no weapon), `RecordFreeze` per freeze, `RecordCrit(dmg)` per crit. **New overload** `ApplyHits(..., TArray<FRHCritEvent>* OutCrits)` fills the crit buffer; the 4-argument form still compiles. **Pending for the game mode:** call the 5-argument form and pass the crits to `DispatchStep`. (5) **Status VFX** (one ISM each, no shadows; all on the hull, never on the ground (= telegraph language), never travelling (= bullet language), nothing magenta/red (= danger)): Burn = two amber (Forge hue tuned off magenta) emissive cone flame licks flickering at 12 Hz; Chill = pale rime cap on the hull top, 60 % / 85 % cover for 1 / 2 stacks; Freeze = pale ice block over the lower hull, top below the sensor so the warm status light still reads; Shock = two thin blue-white arc filaments re-struck at 20 Hz (dark ~30 %); Mark = thin violet scan-plane sweeping the hull at 1.2 Hz; Blind = white overexposed flare swallowing the sensor light. Swap-out: `NS_Status_*` Niagara systems (TDD §3) reading the same mask. (6) Debug: `RH.Status [<Status.X|X> [N|all] [mag] [dur]]` (nearest the hero; no args = live counts, applied/refused, freezes, executions, DoT/effect damage, tick + VFX ms), `RH.Status execute [frac]`; stress `-RHStressStatuses` / `RH.Stress.Statuses 1` keeps every enemy Burning + Chilled ×2 + Shocked + Marked + half-Blind (every 4th Frozen), and the stress profile now logs enemy / hits / sim-render / status-tick / status-VFX ms rows. Tests `RogueHero.Status.*` (Stacking, Freeze, Shock, DotTotals, MarkExecute, ChanceDeterminism): 68/68 `RogueHero` pass headless. (7) **Measured** (RTX 4080, 1080p windowed, L_StressTest 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, 600 frames): all statuses on all enemies — game thread median 4.35 / p95 6.39 ms, GPU 3.76 / 4.42 ms, status tick ≤ 0.05 ms p95 per frame, status VFX build+sync 0.24 / 0.46 ms; baseline without statuses game 2.54 / 4.21 ms, GPU 3.61 / 4.33 ms. Inside TDD §14 (≤ 8 ms GT, ≤ 12 ms GPU).
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 3 Fri + Sat (effect part): the 18 流派 tiers.** (1) **Classes** `URHEffect_<Path>_T1..T3` (`Build/Effects/RHTierEffects`, base `URHTierEffect`: Path/tier identity, activation log, own stat mods removed on deactivation, `SiblingTier(N)`). Tiers stack; where a higher tier upgrades a lower tier's rule the lower tier reads it (Storm T1 reads T2 `Jumps`/Shock, Forge T2 reads T3 Burn, Shade T2 reads T3 explosion), so each class owns exactly its GDD §5.4 row. **Data:** `FRHGodTier::Params` (TMap) on `URHGodDef::Tiers[]`, copied into the effect's `Overrides` on activation; factory fallback `RHTierEffects::MakeFallbackParams` (GDD numbers marked `[GDD]`, the rest placeholders for Week 6 Wed) and `GetTierClass` fill `DA_God_<Path>_CodeFallback`. (2) **Activation:** `URHEffectManager::HandleFavorTierChanged` creates tiers OldTier+1..NewTier in order and removes them highest-first on the way down; `ResetAll` on run start; **new `URHRunState::EndRun(bWon)` / `OnRunEnded`** → every effect and effect-owned stat mod removed (blessing stat mods stay for the summary), later favor changes inert. `bTierEffectsEnabled` (tests isolating blessings, A/B). (3) **Tier toast:** `URHEffectManager::OnTierLine(God, Tier, FText)` per tier crossed upwards, FText = `Tiers[N].TierLine` (ST_Keepers `Keeper.<Path>.Tier<N>`), logged `Tier toast [God.X TN]: …` until the HUD binds it. (4) **Manager additions:** effect stat mods (`SetEffectStatMods`/`ClearEffectStatMods`), `DealDamage[InRadius]` (pre-scaled damage), `GetHitDamage` (bullet spec damage, hero Damage included), `ExecuteEnemy`, `GrantXP` (→ `ARHHero::AddXP`), new batched events **`OnShots`** (`FRHShotEvent`, queued by `ARHHero::TickWeapons` when a weapon's tick added bullets — capped 1 024 pending until dispatch is wired; swap-out: a fired-volley callback from `URHWeaponInstance`) and **`OnStatusDeaths`** from a **status ledger**: enemies an effect successfully applied a status to are tracked (expiry from the applied duration, Freeze sampled with `HasStatus` while Chilled); when one is no longer alive — or its slot jumps > 300 uu in a step (killed + respawned) — a `FRHStatusDeathEvent{Enemy, Pos, Statuses}` is delivered next step. **Deviation (temporary):** `FRHKillEvent` (Core/RHTypes.h, not mine) has no slot or statuses; swap-out = the enemy subsystem fills `int32 Enemy` + status bits on `FRHKillEvent` and the ledger is deleted. Consequence: only statuses applied through `URHEffectManager::ApplyStatus` (blessings, tiers) count, not ones bullets apply directly (`ApplyPatronStatus`). (5) **Shade:** Souls are SoA in `URHEffect_Shade_T1` (cap 400, 12 s life, home in from the pickup radius, collect → `HealHero(1)` + `GrantXP(1)`); Shade minions are SoA in `URHEffect_Shade_T2` (cap 12, no Actor per minion; seek nearest enemy within 800 uu, strike every 0.5 s, explode on expiry with T3). Both draw through one `ARHInstanceRenderer` per effect (cool violet; Art §3). **Quicksilver:** new stats **`ERHStat::DashCharges`** (base 1 in `URHRunTuning`; `ARHHero` sets `MaxDashCharges` from it and grants a gained charge at once) and **`ERHStat::Ricochet`** (flat, base 0) + tags `Stat.DashCharges` / `Stat.Ricochet`; dash trail = SoA segments every 60 uu in `URHEffect_Quicksilver_T2` (cool mint discs); T3 attack speed = +1.0 × (MoveSpeed / base MoveSpeed − 1), re-applied only on change. **"Every 5th shot"** counts volleys per weapon slot (`OnShots`), bolts strike the nearest enemy to the hero within 900 uu. (6) **Not live in game yet** (exact hooks for files I do not own): `ARHGameMode::SimStep` after `ImpactFX->Step(...)`: `if (URHEffectManager* FX = World->GetSubsystem<URHEffectManager>()) { FX->DispatchStep(Dt, HitBuffer, KillBuffer, CritBuffer); }` (crits: `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits` fills `TArray<FRHCritEvent>& OutCrits` with `{e, Pos[e], Dmg, S.WeaponSlot}` when `bCrit`) · run end (hero death / boss kill) in `ARHGameMode`: `URHRunState::Get(this)->EndRun(bWon);` · `RHSpecComposition.cpp` after `S.RicochetCount = ClampCount(Ricochet);`: `S.RicochetCount = ClampCount(Ricochet + FMath::RoundToInt32(HeroStats.Get(ERHStat::Ricochet))); if (S.RicochetCount > 0) { S.Flags |= ERHBulletFlags::Ricochet; }` · HUD: bind `URHEffectManager::OnTierLine` for the toast. **Enemy side must implement** (`IRHStatusTarget`, `Build/RHEffectTypes.h`): `ApplyStatus` returning true when applied (Burn dps / Chill +stacks, 3 = `Status.Freeze` / Shock dmg-taken / Mark / Blind stun), `HasStatus` for Chill, Freeze and Mark, `DamageEnemy` with deaths joining the next kill buffer, and the two new non-pure virtuals **`GetEnemyHealthFraction`** (default 1 = Shade T3 never executes) and **`ExecuteEnemy`** (default = `DamageEnemy(e, 1e9)`), then `SetStatusTarget(this)`. Until then Storm/Forge/Tide/Shade-T3/Radiance-T2-T3 rules run but the stub target refuses statuses and damage; stat tiers (Radiance T1/T3 crit, Quicksilver T1/T2/T3) are live now. (7) Debug: `RH.Debug.Tiers` (per Path favor/tier, each active tier's rule + counters, the hero stats tiers change, dispatch step count), `RH.Later <s> <cmd>` (smoke: `-ExecCmds` run on frame 0, before the game mode restarts the run on frame 1). Tests `RogueHero.Tiers.*` (Activation, Storm, Forge, Tide, Shade, Radiance, Quicksilver, StatusLedger) on synthetic batches against a fake enemy target; older `RogueHero.Blessings/Effects` rigs disable tiers to stay blessing-only. 78/78 `RogueHero` tests pass headless; smoke `RH.Later 2 RH.Favor <each Path> 9, RH.Later 6 RH.Debug.Tiers` shows all 18 tiers on and 18 toasts.
- 2026-10-18 — **Week 4 Mon + Tue, weapon half: weapons 5–10 + archetypes** (GDD §5.2, TDD §5.4; the Fab kitbash enemy models of those days are not part of this). Factory fallbacks (fallback (d) extended) `URHWeaponDef::Make{FlakMortar, ArcBlade, SeekerSwarm, RingEmitter, SpiralProjector, GravityMine}` (`DA_Weapon_<Name>`), `MakeAllFallbacks` (all ten, registry fills from it); new `URHWeaponDef::bUnlockedByDefault` (1–4 true, 5–10 Discoveries — the offer pool should filter on it); `ST_Weapons` rows en + zh-Hans. Every L2–L8 step is one of {+damage, +count, +fire rate, +size} (asserted). **Table** (L1 → L8; all numbers placeholders, tune Week 6): Flak Mortar Lob / DensestCluster / `Shell`, 18 dmg, 1 shell / 1.4 s, blast r 150 → 32 dmg, 3 shells / 1.12 s, blast r 234 (track dmg, count, rate, size, dmg, count, size); Arc Blade MeleeArc / Nearest / `Blade`, 120° swing, reach 220, 14 dmg / 0.7 s → 28 dmg, 2 swings / 0.448 s, reach 344 (dmg, rate, size, count, dmg, rate, size); Seeker Swarm Homing / Random / `Missile`, 4 missiles, 9 dmg / 1.1 s, native turn 300°/s → 16 dmg, 8 missiles / 0.704 s, size ×1.25 (dmg, count, rate, dmg, count, rate, size); Ring Emitter Radial / None / `RingOrb`, 12 bullets / 1.2 s (GDD), 8 dmg → 15 dmg, 20 bullets / 0.768 s (dmg, count, rate, size, count, dmg, rate); Spiral Projector Spiral / None / `SpiralShot`, 1 arm, 17°/shot, 4 dmg / 0.07 s → 7 dmg, 3 arms / 0.045 s (dmg, count, rate, size, count, dmg, rate); Gravity Mine Mine / DensestCluster / `Mine`, 40 dmg / 2.2 s, blast r 170, pull r 320 → 67 dmg, 2 mines / 1.41 s, radii ×1.56 (dmg, size, rate, count, dmg, size, rate). **Sim** (`ERHBulletMotion` on the spec, set from the pattern; modifiers never change it): (1) **Lob** — fixed hang time (`Lifetime`; Velocity Coil / hero Projectile Speed shorten it so Speed × Lifetime stays the nominal reach); the weapon scales each shell's speed so it lands on its point (extra shells on a ring of 160 uu); no collision in flight; on landing it **detonates**: every enemy within `BlastRadius` (base × level size × Magnify × Area) is hit once, knockback outwards; drawn on a parabola (render-only apex). (2) **MeleeArc** — a single *sweep bullet* pivoting on the hero (`Size` = reach, `Speed` = tip speed, swings alternate fore-/back-hand); each step it hits every enemy within reach whose centre the blade passed over (angle in [prev, prev + step)), so successive steps partition the arc and each enemy is hit once per pass, with no hit-history limit; drawn as a 6-segment crescent. (3) **Mine** — tossed like a shell, lands, arms (0.4 s), triggers when an enemy is within 110 uu (or after 5 s), pulls for 0.6 s, detonates like a shell. **Pull deviation:** the enemy side (not ours) skips knockback on zero-damage hits, so the pull is a separate spec (`FRHComposedWeapon::Pull`, 6th spec slot per weapon) whose hits every 0.1 s deal 5 % damage with an *inward* knockback of 260 uu/s; flagged `bNoImpactFX` so ImpactFX draws no sparks for them. Swap-out: a real pull force in `URHEnemySubsystem` (spawn session) replaces the pull hits. (4) **Homing** — homing-native spec; each missile is pre-locked on its own random alive enemy in range (`Spawn(..., HomingTarget)`), launched over a 140° fan; a dead target re-locks onto the nearest. (5) **Radial** rings turn half a step per volley; **Spiral** turns 17° per shot. Both fire with target rule None, i.e. also with no enemy in sight (pure 弹幕). (6) **DensestCluster** scores up to 32 sampled enemies in range by the enemies within `ClusterRadius` (200 / 240 uu) and aims at the winner's centroid; **Random** picks uniformly among alive enemies in range (per-slot `FRandomStream`, replayable). Render-only detonation shockwaves (`Blast`) and the collapsing pull ring (`Field`) are thin rings of 20 tangential streaks, not bullets (no cap, max 256 live); **a filled blast-sized additive disc was tried first and rejected**: at L8 (blast r 234) overlapping discs saturated to opaque white blobs hiding gems and enemies (Art §3 readability). Looks (cool range, ArtDirection §2): Shell periwinkle fat slug on an arc + expanding shockwave ring; Blade icy white-cyan crescent; Missile spring-green thin streak with long trail; RingOrb round electric-blue orbs; SpiralShot blue-violet teardrops; Mine cool-white floor disc pulsing when armed. **Modifier × weapon interpretations** (all 20 on all 10, `RogueHero.Modifiers.AllWeapons` = 200 pairs): *Shells and mines* — the blast is the impact: Split throws that many **bomblets** (small shells, blast ×0.6) from the crater, Lingering Field leaves one zone over the crater (radius ≥ 0.75 × blast), Scatter Shell bullets are bomblets too, Spread Wave stays a straight wave; after the blast the shell **hops**: Pierce → onwards along its line for half its reach (per pierce point), Ricochet → to a fresh enemy within ricochet range, Boomerang → back to the hero's position once (blasting again; a hopping mine re-arms where it lands); Homing Gyro steers the shell in flight; Orbit Lock orbits it round the hero with a **contact fuse** (touching an enemy detonates it — a shield of shells) or releases it after 2 s into a normal lob. *Arc Blade* — the sweep never dies on a hit (melee is pierce-native): **Pierce Rounds widens the swing by 20° per point** (120° → 180° with +3); Ricochet re-swings back-hand at a fresh enemy near the hero (≤ 2×); Boomerang swings back over the same arc once; Homing Gyro drags the swing towards the nearest enemy (×0.25–×2 angular speed, never reverses); Orbit Lock spins the blade round the hero for 2 s (whirlwind) and then **throws it** off its tip as a projectile (radius = reach × 0.15); Split/Linger act per enemy hit (shards fly outwards from the hero); Scatter/Wave leave the tip at the end of the swing; Magnify = longer reach; speed modifiers change the swing speed. *Seeker Swarm* — Homing Gyro **adds** its turn rate to the native 300°/s. Fork/Mirror/Burst wrap every pattern as before (forked swings, mirrored mines, burst rings). **At the cap**, oldest-first recycling takes slow shells and mines before they land (seen only in the synthetic cap storm; accepted). **Perf harness:** the hero has 4 slots (GDD §5.1), so `RH.GiveExtra <Weapon> [level] [Mod.* …]` (`Combat/RHExtraWeapons`, perf only, never in a run) fires extra weapons from the hero in slots 4+ (`URHBulletSubsystem::MaxWeaponSlots` 8 → 12). **Measured** (RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, 1920×1080 windowed, Epic, Development editor `-game`, `-RHStress=400,0` = 400 live enemies held, hero invulnerable; 3 600-frame CSV after 65 s; `RH.Stats` minute 1): **all 10 weapons L8 + 3 modifiers each** (Flak Split/Linger/Chain, Blade Fork/Ricochet/Boomerang, Seeker Split/Fork/Scatter, Mine Pierce/Split/Linger, Ring Fork/Pierce/Wave, Spiral Mirror/Pierce/Ricochet, Rifle Split/Fork/Scatter, Scatter Cannon Split/Pierce/Burst, Drones Fork/Mirror/Burst, Lance Wave/Linger/Split): **live bullets avg 701, peak 1 266** (targets ≥ 150 / ≥ 800 — met), 0 recycled, frame 4.19 ms median / 5.23 p95, GT 2.15 / 2.73, GPU 3.47 / 4.16; bullet step p95 0.067 ms, bullet render p95 0.115 ms, weapon tick p95 0.010 ms. **A legal 4-slot build** (Ring Fork/Pierce/Wave, Spiral Mirror/Pierce/Ricochet, Seeker Split/Fork/Scatter, Flak Split/Linger/Chain): **live bullets avg 601, peak 869**, frame 3.93 / 4.54, GT 1.97 / 2.37, GPU 3.46 / 4.01. So the §3.5 bullet-volume gap noted for weapons 1–4 (avg 74–95) is closed by the 弹幕 emitters (Ring / Spiral), as predicted. The 6 000 cap holds in `RogueHero.Modifiers.CapStormPatterns` (weapons 5–10 with three spawning modifiers each, 6 000 reached, ~112 k recycles, invariants every step). Tests: new `RogueHero.Weapons.*` (Fallbacks, Lob, MeleeArc, Mine, Homing, RadialSpiral, DensestCluster); `AllWeapons` and `CapStorm` extended to 10 weapons — 53/53 `RogueHero` pass headless. Also renamed `RHModTest::Flags` → `ModTestFlags`: with `using namespace RHModTest` it clashed (C4459) with `IConsoleManager.h` once adaptive unity regrouped the test file with UI sources.
- 2026-10-18 — **Game-mode integration pass** (finishes the lost agent's WIP d11bb19, reviewed). `ARHGameMode` now runs TDD §4 in order: `URHSpawnDirector::Step` (replaces the Week-1 `TickSpawning` curve; `SpawnAroundHero` → `SpawnRing`; phase/block log line) → enemies → weapons → bullets → **`ARHGameMode::ResolveStepHits`** (new static, shared with the wiring tests: `ApplyHits` with the crit buffer → `URHBulletSubsystem::ResolveHitOutcomes` → `URHRunState::AddKills` → gems → impact FX → `URHEffectManager::DispatchStep(Dt, Hits, Kills, Crits)`) → gem pickup. The temporary `ResolveHitOutcomes` forward at the top of `URHImpactFXSubsystem::Step` is deleted (Chain Trigger / Lifeline counted once; `RogueHero.Wiring.HitPipeline` asserts it). Run start: `URHRunState::StartRun` → `URHOfferFlow` with `BuildPool(weapon registry, URHContentRegistry)` (weapons, modifiers and blessings filtered by meta unlocks, so offers are 5 cards: weapon / upgrade / modifier→weapon / blessing) → starting weapon → `URHMetaSubsystem::GetRunBonuses` (stat mods under source `Meta.Tree`, run tools, patron favor, starting weapon level). Run end (`ARHGameMode::EndRun`, once): `URHRunState::EndRun` (effects/tiers off) then `URHMetaSubsystem::OnRunEnded`. Modifier cards socket through `URHWeaponInstance::AddModifier` after `URHRunState::AddWeaponModifier`; blessing cards apply through `OnBlessingChanged` → effect manager. **Fix:** `RunTime` advanced per frame while the step loop caps and drops backlog, so under `RH.TimeScale 4` the timeline ran 25.8 s against 15.4 s simulated; it now advances per simulated step. Debug: `RH.Offer.AutoPick worst` (decline every offer; no-pick probe), `RH.EndRun [win]`, director / effects / hit-outcome lines in `RH.Stats`. **No-pick probe:** stationary hero (no input), auto-pick worst, seed varies: dies at **16.1 s** simulated with 11 kills and never levels up (the Pulse Rifle at L1 cannot hold the first Harvest block's ring), so the GDD §3.5 "overrun by ~12:00" check needs a movement bot (none exists) and the full timeline. **Still unwired:** patron (hub, Week 5: runs start with no patron, so `GetRunBonuses(empty)` and no patron ultimate charge path), a win (`EndRun(true)` only from `RH.EndRun win` until the boss), fusions / Legendaries (L16 screen skipped: no `URHFusionDef`), the HUD binding for `URHEffectManager::OnTierLine`, meta ultimate-cooldown and start-with-a-blessing bonuses, and the status ledger swap-out to the enemy kill masks. `ARHHero::GetRunState` still starts a run at BeginPlay before the game mode's (run started is logged twice; harmless; Player/ owner).
- 2026-10-18 — **Ultimates wired into the hero (playable).** (1) `ARHHero` owns `URHUltimateComponent` (`CreateDefaultSubobject` "Ultimate", `Init` in BeginPlay after `BindHero`); `IA_Ultimate` (already in `IMC_Gameplay_KBM` Q / `IMC_Gameplay_Pad` LB) → `ARHHero::OnUltimate` → `TryActivate`; the component's own input binding is gone. `ARHHero::ResetDashCooldown()` makes Quicksilver's dash reset real. `URHUltimateSubsystem` now only looks the component up (still attaches one to a pawn that lacks it). (2) **Charge stays time-based** (60 s × Cooldown, GDD §4.2): the request asked for "charge gain from kills/damage per GDD", but the GDD specifies only a 60 s cooldown and no kill/damage charge, so the docs win; adding kill charge would be a GDD change (swap point: `URHUltimateComponent::Advance`). (3) **Which ultimate:** `GetUltimatePath()` = debug override (`RH.Ult.Path <Path>`) → `URHRunState` Patron → **the run's first Path** (the Keeper of the first blessing taken) until the hub / patron menu sets a Patron (Week 5 / Roadmap W4 Fri menu); no Path = Q does nothing and the meter says "No Keeper yet". (4) **Damage route:** `URHEnemySubsystem` implements `IRHStatusTarget::DamageEnemy` now, so the default route is `StatusTarget` (Shock multiplies, deaths join the kill buffer, damage numbers fire there); pins remain only for pushing hits (Tide) because the interface has no `PushEnemy` yet. (5) **HUD meter** `URHUltimateMeter` (`UI/RHUltimateMeter`, fills the reserved `URHHUDWidget::UltimateSlot`): device-aware `IA_Ultimate` glyph (`URHInputPrompt`), ultimate name, percent / Ready (`ST_Ultimates` `HUD.Ult.*`, en + zh-Hans), a 5 px bar in the Keeper colour (dimmed to 55 % while charging) on the HUD's dark bar back; polls the component per frame and updates only on change (same deviation as `URHHUDViewModel::Sample`). (6) Observed, not mine: `ARHGameMode::StartRunState` (first Tick) restarts the run the hero's `GetRunState()` already started, which wipes blessings / favor given by `-ExecCmds` at frame 0 — hence `RH.Ult.Path` for smoke runs. **Hook line (game mode, integration):** `Enemies->Step(Dt * URHUltimateSubsystem::GetEnemyTimeScale(World), Hero);` in `ARHGameMode::SimStep` (until then Time Dilation uses the world-dilation fallback). Tests `RogueHero.Ultimates.*` +2 (PathSelection, Meter).
- 2026-10-18 — **Run summary screen (Roadmap W4 Sat "win/lose + run summary", W5 Sat summary part).** New `Source/RogueHero/UI/RunSummary/`. (1) **`URHRunSummaryScreen`** (Menu layer, code-built WBP_RunSummary stand-in under the Week-2 UI fallback; swap-out = reparent a `WBP_RunSummary` onto it) shows on win and death: VICTORY / DEFEAT header, time survived (m:ss, culture-invariant digits), kills, level; weapons + level + socketed modifiers; advances (blessings) + rank, coloured by Keeper; 流派 tiers reached (tier ≥ 1, highest first); Ichor as `reclaimed / the Six's share / retained` (exactly `RHMeta::FormatSummaryLines`, display-only split) + the banked balance; Discoveries of this run (`ST_Discoveries` names); `Filed under: <seed>`; one dim RH.Stats line (kills/min, swarm TTK median, dashes/min, %HP lost/min — the "also written to the run summary" of §14); buttons **Return to Vigil** (opens `L_Hub` when `/Game/RH/Maps/L_Hub` exists, **else reloads `L_Arena_Test` — temporary, swap-out = automatic once the hub map lands**) and **Run again** (reloads the current map). Pad + KBM: focus starts on Return, Left/Right (Slate nav) and IA_UI_Prev/Next move between the buttons, Accept / Enter / click press; **Back is bound to nothing on purpose** (leaving the summary must be a deliberate choice). Text re-resolves on a live culture switch. (1) **The hull is one enemy-SoA slot**, not an Actor: `URHBossDef : URHEnemyDef` is `DA_Enemy_Boss` (`/Game/RH/Enemies/Boss/`; until it is authored the C++ factory fallback **`URHBossDef::MakeBossFallback`** is used, fallback (d) extended), registered with `URHEnemySubsystem` like any role, so weapons aim at it (`FindNearest`), bullets / statuses / effects / ultimates hit it (Boss refuses Freeze / Blind and is never executed, as already coded), and its death goes through the normal kill buffer (`RunCounters` books the boss kill → Ichor +500). (2) **State machine** `FRHBossBrain` (world-free; `ERHBossPhase` Dormant/One/Two/Three/Defeated, `ERHBossAction`), driven by `URHBossSubsystem`. Phases from HP (60 % / 30 %), **advance only** (a burst through both thresholds reports One→Two and Two→Three in order; heals never re-run a phase), each change starts a 1.5 s **phase shift** with no attacks. P1: walk 1.2 s, then alternately a **charge** (direction locked, 1.1 s wind-up with a red projected corridor — centre line + two hull-width edge lines — at exactly the charge length, 1 800 uu clipped to the arena wall / hazard ring, 2 100 uu/s, 22 dmg, 0.9 s recover) and a **sweep** (only if the hero is within 900 uu, else another charge: 0.85 s wind-up with a red 130° sector of r 700 and a closing front, 18 dmg). P2: walks to 1 000 uu and holds; a **radial ring every 4 s** (fire-to-fire; 0.6 s projected-arc telegraph tightening onto the hull inside the interval), 24 slots, 430 uu/s; **Bulwark adds** (the role's registered type, else `MakeTank`) 2 every 14 s, cap 4 alive, first right after the shift. P3: rings every 2.5 s, 32 slots, 480 uu/s, plus a 2-arm **spiral** (8 /s per arm, 95 °/s, 340 uu/s); **arena shrink**: a red/magenta hazard ring around the arena centre, shown at 2 800 uu for 2 s, then −60 uu/s to 1 300 uu, 8 dmg per 0.5 s outside (0.25 s grace on crossing); the hull is kept inside it. Interpretations of GDD §6 (no numbers there): charges/sweep are P1 only, adds P2 only (P3 is pure pattern reading), all numbers are `FRHBossTuning` placeholders for the W4 Sun playtest. **Dodge rule (tested):** every ring leaves a lane of 3 empty slots, centred within ±40° of the hero's bearing and shown as the gap in the ring telegraph, ≥ 3 hero diameters wide at the hold range, so a ring is passable on foot; a dash's i-frames pass through boss bullets (they are not consumed). (3) **Deviation — boss bullets are not in `URHBulletSubsystem`.** It has no hostile side (it collides only against enemies) and Combat/* is another session's this week, so the boss keeps `FRHBossBulletField` (`Boss/RHBossBullets`): SoA, dense swap-remove, the same fixed step, one circle test vs the hero, cap 1 500 (drops, never recycles, counted), warm tracer look per ArtDirection §3.2 (orange emissive streak on M_Tracer + a dark core, ISM). **Swap-out point:** when `URHBulletSubsystem` gains enemy-owned bullets (the Flare Pylon needs them too), `FRHBossBulletField::Spawn` becomes a call to it and the file is deleted.
