A rename there is a compile error, never silent. **Swap-out:** add public `float GetHP(int32 i) const { return HP[i]; }`, `float GetMaxHP(int32 i) const { return MaxHP[i]; }`, `void SetScriptedPos(int32 i, const FVector2f& P) { Pos[i] = P; Knock[i] = FVector2f::ZeroVector; }` to `URHEnemySubsystem`, forward the three bridge functions to them and delete the access block. (5) **Stepping:** `URHBossSubsystem::Step(Dt, Hero)` is the fixed-step entry point; until `ARHGameMode::SimStep` calls it (hook below) the subsystem self-steps from `Tick` on its own 60 Hz accumulator (stops while `bRunOver` or an offer is open, scaled by `TimeScale`; Quicksilver's slow reaches it through the global-dilation fallback). - 2026-10-18 — **Placeholder audio layer (Roadmap W6 Tue; GDD §3.5 anti-fatigue audio).** New `Source/RogueHero/Audio/`: `URHAudioSubsystem` (tickable world subsystem, game worlds only), `FRHAudioScheduler` (engine-free voice logic, tested), `URHAudioDef` (`DA_Audio`, Primary Data Asset: per-cue sound / volume / pitch jitter / MinInterval / MaxVoices / aggregate gain, voice caps, crowd thresholds, mix refs), `ERHSfx` cues keyed by mechanic (`Fire_<ERHFirePattern>`, `Hit`, `Crit`, `Kill`, `CrowdLoop`, `LevelUp`, `OfferOpen`, `OfferPick`, `Ultimate`, `TierLine`, `HeroHurt`, `HeroDeath`, `BossPhase`, `UI_Focus/Confirm/Back`). **Content:** `Tools/synth_audio.py` (stdlib-only procedural synth, fixed seeds, nothing downloaded) + `Tools/import_audio.py` / `Tools\CreateAudio.bat` (headless) → 25 `SW_*` SoundWaves in `Content/RH/Audio/SFX`, `SC_Master` ⊃ `SC_SFX` / `SC_UI` (UI-sound: plays while paused) / `SC_Music`, `SMix_Master`, `SCON_Combat` (16, stop quietest; engine guard behind the layer's own caps), `SCON_UI` (4), `DA_Audio` filled from `URHAudioDef::FillDefaultCues` (also the C++ factory fallback when the asset is missing). The WAVs are build intermediates in `Saved/AudioGen` and are not committed; re-run `CreateAudio.bat` after changing the synth. **Rules (GDD §3.5):** combat cues (fire / hit / crit / kill) aggregate: one request per cue per frame carrying the frame's event count (volume × (1 + gain·log2 N), capped 1.6), MinInterval and MaxVoices per cue, ≤ 4 combat starts per frame, combat ≤ 16 voices, everything ≤ 24 (8 always free for stingers / UI); smoothed kill rate (τ 0.35 s) > 20 kills/s switches kill one-shots off and fades in one looping crowd layer (volume 0.35→0.9 by 80 kills/s, off below 15: hysteresis), hit ticks ducked to 50 % under it. Voices are tracked logically (start + duration / pitch), so the cap holds with or without an audio device. **Sources (public events only):** hits / kills / crits / weapon volleys through `URHEffect_AudioListener` on `URHEffectManager` (Source `Audio.Listener`, subscribes Hits | Kills | Crits | Shots; a volley's cue is its weapon's `ERHFirePattern`, so no fire hook was needed: `FRHShotEvent` already exists); level-up `URHRunState::OnLevelUpPending` (plays at once, not after the pick); offer `URHOfferFlow::OnScreenShown` / `OnCardApplied`; ultimate `URHUltimateComponent::OnFired`; tier `URHEffectManager::OnTierLine`. **Deviations / swap-out points:** (a) the listener is an ordinary effect, so `ResetAll` / run end drop it and the subsystem re-adds it the next frame (one frame of events lost per run start; it shows as one live effect in `RH.Stats` / `RH.Blessings`, and run end logs it as deactivated). Swap-out: a batched `OnStepEvents` delegate on `URHEffectManager::DispatchStep` (effects owner). (b) Hero hurt / death are sampled from `ARHHero::HP` / `IsDead()` each frame (no hurt event exists). Swap-out: an `OnHurt` delegate on `ARHHero` (player owner). (c) UI SFX fallback: Slate `OnFocusChanging` (focus) and an `IInputProcessor` on Enter / Space / pad A (confirm) and Esc / pad B (back) while a menu context is up (paused world, offer screen, or no arena hero); never consumes input; the first `URHAudioSubsystem::PlayUI` call turns the fallback off. (d) `DA_Audio` is loaded by path (`URHAudioDef::AssetPath`, like the spawn timeline), not registered with the Asset Manager, because `Config/Default*.ini` belongs to the scalability session. (e) All placeholders are 2D (top-down camera, no attenuation). **Hook lines for owners:** Settings master volume — `UI/RHUserSettings.cpp` `URHUserSettings::ApplyAudio`: replace `if (FAudioDeviceHandle Device = World->GetAudioDevice()) { Device->SetTransientPrimaryVolume(MasterVolume); }` with `URHAudioSubsystem::SetMasterVolume(World, MasterVolume);` (`#include "Audio/RHAudioSubsystem.h"`; drives `SC_Master` on `SMix_Master` and is remembered for later worlds; drop the transient primary volume so the two do not multiply). Buttons (shared UI owner) — `UI/RHButtons.cpp`: in `URHTextButton::NativeOnHovered` and `URHOfferCardButton::HandleFocus` (when `bInFocused`) `URHAudioSubsystem::PlayUI(this, ERHSfx::UI_Focus);`, in a `NativeOnClicked` override on both `URHAudioSubsystem::PlayUI(this, ERHSfx::UI_Confirm);`; in the menu screens' back handling (`NativeOnHandleBackAction` of the `UI/RHScreens` base) `URHAudioSubsystem::PlayUI(this, ERHSfx::UI_Back);`. Boss owner — on each phase change `URHAudioSubsystem::NotifyBossPhase(this, NewPhase);`. Optional (player owner): `ARHHero` hurt delegate as in (b). **Debug:** `RH.Audio.Stats [reset]` (voices now / peak / caps, device sources, played / aggregated / culled by reason: interval, cue cap, combat cap, voice cap, budget, crowd; crowd state, tick cost, per-cue counts), `RH.Audio.Volume [0..1]`; a `LogRH: Audio:` counter line every 10 s. **Measured** (RTX 4080 dev box, `-game` 1280×720, Development editor build): default smoke (`RH.Spawn 300, RH.Bullets 3000`, 18 s until the hero died): 117 voices played from 2 160 events, peak 6 / 24, crowd on once at 21 kills/s, Fire_Aimed 31 (0.6 s cooldown), HeroHurt 16, HeroDeath 1. Stress `RH.Stress 400 5000 lethal`, 35 s after a 10 s warm-up (live bullets avg 4 997, kill-rate spikes to ~3 500 /s): 279 204 events → 788 voices (186 377 culled: 156 348 interval, 30 029 under the crowd layer; 92 039 aggregated), **peak 7 / 24 voices (combat 6 / 16)**, peak 3 starts / frame, engine device sources 5 / 40; **audio tick 0.0099 ms average, 0.496 ms single-frame peak** (first plays / crowd component creation), well inside the ~0.3 ms budget on average. Tests `RogueHero.Audio.{VoiceCapHoldsAtPeakDensity, LowDensityKillsAreIndividual, CrowdLayerHysteresis, IntervalAggregates, CueTable}`.

(4) **CommonUI input findings (project-wide, worked around inside the hub only):** (a) 5.8 defaults `CommonButtonAcceptKeyHandling=Ignore` and the project has no default click action, so **Enter / Space / (A) never click a focused CommonButton** (pause, settings and level-up rows included); the hub runs the focused entry's action from the bubbled Accept key. Proper fix: `[/Script/CommonUI.CommonUISettings] CommonButtonAcceptKeyHandling=TriggerClick` (DefaultGame.ini); the hub fallback then never fires. (b) `CommonUI.EnableVirtualPointer` defaults on, so pad (A) becomes a simulated left click at a software pointer, not on the focused widget, and pointer hovers move focus; the hub binds its own `IA_Hub_Accept` (A, hub-only `IMC_Hub`, non-consuming, removed before travel) as a UI action, and portraits take focus on hover only for mouse input. Proper fix: `[ConsoleVariables] CommonUI.EnableVirtualPointer=0` (DefaultEngine.ini). (c) Pushing a menu over another menu loses `IMC_Menu`: the stack activates the new screen (adds `IMC_Menu`) and then deactivates the one below (removes it outright), so **Settings opened from Pause has no Back** (`IA_UI_Back` resolves to no keys; reproduced in `L_Arena_Test` with `RH.Debug.SettingsUI`). The hub re-applies `IMC_Menu` one frame after it pushes a screen; proper fix belongs in `URHActivatableScreen` (ref-count the context, or re-add it on activation after the previous screen's deactivation). **Verified** (`L_Hub -game`, Development editor binary, scripted real Slate key events via `RH.Hub keys`, focus logged after each key): keyboard ←/→ through portraits, Enter chooses (focus jumps to Begin), → Settings, Enter opens Settings, Esc returns with focus on the chosen Keeper, ↓ Begin, Enter → `L_Arena_Test?Patron=Tide`; pad D-pad / (A) / (B) the same flow → `Patron=Radiance`; with the one-line game-mode hook applied locally (not committed) `URHRunState` logs `patron God.Radiance`; zh-Hans renders live; 101/101 `RogueHero` tests; smoke PASS. Not verified: a physical pad and a foreground window with a real mouse. Still open for other owners: the game-mode hook above, `GameDefaultMap=/Game/RH/Maps/L_Hub.L_Hub` (§1 item 5) and adding `L_Hub` to the packaging map list.

- 2026-10-19 — **Week 4 Thu, fusion half: `URHFusionDef`, six fusion weapons, six Legendaries** (GDD §5.5, §5.6.3, TDD §9). (1) **Data.** `URHFusionDef` (`Build/RHFusionDef`, primary type `RHFusion`): `Tag` `Fusion.<Path>.<Weapon>` (new `Config/Tags/RHFusions.ini`, so `RH.ini` is untouched), `God`, `BaseWeapon`, `FusionWeapon` (a `URHWeaponDef`), FText name/desc from `ST_Fusions` (`.Desc` rows appended, en + zh-Hans), Rarity Epic, `bUnlockedByDefault` **false** (GDD §8.2: the Keeper branch node (5) unlocks it, so a new-player run gets the Legendary at L16); `MakePoolEntry()` fills `FRHOfferPool::Fusions` (Id, God, `BaseWeaponId`). Factory fallback (fallback (d) extended): `RHFusions::MakeFallbackDefs` → `DA_Fusion_<Path>_<Weapon>`, each with `URHWeaponDef::MakeFusion<Path><Weapon>` → `DA_Weapon_Fusion_<Path>_<Weapon>`. **Fiction is never a key:** identifiers, archetypes and factories use Path + weapon or shape words (`ChargedLance`, `HeavyShell`, `WaveCrescent`, `Hunter`, `CritLance`, `Dart`, `BladeDrone`, `ChainArc`, `GroundGlow`); the six fusion names exist only as FText. (2) **A fusion is its base weapon reforged:** the result def is a copy of the base (same **Tag**, pattern, target rule and level track, so RunState ids, upgrade cards and `RH.Give` keep working) plus `URHWeaponDef::Fusion` (`FRHFusionParams`: element status + chance, one `ERHFusionBehaviour`, natives). `URHWeaponInstance::Reforge(def)` swaps the def in place and **keeps level, modifiers and slot** (GDD §5.5); all 20 modifiers work on all 6 fusions (`RogueHero.Fusions.AllModifiers`). Fusion bullets set `bFusion` (protected from oldest-first recycling, TDD §5.2). A 7th derived spec slot per weapon (`FRHDerivedSpecIds::Fusion`, `FRHComposedWeapon::FusionHit`) carries the behaviour's hits. Divine Conduit (patron element) takes precedence over the fusion's own element when both are present. (3) **The six** (numbers placeholders, tune Week 6; every one has more damage per hit than its base at the same level): **Storm × Rail Lance** — 45 dmg, pierce 40, Shock 100 %, every hit throws a **chain arc** (4 200 uu/s, 60 % dmg, Shock) at the nearest enemy within 380 uu that the lance has not hit. **Forge × Flak Mortar** — 30 dmg, 2 shells, blast r 190, apex 520, Burn 100 %; each blast leaves a **burning crater** (fusion zone, r = 0.8 × blast, 4 s per GDD, ticks every 0.5 s for 20 % dmg, Burn). **Tide × Ring Emitter** — 12 dmg, orbs pierce everything and **widen in flight** (`GrowPerSec` 30: r 14 → ~62), Chill 100 % (3 stacks freeze, the enemy status system). **Shade × Seeker Swarm** — **3 persistent hunters** (`bPersistent`: never expire, never spend pierce, lifetime 3 s is only a hit-history reset; idle hunters loop back to the hero; the weapon tops up to Count × Fork/Mirror copies with `URHBulletSubsystem::CountBySpec`), 22 dmg, Mark 100 %; **hits on a Marked enemy use the collect spec (×4 dmg)**. *Deviation:* GDD says hunters "execute Marked"; the bullet side cannot read enemy HP, so the collect hit is ×4 damage, and the real below-threshold execute stays the enemy side's Shade Tier 3 (`MarkExecuteFraction`). Own level track without count steps (3 hunters is the identity). **Radiance × Pulse Rifle** — 16 dmg, pierce 6, crit chance +0.35, crit mult +1.0; *deviation:* "crits Blind" is a 35 % Blind chance on every hit (= the lance crit chance), because crits are rolled on the enemy side after the bullet's status roll. **Quicksilver × Orbital Drones** — 11 dmg darts at 2 400 uu/s with **2 native ricochets**; the **drone bodies are blades** (`SetOrbiters(..., ContactSpec, Look)`: every 0.15 s each drone hits what is within 26 uu for 60 % dmg, no bullets); orbit speed and fire rate × (1 + 0.5 × hero speed / 600, capped at ×2). No element (Quicksilver has none). **Modifier interpretations on fusions:** persistent hunters treat each lifetime renewal as the end of a flight (Scatter Shell / Spread Wave fire then) and bounce to a fresh target on hit with Ricochet (charges restored every renewal); everything else is as on the base patterns. (4) **Legendaries** (L16 fallback, GDD §5.5 "4th-tier blessing of the highest-favor god"): `RHFusions::MakeLegendaryDefs` → six one-rank `URHBlessingDef` (`DA_Legendary_<Path>`, Tag `Blessing.<Path>.Legendary`, Epic, unlocked by default so the fallback always exists): Storm Damage +25 % & Shock ×1.5 / +1 s; Forge Area +25 % & Burn ×1.75 / +2 s; Tide Damage +15 % & Chill ×1.5 / +2 s; Shade Damage +20 %, Regen +1 & Mark ×1.5 / +2 s; Radiance Crit +15 %, Crit dmg +50 % & Blind +0.5 s; Quicksilver Attack speed +25 %, Move +15 %. Name "Final Advance / 终极预支" for every Keeper (Narrative: "the highest-stake Keeper's final advance"; the card shows the honorific) and a rules line, both in `ST_Fusions` (`Blessing.<Path>.Legendary.{Name,Desc}`). `RHFusions::FindLegendary(Id)` is a world-free static lookup like `RHBlessings::Find`. (5) Registry: `URHWeaponRegistry::FindFusion` (`Fusion.Storm.RailLance`, `Storm.RailLance` or `Storm`), `FindFusionFor(God, BaseWeaponId)`, `GetFusions`, `FindLegendary`, `GetLegendaries`. Debug: `RH.Give.Fusion <Fusion.* | Path.Weapon | Path | Blessing.<Path>.Legendary>` adds the base weapon at L8 if missing, reforges it and calls `URHRunState::ApplyOfferCard` with the card the screen would have built. **Hooks for the integration owner (Build/RHOffer*, RunState, UI, effect manager), not applied here:** (a) `URHOfferFlow::BuildPool` (TODO line): `for (const URHFusionDef* F : Weapons->GetFusions()) if (IsInRunPool(F->GetId(), F->bUnlockedByDefault, Content)) P.Fusions.Add(F->MakePoolEntry());` and `for (const URHBlessingDef* L : Weapons->GetLegendaries()) P.Legendaries.Add(RHFusions::MakeLegendaryPoolEntry(*L));`. (b) `URHOfferFlow` apply switch, `case ERHOfferCardKind::Fusion:` → `if (const URHFusionDef* F = Registry->FindFusion(Card.Id)) { RHFusions::ApplyToHero(*Hero, *F); }`. (c) `URHRunState::ApplyOfferCard`, `case Legendary:` additionally `AddBlessingRank(Card.Id, Card.God, 1, Card.Tags);` so the effect manager applies its stat / status mods, and `RHBlessings::Find(Id)` falls back to `RHFusions::FindLegendary(Id)`. (d) `RHCardText::TableFor(Legendary)` → `ST_Fusions`. **Readability:** zones larger than 120 uu (Forge craters, Lingering Field on shells) now draw as a thin ring of streaks instead of a filled additive disc: overlapping filled discs washed the floor pale and hid gems and enemies (same lesson as the blast discs, Art §3); hunter and wave intensities were lowered so their teal / cyan hue survives bloom. Tests: new `RogueHero.Fusions.*` (Defs, Stronger, Reforge, Behaviours, AllModifiers = 120 fusion × modifier pairs, Cap: hunters survive a 6 000-cap storm) — 124/124 `RogueHero` pass headless; smoke with all six fusions + a Legendary SMOKE: PASS.

- 2026-10-19 — **Enemy-owned bullets live in `URHBulletSubsystem`** (TDD §5 "all bullets are data in one subsystem"); the boss's private `FRHBossBulletField` is deleted and the Flare Pylon's interim `FRHEnemyShotPool` is bypassed. (1) **Faction:** `ERHBulletFaction {Player, Enemy}`. Enemy bullets are a second dense SoA inside the subsystem (`EPos/EVel/ELife/ERadius/EDamage/ESource/ELook`), not a flag inside the player arrays, so the player loop (spatial hash vs enemies, behaviours, deferred spawns) is untouched and enemy bullets never hit enemies. **Hero collision is a circle test per bullet, no spatial hash:** with one hero a hash would cost more than the single test it saves (documented deviation from "spatial-hash vs the hero"). `Step()` steps enemy bullets first against the world's hero (first player pawn) on the same fixed 60 Hz step: a bullet touching a vulnerable hero is consumed and the largest single hit of the step goes through `ARHHero::ReceiveContactDamage`; an invulnerable hero (dash i-frames, post-hit, god mode) passes through. A pure overload `StepEnemyBullets(Dt, HeroPos, HeroRadius, bVulnerable)` serves tests. (2) **Cap split:** the 6 000 total is shared; enemy bullets are capped at `MaxEnemyBullets` = 1 500 (an enemy spawn beyond it is **dropped and counted**, never recycling a bullet the player is reading); an enemy spawn at the total cap **recycles the oldest non-fusion player bullet**; player spawns at the cap recycle player bullets only. So the player always keeps ≥ 4 500 and regrows to 6 000 when enemy fire stops. (3) **API:** `SpawnEnemyBullet(Pos, Vel, Radius, Damage, Life, Source, Look)`, `SpawnEnemyRing(Center, Count, Speed, StartAngle, Radius, Damage, Life, Source, Look)`, `NumEnemyBullets([Source])`, `ClearEnemyBullets(Source)`, `GetEnemyStats()` (spawned / dropped / hero hits / damage dealt per `ERHEnemyBulletSource {Turret, Boss, Other}`, player bullets recycled for enemies). (4) **Look** (Art §3): warm glow streak stretched along the velocity (M_Tracer additive, `Warm` orange-red ×6 for the Warden, `Magenta` magenta-red ×3.5 for the Flare Pylon) plus an opaque dark core on the camera side — the boss's existing look, now drawn by the subsystem's three enemy renderers. Player tracers are unchanged (cool). (5) **Repoints:** the subsystem implements `IRHHostileShotSink` and registers itself with `URHEnemySubsystem::SetHostileShotSink(this)` in its own `OnWorldBeginPlay`, so no game-mode hook is needed (bare test worlds without BeginPlay keep the enemies' interim pool, so the enemy tests are unchanged); every Flare Pylon shot becomes a `Turret` enemy bullet. The Warden (`Boss/`) spawns `Boss` enemy bullets, clears them on defeat (`ClearEnemyBullets`), reads `DamageByBullets` from the per-source counter (baseline at spawn), drops its own bullet renderers, and `RH.Boss.Status` reads the subsystem's counters; `GetBullets()` → `NumBullets()`; `RogueHero.Boss.Bullets` now tests the subsystem (dash pass-through, one hit consumed, expiry / arena edge, the 1 500 cap with drops). Side effect for the enemies session: in game, `RH.Enemies`' shot counters (`ShotsFired` still counts, `ShotHits` and `NumShots` stay 0) — the hits now live in `URHBulletSubsystem::GetEnemyStats().HeroHits[Turret]`. Tests: new `RogueHero.EnemyBullets.*` (HeroHit, DashPassThrough, Ring incl. "enemy bullets never hit enemies", CapSplit) — 165/165 `RogueHero` pass headless; smoke with 25 Flare Pylons + the Warden + a fusion SMOKE: PASS (off-screen).
- 2026-10-19 — **Niagara array-DI bullet renderer: not built; ISM stays the renderer (fallback (a) kept), no measurement possible.** Blocked on authoring, not on code: an array-DI renderer needs an `NS_Bullets` system whose emitter reads a `NiagaraDataInterfaceArrayFloat4` user parameter per particle (stock pieces exist: `SelectVector4_FromArray`, array-count dynamic inputs, a per-frame burst of N particles with a one-frame life). Headless authoring was probed and fails: the UE 5.8 Python API creates systems (`NiagaraSystemFactoryNew`) and sets properties but has **no call that adds modules or binds dynamic inputs on an emitter stack** (`NiagaraPythonEmitter` / `NiagaraPythonModule` are read / upgrade-script only), and no engine Niagara asset samples a position array that could be duplicated. The remaining routes need decisions outside the weapons scope: (a) author `NS_Bullets` once in the Niagara editor by hand (≈ 30 min; recipe: empty emitter, CPU sim, Emitter Update *Spawn Burst Instantaneous* count = `ArrayCount(User.Bullets)` every frame, Particle Spawn *Lifetime* = 1 frame, *Position* = `SelectVector4_FromArray(User.Bullets, Particles.SpawnIndex).xyz`, *Sprite Size* from `.w`, sprite renderer with an additive cool material; one emitter per archetype or a colour array), or (b) a small editor C++ module using `FNiagaraStackGraphUtilities` to build it reproducibly; either way (c) `"Niagara"` must be added to `RogueHero.Build.cs` (owned by the UI/loc session) for the runtime side. Swap-out plan once (a)/(b) + (c) land: `ARHNiagaraBulletRenderer` behind `RH.Bullets.Renderer ism|niagara` (default ism), pushing per-archetype `FVector4f` arrays with `UNiagaraDataInterfaceArrayFunctionLibrary::SetNiagaraArrayVector4` from `URHBulletSubsystem::Render`, then the 5 000 bullets + 400 enemies A/B at Epic; Niagara becomes the default only if not worse. Reference numbers for the ISM side today: 10 weapons at L8 + 400 enemies, frame 4.19 ms median / GPU 3.47 ms, bullet render sync p95 0.115 ms (TDD §17 weapons 5–10 entry) — the ISM path is not near any budget, so this is not urgent for v0.1.

(2) **`Tools\LoopCheck.ps1`** plays the Done-when scenario: deletes slot `RHMeta_LoopCheck`, launches `L_Hub` off-screen (`-RenderOffscreen`, no `-log`, killed by PID) per run, drives the hub through the real UI with real Slate key events (Contracts → buy what the trunk affords → back → Shrine → Begin), lets a driver play the run at `RH.TimeScale 5` with `RH.Offer.AutoPick random`, then a last launch reads `RH.E2E.Status`; asserts ≥ 2 Discoveries and ≥ 4 Contract nodes. - 2026-10-19 — **Playtest fix: one language press froze the game ~5 s** (user log 14:08, frame 775: en ⇄ zh-Hans switched 8 times in one frame, each a FontCache flush + full widget invalidation). The Settings row now calls `URHUserSettings::RequestLanguageStep`: one user action → at most one culture switch, applied on the **next frame** from the core ticker (off the input handler); further requests are ignored while one is queued, while a switch runs (the culture change rebuilds every widget and must not feed back into another step) and in the frame a switch was applied. `StepLanguage` / `SetLanguage` stay immediate for debug and tests. The stacked-menu Back rule (Settings over Pause / hub) is now `FRHMenuMappingTracker` (RHScreens), driven by tests in the stack's real activation order. Tests `RogueHero.UI.Shared.LanguageOnePressOneChange` (8 requests + a re-fire from `OnCultureChanged` → exactly 1 switch) and `RogueHero.UI.Shared.StackedMenuKeepsBack`. Verified off-screen: Pause → Settings → Enter ×4 in one frame → 1 switch → Right → 1 switch → Esc → Pause → Esc → game.

- 2026-10-19 — **Balance R2 + R4 + R5 in the weapon factories** (Docs/Balance/recommendations.md; scheduler: the GDD §3.5 live-bullet targets stay binding). (1) **R2 DPS, carried by denser streams:** the per-hit numbers R2 proposed (Rifle 15 / +5 / +8, Scatter 8 / +4 / +6, Drones 9 / +4 / +7) are kept as **DPS**, delivered by more, smaller bullets: Pulse Rifle 3 bolts / 0.25 s at 2.5 dmg (+2 L2, +2 L6, count +1 L3 / +2 L7; lifetime 1.2 s); Scatter Cannon 12 pellets / 0.6 s at 3 dmg over 50° (+2 / +2, count +4 / +4); Orbital Drones 0.4 s per drone at 4.5 dmg (+3 / +3). Model DPS L1 → L8 (single): Rifle 30.7 → 250 (R2 target 28 → 245), Scatter 21 → 131 (19.4 → 126), Drones 23 → 168 (23 → 160). (2) **R4:** Rail Lance `Base.Pierce` 20 → 12 (crowd DPS L8 2 396 → 1 483); the Storm fusion's pierce 40 → 24 (2× the base). (3) **R5 (emission):** Ring Emitter keeps the GDD §5.2 12 bullets at L1 but its count steps grow +4 → +6 (24 at L8) and its lifetime 1.6 → 2.0 s; Spiral Projector lifetime 1.5 → 2.5 s. Their per-hit damage is **not** lowered: for untargeted or spread weapons the swarm TTK is (hits − 1) × re-hit interval, so smaller hits doubled it in the model (Ring 1.5 → 2.7 s, Seeker 1.4 → 3.1 s at 20:00); Seeker Swarm is unchanged for that reason. Fusions that copy a base were rescaled to stay ~1.6× its new per-hit damage (Radiance 16 → 4, Quicksilver 11 → 7). (4) **Model** (`Tools/Balance/balance_model.py` now mirrors these numbers; `weapons.csv` gains `live_bullets_est`; `swarm_oneshot.csv` / `ttk_pressure.csv` regenerated): steered swarm TTK ≤ 0.25 s at every minute 0–20 (was 0.55 s at minute 0), clear ratio ≥ 1.37. Live bullets per weapon (no modifiers, steady state) L1 → L8: Rifle 3 → 10, Scatter 7 → 17, Drones 2 → 5, Seekers 4 → 11, Ring 12 → 38, Spiral 21 → 100 (were 0.5 → 2, 2 → 5, 1 → 2, 4 → 11, 10 → 25, 13 → 60). **The §3.5 targets (≥ 150 at 2:00, ~800 at 10:00, ~2 000 at 18:00) are still not reachable by base weapons:** an auto-aimed bolt dies on its first hit after ~500 uu (≈ 0.25 s), so live count ≈ emission × 0.25 s, and a 4-weapon build without modifiers sits near 60–200; reaching 150 at 2:00 needs ~600 bolts/s from the starting rifle. With Split / Fork / Scatter Shell / Burst the measured multiplier is ≈ 10× (TDD §17 modifiers line), which is how mid- and late-run counts can approach 800 / 2 000. This remains the doc-vs-numbers conflict R5 flagged: GDD §3.5's 2:00 target needs either a re-baseline or bullets that exist mostly to be counted. **Not measured:** the `RH.Bot` live-bullet counts at 2 / 10 / 18 min and the smoke run — the scheduler suspended every game launch during the user's playtest; tests are headless (182/182 pass). Tests: the modifier wrapper tests now use a test-local single-bolt copy of the rifle (1 bolt / 0.55 s), so they test Fork / Mirror / Burst / Chain, not the rifle's balance; the Lance pierce assertion reads the def.
- 2026-10-19 — **Balance R9: threats keep arriving at a full alive cap** (GDD §3.5 "threats are skill checks"; Docs/Balance R9: an unbuilt run sat at the cap from 1:00, dropped every later spawn and met almost no Coursers / Pylons / Breakers, so built runs died earlier than unbuilt ones). `FRHSpawnDirectorCore::Step`: the block cap now binds the **Swarm** only; a non-Swarm pick may exceed it by `URHSpawnTimeline::ThreatCapAllowance` (**0.15**, data; 0 restores the plain cap); past that it is requested with `bDisplaceSwarm` and `URHSpawnDirector` first calls `URHEnemySubsystem::DisplaceSwarm(HeroPos)`, which despawns the **farthest plain (non-elite) Scrapwing** — no kill event, XP, chest or death effect — then spawns the threat (`bThreatsDisplaceSwarm`, data). Displacement stops while the Swarm is at or below **half the cap** (`SwarmAliveHint`, set from `NumSwarmAlive()` each step), so a run that kills nothing never becomes a screen of only threats. Swarm picks over the cap are still dropped (not banked). **Ceiling:** `URHSpawnTimeline::AliveCeiling` = **400** (TDD §14) caps both rules, so cap 300 × 1.15 = 345 is the most regular spawns can reach today; the +15 % above 400 was not taken. **Measured headless** (`RogueHero.Spawn.ThreatCapWorld` perf probe, -nullrhi, all six roles): enemy sim step **0.157 ms at 345, 0.200 ms at 400** enemies per 60 Hz step. Counters on the core: `ThreatOverCap`, `ThreatDisplacing`, `ThreatDropped`, `SwarmDropped`; `RoleCounters.SwarmDisplaced`. Tests `RogueHero.Spawn.ThreatCap` (at a full Swarm cap Coursers, Pylons and Breakers still spawn; displacement past the allowance; the half-cap floor; allowance 0; the 400 ceiling) and `RogueHero.Spawn.ThreatCapWorld` (farthest plain Scrapwing only, never elites or other roles, nothing in the kill buffer); `RogueHero.Spawn.Caps` now expects cap ≤ alive ≤ cap × 1.15 once threats are in the mix. 185/185 headless. **Not yet verified in a bot run** (game launches are on hold for the playtest): re-run the unbuilt / first-pick baselines when allowed.

Sheet: `env_readability_sheet.png` in the shared notes. (3) **Seen, not mine:** the Warden's hull draws ~5× too large on `SM_Enemy_Boss` (`URHEnemySubsystem::Render` still scales by Radius·2/100 on a 1:1 art mesh; already noted by the VFX pass for the enemies owner). `RH.KillAll` with a boss up now ends the run as a win (correct since the boss hook landed); don't use it in screenshot scripts while the boss is alive.
- 2026-10-19 — **Late-game escalation 12:00-20:00** (human playtest `Docs/Playtests/2026-10-19_human.md`: "a little boring after 15 minutes"; GDD §3.5 / §7) **and R9 corrected.** (1) **Timeline** (`MakeDefault`, re-baked `DA_SpawnTimeline_Main`). **Extends GDD §7** (deviation, by the playtest): from 12:00 a Threat beat about every 60 s (was 150 s), each with its 6 s Breath and Harvest between them. 12:30 Courser stampede from **two** sides (14 + 10); **13:35 Breaker pack** (5 walking in from 1 100 uu: five slam rings to read); 15:00 elite wave (4, two affixes; GDD); **16:00 Pylon ring** (8 at 950 uu) + Scrapwing ring (40); 17:30 Pylon nest (6, GDD) + **Breaker pack** (6); **18:30 elite wave** (5, mixed 1-2 affixes) + stampede (16); **19:30 pre-Warden** Pylon ring (8 at 1 000) + Scrapwing ring (60), 25 s so its Breath lands before 20:00. Threat blocks from 12:30 run 4.0-5.0 /s with Rusher + Turret at 26-34 %; the Pylon share of Harvest climbs 6 -> 8 % from 16:30 (the warm-fire ramp into the Warden); Harvest rates from 15:30 7.7 -> 8.6 /s (+10 %, for the minute-18 kill target). Everything telegraphed (`RogueHero.Enemies.TelegraphLeads` covers the new events). (2) **R9 corrected after the bot runs:** (a) the over-cap rules now apply to **skill checks only** (`FRHEnemyStats::bSkillCheck`: Courser, Breaker, Pylon), not to every non-Swarm role — Bulwarks displacing Scrapwings had built a wall of 112 Tanks at 7:30 (an HP wall); Scrapwing / Bulwark / Tallyman are cap-bound **filler** and displacement removes the farthest plain filler enemy. (b) The allowance is a **reserve**: at most `ThreatCapAllowance` × cap skill checks (18 at cap 120) may be alive on it — without that, Coursers a weak build cannot kill accumulated to 38-77 at 6:00. (c) **`FRHEnemyStats::MaxAliveShare`** (share of the block cap from regular spawns; waves and events exempt): Pylon **0.05** (6 at cap 120, 15 at 300), Bulwark **0.35** — static Pylons out of the weapons' nearest-target reach piled up to 10-16 and their crossfire, not the swarm, killed the runs. (d) Pylon **ShotDamage 8 -> 5** (a caught burst ~15 % HP at minute 0). (3) **Bot verification** (`Tools/Balance/RunSims.ps1 -Bot`, patron Storm / Forge / Tide, fresh save slots, -nullrhi, x4). **Mortal bot runs cannot judge 12:00-20:00:** on this main every one died at 6:20-10:53 (AutoPick first: Pulse Rifle stuck at L1, 1 weapon; AutoPick random: 1-3 weapons) — after the fixes above the deaths moved from ~6:30 to 7:32-8:46 and one died with only 19 enemies alive (3 Pylons): `RH.Bot` kites from pressure and dashes only when surrounded, so it never leaves a Pylon burst or a Courser line (0-4 dashes/min vs the 6-10 a human is expected to make) — it fails every skill check by construction. **Invulnerable bot runs** (same setup, -God; damage taken is not recorded under god mode): kills/min at 10:00 **246 / 250 / 296**, 15:00 **306 / 332 / 364**, 18:00 **370 / 388 / 398**, then after the +10 % Harvest rates **425** (Tide; the paired Storm run stalled at 0 kills from L5 because Mod.OrbitLock on a lone L1 Pulse Rifle produced no damage — a Combat-side issue). Live enemies late 23-54 (rate-bound). **Not verified:** damage taken / dashes against the 30-50 %/min and 6-10/min bands — needs a bot that dodges telegraphs and bullets (RH.Bot owner) or the next human playtest; the scheduler's "bot sometimes dies 15:00-20:00" cannot be met by a bot that dies to the first Pylon nest at 7-9 min.
- 2026-10-19 — **Default patron on a direct arena launch.** `ARHGameMode::ResolveRunPatron`: the hub's `URHPatronSelection::GetRunPatron` (Shrine or `?Patron=`), else `URHPatronSelection::GetDefaultPatron` (the save's last patron if still unlocked, else the first open Keeper in GDD order), logged as "Run: no patron chosen (direct arena launch); using default patron …". Playtest finding: launching `L_Arena_Test` directly left the ultimate empty until the first blessing. The hub remains the real path. Test `RogueHero.Wiring.DefaultPatron`.
- 2026-10-19 — **Offer-system depth audit (GDD §5.6), from the playtest ("too repetitive, nothing new to decide").** Tool: `Tools/offer_audit.py <logs>` reads the `OfferFlow:` lines (offers, rerolls, picks) and reports per run the share of offers with a genuinely new option, the repeat-card share, re-offers of passed-over cards, picks by kind and the §5.6 rule checks. **Playtest log** (`RogueHero-backup-2026.10.18-14.46.46.log`, old XP curve, no patron): 39 offers; 67 % had a new option; 50 % of all cards were repeats (upgrades / rank-ups); **110 re-offers** of passed-over cards (2.8 per offer), e.g. `Blessing.Tide.DeepReserve 1->2` (weight 5.1) in the guaranteed slot at L20, 22, 25 and 26 while you passed it each time. Every early upgrade rolled Rare (+2 levels), so the Pulse Rifle went 1→7 by L4. Offers shrank to 1–3 cards at L30+ as the pool ran out, which the R1 XP curve (~L21 by 20:00) now mostly avoids; §5.6 has no filler card, so none was added. **§5.6 deviations found and fixed:** (1) *"< 2 weapons by L5 → force a weapon card"* failed after L8. The rule counted a naturally drawn NewWeapon card without protecting it, and the committed-god rule then replaced the lowest-weight free card, which was that weapon card (weight 1.0); the playtest's L9/L10 offered no weapon to a one-weapon run. `ApplyAntiFrustration` now marks the satisfying weapon card as forced. (2) The committed-god shortfall in the playtest came from the run having **no patron**, so only one god was chosen; fixed by the default-patron fallback (cd93d19). Checked and matching §5.6: 5 cards, card 0 from {patron/second-god blessings ∪ owned upgrades}, no duplicates, no 5th weapon, modifiers only for weapons with a free socket, Reroll ×2 / Banish ×1 / Lock ×1, the 4 / 8 / 18 new-player pool, the L8 three-god choice and the L16 fusion/Legendary screen. **Tuning (levers §5.6 allows; no new mechanic):** `URHOfferTuning::SkipWeightDecay 0.5` / `SkipWeightFloor 0.15` (duplicate suppression: a card shown and not taken N offers in a row weighs × 0.5^N, reset when taken; counts kept in `URHRunState::NoteOfferResolved` from picks, declines and rerolls, passed to the generator as `FRHOfferContext::SkipCounts`), and `NewContentWeightMult 1.5` (cards that add something the build lacks, `URHOfferGenerator::IsNewContent`: a new weapon, the first blessing of a Path, a modifier on no weapon yet). **Before/after** (`RogueHero.OfferFlow.DepthProbe`: 60 seeded runs × L2–L21 through the real offer flow, patron Storm, random picks, new-player pool):

  | | new-option offers | repeat cards | re-offers / offer | longest pass-over streak | §5.6 violations | build per run (paths / weapons / mods) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | before (levers off) | 91.4 % | 32.1 % | 1.20 | 9 | 0 | 2.5 / 2.4 / 5.2 |
  | after (0.5 / 0.15 / ×1.5) | 94.9 % | 29.8 % | 0.89 | 7 | 0 | 2.7 / 2.6 / 6.3 |
  | stronger (0.35 / ×2.0) | 95.6 % | 29.0 % | 0.79 | 8 | 0 | 2.7 / 2.6 / 5.9 |

  The remaining long streak is structural: when only one card qualifies for the guaranteed slot (§5.6.2), the slot repeats it, decayed or not. Stronger levers buy little, so the defaults stay. The playtest's "nothing new to decide" was mostly the missing patron (the guaranteed slot could only offer upgrades) and the passed-over card returning every level. `RogueHero.Offers.Weights.Formula` checks the §5.6 formula with the levers off, then each lever on its own.
- 2026-10-19 — **Card visual language** (playtest 2026-10-19 #2 "cards too pale, Blessing / Modifier / Weapon look the same"; Art §3, §6). New `UI/RHCardStyle` (pure mapping, tests `RogueHero.UI.CardStyle.*`), used by `URHOfferCardButton` on the level-up, L8 commitment and L16 fusion hands. **Kind** = frame colour + header + glyph + ST_UI label: Upgrade azure ▲, New weapon white-cyan +, Modifier mint ◆ (filled header, dark text); Advance (Blessing) and Commitment (GodChoice) in the Keeper's Path colour with a dark header, Path stripe and a monogram from the designation (ATMOS → AT); Reforging (Fusion) Path colour ◈; Legendary Path colour with the only filled Keeper header ★. **Rarity** is separate: ●○○ / ●●○ / ●●● gems in the rarity colour + frame weight 2 / 3 / 4 px; it never changes the kind colour. Bodies are opaque panel + 3 % kind tint (the earlier 0.45-alpha rarity frame on a translucent panel was the "pale" look); every card has a near-black rim so frames stay ≥ 3:1 against the brightest dimmed backdrop; focus = white rim + 4 % scale. Contrast rule (WCAG ratio on linear luminance, ≥ 3:1) is tested for every kind × Path: frame vs rim and body, label vs header, glyph vs badge, text and rarity gems vs body, badges. Fixed kind colours are cool (B ≥ R, G ≥ R); warm only through the Keepers' own palette (Forge, Radiance). Badges are pills: Held, Guaranteed, **Overdue** (new: `bForcedByRule`), Synergy. ST_UI: `Card.Kind.Blessing/Fusion` (labels moved off ST_World), `Card.Glyph.*`, `Card.Rarity.Pip/PipEmpty`, `Card.Marker.Forced` (en + zh-Hans). Glyphs are text rows until card renders exist (Art §6 swap-out point). Debug `RH.UI.CardSheet [1|2]`; review sheet `cards_sheet.png` (en + zh-Hans) in the session notes.
- 2026-10-19 — **Feel pass (Roadmap W6 Mon, GDD §3.5 anti-fatigue rules)**, from the playtest ("nothing memorable"). `Player/RHFeel`: `FRHFeelBudget` (plain struct, unit-tested) inside `URHFeelSubsystem`, ticked by `ARHGameMode` on **real** frame time so no moment can stretch its own budget. **Budgets:** hit-stop ≤ 60 ms, one per 0.25 s; camera shake at most one per 0.5 s with amplitude capped at 18 uu (§3.5); slow-mo never below 35 % speed, ≤ 0.8 s, one per 4 s; FOV punch-in ≤ 5°, one per 0.5 s. `ARHGameMode::GetSimTimeScale()` = debug `TimeScale` × the budget's scale (0 during hit-stop, the eased slow-mo); the fixed-step sim and `ARHHero::Tick` both use it, and `ARHCamera` adds the shake offset and FOV delta. **Triggers:** hit-stop 30 ms + shake 8 uu on elite/boss kills (`URHEnemySubsystem::OnEnemyKilled`, never swarm) and on crits of live elites; hero hurt: shake 6 uu; boss defeated: 60 ms hit-stop, 18 uu shake, punch-in. **Moments (cool colours, no new white flash):** Keeper tier reached (`OnTierLine`): 0.45 s slow-mo at 50 %, 3° punch-in, rig flare in the Keeper's hue for 1 s; power jump after a pick that changes the screen (new weapon, modifier, fusion, Legendary; `URHOfferFlow::OnCardApplied`): 0.5 s at 60 %, 3.5° punch-in, rig flare; ultimate cast (`URHUltimateComponent::OnFired`): 0.5 s at 40 %, 4° punch-in, 12 uu shake, rig flare (the ultimate's own full-screen flash keeps its once-per-60 s gate). `ARHHero::FlareRig` lifts the rig light ×6 (~18 cd) in the given Path's colour. Damage numbers already aggregate above 20 kills/s (`FRHDamageNumberModel`, unchanged). Verified: `RogueHero.Feel.*` (HitStop, ShakeBudget, SlowMo; a 100-kill burst gets ≤ 2 shakes); off-screen smoke (tier-up, 6 elites, ultimate, two picks): `RH.Feel` → hit-stops 3/6, shakes 2/7, slow-mo 2/2, punches 2/2 granted/requested; HighResShot during the ultimate shows the cool bolt with every enemy legible. `RH.Feel` prints the counters.
- 2026-10-19 — **Live-bullet density measured with RH.Bot; the GDD §3.5 bullet targets cannot be met at the same DPS without breaking the §3.5 TTK — flagged, not forced** (CLAUDE.md "stop and say so"). New measurement knob `RH.Bullets.Density` (CVar, default 1 = shipping numbers; `RHSpecComposition`): stream weapons fire Density × the bullets at 1 / Density the damage (Aimed / Cone / Radial / Homing multiply the volley count, Orbiter / Spiral the fire rate down to one shot per sim step; shells, mines, blades, lances untouched), so DPS is identical. **Bot runs** (`RH.Bot` + `RH.Offer.AutoPick random` + god mode, Patron Storm, seed 701, `-nullrhi`, 4× time scale; this seed's random picks give a scattered build: Pulse Rifle with Pierce / Homing / Boomerang, Scatter Cannon from L13), live bullets avg (peak) / swarm TTK median / kills per min at minutes 2 · 10 · 18: **Density 1** (5d219a1 numbers) 13.6 (17) / 0.93 s / 140 · 19.5 (36) / 1.27 s / 214 · 30.5 (65) / 4.5 s / 120; **Density 4** 55 (71) / 1.20 s / 138 · 77 (144) / 2.23 s / 179 · 77 (131) / — / 109; **Density 8** 105 (144) / 1.42 s / 131 · 161 (288) / 2.32 s / 163 · 165 (294) / — / 77. Targets ≥ 150 / 800 / 2 000. So live bullets scale linearly with density, but at Density 8 they are still 1.4× / 5× / 12× short, while swarm TTK roughly doubles and kills per minute fall 6 % / 24 % / 36 % — smaller bullets need more hits per kill and the extra bolts mostly overkill the nearest target. Hitting the targets would take Density ≈ 10–60 (per-bolt damage < 1, 15–50 hits per Scrapwing), which inverts §3.5's "swarm TTK ≤ 0.3 s" and adds hit-event load for a metric rather than a feeling. **Decision:** shipping density stays 1; the §3.5 live-bullet row needs a design decision — re-baseline it (balance R5 option a), or count it differently (e.g. include enemy fire and impact particles, or measure a steered danmaku build) — and the knob is there to A/B whatever is chosen. **GPU check for the VFX pass** (Epic, 1080p back buffer, 5 000 bullets + 400 enemies): not cleanly measurable today — three other sessions' off-screen games held the GPU at 91–94 %, and in-run A/B windows swung 10 → 21 ms with that load (Storm / Forge read as negative cost, Tide +6 ms, Shade / Radiance / Quicksilver ≈ 0, all within the noise). The last uncontended measurement (2026-10-19 14:19, 1280×720, four fusions, no ults) stands: GPU 3.08 ms median / 3.60 p95; rerun the ult A/B when the GPU is otherwise idle.
- 2026-10-19 — **zh-Hans overrides pipeline + first full Chinese quality pass** (TDD §12; playtest 2026-10-19: "the Chinese translation is not good"). `Game.po` is union-merged across parallel branches, so edited msgstr lines can duplicate on merge. Reviewed Chinese now lives in `Content/Localization/zh_overrides.csv` (`Key,Table,zh,en`); `python -I Tools/apply_zh.py` writes it into `Game.po` by msgctxt (`<Table>,<Key>`), collapses duplicated msgstr lines, and is idempotent (`--check` exits 1 when `Game.po` differs). Then `Tools\Localize.bat` compiles the `.locres`. **For every key it lists, the overrides CSV is the source of truth**: re-run apply_zh after every merge that touches `Game.po`. Don't hand-edit those msgstr lines. An override is applied only while its `en` column still equals the CSV source. When the English changes, the override is reported as stale and skipped so it gets re-translated. An override that drops or adds a `{arg}`, or makes a Keeper tier line longer than 20 characters, is refused. New strings still get their first zh in `Game.po` as before. Terms are fixed in `Docs/Localization/zh_glossary.md`. Narrative and GDD names are unchanged. Two GDD-glossary terms are now actually used: Modifier = 模块 (was 改装) and Rank 级 / Tier 阶 / Level Lv. are kept apart. Chill = 冰缓 and Burn = 灼烧 are used everywhere. 153 of 448 strings changed. **Deviation:** 11 zh Keeper tier lines in Narrative §4.2 were re-worded for natural Chinese, with meaning and the ≤ 20-character cap kept. The shipped zh is the overrides CSV, and the §4.2 table stays the draft. Verified: 192/192 tests pass, `TextAudit.py` passes, off-screen `-culture=zh-Hans` captures of the hub, level-up, HUD, Settings, run summary, Contracts and Codex show no tofu or truncation. Screenshots are UI-inclusive `FScreenshotRequest` captures (`RH.Debug.HUDShot`, `… shot`), because `HighResShot` renders no UMG and writes nothing under `-RenderOffscreen`.

No `Saved/Crashes` folder was created by any run. - 2026-10-19 — **Scheduler balance decision (R10–R12, Docs/Balance/recommendations.md).** Swarm TTK ≤ 0.3 s (GDD §3.5) outranks the live-bullet targets when they conflict. Per-bullet damage must one-shot a Scrapwing at the current minute, so starter weapons are never thinned to raise bullet counts (R5's Rifle 2.5 dmg reverted by R10: 14 dmg, 2 shots, 0.5 s). Live-bullet density must come from pattern weapons and modifiers. Late-game pressure is held (R11; measured 4–7× above the damage-taken band on 3ca1450), and the XP curve stays as shipped (R12) until a post-R10 RH.Bot v2 baseline is in.
- 2026-10-19 — **Warden tuning after the playtest ("the boss is a little easy, I didn't die", "never used dash")** (GDD §6 boss, §3.5 "threats are skill checks, never HP walls"). (1) **Phases 2–3 demand dashes:** *crossing rings* — every ring is followed after `CrossRingDelay` 0.55 s by a second ring with its own red lane telegraph 80–120° away (`CrossLaneOffsetMin/MaxDeg`), so the first lane closes behind you: walk round in time or dash through; rings denser (`RingSlots` 24 → 32, enraged 32 → 40, lane still 3 slots). *Dash charges* — every `DashChargeInterval` 7 s (enraged 5 s) a fast charge (`DashChargeWindUp` 0.45 s, 2 800 uu/s, 2 200 uu) with the usual red line; walking out of the 300 uu swath takes 0.5 s at 600 uu/s, one 450 uu dash clears it (asserted in the new `RogueHero.Boss.DashCharges`). Phase 3 keeps the spiral turning through rings and charges (`SpiralRate` 8 → 10). Phase 1 is unchanged (the 1.1 s charge stays walkable: it teaches the line). Every attack still has its red telegraph; the ring test now checks crossing pairs (primary lane within 40° of the hero, crossing lane 80–120° away, intervals 0.55 / 3.45 s). (2) **HP vs the build, not a wall:** the hull's SoA HP is a reserve spawned at `PowerHPMaxMult` 4 × (def HP × director HP multiplier); the fight runs on an effective max HP that starts at def × director and is re-scaled once after `CalibrateSeconds` 8 s to (damage the Warden took / 8 s) × `TargetFightSeconds` 80, clamped to `PowerHPMinMult` 0.6 … 4 × def × director, and the hull is killed when that runs out (bar, phases and `RH.Boss.HP` read the effective fraction; bosses are exempt from the Mark execute, so the reserve is safe). A first attempt sized HP from the run's crowd damage rate (1 514 dps incl. AoE/pierce) and produced 136 k HP that a bot never got through phase 1 in 7 min — crowd damage is the wrong proxy. (3) **Telemetry:** the defeat log line gives fight seconds, hero damage taken (all sources, after armour), dashes, damage by attack and charge / dash-charge / ring counts; a `Boss: hero died …` line is written from the subsystem tick when the run ends on the hero's death. **Measured** (`RH.Bot` + `RH.Offer.AutoPick random`, `-nullrhi`, 2× time scale, invulnerable during a 40 s warm-up at 18:30 then mortal from the boss call at 19:57; *strong* = Rifle 8 / Lance 8 / Scatter 8 / Drones 7 with Split, Fork ×2, Pierce, Overcharge 3, Storm favor 9; *medium* = Rifle 6 / Scatter 4 / Drones 3 + Pierce; both with Deep Reserve 3, Wellspring 3, Tempered Plating 3 = 145 HP, 1.2 regen, 6 armour): strong ×5 — calibrated to 111 k–172 k HP (1 391–2 153 dps on the Warden), all reached phase 3; 1 win in 80.7 s (193 damage taken, 6 dashes), 4 bot deaths in phase 3 at 56–70 s with the boss at 1–14 % (203–225 damage taken, 4–6 dashes; damage by source per fight: charges 88–132, bullets 132–240, sweep 0–36, hazard 0–104). Medium ×3 at the 1× floor died in phases 1–2 (57–70 s, boss at 48–79 %); with the 0.6 floor (32.6 k HP, 16 dps measured in the window) the medium build reached phase 3 at 91 s before the bot died (252 taken). The bot reads no telegraphs (it only kites and dashes when crowded), so it eats every charge (22 each) and most ring bullets (12 each): the Warden's damage over a 60–90 s fight ≈ its 145 HP + regen, i.e. the bot dies near the end, while a player who dodges half of it survives with margin. No human verification yet.

- 2026-10-19 — **RH.Bot v2 (Debug/RHBot) + steered card policy.** Command interface stays as before: `RH.Bot [0|1]` / `-RHBot`, plus new `RH.Bot.Policy first|random|steered|worst` (the same modes as `RH.Offer.AutoPick`, which now also accepts `steered`). **Decision** = pure `URHBot::Decide(FRHBotWorld)` (unit-tested; the world snapshot is gathered each frame): for 16 directions the bot reads a threat map at a 0.35 s look-ahead:
  - **predicted contact:** enemies close in at their own speed;
  - **telegraph zones**, weighted by time-to-impact, all read from public state: Courser wind-up and charge lanes (`URHEnemySubsystem::GetChargeState`, `ChargeSpeed × ChargeDuration`), Breaker slam rings (`SlamRadius`), the Warden's charge lane (`GetAttackDir` / `GetChargeLength`) and sweep sector (`SweepRadius` / `SweepHalfArcDeg`);
  - **enemy bullets:** closest approach along both paths, from `URHBulletSubsystem::GetEnemyPos/Vel` (Pylon shots and boss rings both live there);
  - walls, the boss's shrinking arena, forward pressure, and a tangential preference at mid range, so it kites along the swarm edge.

  It harvests a gem only when the gem and its path are clear of zones and the crowd. It dashes by the real rules (a charge ready, i-frames) when a zone is about to land on it (< 0.35 s), a bullet will hit within 0.25 s, or it is cornered, and aims at the safest landing point (crossing a lane is fine). It fires the ultimate when charged and ≥ 18 enemies are within 600 uu, or the Warden is within 1 200 uu. **Not readable without an Enemies/ change:** Volatile blast centres and the enemy subsystem's interim shot pool are private; the bot dodges every bullet in `URHBulletSubsystem`, which today holds all role shots. **Steered policy** (`URHOfferFlow::PickSteered`, GDD §3.5 "well-steered build": the patron's Path and its weapons): upgrades score 110 − 6 × level (an L1–L2 weapon beats a new blessing), new patron blessing 100, a 2nd / 3rd weapon 90–96 (the patron fusion's base weapon +6), modifiers 86–92, patron rank-ups 78, second-god blessings 60–72, the L8 god by favor, fusion > Legendary. An earlier blessing-first scoring left the Pulse Rifle at L3 all run (60 kills/min, died at 15:42). **Runs** (headless `-nullrhi`, time scale 6, default patron Storm, main 3ca1450 + this branch):

  | run | outcome | level | L8 | L16 | Warden | dashes/min | dmg taken/min | kills/min |

  | steered 1 | died 21:22 (in the boss fight) | L24 | 4:48 | 10:14 | yes | 1.9 | 4.5 % | 274 |
  | steered 2 | died 11:33 | L11 | 9:22 | – | no | 2.2 | 4.5 % | 105 |
  | steered 3 | **WIN 24:26** | L23 | 6:45 | 14:50 | yes | 3.3 | 2.5 % | 114 |
  | steered 4 | died 21:29 (in the boss fight) | L9 | 16:45 | – | yes | 2.7 | 3.4 % | 29 |
  | steered 5 | died 11:27 | L14 | 7:24 | – | no | 3.0 | 8.7 % | 143 |
  | no-pick 1 | died 15:57 | L9 | 9:10 | – | no | 2.0 | 5.9 % | 46 |
  | no-pick 2 | died 55:21 (outlasted the timeline; cannot kill the Warden) | L15 | 9:53 | – | yes | 4.1 | 1.7 % | 19 |
  | no-pick 3 | died 15:27 | L9 | 9:44 | – | no | 3.1 | 5.8 % | 42 |

The steered deaths at about 11:30 come in the 10:00 elite wave, after a slow start (L8 at 7–9 min). **GDD §3.5 contract:** an unbuilt run dies at 15:27–15:57, so the "overrun by ~12:00" target misses by ~3.5 min against a bot that dodges every telegraph. That finding is for the timeline / spawn owner: Harvest blocks between waves deal ~0 damage to a 600 uu/s kiter; elite waves, Pylon fire and Breaker slams do.   **Reading of "never dashed":** nothing in the ruleset is broken. Harvest phases deal ~0 damage to a kiting hero, so dashing is only needed in elite waves and against telegraphs (bot v2: 0 % damage taken in Harvest minutes), and the dash state lived in a corner of the screen. The pips under the hero and the afterimages make it readable; *forcing* dashes is the timeline's job (§3.5 "dashes / min forced by threats", routed to the spawn / balance owners with the bot v2 numbers). New debug command `RH.Dash [x y]` (one press through the same rules; E2E and screenshots). Test `RogueHero.Wiring.DashBuffer` (two charges: a press 0.13 s before the first dash ends chains; an impossible press expires after 0.15 s). In a dense crowd the under-hero pips can be covered by enemies; worth checking in a playtest.

The env pass's "draws ~5× too large" note is stale: since `aabe22c` the enemy renderer draws art meshes at their modelled size, and `SM_Enemy_Boss` is the intended ~11-12 m kitbash. The real mismatch was the hit circle (r 260 = 5.2 m) against a 2.7× Breaker frame + Bulwark plate whose face sits at ~340 uu, so the plate and legs overhung the collision and charges / shots landed inside the drawn hull. **Change (Boss/ only, no Enemies/ edit):** `Stats.Radius` 260 → **340**; status light re-fitted to the model socket (`StatusLightHeight` 404 / 340, `StatusLightSize` 0.168 ≈ the same 114 uu, `BodyScale.X` 117 / (0.45 × 340) so the sensor sits on the optic; BodyScale does not scale art meshes); `SweepRadius` 700 → 780 (same 440 uu band outside the hull), `SummonDistance` 420 → 500, `ShrinkMinRadius` 1 300 → 1 400 (keeps > 4 hull radii). Dash still clears a charge swath (340 + 40 < 450) and walking out still cannot beat the 0.45 s wind-up; the boss tests read the def's radius (`RogueHero.Boss.DashCharges` asserts it). `RH.Boss.Spawn` takes `dist=` / `dir=` for on-screen captures. **Readability (Art §3, 3:1):** frozen pair (`RH.TimeScale 0`, shot, `RH.Boss.Spawn dist=1000 dir=90`, shot; every changed pixel against the exact floor behind it, off-screen 1080p, same luminance-ratio metric as the env pass): **94.7 % of hull pixels ≥ 3:1, median 9.7:1** (lit side 95.7 % / median 8.1; the darker set includes the hull's cast shadow). With a WCAG 0.05 flare term the off-screen frame reads lower (24 %), as the env pass noted for off-screen exposure (~2.4× darker than windowed); windowed re-check left to the next windowed capture. **Difficulty note:** the wider swath makes charges land more often on the bot (strong build died in P3 at 41.8 s, 132 of 194 damage from charges); the fight stays telegraphed (red corridor drawn at the hull width) — re-tune after a human playtest, not on bot deaths alone.

Median / p95 ms, the 3 rounds' medians within 0.05 ms of each other: **Epic (`RH_Epic`): GPU 4.20 / 4.62, frame 4.58 / 5.23, GT 2.15 / 2.91.** **High (`RH_3060`): GPU 2.81 / 3.05, frame 3.07 / 3.72, GT 2.04 / 2.91.** **Medium (`RH_3060_Medium`): GPU 2.27 / 2.44, frame 2.46 / 3.16, GT 1.99 / 2.90.** This is the cleanest Epic number to date (the earlier 4.46 ms ran with light background load) and stays far inside GPU ≤ 12 ms / GT ≤ 8 ms. No RTX 3060 is available, so the 3060 tiers are timed on the 4080. A 3060 has roughly 2.5× less raster throughput, which scales High to ≈ 7 ms and Medium to ≈ 5.7 ms GPU: an estimate, not a measurement, and inside the 16.6 ms frame. Nothing is over budget. Then the parked escalation counts on top (**this commit**): two-sided stampede 14 + 10, Breaker packs 5 / 6, Pylon rings 8 (16:00, 19:30) + nest 8 (17:30), Scrapwing rings 40 / 60, 18:30 wave 5 + stampede 16, R13 shares on every late Threat row (4.0-5.0 /s), late Harvest +10 % with the Pylon share 6 -> 8 %. 5 + 5 runs to minute 24: steered **0 / 1.5 / 0 %/min**, dashes 3.7 / 2.6 / 3.3, **5/5 wins** (20:53-21:57); random 1.4 / 1.4 / 3.2 %/min, 3/5 wins (2 still in the Warden fight at 24:00); kills/min at 18 **441-470** steered (target >= 400 met). **Finding:** counts do not move damage taken — a steered build kills each late skill check *inside its own telegraph* (a Pylon, ~112 HP at 15:00, dies before its 0.75 s aim completes; Breakers inside the 1 s wind-up; Coursers mid-wind-up), and Bot v2 dodges the Pylon shots that do get off; the only steered damage after 13:00 is the Warden's. Moving into the 20-50 %/min band needs a design call (scheduler): skill checks that survive long enough to act (e.g. late skill checks as Shielded / Armored elites, or a per-role HP floor relative to the telegraph), harder-to-dodge patterns (Pylon spread / double bursts), or a different band for a perfect-dodging bot. Numbers stay as committed until then.
- 2026-10-19 — **Scheduler decision: late-game teeth via skill checks, not HP.** R13 met kills/min, but steered builds kill late Pylons/Breakers/Coursers inside their own telegraphs, so they take ~0 % damage per minute after 12:30. GDD §3.5 forbids HP walls, so no HP floor tied to telegraph time. Late threats get their teeth from: Pylons planting just outside the player's current weapon reach, spread/double bursts, overlapping telegraph events, and Shielded escorts (flank checks). Targets for the bot (which out-dodges humans): steered damage taken at 15/18 ≥ 10–20 %/min, dashes ≥ 4/min, steered win rate ~60–85 %.

- 2026-10-19 — **W6 Fri bug bash, first pass (paused by the scheduler before the run-fuzz re-run).** Tools: `RH.E2E.Fuzz <ui|run> <seed> <steps>` (Tests/E2E/RHE2EFuzz.cpp: random keyboard + pad presses through Slate and the viewport, rare macro jumps to every screen and unsaved en ⇄ zh-Hans switches; detectors no-focus, invisible-focus, stuck-pause, stuck-input-mode, (B)-only back-path probe, Esc probe for close / Pause re-open) and `Tools\Fuzz.ps1` (off-screen, one game at a time, PID kill; scans for crashes, ensures, error lines). Edge tests `RogueHero.E2E.Edge.*` (corrupt / old / newer save slots) and `RogueHero.E2E.PIE.QuitDuring*`. **Results:** UI fuzz 6 × 3000 steps (seeds 1–3, en + zh-Hans) visited all screens (hub, Shrine, Settings, Contracts, Codex, pause, level-up incl. commitment, run summary, hub ⇄ arena travel): no crash, ensure, error, focus, pause or input-mode issue; one invisible-focus hit on the Codex screen on the step it was opened (likely pre-layout, unconfirmed). Run fuzz (bot + random picks + pause / dash / ultimate spam + run end / reload), 2 × 3000 steps on the earlier detector set: no crash, ensure or error. **Bugs:** (1) **CRASH, meta** — a damaged save slot crashes the game at boot: `URHMetaSubsystem::Load` → `UGameplayStatics::LoadGameFromSlot` trips the engine's fatal FName-length assert (random bytes) or the CustomVersion assert (`CustomVersion.cpp:314`, valid header without a body); repro: put 4 KB of random bytes or the first 24 bytes of a real save in `Saved/SaveGames/<slot>.sav`, launch. Fix: validate the GVAS magic and size (and/or load via a guarded memory reader) before `LoadGameFromSlot`, keep the bad file as `<slot>.corrupt`, start fresh. Empty and truncated files are handled. Tests `CorruptSlot.RandomBytes` / `.HeaderOnly` are skipped with a warning until `RHE2EEdge::bCorruptSaveGuardLanded`. (2) **CRASH, integration** — quitting while a run is live: `ARHGameMode::Tick` reads `GetHero()->IsDead()` without a null check (`Core/RHGameMode.cpp`, after `bSimReady`), and the hero pawn is gone before the game mode's last tick; repro: PIE `L_Arena_Test`, wait 1 s (optionally `RH.Level 2` with `RH.Offer.AutoPick 0`), `Exit` → access violation. Quitting after the run has ended is safe (`QuitDuringRunEnd` passes; the slot written at run end loads). Test `QuitDuringLevelUp` is skipped until `RHE2EEdgePIE::bQuitMidRunGuardLanded`. (3) Engine noise, not ours: under `-culture=zh-Hans` the engine's startup `UnifiedErrorTest` self-check logs `LogAutomationTest: Error: Condition failed` ×13 on frame 0 (English string compares); Fuzz.ps1 filters it. Old saves migrate (v1 → v2, first win back-filled, stats re-keyed, written back) and a newer-version save is never overwritten.
- 2026-10-19 — **Fix: quitting mid-run crashed `ARHGameMode::Tick` (bug bash).** After `bSimReady`, Tick read `GetHero()->IsDead()` unguarded; on PIE end / `Exit` the pawn is destroyed before the game mode's last tick. Tick now returns early when the hero is gone (a quit is not a death: no `EndRun`, no sim step) or when the bullet / enemy / gem subsystems are gone (world teardown); `SimStep` returns without the hero or its subsystems; spawn director and impact FX lookups are null-checked. Audit of the rest of the game mode, `Player/*`, `UI/RHHUD`, `URHOfferFlow`, `URHRunState` users and `RH.Bot`: every other hero / RunState / OfferFlow / subsystem use was already guarded. `RHE2EEdgeTests` `bQuitMidRunGuardLanded = true`: `RogueHero.E2E.PIE.QuitDuringLevelUp` runs and passes (220/220).
- 2026-10-19 — **Damaged save slot no longer crashes the game** (bug bash repro: 4 KB of random bytes, or the first 24 bytes of a real save, in `Saved/SaveGames/<slot>.sav` → fatal engine assert at boot: FName max length / `FCustomVersionContainer::Serialize` `check(false)` on an unknown format byte). New `Meta/RHSaveFile`: `URHMetaSubsystem::Load` reads the slot as bytes (`LoadDataFromSlot`) and `RHSaveFile::Validate` parses the engine's `FSaveGameHeader` with bounds checks before anything reaches `LoadGameFromMemory` — `GVAS` tag, save-file version 1..3, package versions within [oldest loadable, this engine], engine version + branch FString (≤ 1024 chars), custom-version format = Optimized (the only one the engine writes) + container, class name ending in `RHSaveGame` (case-sensitive), ≥ 9 body bytes. Outcomes: **Damaged** → the file is renamed `<slot>.corrupt` (`<slot>.<n>.corrupt` if taken), warning, fresh save, the next save writes normally; **Newer** (file version / package version / custom-version format from a newer engine) → fresh save in memory, the slot is never written (`IsSlotReadOnly`; the existing `URHSaveGame::SaveVersion` newer-build rule is unchanged); body unreadable by the engine → Damaged. **Checksum going forward:** `Save` = `SaveGameToMemory` + an 8-byte trailer `'RHCK'` + CRC32 (`SaveDataToSlot`); Validate verifies it when present (any flipped byte → Damaged) and hands the engine the payload without it; the engine ignores bytes after the object, so older builds still read new saves, and saves without a trailer are accepted on the header check. Tests `RogueHero.Meta.SaveFile.*` (round trip, every header cut, format / version / class mutations, 3 000-mutation header fuzz, quarantine naming); hub E2E `bCorruptSaveGuardLanded = true` — `RogueHero.E2E.Edge.CorruptSlot.{RandomBytes,Truncated,Empty,HeaderOnly}` all run and also check the `.corrupt` copy. Verified in game off-screen with both bug-bash payloads (boots, warns, keeps `.corrupt`, fresh progress, next save loads back).
- 2026-10-19 — **Blessings imbue bullets** (GDD §5.4 amendment, human playtest 2: "weapons with modifiers feel isolated from the Keepers' blessings"). **Data:** `URHBlessingImbueDef` (`Build/RHBlessingImbue.h`, primary type `RHImbue`, `DA_Imbue_<Path>` under `/Game/RH/Data/Blessings/Imbue/`; until authored, fallback **`DA_Imbue_<Path>_CodeFallback`** via `RHImbue::MakeFallbackDefs`, rooted for the process). One per Path, keyed by `God.<Path>`: accent colour / intensity / `ERHImbueTrail`, `ERHImbueBehaviour` (ChainArc, ApplyStatus, CritPulse, Ricochet: data flags, no fiction keys), `FRHFavorScaled` Chance / Magnitude / ProcDamage / StatValue / KillChance (`Base + PerFavor × Favor`, capped), status, kill proc, card `RulesLine`. **Behaviour:** `URHEffect_Imbue` (`Build/Effects/RHImbueEffect.*`), one per imbued Keeper, Source `Imbue.<Path>`, created / removed by `URHEffectManager::RebuildImbues` on every blessing change (held blessings tracked per god in the manager, so tests without a stats component work); Favor read each step (`GetImbueFavor`: run-state Favor, else summed ranks). Procs are batched per step (MaxProcsPerStep), use the run's Combat RNG, and go through the existing services (DealDamage / ApplyStatus scaled by status blessings / DamageInRadius / GrantXP / SetEffectStatMods). The tiers stay on top unchanged. `bImbueEnabled = false` isolates the blessing / tier / ultimate tests; `RH.Imbue 0` is the runtime A/B switch. **Look:** `URHBulletSubsystem::SetImbueLook(primary, secondary)` from `RHImbue::Blend` (higher Favor = trail, next = tip accent; ties Patron, then Path order); six accent renderers (`RH_Bullets_Imbue0..5`, M_Tracer, cool hues lead, Forge 5 / Radiance 5 intensity as accents only), drawn on straight player tracers, thinner than the core; on every 2nd / 3rd bullet above 1 500 / 3 000 live (instance budget). Proc flashes (arc, ember burst, light pulse, soul wisp) are render-only `ERHImbueFX` in the bullet subsystem (≤ 256), not ImpactFX (that file is the enemies session's now). **Cap:** Quicksilver's ricochet uses `SpawnExtra`, which refuses at the player share of the cap and never recycles (test `RogueHero.Imbue.Cap`). **Cards:** `URHBlessingDef::GetDescription` appends `Imbue.<Path>.Rules` via `Imbue.CardJoin` (ST_Blessings, en + zh-Hans drafts in zh_overrides.csv for the loc session to review; "Favor" = 眷顾 is a new term); Legendaries set `bShowImbueLine = false`. **Balance (Favor 6, RH.Bot god runs at 9:30 with Pulse Rifle L6 + Scatter L5, offers declined, damage the imbue added ÷ the rest, `RH.Imbue.Stats`):** Forge +8.3 %, Quicksilver +7.1 %, Radiance +6.4 % (+ ~3 % from its +6.6 % crit chance, not attributed), Storm +5.1 % (+ Shock from Favor 4, not attributed), Shade ~+6 % and Tide ~+4 % after their proc damage was doubled (measured +3.2 / +2.5 % at half; linear). All inside the +10–20 % ceiling; swarm TTK in those runs 0.03–0.35 s (same band as `RH.Imbue 0`); a live on/off A/B was too target-limited to resolve (±25 % noise). Bullet cap never reached (0 ricochets refused). Sheet: `imbue_keepers_sheet.png` in the shared notes. Tests `RogueHero.Imbue.{Data,Applied,FavorScaling,Blend,Cap}`.

- 2026-10-19 — **Muscle Memory (GDD §4.3) + Heat Ichor (GDD §8.5), meta side.** Save **v3** (`MuscleStage`, `HighestHeatWon`, `LastHeat`; v2→v3 migration derives the stage from existing progress silently — no retro toast — and counts earlier wins as Heat 0 won). **Stages unlock in order** (interpretation: the GDD lists triggers, not an order rule): a first-run win waits for stage 1's 3 runs, then stages 1+2 arrive together; Discoveries alone stop at stage 1. Evaluated in `RHMeta::ApplyRunEnd` after the run is counted → `FRHRunEndResult::NewMuscleStages` → `URHMetaSubsystem::OnMuscleMemoryUnlocked` → toast (always shown, unlike run-end Discoveries), run-summary chip, pause-screen panel, Codex entries `MuscleMemory.Stage<n>` listed after the story (kept out of `BuildCodexEntries`, no layer). In-run effects are the integration session's: read `URHMuscleMemory::GetStage()/IsStageUnlocked(n)`. Thresholds in `URHMetaTuning` (`MuscleRunsForStage1/WinsForStage2/WinsForStage3/DiscoveriesForStage3`); unlock text formats them so text and rule cannot drift. **Heat:** `Ichor × (1 + HeatIchorPerLevel·clamp(Heat,0,MaxHeat))` before rounding; Heat unlock chain = win at Heat n opens n+1 (`MaxSelectableHeat`). Meta owns the selected Heat (`LastHeat`, saved) so it survives hub→arena travel: hub calls `SetSelectedHeat`, run end reads `GetSelectedHeat` into `FRHRunSummary::Heat`; hub reads `GetRunsWon/GetHighestHeatWon/GetMaxSelectableHeat`. Debug: `RH.Meta.Muscle [0..3]`, `RH.Meta.Heat [n]`. For tests/bots, `URHMuscleMemory::SetStageOverride(n)` / cvar `RH.MuscleMemory.Stage n` (non-Shipping, -1 = off) forces `GetStage` without touching the save; `GetStageName(n)` for in-run toasts. Save v3 also carries `DeathsSinceLastWin` (GDD §8.6; +1 per non-won run end, 0 at a win, `URHMetaSubsystem::GetDeathsSinceLastWin`). The run's Heat is read from `GetSelectedHeat` until the hub's `URHHeatSelection::GetRunHeat` (URL `?Heat=n`) is on main; then `URHRunCounters::BuildSummary` switches to it (one line). New ST_UI `MuscleMemory.*` rows ship EN-only; zh-Hans is the loc session's (`RogueHero.Text.Bilingual` fails on those 12 rows until it lands).

(2) **Shrine row** under the patron detail: `Quota  ‹ Quota n ›` (the shared `URHOptionButton`: ←/→ / D-pad step, Accept cycles), the active changes 1..n listed (cumulative), `Ichor ×(1 + 0.15·n)` (display; meta applies it), and the next level's unlock hint; disabled before the first win; navigation Shrine ↓ Quota ↓ actions, skipped while locked. (3) **Return after death**: `URHRecoveryTracker` (Hub/) snapshots the save's completed-run count at Begin; when the hub opens after runs and `URHMetaSubsystem::GetDeathsSinceLastWin()` > 0 it pushes `URHRecoveredScreen` (UI/Hub/): one centred line `Hub.Recovered` ({Count} = deaths since the last win) over a dark scrim, 1.5 s, any key / button / click skips, no animation. `RH.Hub recovered [n]`. (4) **Text:** ST_World `Hub.Recovered`, `Hub.Quota`, `.None`, `.Level`, `.Change1-5`, `.Reward`, `.LockedFirst`, `.Locked` (EN); **provisional zh-Hans** written from the glossary (耀柱 / 驿骑 / 典狱 / 神血, 定额 for Quota) only so `RogueHero.Text.Bilingual` stays green — the loc session rewrites them context-first. Repairs this cycle: 1." for 1.5 s, focus back on the chosen Keeper; a win shows no line and opens Quota 1 (↓ / → selects it, saved by meta; still selected after a relaunch), Begin -> `?Heat=1`; locked levels refused (`max 0` before the first win). - 2026-10-19 — **R15 option A: impact-FX density** (`Docs/Balance/r15_bullet_targets.md` §4, GDD §3.5 "player hit + kill effects on screen" >= 20 / 120 / 200 at minutes 2 / 10 / 18; extends fallback (j)). `URHImpactFXSubsystem`: **debris** pool 600 -> **1 500**, chunks per kill 3 -> **6** (dense 2 -> 4), life 1.4-2.2 -> **2.6-3.4 s** (settled chunks skip integration); a **scorch disc + flattened scrap plate per kill** (ring buffer 640, **8 s**, shrink over the last 2 s, 1.2-2 uu above the floor, far under the 95 uu telegraph overlay); sparks per hit 2 -> **3**, per kill 6 -> **10**, hit budget 48 -> 64 (dense 12 -> 16), spark pool 1 500 -> 3 000; a **kill flash** (cool-white bloom, 0.09 s); sparks and flashes now draw with `M_Tracer` (emissive, intensity 8) instead of the lit tint, so they bloom like the tracers. **Crowd shockwave:** in the dense LOD (>= 20 kills/s, exit 16) kills gather into clusters (350 uu) flushed every 0.25 s; each cluster with >= 4 kills gets one expanding ring (24 emissive segments, ice-blue, intensity 3, r 220-420 uu, 0.4 s) + 8 chunks + 12 sparks, instead of per-kill sparks / flashes; no camera shake added. Shared palette is cool / neutral only (test `RogueHero.ImpactFX.CoolPalette`). **API:** `GetLiveEffectCount()` (sparks + flashes + debris + smoke + haze + decals + shockwaves + imbue; also in `FRHImpactFXStats::LiveEffects`, CSV `RHImpactFX/LiveEffects`, and a once-per-sim-minute `ImpactFX: minute N live effects avg / peak` log line) for `RH.Stats`; `SpawnImbueImpact(God.<Path>, Pos, ERHImbueFX::Hit|Kill)` for the weapons session's imbued bullets (Keeper-tinted streaks + a flash on kills, Keeper colours as the ultimates, 64 calls/step, hits a quarter of that in the dense LOD). Tests `RogueHero.ImpactFX.{KillDensity, Lifetimes, CrowdShockwave, Imbue, CoolPalette}`. **Density measured** (3 steered RH.Bot v2 runs, seeds 921-923, time scale 6, -nullrhi): live effects per-minute average at minute 2 **54-75**, minute 10 **160-177**, minute 18 **213-222** (peaks 107-145 / 273-350 / 340-445; kills/s 1.7-2.5 / 5.1-5.7 / 6.3-6.9); no pool overflows. **Cost measured** (`L_StressTest`, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, RH.God, 1 200 frames after 15 s warm-up, 1920x1080 `-RenderOffscreen`, Epic, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, A/B with `RH.FX.Impact 0`): normal kill rate (2 kills/s, live ~440): impact FX game thread (steps + render) **0.02 ms median / 0.13 p95**, GPU 5.87 vs 6.12 ms off (noise); **lethal stress, dense LOD (~380 kills/s, live ~2 240)**: **0.24 ms median / 0.39 p95** (max 0.59), GPU median **6.93 vs 6.40 ms off (+0.53 ms)**, GT 3.66 vs 3.17. Inside the brief's budget (<= ~0.5 ms GT, <= ~1 ms GPU); not measured on a 4070-class GPU. Swap-out unchanged: one GPU Niagara system fed by the same event array. Screenshot sheet `Docs/Readability/ImpactFX_density_2026-10-19.png` (10:00 / 10:20 / 18:00 / 18:20, via the new `RH.ShotAt <mm:ss> <cmd>`, run-clock keyed); open item: the dense-LOD ground haze (0.10 grey, tuned for a 0.09 floor) reads as a dark patch on the lighter arena floor.
- 2026-10-19 — **Keeper sound signatures (Art §11, v0.1 must).** 6 Paths × 4 variants = 24 waves synthesised procedurally by `Tools/synth_audio.py` (`KEEPERS`, standard library only, deterministic) and imported by `Tools\CreateAudio.bat` as `/Game/RH/Audio/SFX/SW_Keeper_<Path>_<Pick|Tier|PassedOver|Imbued>`. Cue keys are Path keys (`ERHSfx::Keeper_*`), never fiction. Shared tonal centre D, so stingers that follow each other never clash. **Variants:** Pick ≤ 0.8 s (the motif cell); Tier ≤ 1.9 s (motif + texture); PassedOver (low-passed, quieter, the motif bent down a semitone); Imbued ≤ 0.08 s tick with no highs. **Runtime:** `RHKeeperSignature.h`: `FRHKeeperStingerQueue` plays one stinger per 0.45 s with precedence Tier > PassedOver > Pick; a Tier drops the same Path's pending Pick; entries expire after 1.5 s. Stingers are UI-flagged so they play on the paused offer screen. Imbued ticks are their own scheduler group: voice cap 2, 1 start per frame, ≥ 0.12 s apart across all Paths, plus per-Path MinInterval 0.35 s and aggregation at volume 0.14. They never touch the combat budget. A missing Keeper wave falls back to OfferPick / TierLine. **Events:** tier toast = `URHEffectManager::OnTierLine` (live). **Adapters (TODO, swap-out points in `RHKeeperSignature.h`):** (1) blessing pick: derived from `URHOfferFlow::OnCardApplied` (Blessing / Legendary / Fusion / GodChoice card with a Path) until `OnBlessingPicked(Path)` lands; then bind `HandleBlessingPicked`. (2) passed-over line: an `OnTierLine` broadcast whose text is the `ST_Keepers Keeper.<Path>.PassedOver` row (or Tier ≤ 0) until its own event lands; then bind `HandleKeeperPassedOver`. (3) imbued hits: `URHAudioSubsystem::NotifyImbuedHit(WorldContext, Path, Pos)` (nothing calls it yet) until the weapons' `OnImbuedHit(Path, Pos)` lands; then bind `HandleImbuedHit`. **Fix:** `import_audio.py` now force-saves DA_Audio; `fill_default_cues` does not dirty the package, so new cues never landed. Debug `RH.Audio.Keeper <Path> [pick|tier|passed|imbued]`; `RH.Audio.Stats` adds a keeper line. **Verified:** tests `RogueHero.Audio.KeeperSignature.{Mapping,Assets,ImbuedRateLimit,StingerQueue}` (229/229 RogueHero); smoke PASS. An off-screen audition run queued all 18 stingers with 0 dropped and 0 fallbacks; 40 imbued hits in 0.8 s gave imbued peak 1 / cap 2 and 25 culled; audio tick 0.005 ms avg (budget ~0.3 ms).

Per frame it scales the emissive "Intensity" of every player archetype renderer and the six imbue accent renderers (`ARHInstanceRenderer::SetIntensityScale`, MID updated only on > 2 % change; the EnemyOrb archetype and the enemy glow / core renderers are untouched), straight-tracer width by 0.6 + 0.4 × dim and trail length by 0.7 + 0.3 × dim. - 2026-10-19 — **Ring Emitter / Spiral Projector retuned as danmaku weapons** (GDD §5.2 / §3.5, designer R15). Ring: damage 8 → 4, speed 750 → 520, lifetime 2.0 → 3.5 s, pierce 1, L1 still 12 orbs every 1.2 s; track count +6 / +6 / rate / damage +2 / count +8 / rate / size (L6 = 32 orbs every 0.96 s). Spiral: damage 4 → 3, cooldown 0.07 → 0.06, speed 800 → 600, lifetime 2.5 → 3.2 s, pierce 1; arms at L2 and L5 (3 arms by L5). Tide × Ring fusion keeps its own 1.6 s flight (`Lifetime` set explicitly). **Measured** (RH.Bot god runs at 9:30, offers declined, rolling 60 s): live player bullets Ring L6 **81** (peak 118), L8 **101**; Spiral L6 **148** (peak 176), L8 **158**: the 40–90+ band is met. **Not met: build-level swarm TTK ≤ 0.3 s with one finisher.** Ring L6 + Scatter L6 2.08 s, Spiral L6 + Scatter L6 2.40 s, Ring L6 + Pulse Rifle L6 3.15 s (Scatter L6 alone 0.35 s in the same crowd; danmaku alone 2.0–3.0 s; kills/min 216–310 throughout). Cause: `RHRunStats` times a swarm kill from its first hit, and danmaku chip hits now touch most of the crowd before the finisher arrives, so the clock starts early by construction. Not a weapon number: either the metric times the finishing window (e.g. from the first hit of a one-shot-role weapon, or last 2 hits), or danmaku per-bullet damage must one-shot Scrapwings (which is what R10 removed). Routed to the scheduler / balance session; numbers kept as above meanwhile.

- 2026-10-19 — **Heat 1-4 (GDD §8.5), skill checks only.** Data on the timeline: `URHSpawnTimeline::HeatLevels` (`FRHHeatLevel`, cumulative; `GetHeatRules(Heat)` folds levels 1..Heat) and `FRHSpawnEvent::MinHeat` (an event fires only at that Heat or above; `Duration <= 0` = a **companion** that rides the current threat beat instead of restarting it). **H1** `PylonFanFromTime` 360: the late 4-bullet fan (14 deg) from **6:00** instead of 12:00, without the late reach placement / range stretch (the scheduler brief said ~8:00; the GDD table says 6:00 and wins). **H2** `ExtraEliteAffixes` 1: every elite (wave elites and event escorts; regular spawns are never elite) rolls one more affix it does not have (`RHElite::AddAffixes`), so the 5:00 wave carries 2. **H3** companions with `MinHeat 3`: 7:30 ring + a 5-Courser line (7:35), 12:30 stampede + a 4-Pylon nest (12:35), 17:30 nest + a 24-Scrapwing ring (17:32). **H4** `CourserChainCharges` 1: a Courser chains a second charge; its direction is locked when the first charge starts (from the first's end point toward the hero) and its red line is drawn from that end point for the whole first charge, then the Courser holds for `ChargeWindUp - ChargeDuration` (0.35 s) before the second charge — the second line's lead time is the full 1 s wind-up, never shorter (`RHEnemy::StepCharge(..., ChainCharges)`, `FChargeState::ChainLeft / NextDir`, `URHEnemySubsystem::SetCourserChain`, additive telegraph block in `Render`). **H5** (the Warden's enrage at 50 %) is the boss owner's and is not in this table. No HP, damage or telegraph timing changes at any level. **Heat source:** the director reads the hub's `URHHeatSelection::GetRunHeat(this)` each step (`?Heat=n` on the travel URL, else the Shrine's selection; `RH.Hub heat <n> force` for tests and bots). Tests `RogueHero.Spawn.HeatLevels` (rules cumulative, H2 affix count on the 5:00 wave, H3 companion fires and does not cut the 7:30 beat short) and `RogueHero.Spawn.HeatCourserChain` (two charges, chained line shown >= the full wind-up; Heat 0 single charge). **Verified, RH.Bot v2 steered, one run per patron (Storm / Forge / Tide / Shade / Radiance), to minute 24, time scale 6:** Heat 0 (seeds 961-965) 2/5 wins, deaths 13:00 / 14:10 / 16:07; Heat 3 (971-975) 2/5, deaths 11:13 / 14:35 / 16:00; Heat 5 (981-985, H1-H4 here; these runs used a debug `RH.Heat` override that the hub getter has since replaced) 2/5, deaths 9:28 / 11:21 and one in the Warden fight; dashes/min over minutes 4-16 rise from ~4 (Heat 0) to ~5.5 (Heat 3) and ~7 (Heat 5): the levels add movement, not a wall. Two batches of default-patron runs died before 12:00 at Heat 0 as well (seeds 951-953: 9:35-11:24), so the early deaths are patron / build variance, not Heat. Deaths with no enemy-sim source are still the hostile bullets resolved in `URHBulletSubsystem` (open item above). **Swarm TTK finishing window (per scheduler TTK decision 2026-10-19):** `URHEnemySubsystem::FinishHitTime` (reset at spawn) is stamped by the **first** bullet hit, DoT tick or effect hit whose damage is >= 25 % of the enemy's max HP (later heavy hits do not move it, so a multi-heavy-hit kill is not read as ~0 s); at the kill `URHRunStats::RecordKill` gets its 4th argument (integration 6994f03) `SimTime - FinishHitTime`, falling back to the first hit when no hit reached 25 %, so a kill made of chip hits is not counted as a one-shot.

Verified headless, steered bot, `RH.MuscleMemory.Stage 3`, time scale 6: normal runs end with the Form weapon at L7 (steered spreads upgrades, so L9 is a late-run reward); with `RH.E2E.Later 75 RH.Level 40` the first card-taken weapon (Scatter Cannon, taken at L7) was dealt and picked `8->9` and finished **L9** while the given Pulse Rifle and the two later weapons stopped at **L8**; no L9 card was ever dealt for another weapon. Follow-up: `RH.WeaponLevel` now clamps to the weapon's own cap (9 for the Form weapon) instead of 8.

Test `RogueHero.Debug.RunStats.FinishingWindowTTK`.
