New data rule `Offer.Rule.NeedsBackupWeapon` (Config/Tags/RH.ini) in `URHModifierDef::OfferRules` (copied to `FRHOfferPoolEntry::OfferRules`): `URHOfferGenerator::BuildCandidates` never deals such a modifier while the build owns fewer than 2 weapons. `Mod.OrbitLock`'s fallback def carries it (set it on `DA_Mod_OrbitLock` when that asset exists). Rule addition to §5.6's list, not a cut: once a second weapon is owned the card returns for either weapon. Test `RogueHero.Offers.SoleWeaponGuard`.

Rolling 60 s ending at run 9:58: **Scatter L6 alone** — finishing TTK median **0.000 s** (p90 1.85 s), first-hit 0.017 s, kills/min 223. **Ring L6 + Scatter L6** — finishing TTK median **2.32 s** (p90 6.87 s), first-hit 2.88 s, kills/min 277. So the 25 % rule moves Ring + Scatter only ~0.6 s: at minute 10 a Ring orb still deals >= 25 % of a Scrapwing's max HP, so Ring hits open the finishing window themselves and the 2.3 s is mostly the time until the Scatter finisher arrives. Not changed here (balance / scheduler call): a higher threshold (e.g. 50 %), or a per-weapon 'chip' tag on the danmaku weapons, would separate them.
- 2026-10-20 — **Warden Forms I–III (GDD §6, Narrative §10, Art §4/§9; balance session).** `DA_Enemy_Boss` gains `Forms[3]` (`FRHBossFormDef`: `MinWins` 0 / 1 / 3, a whole `FRHBossTuning`, mesh, vane, conduit offsets); C++ fallback `URHBossDef::MakeFallbackForms` until the asset carries them (an asset without forms gets the fallback forms on its own tuning). The form comes from `URHSaveGame::RunsWon` (read-only); `RH.Boss.Form n` / `-RHBossForm=n` force it. **Heat 5** raises `PhaseThreeAt` to `Heat5EnrageAt` 0.5, Heat read from `URHHeatSelection::GetRunHeat` (`RH.Boss.Heat` / `-RHHeat=` override). **Form II:** per-Path phase-2 counters, chosen once from the hero's highest-favor Path when phase 2 begins (interpretation of the GDD [proposal] examples): Storm → 2 Shielded Bulwarks / 13 s; Forge → plating refuses Burn 4 s / 9 s; Tide → refuses Chill + Freeze 4 s and the next ring is a slow 24-slot ring with a double lane / 10 s; Shade → refuses Mark 4 s + 5 Volatile Scrapwings / 12 s; Radiance → 2 Flare Pylons / 14 s; Quicksilver → 2 Swift Coursers and dash charges ×0.7 interval / 12 s. Retreat: hurt in the last 1.5 s → walks to within 450 uu of the centre at ×1.25 speed (attacks still aim at the hero). **Form III:** new `ERHBossPhase::Four` below 10 % (Forms I–II skip it): 2 s stall, then the six Keeper patterns in `KeeperOrder` (Storm fork fan, Forge embers, Tide slow arc, Shade closing ring with a 4-slot lane away from the hull, Radiance spokes, Quicksilver 3 chained dash charges), each with a ≥ 0.75 s red telegraph, beat 2.4 s; phase-4 damage counts ×0.6 so the six beats play (`PhaseFourDamageMult`); the hazard ring stops and pulses with each hero dash (visual). Six Keeper conduits drawn as emissive light on `SM_Enemy_Boss_FormIII` sockets, dim until each beat. Every Warden kill calls `URHMetaSubsystem::NotifyBossFormKilled(Form)`; meta owns the rule (the first Form III kill with RunsWon ≥ 3 records `Codex.Warden.FormIII` once; 2026-10-20 fix, replaces the boss-side fallback). **Enemies hooks (merge hotspot):** `URHEnemySubsystem::SetStatusWard(slot, mask)` (+ `FRHEnemyStatuses::ClearKinds`) and `ClearEliteFlag(slot)` (affixed boss adds drop no chest). Meshes: `Tools/build_boss_forms.py` / `BuildBossForms.bat` reuse `build_unit_models.py`'s kernel without re-saving its assets; new `MI_Unit_Pale`, `SM_Enemy_Boss_FormII` (plate torn open, pale person-scaled figure in the breach; the figure reads only up close, Art §9 "detail can slip"), `SM_Enemy_Boss_FormIII` (+ six conduit housings), review map `L_UnitLineup_BossForms`. Verification (RH.Bot v2 steered, `Docs/Boss/README.md`): Form I in full 20-minute runs 2 deaths / 5 fights (40 %, target ~⅓), fight median 90 s, 16 dashes; 19:40-start probes (n = 8 each) Form I 75 % / II 62 % / III 75 % deaths, fight median 125 / 117 / 141 s, dashes 21 / 20 / 23.

Only the look follows PL.C15/C16: the rings fire from the shoulder mirror heads. No swap-out; GDD §6 and `FRHBossTuning` stay the source.
- 2026-10-20 — **Warden Form slope retune (balance; boss-segment probes, Bot v2 steered, 19:40 start at L21, 6 Patrons).** Before: Form II was the easiest (1/9 deaths vs Form I 4/9): its retreat to the centre parked it mid-arena, so its charges rarely pinned the hero (charge damage ~1/3 of Form I). Now: (1) rings stay R14 (3.2 / 2.0 s) for every form, the designer's P2 answer; (2) the Form II retreat is a beat (`RetreatTime` 2.5 s, `RetreatCooldown` 16 s) that **ends in a telegraphed dash charge** (`bRetreatEndsInCharge`); (3) Form III inherits Form II's retreat (Form III ⊇ Form II); (4) the steps between forms are dash-charge intervals only: I 5.5 / 4.0, II 4.8 / 3.6, III 4.4 / 3.0 s. Result (deaths / fights): I 7/25 (28 %), II 10/18 (56 %), III 11/18 (61 %). I < II < III holds; the I→II step is steeper than "moderate" and is the first candidate if a human playtest finds Form II harsh (lengthen `RetreatCooldown` or Form II's dash interval). Telegraph times unchanged.
- 2026-10-20 — **G15 Warden look from art book PL.C15 / C16 (Cast-off volume), rigid parts instead of `SK_Enemy_Boss`.** The page names a skeletal mesh; a procedural skeletal pipeline is not feasible here (scheduler ruling), so the Warden is a body mesh per form (`SM_Enemy_Boss_FormI/II/III`, drawn by URHEnemySubsystem as the hull) plus four rigid part meshes (`SM_Enemy_Boss_ArmShield`, `_ArmRam`, `_Shoulder` x2, `_Leg` x4) that URHBossSubsystem places on the body's `Part_*` sockets and animates from the brain state (charge brace / thrust, sweep wind-out and swing, shoulder heads aiming and tilting through a ring wind-up, a diagonal-pair gait from the distance walked; phase 3: crown optics x1.6 and the ram forearm's strip red). **Swap-out:** a real `SK_Enemy_Boss` with the same sockets replaces the body + parts and `RHBossParts`. Built by `Tools/build_warden.py` (statuses' `build_enemy_models.py` part builders: stele, Courser pod, Pylon mirror head, Breaker ram, Tallyman drum, Bulwark tracks, Scrapwings; procedural, no external assets). Forms: II tears the brow and **opens the back cage** with the Original's old body inside (designer Q10); III adds the six origin guides' flare housings (code-lit as before). Deviations from the page, for the 62 deg gameplay camera (Art §3): the boiler leans 12 deg, not 30 (at 30 its face and crown, the status light, turn edge-on from above), and the top sits at ~12.7 m (antenna) / ~11.4 m (mirror heads) so it does not swell toward the camera. Rings stay hull-centred (mechanics unchanged, designer 2026-10-19); the shoulder mirror heads flash as each ring fires. The pre-G15 `SM_Enemy_Boss` + vane stay in the kernel lineup, unused by the fight; `Tools/build_boss_forms.py` is retired. Review: `RH.Boss.Turnaround`, `Tools/warden_sheet.py` -> `Docs/Boss/G15_Form<N>_sheet.png`.

Read in dusk and sun: one **neutral, shadowless key light** rides with the hull (`RHBossParts::KeyLight*`: front-above, 900 cd, r 2 600, visible only while the hull is drawn); neutral because warm / magenta mean danger and cool means the player (Art §3), no new emissive. - 2026-10-20 — **Warden stele side (scheduler, deciding for the designer): the board's render wins over its caption.** PL.C15 captions the stele tower shield as the "left arm" (左臂), but every render of the board shows it on the viewer's left from the front, i.e. the Warden's right. The stele stays on the Warden's right (`PART_MOUNTS["ArmShield"]` y +300 in `Tools/build_warden.py`), the ram on its left. No swap-out.
- 2026-10-19 — **Muscle Memory playtest fix (save back-fill + run-end visibility).** Playtest: a veteran save (15 runs, 2 wins, 11 Discoveries) started at stage 0 and all three stages arrived at run end as toasts queued behind the run summary. Root cause: UE writes `SaveGame` properties as a delta against the class defaults, so `SaveVersion` (default = `CurrentVersion`) is never written — a save made while v2 was current loads as v3 and `Migrate()` skips it. Fix: `URHSaveGame::BackfillDerived()` runs on every load and re-derives `MuscleStage` / the Heat-0 win (never lowering), saving if it changed. Run-end stages now show as one framed **Muscle Memory** block on the run summary (badge, name, rules line per stage); `OnMuscleMemoryUnlocked` gains `bAtRunEnd` and the toast only fires off run end (debug `RH.Meta.Muscle`). **Follow-up, not fixed:** the `SaveVersion` delta hazard still affects any future migration; the robust fix is a default of 0 written explicitly before save (needs v1/v2 migration idempotency checked).
- 2026-10-19 — **RH.Stats pressure rows (balance P4, `Docs/Balance/pressure_proposal.md`).** `threat actions/min` (Courser charges, Breaker slams, Pylon bursts started; targets >= 4 at minute 10, >= 8 at minute 18, none at minute 2) and `nearest enemy p10` (10th percentile of the hero -> nearest live enemy distance over the window's per-frame samples, report only). CSV columns `threat_actions_per_min, nearest_enemy_p10_uu` appended. Counting: `URHEnemySubsystem::GetThreatActionCount()` once statuses adds it (compile-time switch in `RunStatsThreatTotal`); until then RunStats counts charger / slammer / turret WindUp starts itself (a Pylon's aim line precedes each burst, so it is the same three actions). First reading (steered bot, Storm, time scale 6): threat actions 0 / 54 / 53 per min at minutes 2 / 10 / 15, nearest enemy p10 398 / 332 / 468 uu. Test `RogueHero.Debug.RunStats.Pressure`.
- 2026-10-19 — **Pressure P2 / P3, per balance pressure proposal, scheduler-approved 2026-10-19** (`Docs/Balance/pressure_proposal.md`). **P2:** Courser `ChargeTriggerRange` 750 -> **1 100** uu, `ChargeDuration` 0.65 -> **0.9** s (line ~1 350 uu, still overshoots the trigger; wind-up 1.0 s unchanged, GDD §6; the Heat-4 chained hold becomes 0.1 s so the chained line still shows for the full 1 s). **P3:** new per-role data `FRHEnemyStats::ThreatSpawnRingMin/Max` — regular spawns in **Threat blocks** use it instead of the role / timeline ring; Courser and Breaker **1 000-1 200** uu (Harvest, waves and events unchanged). DA_Enemy_Rusher / DA_Enemy_Bruiser re-baked. Test `RogueHero.Spawn.ThreatRing`. **Also:** `URHEnemySubsystem::GetThreatActionCount()` (int64, monotonic; integration's balance-P4 `threat_actions_per_min`): +1 per Courser charge wind-up started (chained charges and drop-in lines included), Breaker slam wind-up started (drop-in slams included), Pylon burst started; the Warden's attacks are not counted. `URHEnemySubsystem::HurtHeroFromShot(Hero, Damage, Shooter)`: public hook for hostile bullets resolved in `URHBulletSubsystem`, applies the hit through `HurtHero` and credits `shots <Shooter>` in the hero death report (closes the unattributed-death gap once Combat calls it).
- 2026-10-19 — **Designer-approved P1 drop-in threats** (balance pressure proposal P1; playtest 3 "enemies can almost never get close": strong builds kill at ~1 000 uu, outside every trigger range). Data on the timeline, `URHSpawnTimeline::DropIns` (`FRHDropInRules`): cadence step function **12 s from 6:00 -> 10 s at 10:00 -> 7 s at 15:00 -> 5 s at 18:00**, **x2 in Threat blocks**, none in Breath or the boss fight; mix **50 % Courser line / 30 % Breaker slam / 20 % Pylon perch**; fairness **<= 1 drop-in telegraph on the ground at once (<= 2 in Threat blocks), landings >= 1.5 s apart** (the scheduler's note said "max 1 active"; the designer approved the proposal as written, so its <= 2-in-Threat is used, both are data). Director (`FRHSpawnDirectorCore::StepDropIns`) emits `FRHSpawnRequest` with `DropIn` set; `URHEnemySubsystem::QueueDropIn` draws the red laser ground telegraph (Art §3) and lands the enemy when it runs out: **Courser line** lands 1 000-1 200 uu out with its exact charge line drawn through where the hero stood (the telegraph is its `ChargeWindUp`, 1.0 s) and lands already charging; **Breaker slam** draws its `SlamRadius` (300) circle centred 0-200 uu from the hero for **1.2 s**, lands and slams at once; **Pylon perch** marks a point 1 000 uu out on the screen's wide axis (+-35 deg) for 1.0 s, lands and starts its normal aim. No HP, damage or telegraph-timing change: the proposal's 0.45 s perch aim was **not** used — the Pylon keeps its 0.75 s `FireAimTime` (the 2026-10-19 telegraph audit floor). The minute log `Enemies: approach minute N` now also counts drop-ins landed per kind. Test `RogueHero.Spawn.DropIns` (cadence, Threat x2, caps, landing gap, geometry, telegraph >= 1 s, none in the boss fight); spawn-count tests skip drop-in requests. Pending drop-in slams are also reported by `GetBlastZones` (timed circles), so the hero-side telegraph reader and RH.Bot dodge them; pending Courser lines become visible to them only on landing (a lane accessor in `Player/RHTelegraphs` is a follow-up for its owner). **Sanity (3 steered runs, seeds 1001-1003, to 20:00):** Quicksilver survives with 4-12 dashes/min; Storm / Forge die at 15:03 / 15:59 (contact, not slams); full verification is balance's.
- 2026-10-19 — **Keeper imbued-hit sound is live** (closes adapter (3) of the Keeper signatures line). `URHAudioSubsystem` binds the weapons' `URHEffectManager::OnImbuedHit` (one batched array of `FRHImbuedHit` per sim step) to `HandleImbuedHits`, which counts hits per Path (`RHKeeperSig::CountImbuedByPath`) into the aggregated, group-throttled tick. The dead `NotifyImbuedHit` adapter (no callers, hence "imbued 0" in playtest 3's audio log) is deleted. `RH.Audio.Stats` now reports "imbued hits heard". Verified: 266/266 RogueHero tests; an off-screen run with Live Wire + Lien heard 2 imbued hits, and both played their Keeper tick (Storm, Shade).
- 2026-10-19 — **Fix (playtest 3 P0): aimed fans always put a shot on the hero.** The late / Heat-1 Flare Pylon fan spread its N shots symmetrically about the aim (`(k - (N-1)/2) x SpreadDeg`), so the 4-shot fan's nearest shots flew +-7 deg off and a hero standing still was never hit (the designer stood still to exploit it; GDD §3.5 "threats are skill checks"). `RHEnemy::AimedFanOffsetDeg(K, N, SpreadDeg, bAltSide)`: odd fans stay centred; even fans shift half a step so one shot is exactly on the aim, to alternating sides shot by shot within a burst (`FChargeState::Shots` parity), so the burst still reads symmetric and the fan keeps its width and count (visual density unchanged). Audit of Enemies/: the Pylon fan is the only aimed spread (Courser charges lock onto the hero's position; Breaker slams and Volatile blasts are areas). Warden (Boss/, balance's): the Storm forks use the same symmetric formula (StormForks 5 is odd, fine today; any even value would leave the hole) and the Tide wave's 26 slots over 150 deg put the aim mid-gap 3 deg off (~30-50 uu at 600-1 000 uu); sent to balance with `AimedFanOffsetDeg` to reuse. Test `RogueHero.Enemies.AimedFansHitStationary`: fans of 1-6 at 8 / 14 / 20 deg, both sides, a stationary hero at 200-2 000 uu is inside a shot's path (nearest-shot miss <= hero body radius + shot radius); the old formula fails it from ~450 uu.
- 2026-10-19 — **Enemy approach measure (playtest 3 item 6c, "enemies can almost never get close").** `FRHApproachStats` (`Enemies/RHApproachStats`, a member of `URHEnemySubsystem`, additive hooks at spawn, movement, contact and kill): per enemy the spawn distance to the hero and the closest surface gap reached (centre distance minus hero body radius and enemy radius; <= 0 = touching); per sim minute one log line `Enemies: approach minute N` with Swarm / other medians (spawn distance, closest gap median and p10, gap at death, share that reached contact, spawn->death) plus seconds any enemy overlapped the hero and contact hits landed. Debug measure only, no gameplay change. Test `RogueHero.Enemies.Approach`. **Measured** (RH.Bot v2 steered, Quicksilver / Storm / Forge, seeds 1001-1003, to 20:00): Swarm spawn ~1 500 uu all run; from minute 10 Storm / Quicksilver builds kill the median Scrapwing ~1 000 uu out 1-2 s after spawn (closest 10 % 500-800 uu), Forge ~450-550 uu; **0 % of kills ever touched the hero**, overlap 0-0.2 s per minute; hero 600 uu/s vs fastest walker 330. Handed to balance (shared notes `enemy-approach.md`) with the enemy-side levers (closer partial spawn ring, telegraphed lunge, Courser / Breaker share, pincer arcs, on-screen events) for them to pick.
- 2026-10-19 — **Playtest 3 "safe spot" (enemy bullets don't hit a still hero at a certain spot): not reproduced in the bullet code.** Enemy bullets do not use the spatial hash: each is a circle test against the hero every 60 Hz step (`StepEnemyBullets`). New test `RogueHero.EnemyBullets.NoSafeSpot` fires 1 600+ aimed bullets (16 directions × 650 / 900 / 1 800 / 3 500 uu/s) at a still hero on 200 uu cell edges (199.9 / 200 / 200.1), cell corners, the arena centre, along all four bounds and in the corners: every one hits. Live headless repro (`RH.Hero.Place X Y`, `RH.EnemyFire.Stats`, new debug commands in `Debug/RHGiveCommand.cpp`), still hero at 8:00: centre 126 hits of 299 Pylon shots in 20 s, corner (2960, 2960) 48 of 105 before dying to contact; the misses are the outer shots of the Pylon fan. Remaining candidates outside Combat, pending the designer's location: (a) Pylons are planted and fire only within `FireRange` 1 250 of the hero, so a still hero outside every planted Pylon's range is never shot at — a design gap, not a collision bug (Enemies, statuses session); (b) post-hit / Flinch i-frames let later bullets pass through visibly; (c) bullets are drawn at Z 70 under a −62° camera, so a bullet passing just camera-side of the 40 uu body looks like it goes through it.
- 2026-10-19 — **Imbue visibility (playtest 3: "almost didn't" see a change on a Quicksilver + Shade run).** Diagnosis: **procs were firing, the look failed.** A headless steered Quicksilver run logs 163 ricochets by 5:25 and 399 by 8:48 (`RH.Imbue.Stats`, +8 % damage). The designer's "ImpactFX imbue 0 every minute" is the *live particle count at the log instant* (`now: … imbue N`) of 0.2 s flashes, so it reads 0 almost always; "audio imbued 0" is because nothing binds `OnImbuedHit` yet (audio still on its adapter, routed to loc-zh). What a player saw: Quicksilver = a thin silver streak on a cyan core + invisible bullet speed + ricochets that look like normal bullets; Shade = small wisps, Mark without a hit flash. **Fix (data + render, Art §3 kept):** `URHBlessingImbueDef::bTintCore / CoreTint / CoreIntensity` — while a cool Keeper leads the blend, every straight player tracer's **core** is drawn in its colour (`RH_Bullets_ImbueCore<k>` renderers, density-dimmed like the rest): Storm white-blue (0.72, 0.80, 1.0) ×13, Tide aqua-teal (0, 1, 0.62) ×7, **Shade indigo-violet (0.32, 0.10, 1.0) ×4.5**, **Quicksilver mint-green (0.30, 1.0, 0.42) ×5** (saturated colours at lower intensity so the hue survives bloom instead of washing to white; luminance still ≥ ~45:1 against the floor). Forge and Radiance keep the cool core (warmth stays an accent). Status procs flash a small ring in the Keeper colour on the target; ricochets draw a short spark off the target. Test: core tints are cool and differ from the default cyan bolt (`RogueHero.Imbue.Data`). Sheet `imbue_visible_v2.png` (shared notes).
- 2026-10-19 — **Enemy fire readable at 1 300+ own bullets** (playtest 3: "hard to see enemy attacks, but very 爽"; Art §3 / non-negotiable 11). (1) **Steeper density dimming:** after the 0.4 floor at 240 live player bullets, a second linear segment to **0.12 at 1 200** (`DimDeepBullets` / `DimDeepFloor`); tracer width now 0.45 + 0.55 × dim. The veil stays (≥ 10:1 over the floor) — the 爽 is kept. (2) **Louder enemy bullets** (all in `URHBulletSubsystem::Render`, the enemy bullet renderer is Combat's): glow 2.4 → 3.0 radii, warm orb intensity 6 → 9, magenta 3.5 → 5 (dark core kept), and a **dark outline disc** (5.2 radii, `RH_EnemyBullets_Outline`, opaque, Z 64: above player tracers, under the glow) so every enemy bullet carries a solid dark rim that occludes the cool fire behind it. Tonemapping caps saturated warm hues below white, so the rim — not raw brightness — is what guarantees 3:1. **Measured** (off-screen 1080p, `-RHStress=150,1300`, two rings of parked enemy bullets placed inside the player streams with the new `RH.EnemyFire.Ring`, frozen frame, 20 enemy bullets in the streams): enemy glow vs its immediate surround median **4.9:1 → 33.5:1**, 10th percentile 1.6 → 16.6:1, share ≥ 3:1 **55 % → 100 %**; vs the brightest pixel within 3 radii median 0.8 → 14.2:1 (p10 0.5 → 3.8:1). Before/after `enemy_fire_1300_before_after.png` (shared notes). Test `RogueHero.Bullets.DensityDim` (deep floor, 1 300 bullets ≤ 0.15). Enemy-side visuals (statuses session) unchanged.
- 2026-10-19 — **`SaveVersion` written on every save (closes the delta hazard above).** `URHSaveGame::SaveVersion` defaults to **0** and `URHMetaSubsystem::Save` stamps `CurrentVersion` (`StampVersion()`) before serializing, so the version is always on disk and future bumps cannot be skipped. Saves written before this fix have no `SaveVersion` and load as v0, re-running every `Migrate()` step; the steps are now **raise-only** (v1→v2 was already idempotent; v2→v3 only raises `MuscleStage` / `HighestHeatWon` and no longer resets `LastHeat` / `DeathsSinceLastWin`), so a current-shaped unversioned save keeps all its progress. Rule for future steps: never lower or reset a field. Test `RogueHero.Meta.Save.VersionRoundTrip` (v2-shaped bytes → load → Migrate → save → reload). Also fixes `ResetToFresh` not clearing `DeathsSinceLastWin`.

- 2026-10-19 — **Keeper hues in the world reach 3:1 on lit stone (scheduler ruling on the A1 contrast table).** `RHGods::GetWorldEmissive(PathTag)` = the displayed-luminance scale at which `GetColour` clears `RHRender::MinWorldContrast` (3:1) over lit stone (`RHRender::LitStoneLuminance` 0.20), ≥ 1, rounded up to 0.05 — computed, so the rule cannot drift from the hue: Storm 1.6, Forge **1.55**, Tide 1.15, Shade **3.25**, Radiance / Quicksilver 1 (the ruling quoted 1.5 / 3.2; those land at 2.94:1 / 2.98:1, so the computed values win). Render constants moved to `RHRender` (Core/RHArenaLighting.h) with `DisplayedToEmissive()` = 2^-0.62 for materials under the fixed exposure. Arena conduit trenches and relay lamps (`Environment/RHArenaEnvironment.cpp`, lent 2026-10-19) now take `GetColour` + `GetWorldEmissive × DisplayedToEmissive`: displayed L 0.71–0.78 (3.02–3.30:1), flicker only upward (+10 %) so no panel drops under 3:1, peak L ≤ 0.86 < bloom threshold 1.0 (UE bloom thresholds luminance). Conduits are ~3× brighter than the old dim 0.16–0.28 values — not yet checked on screen. Forge vs enemy fire: accepted (Forge never tints player bullets, Art §12).
- 2026-10-19 — **Weather (G16/G17 M0): variable weather, overcast + sun with volumetric cloud** (designer 2026-10-19: "天气应该是可变的 两个都做", v0.1 scope in the arena too). The loc/env session now owns `Core/RHArenaLighting.*`; the `RHRender::` constants stay unchanged. (1) **Data:** `URHWeatherDef` (`Environment/RHWeatherDef`, primary type `RHWeather`, `DA_Weather`; until it is authored the C++ factory fallback **`URHWeatherDef::MakeFallback`** is used) holds two `FRHWeatherState`s plus the variability rules. **Overcast** = the shipped Art §2 rig unchanged (sun −42° / 35°, 1.6, 4 600 K, 12° source, sky 4, fog 0.006) plus a stratus deck that only shows in the sky. **Sun** = G17: a low 24° warm sun from behind and to the side of the gameplay camera (yaw 155°), crisp 1.2° shadows, clearer air, broken cumulus with drifting cloud shadow (0.4), crepuscular scattering, and the sun in the composition for wide shots. (2) **Variable:** each run rolls a plan from the run seed on its own salted stream (overcast 55 %), and 35 % of runs drift to the other state over 90 s, starting between 6:00 and 14:00, so the sky is settled before the boss. A new run seed re-plans. The hub keeps its own clock. Overrides: `-RHWeather=overcast|sun`, `RH.Weather [overcast|sun|0..1|auto]`, and `RH.Weather.Set <state> <field> <value>` for live tuning. (3) **Rig:** `ARHArenaLighting` gains a `UVolumetricCloudComponent` running the engine's `m_SimpleVolumetricCloud_Inst` through a dynamic instance (coverage, density, albedo, pattern scale). `/Engine/EngineSky/VolumetricClouds` is added to DirectoriesToAlwaysCook. Sun cloud shadows, light-shaft bloom and volumetric scattering are blended per frame on game time. (4) **Readability guard (Art §3, binding):** the exposure never changes with the weather, so each state's lit floor must stay at or under the overcast baseline, and its median must not drop under 0.6× (the dark Cast-off hulls keep their 7:1 against stone, A1). The binding check is **`Tools/env_contrast.py`** on gameplay-view captures from the same capture mode. Calibrated values: sun 2.6 at 24° gives p50 0.87× / p90 0.87× / p99 0.90× of overcast (2.8 sits on the line, 3.0 fails). The clear sky captures little ambient, so the floor follows the direct sun almost linearly. The blend also caps sun intensity so a drift can never bulge brighter than its endpoints (caught by the test). Tests `RogueHero.Env.Weather.{ReadabilityGuard,OvercastIsArt2,Plan,Lerp}`. (5) **GPU** (RTX 4080, 1080p Epic, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, uncontended, median / p95): overcast 4.85 / 5.83 ms, sun 5.38–5.53 / 6.53 ms (volumetric cumulus ≈ +0.6 ms; the overcast deck ≈ +0.4 ms over the pre-weather 4.46). One first-run p95 spike (12.9 ms) looks like the cloud shader's first use; the 3060 tier and cloud quality scaling are M4. (6) Debug `RH.Env.View <x y z pitch yaw [fov]> | off`: a free camera for wide shots that restores the gameplay camera. Also: `UI/Tests/RHCardRulesTests.cpp` got its missing `StringTableRegistry.h` include (LOCTABLE had only reached it through unity-build neighbours, and the build broke when the new files reshuffled the unity groups).
- 2026-10-19 — **Hero rig reads the A1 Keeper hues.** `ARHHero`'s rig light and tabard no longer carry their own Keeper colours: both read `RHGods::GetColour(Path)` (art book A1, meta-ui ac868d3), and the rig emissive is scaled by `RHGods::GetWorldEmissive(Path)` so every hue clears the 3:1 world contrast (Shade ×3.25, Storm ×1.6 ...). Before a patron exists the rig stays the cool default.
- 2026-10-19 — **Art book scope decisions (designer).** (1) In v0.1 the *in-game ≥ art book* rule covers readability + UI (palette, tracers, fusions, statuses, density LOD, ultimates, telegraphs, HUD, cards, hub layout, sigils, hero silhouette / lamp / sockets); environment, full hero model, enemy detail, hub backdrop and shrine renders → v0.2 (extends valve 7). (2) **Sourcing stays procedural** (no downloads, art-book renders not used as textures). **Deviation:** P24 font families need files → v0.1 keeps the engine composite font with P24's scale / weights / colour duties; swap-out `RHUIStyle::Font`. (3) **Density: the art-book LOD wins over R15's wreckage plan** — debris off above 20 kills/s, haze + aggregate above 60/s; the GDD §3.5 effects-row targets are suspended until re-measured; the R15 live-bullet row and the danmaku weapons stand. (4) **Reclaimer wear on the undersuit + rig only**; armour always new (Art §5, PL.05).
- 2026-10-19 — **Art book follow-ups (designer).** The art-book sky (low sun, volumetric cloud, light shafts) is for the hub / menus / shrine images, v0.2; **combat keeps the overcast dusk** (Art §2: long shadows fight telegraphs, volumetric cloud costs GPU). **Warden Forms II–III look waits for the designer's art-book pages**; their mechanics can be built now on the Form I hull (Roadmap v0.1 additions). **No pilgrims / ritual props in the arena** (the Breach is sealed); ritual belongs to Vigil and the shrines (Art §12).
- 2026-10-19 — **Drop-in threats approved** (designer decision 2026-10-19; balance `Docs/Balance/pressure_proposal.md` P1 on `claude/jovial-satoshi-0eb5d9`; GDD §3.5 / §7). Playtest 3: strong builds kill ~1 000 uu out, past every trigger, so no skill check fired between beats. Telegraphed Courser line / Breaker drop / Pylon perch arrive near the hero and act on arrival; cadence 12 s at 6:00 → 10 s (10:00) → 7 s (15:00) → 5 s (18:00), ×2 in Threat blocks, mix 50/30/20, max 1 active telegraph, ≥ 1.5 s between landings, never in the boss fight; **no HP or telegraph change**. `RH.Stats`: `threat_actions_per_min` (≥ 4 at 10:00, ≥ 8 at 18:00), `nearest_enemy_p10_uu`. Proposal P2–P3 (Courser trigger range, Threat ring) are not part of this decision.
- 2026-10-19 — **Environment (G16 M1): arena floor and near-wall detail layer** (art book v0.3 epic rules + v0.3b, Art §12; in-game ≥ art book from v0.1). All procedural, no external assets: `Tools/env_textures.py` writes the detail textures (stdlib PNG writer) and `Tools/create_environment.py` imports them and generates the new meshes and materials headless (`Tools\CreateEnvironment.bat`). (1) **Floor:** `M_Env_Stone` v2 carries five custom floats per slab (tint, moss, wear, **finish** 0 tooled / 1 pitted / 2 polished, **inscription** row set). Finishes roll 58 / 32 / 10 %. About 38 % of the cells are coursed into halves, quarters or three strips with fitted joints. A ring of polished text panels (r 1 500–1 800) carries incised script about 17 cm tall. Six **sigil roundels** (`SM_Env_Roundel` + `M_Env_Roundel`, r 240, one per Path in Path order on grid corners clear of the trenches) sit flush on polished plinths. Their thin channel glows in the Keeper colour at the conduits' brightness, using the same GetColour × GetWorldEmissive × DisplayedToEmissive logic, so it stays under the bloom threshold. Chipped arrises, rain streaks and wall-foot grime on vertical faces, and lichen on up-facing block tops; every term only darkens. (2) **Near walls** (`BuildWallSide`): coursed ashlar in 12 m bays (plinth, five courses, string course, two courses, dentils, cornice), fluted pilasters at every bay, a niche with a hooded statuette in every other bay, and 22 % collapsed bays with rubble outside. The wall face sits 30 uu out, so the deepest projection (the 40 cornice) ends at the arena bound and nothing enters the playfield. The camera-side (−X) wall is a broken balustrade ≤ 120 uu so it hides no floor. (3) **Readability:** the detail normals are kept shallow (`DetailNormal` 0.25) and the pits tile at 4.2 m, because from the 62° camera a deep normal map read as dark grain (p50 0.47×). With the low sun, sun-facing micro-normals lit the brightest pixels (p99 1.12×). Polished roughness is 0.75 for the same reason. `env_contrast` against the M0 overcast baseline: overcast p50 1.00× / p90 0.97× / p99 0.84×, sun 0.81× / 0.80× / 0.69×: **pass**. **Q5 (telegraphs vs long shadows in low sun):** a Warden charge lane crossing the −Y wall's shadow measures 0.094 linear inside the shadow against the darkest shadowed slab at 0.001–0.002 (well over 3:1), and 0.365 against 0.031 in sun (11.8:1). **No shadow clamp is needed.** Note for the telegraph owner: the lane is lit rather than purely emissive (it dims ~4× in shadow); with the (L+0.05) flare form it reads 2.8:1 in shadow. (4) Fix: the Python `tex()` helper now sets the Masks sampler for TC_MASKS textures. Linear Color failed the material compile, and the engine checker showed in its place. (5) **GPU:** the interleaved A/B (400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, 1080p Epic) ran while three other sessions' games shared the RTX 4080 (57–93 % busy before start), so the absolute numbers are not budget-grade: overcast 10.8–11.0 / p95 12.8–13.3 ms, sun 12.0–12.3 / 14.2 ms on a shared GPU. An uncontended rerun is owed and goes in the M2 line. Tests 284/284, smoke PASS.
- 2026-10-19 — **Environment (G16 M2): the Keep beyond the plate** (art book v0.3 epic rules, Art §12 rule 3: megastructure scale, fog layers for depth, ritual as distant silhouettes). New `Environment/RHArenaBackdrop.cpp` (`ARHArenaEnvironment::BuildBackdrop`, its own random stream so the M1 arena layout does not move). Everything is ≥ 48 m from the centre; the gameplay camera (−62°, FOV 50, 29 m) sees at most ~10 m past the hero, so none of it reaches gameplay frames. (1) **Terraces and pilgrims:** a coping course under the plate rim, then three terraces stepping down 3 m each (15 m deep). Ranks of kneeling hooded **pilgrims** (`SM_Env_Pilgrim`, lathed, 1.1 m, ~400) turn to the sealed Breach. They are static, dark-robed (tint 0.5–0.62), unlit and colourless, cast no shadow, and are never on the playfield (designer 2026-10-19). (2) **Colossi:** four hooded figures 250–350 m tall at the corners, 2.2–3.2 km out, on plinths rising from the fog. Each leans on a staff with one dim lamp. (3) **Storm weather mast** (PL.08): a 300 m lattice mast (`SM_Env_Lattice`, generated legs, X braces, crossarms and rod) on a five-tier ziggurat 3.6 km out ahead of the camera, with crossarm lamps in the Storm colour at the conduits' brightness, plus six relay masts at 1.2–6 km. (4) **Skyline:** 150 broad stepped masses 4–25 km out, log-uniform in distance, each with 1–4 set-back tiers and cornice bands: terraces and ziggurats, not towers. The lower Keep is one plate under the fog sea, so view rays end on stone and fog correctly. Nothing is taller than a third of its distance, so at the 24° sun no shadow can reach the arena. (5) **Fog layers:** `FRHWeatherState` gains `LowFog{Density,HeightFalloff,HeightOffset}` (the height fog's second layer: a fog sea under the plate, 0.06 / 2.5 / −15 m overcast, 0.04 / −18 m sun; it adds ~0.0014 density at the floor) and `FogInscatter`. The engine's default black inscattering left only the sky atmosphere's contribution, which turned the fog sea navy; it is now a dim A1 dusk tint (overcast 0.16 / 0.135 / 0.135, sun warmer 0.19 / 0.135 / 0.10). (6) The backdrop uses `MI_Env_Stone_Backdrop` (lichen 0.12) with moss ≈ 0, because `M_Env_Stone`'s wall-foot term treats every vertical face below Z 90 as a wall foot and turned every riser olive. Footings start at −60 m, not deeper: huge non-uniform block scales gave software Lumen black terrace tops. **Readability:** overcast vs the M0 baseline p50 1.03× / p90 1.01× / p99 0.86× (the fog tint adds ~3 % haze; inside the guard). Sun vs overcast on the same empty floor view: 0.80× / 0.83× / 1.00× (p99 is the same fixed bright object in both). `env_contrast`'s p99 on the hero-centred shot is polluted by how many pale Swarm hulls are near the hero at capture time (a sun shot read 2.5× with the floor unchanged), so the per-state check now also uses a fixed gameplay-angle view over empty floor (`RH.Env.View -2861 -1700 2560 -62 0 50`). Tests 284/284, smoke PASS. **GPU:** the uncontended M0–M2 A/B runs in the scheduler's quiet window and is logged here as a follow-up line.
- 2026-10-19 — **GPU, G16 M0–M2, uncontended** (the scheduler held every other session's launches; no other Unreal process; GPU 5–6 % busy before each run). RTX 4080, 1080p Epic, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets; GPU median / p95 ms over two interleaved rounds. **Overcast:** grey box (`-RHNoEnv`) 4.81 / 5.76, M1 arena (`-RHNoBackdrop`, new A/B switch) 5.00 / 5.95, full with the M2 backdrop 5.05 / 6.00. **Sun:** grey box 5.43–5.52 / 6.41–6.53, arena 5.67 / 6.70, full 5.73 / 6.75. So the M1 detail layer costs ≈ +0.2 ms, the whole M2 backdrop ≈ +0.05 ms (Nanite, mostly fogged), and the sun state ≈ +0.7 ms over overcast (volumetric cumulus + scattering). The game thread is 2.9–3.0 ms in every case. Both states sit at about half the 12 ms Epic budget. This supersedes the contended M1 numbers above. The 3060 tier (cloud quality, volumetric fog, VSM) is M4.
- 2026-10-19 — **Environment (G16 M3a): Vigil, the hub backdrop** (art book PL.23, Art §8, §12 rule 3). New `ARHHubEnvironment` (`Environment/RHHubEnvironment.*`, about 4 000 Nanite ISM instances, fixed seed). **Hook:** the hub session adds `ARHHubEnvironment::Spawn(World, HubCamera)` at the end of `ARHHubGameMode::BuildBackdrop`, the agreed one-line hook (no map edits). It hides the hub's grey-box wall and floor, sets the hub camera (−30 m, 15 m up, pitch 12°, FOV 70) and does nothing with `-RHNoEnv`. **Scene:** the 360 m Keep wall 300 m away has a coursed ashlar face, three string courses, four stepped tower buttresses with cap bands and pilaster strips, and pilasters with thin Keeper-colour seams (the 10 % sci-fi). The **six sigils** sit in Path order in stone rings at 85 m, so the composition puts them in the clear middle band of meta-ui's PL.23 UI (≈ 48 % down, below the portrait strip). Below them: the closed gate (dark recess, jambs, lintel, two metal leaves), two 45 m hooded **colossi** with swords, niches with statuettes along the foot, and the **shrine**, a stepped altar with six steles, each lamp in its Keeper's colour, plus two braziers. In front, **the town** steps down toward the camera (22 cm per 2.2 m strip, so the view runs over the roofs as in PL.23): stone houses with gabled roofs (a block turned 45°) and lit amber windows, and the **procession**, kneeling pilgrims in two files up the stepped avenue with candles. Warm light is allowed here: Vigil is not combat, and Art §3 governs the arena. The windows are deep amber at 0.8× the conduit level, because brighter amber tonemapped to white under PL.23's 70 % bottom gradient. All `T_Env_*` textures are now **never-stream**: they are small, and a streamed sigil read as a blur in the hub and on the arena roundels. Tests 296/296, smoke PASS. **Follow-ups:** the hub world is paused under the menu, so `RH.Weather` does not visibly change the hub yet (M4: tick the rig on real time in the hub). The shrine portraits for `URHPatronPortrait::Body` are M3b.
- 2026-10-19 — **Environment (G16 M3b): Shrine portraits** (art book PL.23: "the portraits are crops of the six infrastructure renders"; PL.08–13). New `ARHShrineDiorama` (`Environment/RHShrineDiorama.*`), a render rig, not level content: `RH.Env.Shrines` spawns the six shrines 4 km out over the fog sea, `RH.Env.ShrineView <0-5>` frames one, and `RH.Env.Shot <file> noui` captures it without UI. Captures run in the arena map, because the hub world is paused under its menu, so its camera and weather do not update. The shrines are: **Storm**, a 90 m lattice weather mast on a five-tier ziggurat with crossarm lamps. **Forge**, a furnace temple with a portico, twin stacks with ember mouths, the amber furnace mouth and the empty assembly table. **Tide**, the six-tier octagonal well stepping down to still water, with cyan rim seams. **Shade**, the ledger drum, ringed with rows, one row black, and a violet reader line. **Radiance**, a heliostat field of mirrors turned to a tower with a pale-gold receiver. **Quicksilver**, a viaduct on tall arches with one lime relay capsule. Each shrine also has two steles with its sigil, braziers and kneeling pilgrims. All are rendered in the sun state. `Tools/shrine_portraits.py` crops the centred 4:5 into `Docs/Art/ShrineRenders/T_Shrine_<Path>.png` (400 × 500). `CreateEnvironment.bat` imports them as UI textures `/Game/RH/Environment/Shrines/T_Shrine_<Path>` (sRGB, UI group, no mips, never stream). **Swap-out (meta-ui):** `URHPatronPortrait::Body` shows `T_Shrine_<Path>` (the live sigil can stay as an overlay). **Gap vs the art book:** these are procedural block-outs of the PL.08–13 plates. They have the plates' silhouettes and lights, but not their rain, lightning, crowds or relief density. A detail pass on the six shrines is owed (G16 M4, or the art-book page drop); re-running the rig and `shrine_portraits.py` refreshes the textures. Tests 296/296, smoke PASS.
- 2026-10-19 — **Environment (G16 M4a): shrine detail pass to the PL.08–13 plates** (scheduler: required, at least as detailed as the plates). `ARHShrineDiorama` is rebuilt to the plates. It uses seven new procedural meshes (`SM_Env_RingWall`, `SM_Env_ColumnFluted` with base, 20 flutes and capital, `SM_Env_Icicle`, `SM_Env_MirrorFrame`, `SM_Env_SunDisc`, `SM_Env_Feather`, `SM_Env_Brazier`) and `M_Env_Mirror`. All six share paved plazas, crowds of 140–650 kneeling and standing pilgrims with candles, tripod braziers, banners with the Keeper medallion, embers or sparks, and a few point lights per shrine (in candelas tuned for the fixed 0.62 exposure; the first pass was ~60× too bright). **Storm:** an ashlar five-tier ziggurat with pilasters, cornices and corner braziers, the great medallion over the door, a 120 m guyed lattice mast with lamps, two lightning bolts and rain, and an avenue of tall dark steles with medallions. **Forge:** an ashlar temple front with a round-headed opening cut through the courses, a lit portcullis before the furnace glow, the medallion above, engaged fluted columns, entablature, hooded colossi with staves, niche statues, the round dais with the empty table, and a ring of braziers. **Tide:** the round six-tier well with ring walls, ledges, fluted galleries and frost lights, cyan rings in the still water, a rim balustrade with urn columns, and pilgrims round it. **Shade:** a hypostyle nave of 9.6 m fluted columns with beams, cell walls (lit records), and the ringed ledger drum with lit rows, one row dark, a violet reader line and violet light. **Radiance:** a stepped ashlar tower, the spiked sun-disc receiver and its beam, a heliostat field of mullioned mirrors facing the tower, a stele avenue, and distant stepped towers. **Quicksilver:** the winged hooded colossus (two 15-feather fans with coverts), the two-tier arcaded viaduct with finials and lime deck lights, the gate of four green portals, and water. Rendered in the overcast (dusk) state and re-cropped into `T_Shrine_<Path>` (same asset paths, so meta-ui's hookup is unchanged). **Remaining gaps vs the plates:** no animated lightning or rain (the portraits are stills), and the colossi reuse the lathed statuette (no modelled drapery folds). - 2026-10-19 — **Weather (G16 M4b): hub weather on real time; environment/weather per scalability tier; contrast on every tier.** (1) `ARHArenaLighting` ticks through pauses (`bTickEvenWhenPaused`). Vigil's paused menu world now gets its weather (overcast and sun verified under the PL.23 UI). The arena is unchanged, because its weather follows the run clock, which stops while a run is paused. (2) **Tier split:** the tier owner keeps `RH.Scalability` and `DefaultScalability.ini` (the engine groups). The environment applies only its own costs, from code, when `RHScalability::GetCurrentTier()` changes: `FRHEnvTier` / `RHEnvTiers::For`. Epic: clouds on, 768 cloud ray samples, full cloud shadow and shafts. High (the 3060 target): 384 samples. Medium: 256 samples, no cloud shadow, half shafts. Low: no volumetric cloud, no shafts. Volumetric fog stays on the engine's EffectsQuality defaults (no overlap with the tier owner). (3) **Readability fix:** measured on a fixed empty-floor gameplay view, the lower engine tiers lit the floor far brighter at the same sky intensity: overcast p50 High 1.85×, Medium 1.70×, Low 1.62×; sun up to 1.71×. The cheaper paths lose Lumen's sky occlusion, so the §3 contract (tracer core / lit stone ≥ 3.9:1) was broken on the 3060 tiers. `FRHEnvTier::SkyScale` = 0.25 on High / Medium / Low restores it. After the fix, against the Epic overcast baseline (p50 / p90): High overcast 0.99 / 0.91, sun 0.77 / 0.74; Medium 1.00 / 0.91 and 0.84 / 0.77; Low 1.02 / 0.91 and 0.97 / 0.88; Epic 0.99 / 0.97 and 0.76 / 0.75. All pass. Test `RogueHero.Env.Weather.TierTable`. The per-tier GPU cost (High = 3060 proxy on the 4080) is owed and runs in the next quiet GPU window. Tests 298/298, BuildNonUnity OK, smoke PASS.

- 2026-10-19 — **GPU per tier × weather, G16 M4 close** (quiet-GPU window, no other Unreal process, GPU 0–5 % busy before each run except the first at 23 %). RTX 4080, 1080p, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, the full environment (M1 arena, M2 backdrop), GPU median / p95 ms over two interleaved rounds. **Epic:** overcast 4.96–5.21 / 6.23–6.48, sun 5.59–5.83 / 6.99–7.42. **High (the 3060 target):** overcast 3.17–3.47 / 4.46–4.85, sun 4.02–4.11 / 5.17–5.20. **Medium:** overcast 2.40–2.60 / 3.87–4.24, sun 2.41 / 3.89. All are well under TDD §14 (12 ms Epic). On the 4080, High's ~4 ms leaves about 3× headroom for a 3060-class GPU; the weapons session's 3060 rounds are the binding measurement.

- 2026-10-19 — **G9 type (art book PL.24): role-based type + colour duties; OFL faces on engine stand-ins.** `RHUIStyle::Type(ERHType)` is the P24 11-step scale at 1080p (Title64, Timer52, Header44, Level40, Crit32, CardName26, Damage22, KeeperLine17, Body15, Rule13, Tag11) with family, weight and tracking per step; four code-built composite families (`ERHTypeFamily` Numbers / Body / System / Inscription), each with a CJK sub-font in every culture. **Deviation (asset, no downloads):** the five OFL families (Barlow Condensed, Noto Sans SC, IBM Plex Mono, Marcellus SC, Noto Serif SC) are not in the repo; each family loads its files from `Content/RH/Fonts/` when present (staged NonUFS) and otherwise runs on an engine stand-in (Roboto Light/Regular/Medium/Bold, DroidSansMono, DroidSansFallback for CJK) — swap-out point: drop the files there, no code change (`RHUIStyle::IsFamilyStandIn`). Inscription has no serif stand-in in the engine (Roboto until Marcellus SC / Noto Serif SC land). Legacy `RHUIStyle::Font(face, size)` now snaps to the nearest step (`SnapToScale`), so every existing text sits on the scale. Colour duties: `TextMain` = bone #E8E4DC, `TextDim` = 55 % bone (opaque equivalent), `CritGlow` #FFF8DC, `FocusEdge` #EEEAE2 (P23 focused frame; bone failed 3:1 on the brightest dimmed backdrop by 0.02), P24 panel-state constants (`PanelP24*`, `LockedOpacity` 0.45, `FocusLift` 14) for G10/G12; test `RogueHero.UI.TypeScale` also asserts no magenta / red in the UI palette. Applied first to the run summary (title Inscription, stats Numbers).
- 2026-10-19 — **Art book G7 (PL.16): telegraph timings and sequence.** **Breaker:** `SlamWindUp` 1.0 -> **1.2 s** (longer, never shorter) and the ring now **fills inside -> out** (`AddRingFill`: thin rim at `SlamRadius` + concentric bands growing to the rim, ease-out `RHEnemy::BreakerFillFront`; the slam lands when the fill reaches the rim); P1 drop-in slam circles use the same fill. **Courser:** the 1.0 s wind-up runs headlamp -> ground arrow: for the first 30 % (`RHEnemy::CourserHeadlampShare`) only the status light swells red, then the red ground **arrow** (shaft at exactly the charge length + a 30-deg arrowhead, `AddArrow`) ramps in and widens to the charge; Heat-4 chained lines and P1 drop-in lines draw the same arrow for their whole telegraph (no hull yet). **Pylon:** a red aim line to the hero shows while it aims and **flashes three times over the last 0.30 s** (`RHEnemy::PylonAimLineOn`, 0.05 s halves) before the 3-round burst; the aim itself stays 0.75 s (the 2026-10-19 telegraph floor) — the art book's 0.30 s is the flash window, not a shorter aim. Scrapwings have no telegraph (unchanged). Test `RogueHero.Enemies.TelegraphSequences`. DA_Enemy_Bruiser re-baked. Also a one-line build fix in `UI/Tests/RHCardRulesTests.cpp` (missing `Internationalization/StringTableRegistry.h` for `LOCTABLE`; it compiled only through unity grouping).
- 2026-10-19 — **Art book G4 (PL.17): statuses as "one hull, four zones".** `URHEnemySubsystem::AddStatusVfx` rebuilt by zone, each effect on its own part of the hull: **edge = Shock** (1-2 arcs of two kinked chords crawling along the hull rim, re-struck every 0.1 s); **top plate = Burn or Chill** (Burn: 2-3 flame tongues at 12 Hz plus rising embers; Chill: frost cap by stack -> Freeze ice sheath at 3 stacks -> **6 ice shards on death**; **fire melts frost**: Burn takes the plate); **midline = Mark** (a 2 uu violet outline round the hull + an entry box on the front that **closes as HP nears the execute threshold**; execution = the hull vanishing, no blood); **sensor = Blind** (0.5 s overexposure flare + a 4-point star, the only effect over the status light). Priority sensor > midline > top > edge. **Density LOD** (`bStatusLOD` = the ImpactFX dense state > 20 kills/s, or > 120 Statused hulls): only sensor + midline stay drawn; edge and top become a **hull tint** through **4 ISM custom-data channels** on the enemy hull ISMs (0 edge/Shock, 1 top/Burn +, Chill -, 2 midline/Mark, 3 sensor/Blind; `ARHInstanceRenderer::SetNumCustomData / SyncCustomData`, re-uploading only changed instances) read by `M_Unit` (`PerInstanceCustomData` 0-3 x zone colours x `StatusTintIntensity` 1.5, added to emissive; 0 = no tint, so hero / props are unchanged). Tint channel values are kept low (0.04-0.08) so a tinted Scrapwing stays below the bloom threshold and its warm status light still reads (a first pass at 0.3-1.0 washed the crowd out to white). `M_Unit` patched in place by `Tools/build_unit_models.py master` (`patch_status_tint`, idempotent; `DeleteAllMaterialExpressions` asserts on the existing asset in 5.8, so the full rebuild path is for a fresh asset only). Screens in the shared notes (`g4_status_zones.png`, `g4_status_lod_tint.png`). Not measured: status VFX cost at 400 Statused enemies after the rework (the LOD path draws fewer instances than before).

Damage-number aggregation and crowd audio of PL.18 are their owners' (HUD / audio). **GDD §3.5 "hit + kill effects on screen" target suspended** until balance re-measures: the metric stays (`GetLiveEffectCount`, RH.Stats, the `ImpactFX: minute N` log) but nothing gates on it. Measured on this tiering (3 steered RH.Bot v2 runs, RH.God, Quicksilver / Storm / Forge, seeds 1101-1103): per-minute average live effects **24-25 / 59-79 / 83-112** at minutes 2 / 10 / 18 (peaks 65-85 / 183-212 / 248-299). - 2026-10-19 — **G10 cards as procedural stone tablets (art book PL.21 / PL.22), static windows.** `UI/Cards/RHCardSlab` bakes every texture in C++ at 2× (no assets, no downloads): one slab per rarity stone — Common weathered limestone (pits, hairline cracks, worn edges, lichen), Rare fine stone (veins, bronze inlay), Epic basalt (speckle, gold inlay, light-channel groove) — lit from the art book’s top-left light off a height field (chamfer, raised frame band, window moulding, polished rules face, recessed tag / stud slots), the arched window cut to alpha; box-filtered mips. Static window pictures per kind: cool tracers (NewWeapon; Upgrade adds rising chevrons), a socketed part (Modifier), the Keeper’s medallion with its channel lit and rotated 60° per Keeper (Blessing / Fusion with a lance / Legendary with rays / GodChoice) — the medallion is the swap-out point for G11’s sigil. `URHOfferCardButton` (now `UI/Cards/RHOfferCardButton.cpp`) lays text on the zones in the stone’s ink (incised / bronze / gilded, contrast-tested ≥ 3:1 against the **baked** pixels in `RogueHero.UI.CardSlab`), markers + Keeper designation in the dark mono system slot, bronze progress studs (new ones breathe); Rare bronze sweep 4.5 s, Epic gold sweep 3.2 s + channel breathing in the Keeper hue + a rim light circling in 5 s + six rising motes; P24 focus (lift 14, bone edge, glow in the content hue). L8 commitments (GodChoice) are small steles: medallion + honorific + one line. **Deviations:** (1) the plaque carries the KIND colour + glyph (playtest-1 “kinds must read at a glance”), not the rarity metal — rarity is the stone + gem; (2) the tablet is 270×476 design (art book 440 + 36 px rules face) because long blessing rules shrank below legibility in zh (Art §3); (3) Fusion stays a vertical basalt tablet (a horizontal stele would break the five-card row); (4) the Epic channel breathes instead of flowing (a flowing mask needs a material; v0.2 with live windows); (5) no `T_CardSlab_*` asset set — `RHCardSlab::Slab / Window` are the swap-out points.

- 2026-10-19 — **G11 Keeper sigils, procedural (art book PL.07).** `UI/Sigils/RHSigil` composes each sigil from signed-distance primitives (cull grid; all six reliefs + card + HUD forms bake in ≈ 0.4 s) and lights the height field from the art book light: Storm weather mast + thunderclouds + two bolts; Forge furnace arch with fire, anvil, hammer and tongs; Tide tiered well with lit water, curling wave, frost crystal; Shade ledger drum with the one black row, moth, reading line; Radiance sun, heliostat field, Vigil; Quicksilver pod head-on, twin wings, rails and relay chain. Founder circuit traces under every relief; the incised stone ring carries a per-Keeper frieze at the sides, six ports and the channel cut through it at 60° + 60°·Path. Forms: `Relief` 512 (ring; menus / Shrine / Codex), `Card` 256 (static frame, glows baked in the Keeper colour; now in the Keeper card windows, replacing the G10 medallion), `Hud` 96 silhouette mask (tint at draw, 22–48 px). `URHSigilWidget` stacks Base + three glow layers (channel / traces / facility) on the PL.07 6 s cycle (`CycleLevels`) with a 16 s skylight round the ring, and cuts the honorific (top) and designation (bottom) into the ring as per-glyph text on the arc (FText, en + zh); the Codex reader opens Keeper entries on the live sigil. **Deviation / gap:** procedural reliefs are stylised and less detailed than the art book’s rendered sigils (A4 calls those references; matching them needs the `T_Sigil_<Path>_Relief` H/N/ORM/E 2K set — swap-out point `RHSigil::Texture`); Shade’s “goes dark when another Keeper wakes” beat is not in v0.1.

- 2026-10-19 — **G14 M3: enemy-role LODs and performance.** Hand-built LOD chain per role from the same builders (`build_<role>(lod)`): **LOD0** full art-book detail, **LOD1** with the fine kit parts off (bolts, panel seams, glyphs, moss, cable clamps, rotor spokes; kit switch `FINE`), **LOD2** the base silhouette without the detail pass. Tris LOD0 / 1 / 2: Scrapwing 912 / 840 / 512, Bulwark 5 972 / 2 656 / 1 340, Courser 3 300 / 3 040 / 1 460, Flare Pylon 2 524 / 1 616 / 936, Breaker 4 164 / 3 072 / 1 800, Tallyman 2 296 / 1 824 / 1 108. **Screen sizes 1.0 / 0.025 / 0.012**: at the gameplay camera (TDD §13) units are ~0.04-0.09, so play shows LOD0; LOD1 / 2 take over when zoomed out (lineups, hub, boss camera). Nanite stays off (Art §7), ISM unchanged. Tooling: `Tools/BuildEnemyModels.bat` = commandlet pass (meshes + LOD0-2 via `build_from_static_mesh_descriptions` with one description per LOD) then a short off-screen editor pass (`screensizes`) that sets the LOD screen sizes — `UStaticMeshEditorSubsystem::SetLods / SetLodScreenSizes` refuse to run in a commandlet (`CheckIfInEditorAndPIE`), and rebuilding meshes in an editor process with live render resources crashes (`FRenderResource deleted without being released`), hence the split. **Measured** (`L_StressTest`, `-RHStressMix=2` all six roles, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, RH.God, 1 200 frames after 15 s warm-up, 1920x1080 off-screen, Epic, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, warm runs): GPU median **8.52 ms old meshes / 8.81 new without LODs / 8.02 new with LODs** (p95 10.65 / 11.04 / 10.54), game thread 4.0-4.9 ms — the detail pass is inside run-to-run noise and well under the 12 ms GPU / 8 ms GT budget. A first cold run (shader compile) read 16.8 ms and was discarded. Not resolved: `r.StaticMeshLODDistanceScale 4` measured *slower* (12.6-13.1 ms GPU median, twice), probably the environment's static meshes changing LOD too, with other sessions' editors on the GPU; so the 3060 tier should not use that cvar for enemy LODs until it is profiled alone. Not measured on a 4070-class GPU.

Coverage audit `Tools/font_coverage.py` (fontTools; every Game.po msgstr + ST EN source vs. each family’s face → CJK sub-font → composite fallback): **0 tofu** — 769 zh-Hans and 97 en characters covered in every weight; the UI symbols the Latin faces lack (← ↑ → ↓ ▲ ◆ ◇ ◈ ○ ● ★) render from the composite fallback (DroidSansFallback). - 2026-10-19 — **`threat_actions_per_min` counts only threats aimed at the hero (hub LoopCheck fix).** The P4 row read 60–256 / min because `GetThreatActionCount()` counts every wind-up in the arena. `RH.Stats` now counts an action only if it is a drop-in, within `URHRunStats::ThreatRange` (1 400 uu) of the hero, or on screen (projected through the player camera; headless runs have no viewport and use the range alone) — `URHRunStats::IsThreatAimedAtHero`, pure. It also counts the executed action (a wind-up that completes while the enemy lives: charge starts, slam lands, burst fires), not a wind-up cut short by a kill, per the P4 definition; the log shows the split (charges / slams / Pylon aims). **Interim:** this runs on RunStats' own wind-up scan; switch-over point is statuses' per-action ring `URHEnemySubsystem::GetThreatAction(Index, {Pos, bDropIn})` (requested; detected at compile time in `RunStatsAimedThreats`). Steered bot after the fix: ~124–163 / min at 10:00 (mostly Courser charges within range), 16–36 at 15:00–18:00; targets (>= 4 / >= 8) are minimums, so the remaining mid-game count is real pressure, not a metric artefact. **Final (ring live, statuses 1b07bb5 + 902da77: entries on execution — charge start, slam landing, burst), main 6486f6f, 2 steered god-mode runs:** 102 / 109 at 10:00, 69 / 82 at 15:00, 55 / 90 at 18:00. Test `RogueHero.Debug.RunStats.Pressure`.

- 2026-10-19 — **3060-class tier verified with all of today's art in (CLAUDE.md rule 9, TDD §14), uncontended; Low raised from 50 % to 67 %.** Scene: L_Arena_Test, 400 mixed enemies (10 % elites, every status) + 5 000 bullets held, Storm + Shade imbues (accents and core tint), **sun** weather (env measured it as the costlier one), and three ultimates fired during the sample (Tide wall, Storm, Quicksilver Time Dilation with the masked grade). The HUD and the Reclaimer wear are on. RTX 4080, 1920×1080 output (`r.SetRes 1920x1080w -ForceRes`), `-game -RenderOffscreen`, `RH.Scalability <Tier>`, `-RHStressProfile=1200`. The scheduler held every other launch; each run was gated on GPU < 30 % with no foreign game running. Results: 3 interleaved rounds (Low@67: 2 rounds), median of the round medians, range in brackets, ms:

  | Tier (profile) | Screen % | GPU median | GPU p95 | Frame median | Game thread median | RTX 3060 proxy, GPU median (×2.5–3.2) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | Epic (`RH_Epic`) | 100 | 5.64 (5.57–5.89) | 7.49 | 6.03 | 4.83 | — (4070 ≈ ×1.5 = 8.5) |
  | High (`RH_3060`) | 75 | 3.91 (3.87–3.96) | 5.89 | 4.66 | 4.66 | 9.8–12.5 |
  | Medium (`RH_3060_Medium`) | 70 | 3.03 (2.96–3.08) | 5.10 | 4.48 | 4.48 | **7.6–9.7** |
  | Low | 67 (was 50: 2.10) | 2.43 (2.39–2.46) | 4.42 | 4.49 | 4.48 | 6.0–7.8 |

  **Proxy method (no RTX 3060 here):** the 4080 GPU time is multiplied by the raster-throughput ratio. ×2.5 is the factor used before in this log; ×3.2 is a conservative 3DMark Time Spy ratio (≈ 28 k / 8.8 k). This is an estimate, not a measurement. The game thread is CPU-bound and is not scaled. **Medium meets the 3060 budget at both factors** (GPU median ≤ 12 ms, p95 12.8–16.3 ms, inside the 16.6 ms frame; game thread ≤ 4.9 ms ≤ 8 ms). **High meets it only at ×2.5** (12.5 ms at ×3.2), so `RH_3060_Medium` is the guaranteed 3060 profile and `RH_3060` (High) is the "quality" option. The game thread rose from the 2.0–2.2 ms measured earlier to 4.4–4.9 ms: this run adds statuses, imbues and ultimates. **Readability per tier** (captures `rd_<Tier>`, Art §3): player tracers, the warm enemy rims, the telegraphs and the status lights read the same on Epic, High and Medium. At **Low 50 %** the thin early-state telegraph lines broke into dashes under TSR. At **67 %** they are continuous for +0.3 ms on the 4080, so `PerfIndexValues_ResolutionQuality` is now `"67 70 75 100 100"`, and `RHScalabilityTests` asserts the 67 % floor on every tier. **No ShadowQuality / GI / Reflection deltas were needed**: the engine levels plus env's per-tier cloud/sky split (105d0c7) fit, and GI is unchanged, so env's `env_contrast.py` needs no re-run. Env's own window (overcast + sun, same scene minus ultimates): Epic 5.0–5.8 ms, High 3.2–4.1 ms, Medium 2.4–2.6 ms, consistent with this run.

That gives G/R 0.45 (target ≤ 0.6) and still blooms, with lane-to-floor contrast ≥ 3:1. - 2026-10-19 — **G14 M6a: Vertex Animation Textures, Scrapwing pilot** (TDD call: Swarm / Tank / Rusher are ISM + VAT, art book PL.C17). `Tools/build_enemy_models.py` builds each VAT role 32 times at `ANIM` = f / 32 per LOD; the Scrapwing animates two counter-rotating rotors (2 turns per loop), the anemometer cups (1 turn) and the vane swing. Every corner of a moving patch gets a texel. Its local offset and normal per frame go into two frame-major atlases shared by all VAT roles and LODs: `/Game/RH/Models/VAT/T_VAT_Offset` and `T_VAT_Normal`, 512 × 96 RGBA32F, uncompressed, nearest, no mips. Frame f of texel n is at (n % 512, f · rows + n / 512), so the material reads UV1 + (0, f / 32) with no per-role parameters. Texel 0 is static (zero offset, normal alpha 0). The texel goes in **UV1** (full-precision UVs). The Python mesh-description API cannot add a UV channel, hence `URHVatTools::SetUVChannelCount` (editor tooling). `M_Unit` is patched in place (`patch_vat_material`, idempotent): WPO = lerp of the two frames' offsets × `VATScale` (128 cm) × the VAT switch, local → world. The normal is sampled in the vertex shader and passed through a vertex interpolator, then local → tangent; alpha 0 keeps the mesh normal, so non-VAT users of `M_Unit` (other roles, Warden, props) render unchanged. Per instance: `PerInstanceCustomData` 4 = frame (0..32), 5 = on. The code (`URHEnemySubsystem::Render`) advances `VatPhase` by time (`ERHVatClock::Time`, `VatCycle` 0.4 s for the Swarm) or by distance moved (`Distance`, for the Courser / Bulwark in M6b), spread per instance. These are new `FRHEnemyStats` fields; `DA_Enemy_*` are patched in place (`build_enemy_models.py stats`). `RH.Enemy.VAT 0` draws the rest pose (perf A/B, low tier). ISM / 400-enemy path unchanged. **Gate** (`L_StressTest -RHStressMix=1`, Swarm / Tank / Rusher, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, 1 200 frames after 15 s, 1080p off-screen, Epic, RTX 4080): GPU median **VAT on 4.20 / 4.13 ms vs off 4.30 / 4.20 / 4.20** (p95 ≤ 5.75), game thread 3.5–4.6 ms. No measurable cost, far under 12 / 8 ms. One run read 14.0 ms GPU while another session's editor was up (PID not ours) and is discarded. Not measured on a 4070-class GPU. Known: `BuildEnemyModels.bat` pass 2 returns an access-violation code at editor shutdown after its work is saved.

Lamp states (breathing while the ultimate charges, off for the dash i-frames) untouched.
