Frames `Docs/Playtests/2026-10-19_human_4_rig_light_before_after.png`.
- 2026-10-19 — **G14 M6b: VAT for the Courser and the Bulwark** (same pipeline as M6a, `ERHVatClock::Distance`). **Courser**: an alternating-tripod gait (front + rear of one side with the middle of the other). In stance the foot slides back 16 cm under the body; in swing it lifts 10 cm and returns, with the knee following. One loop = 32 cm travelled (`VatCycle` 32). **Bulwark**: the ground run of track links slides back one 7 cm link pitch per loop (`VatCycle` 7), and the road-wheel lugs make a quarter turn per loop (LOD0 only; LOD1 / 2 are rigid). The phase advance is capped at 0.3 loop per frame, so a charging Courser or a knocked-back hull blurs its gait instead of strobing. Atlas: 7 561 texels, 512 × 480 RGBA32F per texture, peak offset 17 cm. `DA_Enemy_Tank` / `DA_Enemy_Rusher` were patched in place. **Gate** (quiet GPU, after balance and hub paused; same setup as M6a, M7 surface pass included): GPU median **VAT on 4.22 / 3.88 ms vs off 3.76 / 3.87 ms** (p95 ≤ 5.27), game thread 3.5 ms. Cost ≤ 0.4 ms, inside run-to-run noise, far under 12 / 8 ms. An earlier attempt read 8.9–10.6 ms for both on and off while two other editors held the GPU (66 % busy at idle) and was discarded.
- 2026-10-19 — **G14 M7: Cast-off surface pass + real LOD2** (art book PL.C01 / C17: one shared hard-surface material with the rust, moss and chip masks fully on). `M_Unit` gains three procedural wear masks after its existing rust / grime. They are patched in place (`patch_surface_material`, idempotent), and every new parameter defaults to 0, so the Warden, props and the kernel's `MI_Unit_*` are unchanged:
  - **chip** = thresholded T_Noise01 at `ChipScale` → `PrimerColor` (dark primer);
  - **streak** = noise stretched down the box-projected UVs, on vertical faces only → `StreakColor` (rust bleed);
  - **moss** = noise × below `MossHeight` (25 cm) × upward faces → `MossColor`.

  New Cast-off-only instances use the book's swatches (linear): `MI_Enemy_Ceramic` hw #D8D2C5 (chips 0.24, streak 0.30, moss 0.32), `MI_Enemy_Metal` dark metal #4A4A52 (streak 0.22, moss 0.18) and `MI_Enemy_Rust` #7A4A2E (moss 0.25). They remap the `Ceramic` / `Metal` / `Rust` slots for the six roles and the affixes. The chips were first at 0.34, which read as uniform dots, then cut to 0.24. Not done: stencils and Founder script bands as decals (the glyph geometry stays), and rivet-specific rust bleed (the streaks are procedural, not placed under each rivet). **LOD2** is now a real cut: every part under 6 cm in all axes is dropped (`lod2_trim`, decided on the rest frame and applied to every VAT frame). LOD tris 0 / 1 / 2: Scrapwing 1 420 / 1 232 / 748, Bulwark 5 544 / 3 624 / 2 452, Courser 3 612 / 2 136 / 1 956, Pylon 2 252 / 1 432 / 1 196, Breaker 4 344 / 3 280 / 2 976, Tallyman 2 128 / 1 644 / 1 512.
- 2026-10-19 — **G14 M8: stencils, Founder-script bands and per-rivet rust bleed as decals** (art book PL.C01 family language, C02–C13). A decal is a 2-triangle quad 0.15 cm proud of its surface, in its own material slot on the new masked `M_EnemyDecal` (Cast-off-only `MI_Enemy_DecalInk` / `MI_Enemy_DecalBleed`). It is UV-mapped into `/Game/RH/Models/Decals/T_Enemy_Decals` (1024², BC masks: R ink, G wear, B rust). The atlas is generated by `build_enemy_models.py` from env's script (`env_textures.make_alphabet`, the same seed as `T_Env_Glyphs`), so hull bands and arena inscriptions share one invented script. The atlas holds four script bands with framing lines, the fret, bridged stencil glyphs, chevrons, arrows, warning panels, entry-number plates (six dots + a number, as on the seals) and rust streaks / halos.
  - **Placements per role:** wing plate + band + tail chevron (Scrapwing); gantry band, hull fret, side chevrons / plates / bands (Bulwark); capsule band + fret, route-plate number, flank stencil (Courser); turntable band, mast panel, vertical mast script (Pylon); two boiler bands + fret, side plate, brow chevrons (Breaker); eight drawer labels, side plates and bands (Tallyman). Every Ichor seal gets its six-dot plate.
  - **Per-rivet rust bleed:** ~60 % of the rivets in `armor_plate` / `bolt_row` bleed — a world-down streak on vertical faces, a halo on faces looking up, none on faces looking down.
  - **Scope and cost:** decals are LOD0 only and gated by `DECALS`, which only this script's `main` sets, so balance's Warden build keeps its slot set; `RH_NODECALS=1` bakes without them for A/B. Cost: +8–88 tris and +1–2 material sections per role at LOD0. **GPU** (quiet, balance and hub paused; `L_StressTest -RHStressMix=2`, all six roles, 400 + 5 000, 1080p Epic, RTX 4080): median **decals 4.08 / 4.23 ms vs none 3.85 / 3.89 ms** (≈ +0.3 ms, p95 ≤ 5.62), game thread unchanged (3.7–4.1 ms).
  - **Side-by-side sheets** against the C-pages: `g14_m8_<Role>_vs_art.png`. Gaps flagged there go to **M9**: dense panel lines / machined plating on every surface, the Bulwark stele's larger, deeper glyphs and rough stone, and denser rivets on Scrapwing / Courser / Pylon / Tallyman.
- 2026-10-19 — **Enemy scale stays at the GDD sizes** (designer decision, gap (2) of the G14 M8 sheets): the Cast-off boards draw units ~1.5–2× larger next to the hero; the collision radii and meshes keep the GDD §6 sizes, and the boards are read for design, not scale.
- 2026-10-19 — **G14 M9: machined plating, the stele, denser rivets** (closes gaps (1), (3) and (4) of the M8 sheets).
  - **Plating:** `T_Enemy_Panels` / `_N` (1024², tileable, generated by `build_enemy_models.py`): 78 recursive panel splits with V-cut grooves, vent slots, hatch rings, inset panels and corner bolts. They feed an in-place `M_Unit` detail layer on the box-projected UV0 × `DetailTiling` (2 → a 50 cm tile): the grooves darken the base colour, and the normal is whiteout-blended with the existing (VAT) normal. `DetailAmount` defaults to 0, so the Warden, props and the kernel's `MI_Unit_*` are unchanged. The Cast-off instances set it: ceramic 1.0, dark metal 0.6, rust 0.3, stone 1.0.
  - **Stele:** `MI_Enemy_Stone` swaps in env's `T_Env_StoneDetail` / `T_Env_Pitted_N` (read-only) for rough stone. The four names grow to 25 cm glyphs, 1.6 cm deep, on dressed panels.
  - **Rivets:** `rivet()` (any orientation, with the M8 rust bleed) adds rows on the Scrapwing's wing roots and spine, rings by the Courser's panel bands, the Pylon's mast faces and turntable, and the Tallyman's cabinet edges.
  - **New LOD0 budgets** (scheduler call: trim or measure): Scrapwing ≤ 1 850 (was 1 500; now 1 804), Courser ≤ 4 200 (was 4 000; now 4 138), Pylon 2 974, Tallyman 2 774, Bulwark 5 616, Breaker 4 432; LOD1 / LOD2 unchanged. **Measured** (quiet, balance and hub paused; `L_StressTest -RHStressMix=2`, 400 + 5 000, 1080p Epic, RTX 4080): GPU median **4.26 / 4.25 ms vs M8 4.08 / 4.23 ms** (≤ +0.1 ms, p95 ≤ 5.65), game thread 3.8–3.9 ms. The 400 + 5 000 number holds, so the rivets stay as geometry.
  - Sheets: `g14_m9_<Role>_vs_art.png`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Heat 3 Bulwark soft cap + companion moved** (per balance pressure verification, scheduler decision 2026-10-20). At Heat 3 / 5, runs died to contact from piled-up Tanks: 28–30 alive, which is the role's `MaxAliveShare` 0.25 × cap 120, among 84–134 enemies at 9:00. That is an HP wall, which GDD §3.5 forbids. Both changes are data, with no HP or telegraph change:
  - **(a)** `FRHHeatLevel` gains `RegularCapRole` / `RegularCapAlive`. H3 sets `Enemy.Tank` / 12, so from Heat 3 `FRHSpawnDirectorCore` drops regular Bulwark picks while 12 are alive (counter `HeatCapDropped`). Event escorts and elite-wave Tanks spawn on other paths and are unaffected.
  - **(b)** The H3 overlap companion moves from 7:35 to **10:00**: the 5-Courser line now rides the 10:00 elite wave, not the 7:30 ring.
  - `DA_SpawnTimeline_Main` is patched in place (only those fields, so balance's tuning is untouched). The factory `MakeDefault` matches.
  - Tests: `RogueHero.Spawn.HeatTankCap` (none at 12 alive at Heat 3, one at 11 and then the cap holds, unchanged at Heat 2) and `HeatLevels` (the companion at 10:00, none at 7:35). Balance re-verifies after the merge.
- 2026-10-20 — **Layered combat VFX without Niagara (playtest 2026-10-20 item 1, "现在的特效看起来太廉价了"; art book PL.16–19 / A3); scheduler D1.** Niagara systems still cannot be authored headless (entry above: the UE 5.8 Python API cannot add emitter modules), so each layer of what would be an NS_ stack is one ISM draw on `/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane` with a headless-authored material. **Materials:** `Tools\BuildVfxMaterials.bat` → `Tools/build_vfx_materials.py` → `/Game/RH/Materials/VFX/`, all unlit, ISM-flagged, with "Color" / "Intensity" params and per-instance custom data:
  - `M_VFX_Glow`: additive radial. CD0-2 colour, CD3 strength.
  - `M_VFX_Arc`: additive ribbon with a white core and coloured glow, 60 Hz flicker, tapered ends. CD0-2, CD3 strength, CD4 seed.
  - `M_VFX_Flame`: additive camera-facing card: a noise-swayed teardrop that erodes from the tip, white root → Burn amber → red. CD0 seed, CD1 life, CD2 strength.
  - `M_VFX_Scorch`: modulate, darken-only.
  - `M_VFX_Smoke`: translucent, ≤ 0.24 opacity.

  **Runtime:** `Combat/RHCombatVfxSubsystem`, a fire-and-forget data subsystem that never spawns Actors, with pooled bolts 64 / fires 192 / scorches 256 / sparks+embers 1 500 / flashes 160 / smoke 240, plus at most 8 shadowless flicker point lights for the brightest fires and strikes. It follows ImpactFX's PL.18 density LOD (`bDense` halves embers and caps flame cards at 5; `bCrowd` stops embers). A field of more than 60 fires drops to 4 cards each. `RH.Vfx [fire|bolt|sky|flash|clear]` spawns test effects and prints the counters; `RH.Vfx.Scale 0` switches every new layer off for A/B runs; `RH.Vfx.Lights 0` turns the lights off. Shapes: header-only `Combat/RHVfxShapes.h` (`RHVfx::BuildBolt` midpoint-displacement bolt with forks; `FacingAlong` and `Billboard` card rotations), shared with the statuses session's hull looks. Tests: `RogueHero.CombatVfx.*` (bolt determinism, pools). **Swap-out point:** this subsystem only. Once NS_ systems can be authored, each layer becomes an emitter fed from the same arrays.
- 2026-10-20 — **Enemy rounds layered (playtest 2026-10-20 item 1, art book PL.16; Art §3 rule 2 kept).** The designer asked for "a hot core". Art §3 binds a dark core, so the scheduler approved this reading: the #2A0A16 dark centre stays, and a thin **white-hot ring** (a flat `M_VFX_Glow` disc at the centre's height, 3.4 R; the opaque centre hides its middle) sits between it and the #FF2D8A body. A **soft halo** in the round's hue (7 R, strength 0.3, above the 5.2 R dark outline) and a **3-segment tapered trail** (speed × 0.07 s, 2–14 R, dimming 0.45 → 0.11) are added. **Impacts:** a spent round fizzles in its hue (0.07 s). A hit on the hero flashes #FF7A3A with 6 sparks. Muzzles add a warm bloom and 2 sparks while fewer than 48 muzzles are live. `RH.Vfx.Scale 0` drops the new layers. Test `RogueHero.CombatVfx.EnemyRoundImpacts`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Storm arcs as real bolts (playtest 2026-10-20 item 1, art book PL.08 / PL.15 / PL.19).** **Thunderclap was invisible** before this change: it damaged and Shocked, but drew no strike. It now drops `AddSkyStrike`, a forked sky-to-ground bolt with a white flash, 8 sparks and a scorch. Every Storm-ultimate strike draws the same (width 32). The Storm imbue chain arc and the Arc Line fusion's jumps (`SpawnChainArcs`) draw forked, 20 Hz-flickering bolts through `AddMinorBolt`. **Budget:** at 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets the imbue procs about 5 000 arcs a minute and the 64-bolt pool sat full (7 756 drops in 22 s). Minor bolts are now skipped while 32 bolts are live, which keeps half the pool for strikes. They're counted as `throttled` in `RH.Vfx`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Ground fire, and Ember Trail made visible (playtest 2026-10-20 items 1 + 8, "踏火冲刺").** **Finding:** `URHEffect_Forge_EmberTrail` dropped real burning patches (every 60 uu along the dash, 2 s each, a Burn pulse in R 120 every 0.5 s), but **nothing drew them**. Each patch now calls `AddFire(radius × 0.6)`, so the patches merge into one burning trail with flame cards, embers, smoke, a char scorch that outlives the flames by 1.5 s, an amber ground glow and flicker light. Verified by capture (`vfx4`: a line of flames and scorch along the dash) and by counter (`RH.Vfx`: about 9 fires started per 450 uu dash). The Forge ultimate's patch rings draw the same fire (radius × 0.8, 1.2 strength). **Cost:** A/B with `RH.Vfx.Scale 0/1`. Scene: 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, sun, Storm + Shade imbues, Ember Trail + Thunderclap, the Forge ultimate, 2 Storm ultimates, 4 dashes. RTX 4080, 1080p, 2 interleaved rounds per tier. GPU median on vs off, in clean pairs only: **Epic 6.68 vs 6.95 ms, Medium 3.00 vs 3.43 ms**. In the contended pairs: Epic 14.52 vs 14.37 ms (+0.15), Medium on 2.81 ms. **No measurable GPU cost: inside noise, under the +0.6 / +0.4 ms budgets.** Game thread: the subsystem's own tick is 0.16–0.17 ms at its peak (1 431 instances, 64 bolts, 8 lights); GT medians on vs off are within ±0.6 ms of each other (noise). Before the bolt throttle (previous entry) these were the pool-full worst case.
- 2026-10-20 — **Imbued bullets: Keeper-coloured tails, cool core (designer decision, playtest 2026-10-20 item 2: "子弹有附魔吗？从颜色上没看出来" → "尾迹用司色，弹芯保持冷色").** This **supersedes Art §12's "no Forge amber on imbued bullets", Quicksilver's colourless treatment, and playtest 3's Storm / Shade core tint.** While a Keeper leads the blend (higher Favor), the **tail of every player tracer** (straight tails and fusion shapes; shells excepted) is drawn in that Keeper's A1 hue from `RHGods::GetColour`, through a per-Keeper tail renderer (`bTintTail` / `TailTint` / `TailIntensity` on `URHBlessingImbueDef`). Hues include Forge amber and Quicksilver lime #A8F25A. The accent ("glow") uses the same hue, and the second Keeper still adds its accent at the tip. **The near-white core is never recoloured.** Tail intensity: Storm 6, Shade 6 (Shade needs the most for 3:1), Tide 5, Quicksilver 4, Radiance 4, Forge 3.5. Warm tails stay dimmer than enemy rounds (5–9). **Readability check (Art §3)**, measured on capture `tails1` against 18 enemy rounds:
  - Forge amber tails 3.8:1 against the floor; Quicksilver lime 5.6:1. Both pass 3:1.
  - Both separate from enemy fire by hue (amber / lime vs magenta #FF2D8A) and by shape (a thin tracer with a white core vs a disc with a dark centre and halo).
  - The test `RogueHero.Imbue.Defs` now requires: tail = Keeper colour, never magenta / enemy red, and warm tails ≤ 4 intensity.
- 2026-10-20 — **HUD XP bar restored (playtest 4 item 5: "经验条没有了？现在很难跟踪经验进度").** Deviation from art book PL.20, where XP is only ③, the 3 px line along the top edge: in play that line was not trackable. ① now carries a real XP bar directly under the level row: 358 × 5 px, cool #D6F7FF fill on the dark panel track with a 1 px bone edge, so it reads on sunlit floor too. The HP bar sits 8 px below it, and the two are told apart by height and hue (cool XP vs bone HP). The XP figures are raised from 55 % to 80 % bone. ③ stays. **Level-up flash** (`URHHUDWidget::TickLevelFlash`, 0.8 s, from the `Level` field change): the level figure swells 18 % and goes white, and the XP bar and ③ show full and white for the first half, then fade back to the new level's progress. Cool white only, never a warm or danger hue (Art §3). Swap-out point: the art book's next HUD pass should draw the ① XP bar so this is no longer a deviation.
- 2026-10-20 — **Playtest 5 designer decisions.** (1) **Imbued and fusion bullets: Keeper-colour tail + glow, cool / near-white core** (「尾迹用司色，弹芯保持冷色」), Forge amber and Quicksilver green included. Replaces "Forge amber never on player bullets / Quicksilver colourless" and amends Art §3.2's "nothing the player fires is ever warm". Guard rails: amber at A1 hue 32°, never on the tracer head; density dimming applies; a contrast test of warm tails vs enemy rounds in the densest frame (reduce tail saturation / length on failure). **Note:** CLAUDE.md non-negotiable 11 ("player bullets are cool … warm only for danger") now has this documented exception. (2) **Cards:** weapon / upgrade / modifier = high-tech (metal, glass, holographic window, circuit traces); blessing / Keeper / fusion / Legendary = stone tablets; **rich rarity reveal** (pack-opening style, original visuals, skippable, level-up screen only). Art §12, GDD §5.6 / §9, gap G10 (now L).
- 2026-10-20 — **RH.Stats enemy-bullet rows (playtest item 6, "too dense" as a number).** `enemy bullets/min` = enemy bullets spawned over the 60 s window (sum of `URHBulletSubsystem::GetEnemyStats().Spawned` across sources) and `peak aimed at the hero / s` = the window's peak of enemy bullets on a collision course with the hero (closest approach <= `URHRunStats::IncomingRadius` 120 uu, reached within `IncomingHorizon` 1 s, so they arrive within a second: `CountIncoming`, pure). Report only (no GDD target yet). CSV columns `enemy_bullets_spawned_per_min, peak_aimed_at_hero_per_s` appended. First reading (steered bot, god mode, Storm, main 1691f35+): 0 / 97 / 598 / 647 per min and peak 0 / 7 / 9 / 8 per s at minutes 2 / 10 / 15 / 18. Test `RogueHero.Debug.RunStats.Pressure`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Flare Pylon density: aim tokens (D1) and no close fan (D3)** (playtest item 6, per balance's analysis: 13–18 Pylons alive late, ≈ 60 bullets/s from 10 bearings; scheduler decision 2026-10-20). No HP or telegraph changes; the data sits on the timeline (`URHSpawnTimeline`, so `DA_SpawnTimeline_Main` takes the class defaults).
  - **D1:** at most `PylonAimTokens` = **3** Pylons are in their tell or burst at once, `PylonAimTokensThreat` = **4** in Threat blocks. The spawn director pushes the count every step (`SetTurretAimRules`). At each step start `URHEnemySubsystem` counts the Pylons in their tell or burst and grants the free tokens to the ready ones that aimed least recently (`RHEnemy::GrantAimTokens`, FIFO-fair). A first index-order version let four Pylons recycle the tokens while eight never fired. The others hold before their tell (`StepTurret(..., bMayAim)`), so every shot still follows its full tell.
  - **D3:** no fan inside `PylonFanMinRange` = **600 uu** (`RHEnemy::TurretFanCount`): a close Pylon fires its burst as single aimed shots.
  - Test `RogueHero.Enemies.PylonAimTokens`: the defaults; the fan count either side of 600 uu; a Pylon with no token holds; 12 Pylons over 30 s never exceed 3 at once and every one still fires. D2 (a Pylon soft cap) is held back unless re-verification still shows too much density.
- 2026-10-20 — **Status looks on enemy hulls, layered (playtest item 1: "the effects look cheap"; art book PL.17).** Split agreed with weapons: the hull statuses are ours (`AddStatusVfx`); enemy rounds, Storm arcs and ground fire are theirs.
  - **Layers:** `Enemies/RHStatusLook` replaces the single-primitive look per status with 15 drawn layers, one `ARHInstanceRenderer` each (a hot core inside a dim, wider glow, plus particles and translucent media):
    - **Burn:** 3–4 twisted flame tongues (`SM_VFX_FlameTongue`) as an orange outer tongue with a yellow-white core, flickering smoothly (smoothstepped 9 Hz noise) and leaning, with 5 rising embers and 3 soft smoke puffs (`SM_VFX_SmokePuff` on the new translucent `M_VFX_Smoke`, soft rim from N·V).
    - **Chill:** 2–6 hex ice crystal clusters (`SM_VFX_FrostCluster`) by stack, over a faint glaze.
    - **Freeze:** a clear fresnel ice shell (new `M_VFX_Ice`) with six crystal clusters splaying from the base; the shatter shards are crystal clusters too.
    - **Shock:** 2–3 jagged arcs of 6 kinks (`RHStatusLook::BuildArc`: core + 3.5× glow) with a side branch and sparks, re-struck at 20 Hz.
    - **Mark:** the scan line as core + glow, a second line sweeping, and corner brackets that close toward the execute threshold.
    - **Blind:** a hull-sized flare with an eight-arm star. It was invisible at first, because the optic is the small hooded lamp since M5b.
    - Hues as PL.17; nothing magenta or red.
  - **Tooling:** `Tools/CreateStatusMaterials.bat`; the meshes come from `build_enemy_models.py` (`VFX_BUILDERS`).
  - **Density LOD:** above `StatusVfxFullBudget` (120 Statused) only the scan line and the flare + two arms are drawn. The first version drew Mark's full set there, and `-RHStressStatuses` (400 Statused, 5 000 bullets) measured status VFX 1.0–1.2 ms and game thread 6.8–7.7 ms, against 0.28 / 4.9 ms on the old code. With the LOD fix: **status VFX 0.25–0.35 ms, game thread 4.95–5.5 ms**. GPU not measured: another session's editor held it. Swap point: weapons' shared `M_VFX_Arc / Flame / Glow / Ember` and `Combat/RHVfxShapes.h` replace our flame material and `BuildArc` once they land on main (a path change in `RHStatusLook.cpp`), for one look across the game.
- 2026-10-20 — **Tech cards for weapons and modifiers (designer decision 2026-10-20, "weapons and modifiers 可以使用高科技感强的卡片，god's bless 继续使用石板").** Supersedes the "every offer card is a stone tablet" reading of Art §12 PL.21 for `NewWeapon` / `WeaponUpgrade` / `Modifier` only; Blessing, Keeper choice (stele), Fusion and Legendary keep the tablets. New procedural baker `UI/Cards/RHCardTech`: machined bezel (brushed metal, chamfered corners, screw bosses), smoked-glass face with scanlines, holo window (grid, corner brackets, scan band), circuit traces with running pulses and a breathing emissive edge in the kind colour; same 270 x 476 zones as the tablet, so the text layout is shared. Rarity is the finish (Common gunmetal, Rare anodised blue trim, Epic black chrome + violet trim), on top of the gem and label. Light ink on dark glass; `RogueHero.UI.CardTech` checks 3:1 on the baked glass (mean and brightest texel). Swap-out point: `T_CardTech_*` textures replace `RHCardTech::Frame()` / `HoloGrid()`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Tech cards for weapons and modifiers (designer decision 2026-10-20, "weapons and modifiers 可以使用高科技感强的卡片，god's bless 继续使用石板").** Supersedes the "every offer card is a stone tablet" reading of Art §12 PL.21 for `NewWeapon` / `WeaponUpgrade` / `Modifier` only; Blessing, Keeper choice (stele), Fusion and Legendary keep the tablets. New procedural baker `UI/Cards/RHCardTech`: machined bezel (brushed metal, chamfered corners, screw bosses), smoked-glass face with scanlines, holo window (grid, corner brackets, scan band), circuit traces with running pulses and a breathing emissive edge in the kind colour; same 270 x 476 zones as the tablet, so the text layout is shared. Rarity is the finish (Common gunmetal, Rare anodised blue trim, Epic black chrome + violet-blue #8A7CFF trim), on top of the gem and label. Light ink on dark glass; `RogueHero.UI.CardTech` checks 3:1 on the baked glass (mean and brightest texel). Swap-out point: `T_CardTech_*` textures replace `RHCardTech::Frame()` / `HoloGrid()`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Rarity reveal for offer cards (designer decision 2026-10-20, "不同稀有度的卡片背后的特效太简陋了…参考《炉石传说》抽卡界面"; GDD §9, Art §12 G10; original visuals).** New `UI/Cards/RHCardReveal`: per card slot a back `URHCardRevealFx` (row behind the cards) and a front one (row over them), both following the card's size and focus lift, on the level-up screen only. Cards deal in staggered (0.07 s; 0.32 s scale / fade / drop) only when the slot's card changed (new offer, reroll, refuse replacement), not on hold. Beat per tier (`RHCardReveal::FxFor`, build-up → burst → settle): Common one shimmer pass + faint glow; Rare a looping material sweep + corner glint, blue backlight, 8 rays, dust; Epic (violet-blue #8A7CFF, never pink) a 0.25 s charge (backlight swells, ring contracts, dust converges), burst, rim light, 16 rays, dust; **fusion and Legendary the full reveal** (gold, 0.45 s charge, counter-rotating rays, spark shower into embers, light pillar, 3 rings). `ERHOfferRarity` tops out at Epic, so the top tier is the Legendary / Fusion card kind (the docs' "fusion / Legendary full reveal" overrides the plan's "Fusion as Epic"). Settled, everything is behind the opaque card (aura glow round the silhouette, backlight, rays, dust round the outline), so Art §3 text contrast is unchanged; the front layer carries only the burst (flash, rings, sparks) and is empty after ~1.1 s. Cool / gold only (`RogueHero.UI.CardReveal` rejects red / magenta hues). **Skippable:** any key or click finishes every reveal (`URHLevelUpScreen::SkipReveal`) and still acts, so A confirms at once. Dev cvar `RH.UI.RevealRate` slows it for review / captures. Procedural textures + UImages (≤ ~120 per Legendary slot). Swap-out point: a Niagara UI system replaces `URHCardRevealFx` with the same tier table.
- 2026-10-20 — **SFX v4: the Pantheon sound principles (playtest item 7, third round; designer: "保证音效不管如何组合都不能刺耳难听"; the scheduler set the gates).** Every pitched cue is in A minor pentatonic, transposed per run by `URHAudioSubsystem::RootSemis` (from the run seed; the Keeper motifs are not transposed). Fire, hit, crit and kill voices draw a pentatonic degree (`FRHAudioCue::bPentatonic`) and ±1 dB (`VolumeJitterDb`). Fire and hit voices wait for a shared 84 BPM 1/64 grid of 44.6 ms (`EmitQuantised`); only the sound waits, never gameplay. The waves are soft sine/triangle bodies with linear attacks, every voice is low-passed, and each is tanh soft-clipped at drive 2.6 (`Tools/synth_fire.py`). `Tools/import_audio.py` makes `SMX_SFX` (−1.9 dB in, compressor knee 22 / ratio 6 / 4 ms / 180 ms, limiter −1 dB) and its child `SMX_Echo` (ping-pong 240 / 360 ms, feedback 0.3, LP 2.4 kHz). Every `SW_` wave plays into `SMX_SFX` and sends to the echo. The parent link uses `URHAudioDef::LinkSubmix` because `ParentSubmix` is read-only from Python. **Deviation from the Pantheon numbers:** the compressor threshold is −20 dB, not −14 dB, with +8 dB of make-up. The designer heard the first render get quieter as combat got busier ("第四段声音特别小"), so the scheduler set a loudness target: combat at about −16 LUFS short-term, sparse or dense, with 3–6 dB of typical gain reduction. Gates, measured by `Tools/sfx_bus.py` (an offline model of the game frame by frame, then the chain; `Docs/Audio/SfxAudition.wav`): the 2–5 kHz presence of every class is ≤ −32 dB (worst MeleeArc −32.3 / −32.4 per variant); mixed combat is −15.9 LUFS short-term median and the worst case −16.0 (integrated −15.9 / −15.4); the worst case has a 13.9 dB crest, a −3.4 dBFS peak, a median gain reduction of 4.1 dB and 0 dB of limiter gain reduction. A lone weapon is about 9 LU under busy combat (−25 LUFS); raising the fire volume to close the gap broke the crest gate, so it stays at 0.30; `RogueHero.Audio.WorstCaseNoStarvation` checks that no hit or kill loses to a shared cap. The model is offline: if the in-game mix differs, re-measure with `RH.Stats` in the m10 stress run.
- 2026-10-20 — **Lifeline soft cap + heal from damage dealt (playtest 2026-10-20 item 9, "lifesteal ≈ immortal"; balance L1 + L2).** **L1:** over any 1 s window (a 60-step ring in `URHBulletSubsystem::CapLifelineStep`) the hero is healed `C (1 − e^(−raw / C))`, where `C = 1.2 % max HP / s × active Lifeline weapons` (slots whose hits produced heal inside the window). A trickle passes almost unchanged; a flood saturates at C. Weapons report the hero's max HP through `NoteHeroMaxHP`; an unknown max HP means uncapped (tools, tests without a hero). The granted heal is shared out per slot by raw share. `HealQueued` stays the raw total; the new `HealGranted` is what the hero gets. **L2:** the raw heal is `Lifesteal × HP actually removed` per hit (`URHEnemySubsystem::GetLastHitDealt`, statuses b6c8702: after armour / Shock / shields, no overkill, 0 on blocks). It falls back to spec damage only when no matching `ApplyHits` ran (unit tests). Tests in `RogueHero.Modifiers.Wrappers`:
  - a trickle ≈ `C (1 − e^(−raw/C))`;
  - a 40-hits-per-step flood heals ≤ C (and > 0.5 C) over its last second;
  - a 50× overkill hit heals 3 % of the enemy's HP.

  `RogueHero.GameMode.Wiring`'s Lifeline line now expects 0.5 × the targets' HP instead of 0.5 × 1 000 per hit. That is one assertion in `Core/Tests`, changed for this fix.
- 2026-10-20 — **Status looks moved onto the weapons' shared combat VFX** (the swap point logged with the layered status looks; one look across the game). In `RHStatusLook`:
  - **Shock:** `RHVfx::BuildBolt` arcs (re-rolled at 20 Hz, one fork) as `M_VFX_Arc` ribbons on the engine Plane, plus an `M_VFX_Glow` halo and glow sparks.
  - **Burn:** two `M_VFX_Flame` cards over our tongues; embers are stretched `M_VFX_Glow` streaks on the weapons' ember ramp.
  - **Smoke:** the weapons' `M_VFX_Smoke` cards. Our `/Game/RH/VFX/Status/M_VFX_Smoke`, `SM_VFX_SmokePuff` and `RHStatusLook::BuildArc` are deleted.
  - **Blind:** the flare is an `M_VFX_Glow` billboard.
  - **Kept as ours:** frost and ice (`M_VFX_Ice`, `SM_VFX_FrostCluster`), the flame tongues and Mark's bars.
  - **Plumbing:** the card layers carry per-instance custom data (`StatusCustom`, `FLayer::NumCustom`) and face the camera (`StatusCamPos`).
  - **Measured** (`-RHStressStatuses`, 400 Statused + 5 000 bullets): status VFX 0.37 ms, game thread 4.81 ms median.
- 2026-10-20 — **Lifeline cap is one total, not per weapon (fixes the L1 entry above).** Balance's verification found `C × active Lifeline weapons` still let 2 weapons heal ≈ 144 % max HP/min and 3 weapons ≈ 216 %/min. The scheduler's brief meant one total. `CapLifelineStep` now uses a single `C = 1.2 % max HP / s` for every Lifeline weapon together. The granted heal is still shared per slot by raw share. One weapon is unchanged (at most 72 % max HP/min). The `RogueHero.Modifiers.Wrappers` flood test runs 1, 2 and 3 Lifeline weapons and checks that each heals at most C per second (and more than 0.5 C).

- 2026-10-20 — **Playtest telemetry (v0.2, external playtest; scheduler).** New `URHRunTelemetry` (Source/RogueHero/Telemetry, game worlds, Shipping included) appends one JSON object per line to `Saved/Playtests/Events_<utc>_seed<N>.jsonl` (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\RogueHero\Saved\Playtests` in a packaged build; `FILEWRITE_Append` per line, so a crash keeps what happened; UE_LOG is compiled out of Shipping). Events: run_start (seed, patron, heat, weather plan, build, version), offer_shown / offer_pick / offer_decline, keeper_line (the L8 passed-over Keeper), tier (each tier crossed, with its minute), boss_start / boss_end (form, heat, top Path, fight stats), death, run_end (won, level, second Keeper, build, favor), run_quit. It only listens (RunState / OfferFlow delegates, OfferFlow state polled per tick, the boss subsystem's public stats); no other owner's code changed. **Open hooks, with swap-out points:** (1) `URHOfferFlow::OnOfferShown` from integration, which replaces the per-tick poll in `PollOffers` (the poll misses offers picked in the same tick and cannot tell banish from lock); (2) statuses' last hero-death damage-by-source report, which replaces `"cause":"unknown"` (today only Warden deaths are broken down). Report: `Tools/playtest_report.py`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Playtest report tool.** `python Tools/playtest_report.py <tester zip or Saved folder> ... [--out dir]` pairs each run's `Events_*.jsonl` with its `RunStats_*.csv` (same seed) and writes one Markdown page per tester (per run: patron, Heat, weather, the minute each Path reached Tier 2 / 3, L8 second Keeper and the passed-over one, L16 fusion offered / taken, Warden form and result, death minute and cause or WIN, first dash; offers shown / picked / rerolled / declined; dashes and damage at minutes 5 / 10 / 15 / 18) and `aggregate.md` with the Roadmap W6 go criteria: telemetry next to blank observer columns, because naming the 流派, "one more run" and the designer's verdict are observed, not logged. Standard library only; a crash-cut last line is skipped.
- 2026-10-20 — **Telemetry event-driven (the v0.1b Shipping bot runs had no offer_pick and no tier lines).** Cause: `URHRunTelemetry` bound once to the first `URHOfferFlow`, but `ARHGameMode::StartRunState` makes a new flow at every run start (the frame-1 restart included), so offers were polled from a dead flow (the spurious level-0 `offer_decline` lines). Fix: the recorder re-binds whenever the game mode hands out a new flow and writes offers from integration's `OnOfferShown` (`reason` new / reroll / banish / lock) and `OnOfferResolved` (`offer_pick` / `offer_decline` / `offer_banish` / `offer_reroll` / `offer_lock`, auto-picks included); tiers are polled from each Path's favor (`RHGods::GetTier`) every tick, one line per tier crossed, instead of the tier delegate. The death line now carries the enemy sim's last-10-s report (`GetLastHeroDeathReport`) plus enemy-bullet damage by source. Verified on a Shipping package (ShipCheck): each run has tier, offer_shown, offer_pick and the L8 god_choice. `playtest_report.py` reads the new reasons (old files' "changed" still counts).

Text ST_Blessings (18 branch names + 84 card strings, build_depth_text §2) and ST_Discoveries (12, authored here) are EN; zh is loc-zh's (RogueHero.Text.Bilingual fails until it lands). **All numbers are placeholders** next to the existing blessings (spec: GATED, step 10 bot pass). **Approximations, each with its swap-out point:** Slow Burn keeps its own per-enemy Burn stacks (the status system has none); Spring Tide keeps its own shield (regen at full HP fills it, lost HP is given back from it; the hero has no shield); Low Ceiling / Breakwater run on Thunderclap's / Cold Front's period from their own timer; Radiant Wake is a short light segment behind each hit (no per-bullet trail in the bullet sim); Blink Strike uses the largest recent hit as "your strongest weapon's hit". **Hooks other owners call (`RHBranchHooks`, inert until wired):** `NotifyBolt` (Thunderclap -> Rolling Thunder; branch bolts already call it), `GetExtraSockets` (Masterwork: offer flow / weapon instance), `TakeOwedShades` (Arrears: Shade T2 raises), `TakeSureCrit` (Perfect Sight: the crit roll), `GetExtraPierce` (Terminal Velocity: spec composition), (Carom / Relay Chain: wired 2026-10-20 on weapons' `OnRicochets` batch, c64ce3d; Carom pre-pays Bonus x BouncesMade x spec damage on the bounce's target, Relay Chain treats a 2nd+ bounce off an enemy that is dead at dispatch as a ricochet kill and fires a real bullet of the same spec via `URHBulletSubsystem::SpawnExtra`). `IsCapstoneOpen` is the E2 gate for tiers' offer extensions (step 6). **Re-keyed against the text doc (fiction is never a key, TDD §3):** `Branch.Shade.Ledger` -> `Branch.Shade.Economy` (LEDGER is Shade's designation), `Blessing.Shade.DebtCollectors` -> `Blessing.Shade.ShadeBoost` ("Debt Collectors" is a fusion name), `Blessing.Quicksilver.RelayChain` -> `Blessing.Quicksilver.RicochetKill` (RELAY is Quicksilver's designation); the display names (Ledger, Debt Collectors, Relay Chain) stay as FText. **Divergence metric:** `python Tools/Balance/branch_divergence.py <Events_*.jsonl | folder | zip> [--last 20]`: a run's set = branches with >= 2 of its blessings (telemetry offer_pick), divergence = distinct sets / runs, target >= 60 %; the table is parsed from RHBranches.cpp. Tests: `RogueHero.Branches.*`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Power-share baseline tooling (balance; keeper_identity_spec §11, D-43 gate).** `Debug/RHBalanceProbe.cpp` (debug only): `RH.Balance.Probe <crowd|single>` measures the current build's DPS on a stationary, refilled field of 120 Swarm-stat dummies (no move, no hit, no XP; timeline off, god, bot off), `RH.Balance.Batch <file>` runs many configurations in one process (re-opens the arena per line), `RH.Balance.NoReactions` unbinds the reaction subsystem. `Tools/Balance/power_split.py` replays steered bot runs' telemetry picks into fresh arenas once per configuration (full / without blessings, modifiers, levels, extra weapons, transformation, reactions, each Keeper, each branch / +1 next pick of each kind) and writes `Docs/Balance/power_split.md` (+ `_raw.json`). Method choices, each a known bias: dummies at **10×** the minute's swarm HP, because at 1× strong builds wipe the field every frame and the reading is refill-capped (kill-chain effects are therefore under-weighted); one 10 s window (±10–20 %); shares = single-removal drop / sum of drops. Launch with `-nosound` (each map reload otherwise logs an audio line per frame, 12 GB) and `RH.Offer.AutoPick worst` (a stray level-up would pause the probe). Baseline on main 065edd4, six patrons, rh.Omens 0: blessings 16 / 16 / 19 % at L8 / L16 / 20:00, modifiers 1 / 19 / 22 %, levels 20 / 21 / 24 %, new weapons 62 / 34 / 22 %, transformation – / 7 / 8 %, reactions 2 / 2 / 5 %.

Started by the arena URL option `?Daily=1` (`RHDailyRun::GetTravelOption()`, for meta-ui's hub entry) or `-RHDaily`; `-RHDailyDate=YYYYMMDD` pins the date. The patron stays the player's choice. **Local daily-best record:** `URHDailySave` in slot `<meta slot>_Daily`, best per date (a win beats a loss; faster win / longer survival; then kills), written at `ARHGameMode::EndRun`; `RHDailyRun::GetBest` for the hub. Steam leaderboards later (hub). Test `RogueHero.Build.DailyRun`.
  - **(a)** The 12-alive soft cap on regular Bulwarks moves from H3 to the timeline itself (`URHSpawnTimeline::RegularCapRole / RegularCapAlive`, the base of `GetHeatRules`, which a Heat level can still override), so it holds at every Heat.
  - **(b)** `MaxAliveShare` (the Tank / Pylon mechanism) is set on **Coursers 0.10** (12 at cap 120) and **Breakers 0.05** (6 at cap 120). It applies to regular spawns only; events, drop-ins and elites are unaffected.
  - `DA_SpawnTimeline_Main`, `DA_Enemy_Rusher` and `DA_Enemy_Bruiser` are patched in place (only those fields; H3's own cap is cleared, since the base cap covers it). The factories match.
  - Tests: `RogueHero.Spawn.HeatTankCap` (12 at Heats 0–5; none at 12 alive at Heat 0 and 3; one at 11; still spawning under the cap) and the new `RogueHero.Spawn.SkillShares` (no regular Courser at 10 % of the 12:10 cap, no Breaker at 5 %, both still spawn under it). Balance re-verifies.
- 2026-10-20 — **Multi-arena framework, M1 (v0.2 core, ahead of Arena 2).** New `Environment/RHArenaDef`. **`URHArenaDef` (`DA_Arena_<Key>`, primary type `RHArena`)** holds:
  - `Key`, a gameplay tag. **Arena keys are structural: `Arena.Depth1`, `Arena.Depth2`, … (how far downstream, Narrative §11).** "Breach" is Vigil's name, and the Keepers call the same place "Yard" (Narrative §3), so fiction is never a key (CLAUDE.md rule 6). The player-facing name is `DisplayNameTable` / `DisplayNameKey` (`ST_World` / `World.Breach`).
  - `Level` (soft); `Builder`, an instanced `URHArenaBuilder`; `Dressing`, a soft `URHDressingSet` (primary type `RHDressing`, the Founders-ruin scatter on runtime Movable ISMs).
  - `Weathers`: weather defs with weights, picked from the run seed on their own stream. A single set never draws, and an empty list means the default `DA_Weather`.
  - `LightingRig`, a subclass of `ARHArenaLighting`; `SpawnTimeline`; `Music` and `Ambience`, looping 2D beds started by `URHAudioSubsystem`; `NextArena`.

  **Readers:**
  - `ARHGameMode::BeginPlay` resolves the arena and spawns its rig with the picked weather set.
  - `ARHArenaEnvironment::Build` runs the arena's builder and then its dressing. `URHArenaBuilder_Plate` holds the v0.1 tunables (seed 4242, extent 4800, backdrop) and calls `BuildPlate`, which is the old `Build` unchanged. The actor's `Seed` / `FloorExtent` are no longer `UPROPERTY`s; values saved in the map are ignored.
  - `URHSpawnDirector` takes the arena's timeline before `DA_SpawnTimeline_Main`.

  **Which arena a world plays:** `URHArenaDef::ForWorld` checks `RHArena=<leaf>` on the travel URL or the command line first, then the `MapArenas` table in `DefaultGame.ini`. If neither matches, L_Arena_Test uses the C++ fallback `MakeFallbackDepth1` (rule 4). **Deviation:** the map lookup is a config table, not an asset-registry scan. With the scan, the Breach differed at load by 0.03 mean / 33 max codes. With `r.TextureStreaming 0` the two paths matched, so the scan's load-time cost was shifting texture streaming. `RogueHero.Arena.MapTableMatchesAssets` keeps the table in step with each `DA_Arena_*.Level`. L_Arena_Test plays `DA_Arena_Depth1`, made by `Tools\CreateArenaDefs.bat` with the same values as the fallback (`RogueHero.Arena.Depth1MatchesFallback`).

  **Travel hook for the run-flow owner:** `ARHGameMode::OnArenaCleared(Cleared, Next)` fires when the Warden falls. If `bHoldRunEndForTravel` is set and a next arena exists, the run does not end; the owner then calls `TravelToNextArena()` / `TravelToArena(Def)`, which opens the level with `?RHArena=<leaf>`. Carrying the run across the travel stays with the run-flow owner. `RH.Arena` and `RH.Arena.Travel [leaf|self]` are on the console. The default flow is unchanged: Depth1 has no `NextArena`, and a win ends the run.

  **Pixel check:** `Tools/ArenaCapture.ps1` takes 6 views at fixed frames (`-benchmark -fps=30 -RHSeed=1234`, overcast, 1920×1080 off-screen), and `Tools/arena_diff.py` compares them. Before vs after with the DA: 99.87–99.99 % of pixels identical and mean difference 0.000–0.001, the same as two captures of the old build (run-to-run noise from Lumen / VSM temporal effects, max 3–6 codes).

- 2026-10-20 — **Steam scaffold, piece 1: platform core (v0.2, Roadmap backlog; dev only, nothing published).** The `OnlineSubsystem`, `OnlineSubsystemUtils` and `OnlineSubsystemSteam` plugins are enabled. `[OnlineSubsystemSteam]` in DefaultEngine.ini sets the dev App ID 480, `bRelaunchInSteam=false`, no server and no Steam networking. `DefaultPlatformService` stays unset. `URHPlatformSubsystem` (Platform/, a game-instance subsystem) requests the Steam OSS by name and is the only door to platform features. It goes through the engine interfaces only, never the Steamworks SDK. **Gate:** `rh.Steam.Enabled` (default 1), `-NoSteam` (the engine's Steam module honours the same flag), and never in the editor, unattended or commandlet runs, so automation and Shipping-without-Steam stay hermetic. If the OSS fails to init (client not running), `IsSteamActive()` is false and every feature is a logged no-op. Measured on this machine (Steam client not running): the SDK loads and `SteamAPI` fails to init with engine warnings only, the game logs "Platform: Steam OSS unavailable …", smoke PASS with 0 errors. `RH.Platform` prints the state. `-SteamDeck` / env `SteamDeck=1` mark a Deck. **Swap-out:** the real App ID replaces 480. The Steam-side paths are compiled but unexercised until a session runs with the client logged in.
- 2026-10-20 — **Steam scaffold, piece 2: achievements from Discoveries.** One Steam achievement per Discovery (GDD §8.3), mapped in a data table: `Content/RH/Data/Platform/DT_SteamAchievements.csv` (`FRHSteamAchievementRow`, row = `DiscoveryId`, `SteamId` = the API name). It is loaded at first use with `UDataTable::CreateTableFromCSVString` and staged as UFS like the string tables. **Deviation:** a CSV loaded at runtime instead of a `DT_` asset, because a code session cannot author a uasset; the CSV stays the source the importer would read. **The Steam ids are placeholders** until the partner-site achievements exist (notes in `Docs/Platform/Steam.md`). Unlocks go through `IOnlineAchievements::WriteAchievements` (value 100 = unlocked) on every `URHMetaSubsystem::OnDiscoveryUnlocked`, and all owned Discoveries re-sync at start (idempotent). Both are no-ops when Steam is off. `RogueHero.Platform.AchievementsCoverDiscoveries` holds the table to the Discovery set: every Discovery has a unique id, and no row names a missing Discovery.
- 2026-10-20 — **Steam scaffold, piece 3: cloud save of the profile.** The profile is the one `RHMeta` slot (settings stay per machine). Behind `rh.Steam.CloudApi` (default 1): `URHPlatformSubsystem` uploads the slot's exact bytes, checksum trailer included, through `IOnlineUserCloud` (Steam Remote Storage) as `RHMeta.sav`. That happens after each run end (`OnRunEndProcessed`) and on quit, throttled to once per 30 s otherwise. At start, if no local slot existed (checked before the meta subsystem writes a fresh one), it reads the cloud copy. It writes the copy only onto a still-untouched fresh profile and reloads through `URHMetaSubsystem::Load()`, so a damaged or newer-build download gets the existing quarantine / read-only handling. A local profile is never overwritten, and conflict UI is out of scope. **Steam Auto-Cloud** (no code: `WinAppDataLocal` / `RogueHero/Saved/SaveGames` / `*.sav`, works under Proton on Deck) is documented in `Docs/Platform/Steam.md` as the release alternative. Use one or the other, never both (`rh.Steam.CloudApi 0` with Auto-Cloud).

- 2026-10-20 — **The Outfall water state (`URHOutfallWater`, Environment/; scheduler ownership call; full_game_specs §1.3 / §1.5 / §1.6).** It is a world subsystem and is active only when the world's arena is `Arena.Depth2`.

  **State:**
  - three radial channels at 120° (500 uu wide, wall r 2 700 → grate r 450), with a per-channel current multiplier (base 150 uu/s inward, eased to 40 % at the banks);
  - per-channel surges: a telegraph, then a front running from the wall at 1 200 uu/s, with ×2 flow behind the front;
  - the flooded radius, with a pending radius and its telegraph seconds;
  - the drain pull toward the centre (P3, 300 uu/s, easing off on the grate).

  **Readers:** `GetCurrentAt(Pos)` → uu/s, `IsFlooded`, `IsInChannel`, `GetSurgeFront`, `GetSurgeTelegraph`, `GetPendingFloodRadius` / `Seconds`.

  **Writers:** `StartSurge`, `SetChannelMultiplier`, `FloodTo`, `SetDrain`. They are called by the Floodgate (balance) and the timeline events.

  **Stepping:** `ARHGameMode::SimStep` advances it, so it follows hit-stop and time scale.

  **Tests:** `RogueHero.Outfall.*`.

  **Not yet wired:** physics consumers (hero / enemies / gems / enemy bullets) and the water render come with the Outfall map milestone.
- 2026-10-20 — **Keeper Bids built to `full_game_specs.md` §4 (v0.2).** Gate: `RHKeeperBids::IsUnlocked` = story Act >= 2, read through `RHStoryLink::GetAct`. **TEMPORARY:** until meta-ui's `Meta/RHStoryState.h` lands (picked up with `__has_include`, so no edit is needed: `RHStory::GetAct`, `RHStory::IsShadeOffBooks`), the act comes from `bFirstWin` (Act I → II on the first Breach win, §2.2) and Shade is never Off the Books. Dev overrides are `rh.Story.Act`, `rh.Story.ShadeOffLedger` and `rh.Offers.Bids`. Shade retaliation (Binding + Levy) needs both Shade's fate Off the Books **and** a Shade blessing held in the run (§5.3). Rules: `URHOfferGenerator::PickBidder` / `PickBidSlot` / `MakeBid`, inserted by `URHOfferFlow::TryInsertBid` on L4–L7 level-up hands, with a 50 % roll on the new `ERHRunStream::Bids` stream so other streams stay seed-stable. Run state: `BidsOffered/Taken/Called`, Levies per Keeper, the bound Keeper + blessing. Numbers: `URHOfferTuning` "Bids" plus `DA_BidTerm_<Term>` (`URHBidTermDef`); the code fallback is `DA_BidTerm_<Term>_CodeFallback` (Binding 2 favor, Levy 0.2, Collateral 1), and all of it is GATED. Choices made where the spec is silent: (a) a Bid hand counts toward the 2 per run when it closes (pick or decline). A Renegotiate removes the Bid and does **not** roll a new one into the re-dealt hand, so Bids can't be fished for. (b) A Bid pick's favor is set by the rules (+1, Binding +2) whatever ranks the card grants. Ranks follow the rarity, like an upgrade (`UpgradeLevelsByRarity`), and Epic gets +1 rank. (c) Binding's L8 guarantee replaces the last of the 3 L8 cards when the bound Keeper is missing. Choosing it clears the debt; passing it over calls it (`CallBindingDebt`: −1 rank, `Keeper.<Path>.PassedOver.Act2`). (d) A Shade off-ledger hand shows the Binding line. UI: plaque `Bid.Header`, Term names in the system slot, Term rules under the blessing's rules, the Keeper's line in italic under them, a Keeper-coloured contract seal on the window. Hold / Refuse are disabled on a Bid, with the `Bid.NoHoldRefuse` tooltip. **Levy banking:** the run summary shows `Summary.Levy` per Keeper as Reclaimed × fraction. Taking it out of the banked Ichor is meta-ui's (590921e: `FRHRunSummary::IchorLien01`, `FRHIchorBreakdown::Lien`). `URHRunCounters::BuildSummary` fills `IchorLien01` and the summary line shows the banked `Lien` split by Keeper, both picked up at compile time (`requires`) once that commit merges; until then the line is display-only. Telemetry: an `RH.Stats` bids row, the `RH.Debug.Offers` eligibility reason (`URHOfferFlow::DescribeBidEligibility`), and an `offer_audit.py` bid column with checks. EN rows are added; zh-Hans is loc-zh's, so `RogueHero.Text.Bilingual` fails on those 33 keys until it lands. The Bid Term Lien was renamed **Levy** (planning ffd1ff4; Shade's blessing keeps the name Lien); meta-ui's field names `FRHRunSummary::IchorLien01` / `FRHIchorBreakdown::Lien` are theirs and unchanged.
- 2026-10-20 — **Build depth step 2: transformation framework + the L16 hand (`build_depth_spec.md` §1.2, D-31, D-32).** `URHTransformDef` (primary type `RHTransform`, `Build/RHTransformDef.h`): Kind Fusion / Path / Weapon. Tags are `Transform.<Path>`, `Transform.Tide.SlackWater` and `Transform.<Weapon>` (`Config/Tags/RHTransforms.ini`). **Deviation:** a wrapped fusion keeps its own `Fusion.<Path>.<Weapon>` id rather than a `Transform.*` tag, so Contracts unlocks, run commitments and card ids stay unchanged. **Fallback (TDD §17 (d) extended):** until `DA_Transform_*` exist, `RHTransforms::MakeFallbackDefs` builds the 7 Path + 10 evolution defs as `DA_Transform_<Key>_CodeFallback`. Their behaviour is registered by the owning sessions (`RegisterFallbackEffect`, statuses; `RegisterFallbackWeapon`, weapons; `RegisterFallbackParams`, `Text0` = the card's `{0}`), so this file never imports their classes; an unregistered def is still dealt and recorded (no behaviour yet). VFX overrides are soft `UObject` refs, so the header needs no Niagara module. The `PrimaryAssetTypesToScan` line for `RHTransform` in DefaultGame.ini is the scheduler's (that file is not ours). **The hand** is `URHOfferGenerator::GenerateTransformHand`, called from `GenerateFusionChoice`; the new card kind is `ERHOfferCardKind::Transform`, appended so stored values don't shift. Rules: 3 cards (`TransformHandSize`); a fusion when one qualifies; at least one card tied to the patron / second Keeper; the rest by weight. Weight = (1 + 0.1 × weapon level) × (1 + 0.05 × Favor) × 0.6 if picked in the last 3 runs [GATED]. Evolutions need the weapon at level ≥ 6. Taken transformations never return, which also gives L24's "not the first pick's kind for the same weapon", since each weapon has one evolution and at most one fusion. **Sealed 4th card (D-31):** a qualifying fusion that is still locked (`FRHOfferPool::SealedFusions`, filled by `AppendFusions` from locked defs) is appended last as `bSealed`. `Choose` refuses it, auto-pick never picks it, and the card shows `Transform.Sealed` at 55 % opacity. **L24 (D-32):** `bSecondTransformHand`, off by default (GATED on bot level data), at `SecondTransformLevel` 24. **Path transformations replace the stat Legendaries (D-33)** in practice: the Legendary is now only the fallback when no transformation qualifies at all (pools without transforms). **Run memory:** `URHOfferFlow::SetRecentTransforms` takes the last-3-runs set; meta-ui fills it at run start, and it is empty until then. **Refund-as-Reroll:** `URHRunState::RefundModifiers` (+1 Renegotiate per removed modifier, `OnModifiersRefunded`). `URHOfferFlow::ApplyToHero` passes weapons' `RHEvolutions::IsModifierCompatible` (step 4, picked up with `__has_include`) as the capability predicate; without it every modifier fits. The weapon instance only reforges into defs with `IsFusion()`, so weapons widens `Reforge` for evolutions; the instance trims its own modifiers on `OnModifiersRefunded`. EN rows are added (ST_Fusions `Transform.<Path>.*` / `Evolve.<Weapon>.*`, ST_UI `Transform.*`); zh is loc-zh's. Tests: `RogueHero.Transforms.{Defs,RefundAsReroll,L16Hand,L16Flow}`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Build depth step 6: offer extensions E1–E7 + 12 omens (`build_depth_spec.md` §2.3, D-35, D-36).** Every GDD §5.6 rule stays: the 5-card hand, the guaranteed slot, ≥ 2 of 5 from the pair after L8, and the anti-frustration rules. **E1:** blessing weight × (1 + 0.4 × owned in its branch); once a Keeper has a branch with ≥ 2 owned, its other branches weigh × 0.8. Branch = the `Branch.<Path>.<Branch>` tag in the def's Tags, and the 18 tags are registered in `Config/Tags/RHBuildDepth.ini`. **Deviation:** Shade's branch C is keyed `Branch.Shade.Economy`, not the spec's `Branch.Shade.Ledger`, because LEDGER is Shade's designation and fiction is never a key (TDD §3; `RogueHero.Text.FictionNotKeys`). Its name stays "Ledger" in ST_Blessings (`Branch.Shade.Economy.Name`). Step 5 tags the blessings, so until then E1/E2 are dormant. **E2:** `Offer.Rule.Capstone` (OfferRules or Tags) is offered only with ≥ 2 of its branch owned, at Rare+. **E4 cross cards (D-35):** behind `URHOfferTuning::bCrossCards`, **off by default** (GATED), with spec numbers L10 / 10 % / ≤ 1 per 3 levels / Rare+. `URHOfferGenerator::TryInsertCross` runs after the hand is built, on level-ups only: it replaces the lowest-weight card among slots 2–5 that no rule placed, never one whose loss would break "≥ 2 of 5 from the pair", and the cross card doesn't count toward it. The Crossed Lines omen switches it on from L6 at × 2. **E5 duo:** `Offer.Rule.Duo` or a `Blessing.Duo.*` id, with both Keepers' `God.*` tags. It is offered only after L8, only for that committed pair, Rare+, weight × 1.5, at most once (its MaxRank). A duo pick gives the pair's other Keeper +1 Favor (`URHRunState::ApplyOfferCard`). **E6 run memory (D-36):** `bRunMemory`, **off by default** (GATED), × 0.8. `URHOfferFlow::SetRecentBlessings` / `SetRecentTransforms` take meta-ui's last-3-runs sets and are empty until meta fills them. **E3 omens:** `URHOmenDef` (primary type `RHOmen`; fallback `DA_Omen_<Id>_CodeFallback`, 12 as in `build_depth_text.md` §5). `RHOmens::RollForRun` rolls one at run start in `ARHGameMode::StartRunState`. The roll is deterministic from the seed (a daily run shares it), uses its own hash so no run stream shifts, and skips omens whose content the pool lacks (Favoured Work without branches, Old Alliance without duos, Spare Fitting without a second weapon). Run tools apply at the roll. Hero stat mods (source `Omen`) and **E7**'s second weapon apply in `ARHGameMode::ApplyOmen`. Offer mods reach the generator as `FRHOfferContext::Omen`: Fine Parts / Heavy Arms rarity floors; Quiet Keepers no Bids + post-L8 blessings +1 rarity; Old Alliance's duo is forced into L9; Bounty makes chests 4 cards. `IchorMult` (Dry Spell, meta-ui banking) and `SpawnRoleMult` (Swarm Day, spawn director) are read by their owners. Dev: `rh.Omens 0` turns omens off (bot sims); `rh.Omen <Id>` forces one. The Shrine row / banner text (`Omen.Label`, `Omen.Banner`, `RHOmens::GetName/GetDescription`, plus `Omen.BranchArg` "{0} · {1}") is in ST_World; the UI is meta-ui's / hub's. The re-roll Discovery calls `RHOmens::Roll(Seed, Pool, Start, 1)`. Tests: `RogueHero.Offers.{Omens,BranchMomentum,DuoAndCross,OmenFlow}`.
- 2026-10-20 — **v0.2 Niagara pipeline, step 1: `Niagara` module dependency + `RH.Vfx.Niagara`.** `RogueHero.Build.cs` (lent by the scheduler; meta-ui informed) adds the runtime `Niagara` module. The engine plugin is `EnabledByDefault`, so the `.uproject` is unchanged. `RH.Vfx.Niagara` (default 0) selects the combat-VFX renderer: 0 = today's ISM layers (`M_VFX_*`), 1 = NS_ systems, falling back to ISM wherever an NS_ asset is missing. Plan for the next steps: an editor-only `RogueHeroEditor` module authors the NS_ systems headless, through `FNiagaraStackGraphUtilities` (`AddScriptModuleToStack`, `SetDynamicInputForFunctionInput` with the engine's `SelectVector4_FromArray` / `Get*ArrayCount` dynamic inputs, `SetLinkedParameterValueForFunctionInput` to User parameters) and `FNiagaraEditorUtilities::AddEmitterToSystem`. Every tunable becomes a User parameter, so it's set from C++ with no rapid-iteration constants. Each ISM layer becomes one array-fed sprite emitter with the same data, then the perf A/B runs.
- 2026-10-20 — **`RogueHeroEditor` editor-only module (skeleton).** Scheduler decision: one shared tooling module, never folded into the runtime behind `WITH_EDITOR`. `.uproject` entry `{ RogueHeroEditor, Type Editor, LoadingPhase Default }`; `RogueHeroEditor.Target.cs` `ExtraModuleNames += RogueHeroEditor`; the game target (`RogueHero.Target.cs`) is untouched. Shipping builds without the module (verified with `Build.bat RogueHero Win64 Shipping`). Folders are include roots: `Niagara/` (weapons session: NS_ authoring commandlet) and `Skeletal/` (statuses session: skeletal / animation build library). Shared `RogueHeroEditor.Build.cs` dependencies:
  - Public: Core, CoreUObject, Engine.
  - Private, shared: UnrealEd, AssetTools, AssetRegistry, RogueHero.
  - Private, `Niagara/`: Niagara, NiagaraCore, NiagaraEditor.
  - Private, `Skeletal/`: MeshDescription, StaticMeshDescription, SkeletalMeshDescription, SkeletalMeshUtilitiesCommon, AnimationCore.

  No startup code (`FDefaultModuleImpl`). The `.uproject` and the editor target return to "no owner" after this commit.

The token is `#Status_Vigil` in the hub, or `#Status_Breach` / `#Status_BreachQuota` in the arena, where the `heat` key comes from `URHHeatSelection::GetRunHeat`. The partner-site localization file is `Docs/Platform/rich_presence.vdf` (english + schinese). Names follow the string tables (Vigil 守夜镇, the Breach 裂口, Quota 加征). It never uses "The Yard" (a Narrative §3 clue) or "god" (Narrative §2), and `RogueHero.Platform.PresenceTokens` checks both and that every sent token exists in both languages. It is a no-op when Steam is off.
- 2026-10-20 — **Steam scaffold, piece 5: Steam Deck.** **Detection:** env `SteamDeck=1` or `-SteamDeck`. **Glyphs:** `rh.Input.PadGlyphs`: auto shows Deck labels on a Deck (bumpers / triggers / stick clicks read L1 R1 L2 R2 L3 R3, `ST_UI Glyph.Deck.*` en + zh), 1 forces Xbox, 2 forces Deck. KBM vs pad still follows the last-used device. **Text, measured at 1280 × 800:** the engine DPI curve is 0.74 there, so the 11 px label step renders 10.9 px. A global `ApplicationScale` of 1.15 pushes the five-card level-up row off both edges (captured), and the row stops fitting above about 1.08. **Deviation:** Deck-safe text is therefore two parts: `rh.UI.DeckScale` 1.05 (global) plus a font floor of 13 in `RHUIStyle::Type` / `Font` on a Deck only, so the smallest text renders 13.5 px with no layout growth. Desktop is unchanged, and the floor is a two-line touch in meta-ui's `RHUIStyle.cpp`. `RogueHero.Platform.DeckTextScale` holds the smallest step at ≥ 12 px and the scale at ≤ 1.08. **Layout check:** the arena HUD and level-up fit at 1280 × 800. **Hook (UI owner):** the hub's menu column overlaps the sixth Shrine portrait at 16:10, already at scale 1.0, so the hub layout needs a 16:10 rule. Captures are in the notes folder under `steam_deck/`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Steam scaffold, piece 6: daily leaderboard hook.** No daily-run system exists yet (tiers owns daily seeded runs), so this is the API they call once at a daily run's end: `URHPlatformSubsystem::SubmitDailyScore(DateKey, Score)`. It writes to the board `RH_Daily_<YYYYMMDD>` (keep-best, descending, number) through `IOnlineLeaderboards::WriteLeaderboards` + `FlushLeaderboards`. The Steam OSS finds or creates the board on first write. The date key is UTC, so the daily seed and the board agree on the day boundary (`TodayKeyUtc`, `IsValidDateKey`). `RH.Platform.Daily <score> [YYYYMMDD]` submits from the console. It is a no-op without Steam (smoke: logged, 0 errors). **Hook (tiers):** call it with the same key the daily seed is built from (snippet in `Docs/Platform/Steam.md`). What the score counts is the daily design's call.
- 2026-10-20 — **RC gate gains a Shipping compile (`Tools\BuildShipping.bat`).** The v0.1 package exposed test files compiled outside `WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS`, which the editor build cannot catch. The RC gate is now `Tools\Build.bat` + `Tools\BuildNonUnity.bat` + `Tools\BuildShipping.bat` (game target, Win64 Shipping, compile and link only, about 45 s warm) + tests + smoke. It is run before every RC / packaging report. First run, with the Steam scaffold (OnlineSubsystemSteam enabled, Platform/ tests guarded): `SHIPPING: PASS`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Niagara pipeline step 2: NS_ systems authored headless from C++ (`RogueHeroEditor/Niagara`, `Tools\BuildNiagara.bat`).** The `RHNiagaraBuild` commandlet (`URHNiagaraBuildCommandlet` plus the `FRHNiagaraAuthor` wrapper) creates `/Game/RH/VFX/Niagara/NS_RH_CombatVfx` from scratch on every run; the code is the source of truth. It uses only exported NiagaraEditor API:
  - `UNiagaraSystemFactoryNew::InitializeSystem` and `UNiagaraEmitterFactoryNew::InitializeEmitter` (empty), then `FNiagaraEditorUtilities::AddEmitterToSystem`.
  - `FNiagaraStackGraphUtilities::AddScriptModuleToStack`, `AddParameterModuleToStack`, `GetOrCreateStackFunctionInputOverridePin`, `SetDynamicInputForFunctionInput` and `SetLinkedParameterValueForFunctionInput`.
  - Static switches through `GetStackFunctionStaticSwitchPins`.

  User parameters go straight into the exposed store, because `AddUserParameterToSystem` builds a system view model that asserts with no message asset key on a never-opened system. On a missing input the log lists the module's real inputs and switches (enum values included), so the build log documents the engine scripts. **One emitter per `RHCombatVfxSubsystem` layer** (Glow, Bolt, Flame, Scorch, Smoke), each a CPU array-fed sprite emitter:
  - Emitter update: `EmitterState` plus `SpawnPerFrame` with Spawn Count = `GetPositionArrayCount(User.<L>_Pos)`.
  - Particle spawn: one Set Parameters module sets Position / Color / SpriteSize / SpriteAlignment / SpriteFacing / DynamicMaterialParameter from `Select*FromArray(User.<L>_*, Direct Set, index = ReturnExecIndex)`, with Lifetime **0**. `ParticleState` then kills each particle on its first update, so it draws exactly one frame; 0.001 left a double image.
  - Rendering: a sprite renderer with custom alignment and custom facing.

  "Arc" is renamed **Bolt** (the emitter and its User arrays). **Materials:** `build_vfx_materials.py` also writes `M_VFX_*_NS` sprite variants: the same graphs, with each custom-data input read from the particle (colour rgb + `DynamicMaterialParameter`; Glow / Bolt strength in .w because `ParticleColor`'s first output has no alpha). Test `RogueHero.CombatVfx.NiagaraAsset` checks the 5 emitters and all 30 User arrays.
- 2026-10-20 — **Niagara pipeline step 3: combat VFX drawn through `NS_RH_CombatVfx` behind `RH.Vfx.Niagara 1`.** `URHCombatVfxSubsystem` spawns one `UNiagaraComponent` (on its light-holder actor; inactive until the cvar is on). Each frame it converts the layers' cards to sprites and pushes them with `UNiagaraDataInterfaceArrayFunctionLibrary::SetNiagaraArray*` (Pos / Col / Size / Align / Face / Dyn per layer); the ISM layers then sync empty. The conversion is 1:1: an engine Plane card (local X = length, local Y = width, normal +Z) becomes a sprite with alignment = −Y, facing = +Z and size = (X, Y) extent, so the `_NS` materials see the same UV layout. Everything drawn by the subsystem ports with it: Storm bolts and Thunderclap / Storm-ultimate strikes, Burn ground fire with Ember Trail and the Forge ultimate's patches, embers, sparks, smoke, scorch and flashes. The enemy rounds' halo and white-hot ring hand their cards to the same Glow layer (`AddFrameGlow`). The rounds' bodies (M_Tracer spheres, dark centre, outline) and the trail stay ISM: those are the bullet renderer, whose Niagara swap-out (`RH.Bullets.Renderer`) is a separate decision (entry above). **Not ported:** ImpactFX's per-kill FX (`Combat/RHImpactFXSubsystem`, owned by the statuses session); they can hand cards to `AddFrameGlow` the same way. **Parity:** captures `nsab0` / `nsab1` (same scene, cvar 0 / 1, shaders warm) show the same rounds, trail flames, scorch, bolts and glows. Watch out: after any `_NS` material rebuild, the first runs show blank layers until shaders compile ("Preparing Shaders"). Default stays 0 until the step-4 A/B. `RH.Vfx` prints the renderer in use and per-layer counts.
- 2026-10-20 — **Outfall arena boss (full_game_specs §1.5, D-3), P1 "Pumps".** Keys: `Boss` + arena `Outfall` (`URHBossOutfallSubsystem`, `FRHBossOutfallBrain`, `URHBossOutfallDef` / `DA_Enemy_Boss_Outfall`, named fallback `MakeOutfallFallback`); "the Floodgate" is FText only (`ST_Enemies` `Enemy.Boss.Outfall.Name`, requested from loc-zh, en + zh). World-free brain + tests (RogueHero.BossOutfall.*), as the Warden. The stationary frame and three pump housings are enemy hulls; the bar is an effective HP: in P1 the frame takes `FrameArmorMult` 0.15 and each destroyed pump knocks `PumpKillShare` (1/3 of the P1 band) off once. Every `FlushInterval` one live pump flushes its channel (the hero's channel first): a red wall line runs sluice → grate over `FlushTelegraph`, then a 4 s surge doubles that channel's current and its front (depth 180) hits a hero inside the channel once; a destroyed pump never flushes again. **Addition to the spec (logged here):** each live pump also spits an aimed odd fan (3, one shot on the hero, `FanOffset`), so a still hero on an island is never safe in P1 (playtest 3 P0 still-hero rule; `RogueHero.BossOutfall.StillHero`). The kill raises `URHBossSubsystem::OnBossDefeatedNative` (the game mode's one boss-win signal, so the WIN / run summary needs no new hook). **Water state:** `URHOutfallWater` (Boss/Outfall) holds the channel multipliers, surge fronts, flood line and drain pull; `GetCurrentAt` / `IsFlooded` are for the Outfall arena's current physics and water render (Roadmap v0.2 item 2). Swap-out point: an adapter if that arena ships its own water API. Numbers are placeholders (tuning GATED). Codex hook pending meta-ui (`NotifyArenaBossKilled`, requested).
- 2026-10-20 — **Outfall arena boss P2 "Rising water".** The basin floods in `FloodSteps` bands of `FloodBand` from the wall inward (`URHOutfallWater::FloodRadius`), each preceded by a red edge circle `FloodTelegraph` (1.5 s) ahead. Dredges (statuses' `Lurker` role once `DA_Enemy_Lurker` exists; a named Scrapwing stand-in until then) surface in the flooded band near the hero's bearing, or in the nearest channel before the first band. The frame's intake fires **staggered pairs** of radial rings: each ring is laid so one slot points exactly at the hero and its 3-slot lane opens whole slots away (≥ 1 slot clear, ≤ ~45°), the second one `RingStaggerDelay` later with its lane on the other side of the hero (the Warden's dodge-lane rule, `FRHBossOutfallBrain::AlignedLaneAngle`). Tests: RogueHero.BossOutfall.RisingWater, StillHero now at 50 % HP too. "Islands shrink" is modelled as the flood line moving inward over them; what being in the water does (slow, Dredge cover) is the Outfall arena's, read through `IsFlooded`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Outfall arena boss P3 "The gate cracks".** Below 30 % the flood drains (`FloodRadius` back to the wall) and a `DrainPull` of 300 uu/s toward the grate comes on everywhere in the basin (`URHOutfallWater::GetCurrentAt`; the Outfall arena's current physics applies it, so the hero fights while being drawn in). The intake adds a two-arm turning spiral to the aimed ring pairs (now every 2.6 s). Every `LinesmanInterval` a Linesman pair (statuses' `Tether` role; a named Courser stand-in until `DA_Enemy_Tether`) is anchored on the island nearest the hero, `LinesmanSpan` apart. The kill is the WIN (P1 line). Tests: RogueHero.BossOutfall.GateCracks; StillHero at 20 % too (the spiral is continuous, not a volley, as the Warden's).

The Keep's wall and backdrop frame it.

- 2026-10-20 — **D-31 … D-37 decided as recommended** (scheduler decision, full-auto): sealed fusion card at L16; L24 second hand (GATED); Path transformations replace the stat Legendaries; starter pool = 18 branch entries, Contracts nodes (3) / (4) → branch cores, capstones by Discovery; cross cards from L10 at 10 % (GATED); run memory × 0.8 (GATED); reactions need a blessing or the imbue of both Keepers. **GDD §5.4 imbue *Look* paragraph rewritten** to match Art §3.2 / CLAUDE.md rule 11: cool core, Keeper-colour tail + glow for every Path (Forge amber and Quicksilver green included); the old "Storm / Shade recolour the core" and "Forge never amber / Quicksilver colourless" rules are retired.
- 2026-10-20 — **Daily-run determinism: spawns get their own stream (fix) + test.** **Found:** `ARHGameMode::SimStep` passed `Rng` (seeded with `Seed`) to both `URHSpawnDirector::Step` and the hit resolution (crit rolls). So the daily spawn timeline (positions, mixes, elite affixes, drop-ins) shifted with how the player fought, and two players on the same date got different runs, against `RHDailyRun`'s promise. **Fix (GM, `Core/RHGameMode.cpp`):** the director draws from `URHRunState::GetStream(ERHRunStream::Spawns)` (the stream reserved for it, until now unused), and combat keeps `Rng`. Spawn sequences change once for every seed; no tuning changes. **Test:** `RogueHero.E2E.Daily.SameDateSameRun`. The same date gives the same 8-minute spawn timeline, also with extra combat rolls between steps, and the same offers for the same choices (levels 2–7, real content pool). The weather plan is the same too. A different date changes all three, and two weeks of dailies do not share one weather plan. The test holds the stream contract; the game-mode wiring itself is checked by review, since the run-world fixture has no hero. `Tests/E2E/RHE2EFixture.h` now includes `Engine/GameInstance.h` itself (it holds a `TStrongObjectPtr<UGameInstance>`; the include gate caught it).
- 2026-10-20 — **RC gate as one script (`Tools/RCGate.ps1`), run by QA on every main merge the scheduler reports (from v0.2).** In order: `Build.bat` (rc 0 and 0 compiler warnings), `BuildNonUnity.bat` (files changed vs main; `-AllFiles` for all), `BuildShipping.bat`, the headless `RogueHero` tests, then `Smoke.ps1`. It stops at the first failure (`RC GATE: FAIL at <step> (<hash>)`) and the report line is `RC GATE: PASS (<hash>)`. First run on 7a7fff2: build 0 warnings, include gate PASS, SHIPPING PASS, 328/328 tests, smoke PASS.
- 2026-10-20 — **Build depth step 4: the ten weapon evolutions (`build_depth_spec.md` §1.4; numbers GATED).** `URHWeaponDef::MakeEvolution(Outer, "<Weapon>")` reforges the base factory like a fusion (`DA_Weapon_Evolve_<Weapon>`). Common rules:
  - **Same Tag**, so upgrades and RunState still find the weapon; `Fusion.bIsEvolution`; text `ST_Fusions` `Evolve.<Weapon>.Name/.Desc` (EN from `build_depth_text.md` §1.2, zh by loc-zh).
  - The §1.4 **capability change** applies (§3 caps; `RogueHero.Evolutions.Defs` asserts them).
  - `RHEvolutions` (`Combat/RHEvolutions`) caches the ten defs (`Find`, `GetAll`). `ApplyToHero(Hero, Weapon, OutRefunded)` evolves the owned weapon through `URHWeaponInstance::Evolve`: level, slot and compatible modifiers are kept; refused modifiers are removed, returned, and paid back as one Reroll each (`URHRunState::AddTools`). The toast is `ST_UI` `Transform.Refund`, shown by UI.
  - `RH.Give.Evolve <Weapon>` is the debug pick.
  - Integration's `URHTransformDef` (Kind = Weapon) wraps these defs as its weapon-def swap and calls `RHEvolutions::ApplyToHero`. Not built against the header yet: it isn't published.

  **Behaviours**, built on the existing fusion plumbing (`ERHFusionBehaviour` gains OrbitLaunch / EmbedBlast / Carpet / Murmuration / PulseWall / Vortex / Singularity; no Actors; bullets stay data):
  - **Railstorm**: 6 orbiting blades (new `OrbitBlade` look); each "shot" launches a boomerang blade (pierce 3) that returns, one every 0.4 s.
  - **Flechette**: 8 pellets; each hit lodges a still charge (a shell with no flight) that blasts after 0.5 s (r 60).
  - **Blade Halo**: 4 contact blades with no bolts, orbit radius × (1 + 0.8 × move speed / 600).
  - **Sweep Beam**: a `MeleeArc` sweep, 950 uu long, 120°, 1.5 s, aimed at the densest side, with a new `SweepBeam` look that draws the full beam. Its pierce, extra-lance and size steps become damage steps, because a sweep widens its arc per pierce and the 12 pierce made it a 7 s, 360° spin.
  - **Carpet**: every detonation queues 4 more blasts 150 uu apart, 0.12 s apart (`Defer` gained a per-spawn lifetime).
  - **Cyclone**: a 360° spin twice a second, only while the hero moves.
  - **Murmuration**: missiles live 6 s with pierce 200 and pick a new target after each hit.
  - **Pulse Wall**: each volley is one zone that grows to r 600 over 0.6 s, holds 3 s and knocks radially outward (`bRadialKnock`).
  - **Vortex**: spiral bullets curl at 70°/s; the end of flight leaves a pull zone (r 260, 1.2 s, radial knock −180), at most one per weapon at a time.
  - **Singularity**: single mines no longer trigger on approach. When 3 are on the floor they merge at their centre into one mine (instant arm, pull r 900 for 1.5 s, blast r 380, ×3 damage). A lone mine still goes off after 8 s.

  Visuals keep each weapon's cool hue (R2); the density LOD and the 6 000-bullet cap are unchanged. Tests: `RogueHero.Evolutions.Defs` (10 defs, Tag, caps), `.Refund` (Railstorm drops Homing, Blade Halo and Sweep Beam drop Pierce, no double evolve, wrong weapon refused), `.Behaviour` (one sim check per evolution). `RogueHero.Text.Bilingual` waits on loc-zh's zh for the 21 new rows, sent with the hash.

All of it is data on the DA_s.

  - **Matrix:** `Docs/Design/modifier_weapon_matrix.md`, the 20 × 10 audit generated from the test log. Every weapon keeps at least 9 modifiers (the 9 universal ones), and Homing / Orbit Lock / Boomerang / Fork / Mirror / Spread Wave never reach the drones.

A weapon missing from the pool (tools) keeps the old behaviour.

    - Parting beyond 900 resets it, so it pulses again on reconnect.

    - Killing either Linesman cuts it (`Kill` unlinks the pair).

Move speed × the multiplier while it lasts; overlapping slows keep the lowest multiplier and the longest remaining time; dashes are not slowed.

The trigger kinds are StatusMeets, CritOnStatus, DeathWith, DashThrough and Passive (Afterglow). **Rule D-37:** a pair is on while the run holds a blessing, which is also what imbues, from both Keepers; a duo blessing counts for both. **R4:** at most 1 fire per (enemy, reaction) per second, at most 30 reaction FX per second (a token bucket; over it the reaction still applies, the FX is skipped and counted), at most 48 fires per step. **First trigger in a run:** "{Name}: {Toast}" on the HUD Keeper-line slot (`OnTierLine`, tier 0). **Looks (R2):** cool core (the hue lifted toward white) with the two Keepers' hues as rim / tail, via `URHCombatVfxSubsystem`; Forge amber appears only on Burn ground. **Duo blessings:** `Blessing.Duo.<Pair>`, Rare, rank 1; God is the first Keeper and Tags carry both. Owning one amplifies its reaction (`Duo*` Params). `RHBlessings::Find` falls back to them so cards / effects resolve. **Hooks used:** `URHEffectManager::OnStatusesApplied` (new, batched per step like `OnImbuedHit`; 6 lines in the manager); one `URHReactionDriver` effect receives crits / status deaths / dashes / hits / ticks; no Combat or Enemies edits. **RH.Stats:** a `reactions/s` line (report only). **Strings:** `ST_World Reaction.*` and `ST_Blessings Blessing.Duo.*` en from `build_depth_text.md` §3–4, provisional zh for loc-zh. **Approximations until owners' hooks land (requested):** Steam does not consume both statuses (needs a statuses `RemoveStatus`); Flare detonates a GATED `BurnLeft` stand-in (needs remaining Burn per enemy); Deep Ledger / Cold Storage Souls and Cremation's +5 % execute wait on Shade's soul / execute API; Repossession's 20 % and Lightspeed's 2 % dash refund need `ARHHero::RefundDashCooldown`; ricochet triggers (Live Rail, Black Ice, Afterglow) need a ricochet event from weapons; Glide's water / ice immunity waits on the Outfall current API; Afterburner's speed applies on any dash, not only near Burning enemies. **Tests** (`RogueHero.Reactions.*`): data (15 + 15, every pair once, strings and defs resolve), the D-37 gate plus duo Favor, every reaction firing on its trigger once per enemy per second and again after the cooldown, effect spot checks (Plasma spread, Conduction cap 6, Exposure +40 %, Repossession HP gate), the FX cap (40 in a step → ≤ 30 FX), and the duo offer after L8 only, committed only, once.
