  - **Shell:**
    - `RHEnemy::StepArtillery` lobs ONE shell every `ShellPeriod` (3 s) while the hero is within `ShellRange` (1 900), at `RHEnemy::PredictLanding` (the hero's position led by its velocity × flight × `ShellLead`).
    - The shell flies `ShellFlightTime` (1.2 s) on an arc, drawn as a sphere in the A1 enemy-round hue #FF2D8A. Under it, a red landing circle r `ShellRadius` (250) fills inside → out (`AddRingFill`, the Breaker's fill) — that's the tell.
    - On impact it hits a hero inside the circle ("Slagger shells").
  - **Puddle:** each impact leaves a slag puddle for `PuddleTime` (3 s): a red rim (the telegraph look) over a dim molten fill. It ticks `PuddleDamage` every 0.5 s on a hero inside ("slag puddles"); a dash ignores it.
    - The puddle has its own look, not the weapons' `AddFire`, which is reserved for the player's Burn amber.
    - Shells and puddles are data pools in `URHEnemySubsystem`, not actors.
  - **Water (open):** "evaporates in channels" waits for the Outfall water query; until then every puddle burns.
  - **Data:** `MakeArtillery` → `DA_Enemy_Artillery`; tag `Enemy.Artillery`; "Slagger" / zh 抛渣机 (proposal, loc-zh to review). Placeholder numbers (GATED tuning).
  - **Model:** `SM_Enemy_Artillery` (1 826 / 1 484 / 1 204 tris): a hexagonal Keep-stone plinth and turntable, a ceramic furnace body with a chimney, a Forge guide and a front grille, and a cocked throwing arm ending in a slag crucible (dim molten glow at guide level), with a counterweight and rams. Plus the hooded magenta optic, the Ichor cell + seal, decals and rivets. Planted, so ISM without VAT. Top-down a hex disc with a long ladle arm.
  - Test `RogueHero.Enemies.Outfall.ArtilleryShell`.
- 2026-10-20 — **v0.2 Outfall timeline structure: `DA_SpawnTimeline_Outfall`** (full_game_specs §1.6; CORE structure, GATED numbers).
  - **Factory:** `URHSpawnTimeline::MakeOutfall`, baked by `Tools\BakeEnemyData.bat`. It carries the spec's beats, role arrivals and caps: Swarm-only cap 80 → Tank 1:30 → Dredge 2:30 → elite wave (2) 5:00 → Slagger + drop-ins 6:00 → Sluice flush 7:30 → Breaker + Linesman, cap 220, 9:00 → elite wave (3) + Tallymen 10:00 → Flood tide 12:30 → elite wave (4, affixed), cap 320, 15:00 → Linesman net 17:30 → the Floodgate 20:00. No Turret anywhere.
  - **Placeholders (balance tunes):** every rate (a 1.6 → 8.0 ramp, ×0.6 in Threat blocks), mix share (skill checks ×2 in Threat), wave mix and event size. **Interpretation:** Coursers keep their Breach arrival at 3:00, because the spec keeps `Rusher` in the mix without giving it a row. Heat: the Bulwark soft cap, H2 and H4 carry over; H1 (the Pylon fan) and H3 (companions) are left empty until the Outfall's Heat design exists.
  - **Scaling bases:** `URHSpawnTimeline::HPBase` / `DmgBase` (Breach 1, Outfall 1.4 / 1.2), so `HPMultAt` = authored × (HPBase + HPPerMinute · min).
  - **Arena events:** new `ERHSpawnEventKind::Arena` + `FRHSpawnEvent::ArenaEvent` (`SluiceFlush` 30 s, `FloodTide` 45 s). The core fires them like any event (Threat + Breath) and `URHSpawnDirector::OnArenaEvent(Name, Duration)` broadcasts them. **Open:** the flush and the flood are map mechanics. Whoever builds `L_Arena_Outfall` binds `OnArenaEvent`; until then the director logs "no listener" and the beat is an empty Threat window.
  - **Linesman net:** a `Ring` event of four Linesmen at 1 100 uu, each spawning its partner beside it, so four pairs (four beams) close in around the hero.
  - **Dredge drop-in:** new `ERHDropInKind::DredgeSurface` (Dredge in place of the Pylon perch). The bite ring is queued under the hero; the telegraph is max(`SurfaceLag`, `DredgeTelegraph` 1 s) and draws the Dredge's own tightening red ring. On landing it bites at once (Bite source), then stays surfaced for `SurfacedTime` and dives like any Dredge. Hero-side readers see it as a timed blast zone, like the Breaker slam. The approach log gains "Dredge surface %d".
  - **Arena selection:** `URHSpawnDirector::GetRunArena` reads `?Arena=Outfall` on the travel URL, else `-RHArena=`, else Breach. With Outfall the director loads `DA_SpawnTimeline_Outfall`; failing that it uses `MakeOutfall` with the Lurker / Tether / Artillery defs. The Shrine's arena selector (§1.1) is the hub owner's; it only needs to add `?Arena=Outfall`.
  - Tests `RogueHero.Enemies.Outfall.Timeline` (structure, scaling, arena events fired once in order, Dredge drop-in under the hero with a ≥ 1 s tell) and `RogueHero.Enemies.Outfall.DredgeDropIn` (timed zone, surfaces after the tell, then targetable).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon evolutions hooked into integration's transformation framework (`Build/RHTransformDef`, now on main).** `RHEvolutions.cpp` registers the ten factories at static init with `RHTransforms::RegisterFallbackWeapon("Transform.<Weapon>", MakeEvolution)`, so the fallback `URHTransformDef` (Kind = Weapon) carries its `WeaponSwap`. `URHWeaponInstance::Reforge` (called by `RHTransforms::ApplyToHero`) now also accepts this weapon's evolution: it routes through `Evolve`, which drops the modifiers the new caps refuse, while `RHTransforms::ApplyToHero` refunds them through `URHRunState::RefundModifiers`. For that refund, `RHEvolutions::IsModifierCompatible(ModifierId, NewDef)` is the §3 check by id, for `ApplyToHero`'s `IsCompatible` argument; integration passes it from the L16 / L24 hand. `RogueHero.Transforms.*` pass with the registered defs.
- 2026-10-20 — **The Outfall timeline's 20:00 calls the Floodgate; status hooks for the duo reactions.**
  - **Floodgate:** with `?Arena=Outfall`, `URHSpawnDirector::SpawnBoss` calls balance's `URHBossOutfallSubsystem::SpawnBoss(HPMult)` (stationary, no spawn point) instead of the Warden.
  - **Status hooks** (build depth §4, hub's step 7): `IRHStatusTarget` gains two non-pure methods, both implemented by `URHEnemySubsystem`:
    - `RemoveStatus(Enemy, Tag)`: Steam consumes Burn and Chill. Removing Chill also thaws a Freeze, because the Chill stacks were spent on it.
    - `GetRemainingStatusDamage(Enemy, Tag)`: for Burn, dps × time left, before Shock; 0 otherwise. Flare uses it.
  - Test `RogueHero.Status.RemoveAndRemaining`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Duo reactions wired to the statuses hooks (efdcc0b): Steam consumes, Flare detonates the real Burn.** Steam calls `IRHStatusTarget::RemoveStatus` for Burn and Chill (which also thaws a Freeze) before its blast. Flare detonates `GetRemainingStatusDamage(Burn)` ×1.5 and spends the Burn. Both are detected at compile time (`requires`), so `Build/Reactions` builds before and after that merge, keeping the GATED `BurnLeft` stand-in until then. Still open: Souls and the per-enemy execute bonus (tiers' Shade API; statuses notes a Mark re-apply with a larger magnitude raises the threshold), the hero dash refund, the ricochet event and Outfall water.
- 2026-10-20 — **Batched ricochet event (build depth step 7 duo reactions; balance's Carom / Relay Chain).** Whenever a bullet spends a bounce, `URHBulletSubsystem::NoteRicochet` queues an `FRHRicochetEvent` on `URHEffectManager::QueueRicochet`. That covers all five sites: projectile ricochet, hunter bounce, expiry bounce, sweep re-swing and shell hop. The event carries `{Enemy bounced off, Pos, WeaponSlot, Spec, Target, BouncesLeft, BouncesMade}`, capped at 1 024 per step like shots. The next `DispatchStep` delivers one `URHEffect::OnRicochets` batch to effects subscribed to the new `ERHEffectEvents::Ricochets` (`NumEventTypes` 9 → 10). Effects react from there; nothing reacts inside the bullet loop, which keeps the sim deterministic and cheap. Test: `RogueHero.Ricochet.Event`. Build note: a new source file can be missing from the unity blobs when UBT's cached makefile misses it, so its tests silently don't exist. Delete `Intermediate/Build/Win64/RogueHeroEditor/Development/Makefile.bin`, or run `Tools\BuildNonUnity.bat`, after adding files.
- 2026-10-20 — **v0.2 accessibility (Roadmap backlog "accessibility"; scheduler brief).** All in `URHUserSettings` (GameUserSettings.ini, per machine, not the save). (1) **Remapping:** `Core/RHInputBindings` slot table (12 slots: Move ×4, Dash, Intercession, Restart; level-up Prev / Next / Renegotiate / Hold / Refuse), one key per device; `URHInputConfig::MapBindings` builds the three mapping contexts from it (saved override, else default) and `RHBindings::ApplyToWorld` rebuilds them live, so every prompt follows. Fixed, never remappable: Pause / Back (Esc, pad Menu, pad B in menus), Accept, the left stick, arrow keys as a second set, Q / E as second Prev / Next. A key already used in the same group swaps; reserved / wrong-device keys are refused. New Controls screen (Settings → Controls): capture on Accept / click, Esc / Menu cancels, Reset to defaults. Limitation: if CommonUI routes pad B to Back before the capture handler sees it, B can only be bound in play from the console (`RH.Bind`). (2) **Text size** 100 / 125 / 150 %: `RHUIStyle::Font / Type / FamilyFont` and the key-glyph badge multiply by it; widgets take it when built, the Settings screen rebuilds itself on a change, other screens and the HUD on their next open / level load. (3) **Motion:** screen shake (+ FOV punch), hit-stop, full-screen flash (`URHUltimateComponent::RequestScreenFlash` gate) and slow-mo toggles; "Reduced" preset = all four off. The world `FRHFeelBudget` follows the settings (`bFollowUserSettings`); plain budgets in the §3.5 tests keep everything on. (4) **Danger-outline assist** (off by default): `URHDangerOutlineSubsystem` draws achromatic white + dark bands round enemy rounds (one-line hook in `URHBulletSubsystem`) and red ground telegraphs (hooks in `URHEnemySubsystem`, `URHBossSubsystem`); boxes stack up (dark, white, red redrawn on top), discs drop under the round. (5) **Colour vision:** `RogueHero.Accessibility.ColourVision` simulates protan / deutan / tritan (Machado 2009) and requires CIELAB ΔE ≥ 20 between enemy rounds / telegraphs and every player round (worst today: telegraph vs Quicksilver dart under deutan, 26.2), ≥ 15 for the two-frame enemy muzzle flash (17.2, same pair). UI strings ST_UI (Settings.* / Controls.*), EN only; zh by loc-zh. Dev commands `RH.UI.Settings`, `RH.UI.Controls [capture <Slot> <KBM|Pad>]`, `RH.Bind`, `RH.A11y`.

Save v4.** New `Meta/RHStoryState` (`RHStory::*`) over the spec §2.1 / §5.1 fields on `URHSaveGame`: `StoryAct`, `StoryFlags`, `RecollectionsRead`, `ArenaWins`, `ArenaHeat`, `FateTide`, `FateShade`, `FateSeen`, `bRefusalUnlocked`. **Acts (§2.2):** I → II on the first Breach win (`RHMeta::ApplyRunEnd` → `RecordArenaWin` with the new `FRHRunSummary::Arena`, None = Breach); II → III ⟨D-5⟩ when the Outfall has a win, all six `Codex.Facet.<Path>` entries are read and ≥ 20 Recollections are read (`URHMetaSubsystem::MarkCodexSeen` → `OnCodexRead`). **Fates (§5.2, ⟨D-6⟩):** Shade goes off the books the first time `Codex.Root.Entry` is read in Act III; Tide returns on the first Sump win after that; the Refusal unlocks once both have happened. Spec deviation: the Shade fate is `ERHKeeperFate::OffBooks`, not `OffLedger` — identifiers never carry a Keeper designation (CLAUDE.md 6, Narrative §13). Default fate `Bound`. **Effects exposed:** `IsPatronAllowed / ResolvePatron` (a Returned Tide is no patron, a saved Tide patron falls back), `HasSecondIntercession` ⟨D-7⟩ (the input + ultimate wiring is its own commit). **Layer gate (§2.3):** `MaxOpenLayer / IsLayerOpen` = ≤ 2 before the first win whatever the Act (non-negotiable 8), 3 in Acts I–II, 4 in Act III; `RHMetaUI::BuildCodexEntries` asks it. **Migration v3 → v4** raise-only: a save with wins is Act II, its Breach wins / best Heat come from `RunsWon` / `HighestHeatWon`, `RecollectionsRead` from `CodexSeen`; `BackfillDerived` re-applies the same for saves whose version was never written. `URHMetaSubsystem::NotifyArenaBossKilled(ArenaKey)` records `Event.ArenaBoss.<Arena>` on the first kill (bestiary entry gate; the arena win itself is counted at run end). Dev: `RH.Story`, `RH.Story.Set`, `RH.Story.Flag`, `RH.Story.Win`, `RH.Story.Read`. Tests `RogueHero.Meta.Story.*`.

  - `URHWeaponInstance::FireVolleyFrom(Origin, Aim, Bullets, SpecOverride)`: one volley of the weapon's pattern from any origin, for Quicksilver Vector and Shade Collection. It skips the cooldown and the Burst / Chain wrappers, uses an optional override spec (e.g. statuses' 25 % spec), and does nothing for orbiters.

  - `URHOutfallWater::IsWater(P)` returns true when the point is flooded (a channel or the flooded ring) or inside a pool registered by `BuildBasin` (lost pavers, the crater pool) through `AddPuddle`. The Dredge swims in all of it.
  - `HandleArenaEvent` is bound to `URHSpawnDirector::OnArenaEvent` at world begin play:

    - `FloodTide` (12:30, 45 s): the water rises from the wall inward, telegraphed, until the islands keep 60 % of their area; then it recedes.

- 2026-10-20 — **The Outfall, M3 (art polish against the gameplay camera).**
  - **Water:** `M_Env_WaterFlow` is lighter and carries more streaks, and a broken grey foam line runs where the water meets the channel walls, so it reads as moving water rather than a dark strip. There is still no warm colour and no bright specular.
  - **The crater:** its centre lost its pavers. The bed shows there, with a small black pool and a ring of charred rubble thrown outward. Lost pavers are bare holes now; the grey puddle discs read as plastic from above.
  - **Ruins on the islands:** Founder ring arches lie flat, half sunk into the paving (top ≤ 14 uu), and the drum courses of a toppled column lie in lines thrown away from the crater (top ≤ 30). Neither is ever cover, and both read from the gameplay camera. The island turbines are dark iron, since warm rust stays off large playfield shapes (Art §3).
  - **Checks:** captures are in the shared notes (`env/outfall_m3_*.png`). BuildNonUnity is clean, tests pass 352/352, and Outfall smoke passes.
- 2026-10-20 — **Build depth step 3, part 1: Path transformations Storm / Forge / Radiance** (build_depth_spec.md §1.3; statuses).
  - **Classes:** `URHEffect_Transform_<Path>` in `Build/Transforms/`, on a shared `URHTransformEffect` base. `RHTransformRegistry.cpp` registers them with integration's fallback defs (`RHTransforms::RegisterFallbackEffect` / `RegisterFallbackParams`, at `ObjectSystemReady`).
  - **Shared base:** derived plain bullet specs, an R4 FX budget (`FxPerSecond` 30), per-enemy 1 s cooldowns, and Keeper-coloured flashes through the weapons' imbue FX.
  - **Debug:** `RH.Transform <Key>` applies one; `RH.Transform.Status` prints the counters. All numbers are GATED Params.
  - **Storm, Overload:**
    - Shock now **stacks** (`RHStatus::MaxShockStacks` 3, `FRHEnemyStatuses::ShockStacks`, read through the new `IRHStatusTarget::GetStatusStacks`); stacks change nothing else.
    - A hit on a 3-stack enemy discharges: 4 jumps within 380 uu at 0.5 × the hit, 1 stack each; the source drops back to 1 stack.
    - A kill on a Shocked enemy chains from its death point, at 0.5 × the first weapon's spec damage. Interpretation: a death has no hit to scale from.
    - Cascades are bounded by the 1 s per-enemy cooldown and 12 per step.
  - **Forge, Kiln:**
    - A burning death raises a slag husk for 4 s, max 8 (the oldest cools first).
    - It fires a cinder bolt (`SpawnExtra`, never recycles) at the nearest enemy within 650 uu every 0.6 s, at 0.2 × the first weapon's spec. Pellet look, so the Forge imbue colours the tail.
    - Each bolt has a 25 % Burn chance, so the fire sustains itself. Interpretation: the spec says only "spits cinder bolts".
    - Drawn as a dark, low slag mound with a Burn-amber seam, vent glow and flame card (status colours, R2), ≤ 70 uu tall, so it never hides a telegraph (R3).
  - **Radiance, Prism:**
    - Each crit fires 3 lances (`SpawnExtra`, `CritLance` archetype) fanned ±22° along the hero → crit line, since the crit records no direction.
    - Each lance does 0.4 × the crit, pierces 2, flies 420 uu, and cannot crit (no cascades). Max 10 splits per step.
  - **Measured in game** (Smoke, favour 9 in each Path, 250 spawned, 35 s): Overload 215 death chains / 485 jumps; Kiln 9 husks / 48 bolts; Prism 8 splits / 24 lances. The R5 DPS budget is for balance's bot pass (spec step 10).
  - Tests `RogueHero.Transforms.Path.*`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Build depth step 3, part 2: Path transformations Tide Glacier, Tide Slack Water, Shade Collection, Quicksilver Vector** (build_depth_spec.md §1.3). This completes all 7. They ship in one commit because the two Tide ones share a file.
  - **Tide, Glacier:**
    - Each newly frozen enemy (any Freeze source, polled each step) is encased in an ice pillar for 3 s, and its Freeze is held that long. Max 12; the oldest shatters first.
    - **Interpretations:**
      - The ice outlives its enemy. A frozen Scrapwing dies to the next bullet, and a pillar tied to it never stood.
      - A pillar shatters after 10 player hits on its living enemy, or on expiry. Measured: 4 hits shattered every pillar within a second under real fire.
    - **Shatter:** 1 × the first weapon's spec within 220 uu, plus 1 Chill stack.
    - **Walls:** new `URHEnemySubsystem::SetObstacles`: circles that walking enemies are pushed out of (the encased enemy at the centre is left). Screenshot: 12 pillars held a mob outside around the hero.
    - **Look:** translucent pale-cyan `M_VFX_Ice` columns with a dim glow cap that brightens as the pillar cracks. The first version washed out the hero; intensity was cut to 0.16–0.3 (R3).
    - **Swap-out (open):** blocking enemy bullets needs weapons' `URHBulletSubsystem::SetEnemyBulletBlockers` (requested 2026-10-20). Until then pillars block enemies, not shots.
  - **Tide (Outfall), Slack Water:**
    - Chill is held (new `URHEnemySubsystem::HoldStatus`) on enemies standing in water: balance's `URHOutfallWater`, `ChannelAt || IsFlooded`.
    - A Frozen death in water shatters for 2 × the Glacier shatter, plus Chill.
    - Pale frost discs sit on the water under chilled enemies. Outside the Outfall it does nothing.
  - **Shade, Collection:**
    - A Marked death rises as a Shade (max 10; the oldest is replaced). Shades orbit the hero at r 150 (70°/s), rising from the corpse over 0.5 s.
    - Each fires at the nearest enemy within 700 uu at the first weapon's interval, 0.25 × its spec.
    - **Look:** violet glow-card wisps with a small bright core (cool).
  - **Quicksilver, Vector:**
    - +1 dash charge (effect stat mod).
    - Each dash start fires 3 shots per weapon (±6°) along the dash line, full damage, plain specs.
    - The dash path leaves blades every 45 uu for 1 s, cutting within 55 uu every 0.25 s for 0.6 × 14 (Pulse Rifle base) × hero Damage.
    - **Look:** colourless afterimage cards with a thin white arc edge.
  - **Swap-out (open):** "fire the weapon's pattern" (Vector volleys, Shade shots) waits for weapons' `URHWeaponInstance::FireVolleyFrom` (requested). Until then each shot is one plain copy of the weapon's spec.
  - **Measured in game** (forced statuses, Bot on): Glacier 10 raised; Collection 10 Shades / 26 shots in 7 s; Kiln 8 husks / 36 bolts; Vector 2 volleys / 44 cuts. The R5 budget is for the bot pass.
  - Tests `RogueHero.Transforms.Path.{TideGlacier, TideSlackWater, ShadeCollection, QuicksilverVector}`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Backwash modifier (v0.2 D-4; full_game_specs §1.7, act2_text §10, build_depth_spec §3.3 row 21).** `DA_Mod_Backwash_CodeFallback` (`Mod.Backwash`, not in the starting pool: integration unlocks it from the DredgeSurfaced50 Discovery), RequiredCaps `Projectile Lob`, matrix row `YYYYY-YYY-` asserted by `RogueHero.Modifiers.Compatibility`. Data: `URHModifierDef::BackwashPullAdd` → `FRHBulletSpec::BackwashPull` (uu/s; zones and pull zones drop it: a field has no shooter; split / scatter children keep it). Behaviour: each hit queues a tug of `-travel dir × BackwashPull`, i.e. back along the shot toward its muzzle (or its last bounce); on a Flak blast every enemy in it is drawn back toward the launcher. `ResolveHitOutcomes` applies the tugs after `ApplyHits` through `IRHStatusTarget::PushEnemy` (same knock channel and decay as knockback; bosses take a quarter; enemies killed by the hit are skipped). **Why not negative `KnockbackAdd`:** `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits` ignores knockback ≤ 0, and a blast's hit direction points outward, so the sign would pull into the crater, not toward the shooter. Heavy Slug + Backwash both apply along the same axis (they partly cancel). Visual: a short cool cyan tug streak (`AddMinorBolt`, 46 uu, 0.09 s) from the enemy toward the pull, at most 24 per step (player-side hue, Art §3). Strength 700 (~70 uu tug) is a **placeholder; tuning is GATED** (Roadmap row 11). Counted in `GetBehaviourStats().BackwashPulls`. Text: `Mod.Backwash.Name/.Desc` EN per act2_text; zh 回流 / 命中会把敌人拉向你开火的位置。 (reviewed by loc-zh 2026-10-20), applied through `zh_overrides.csv`. Tests: `RogueHero.Modifiers.Sim.Backwash` (pulled back vs an unmodded hit, Heavy Slug still pushes, one tug per hit); `Modifiers.Fallbacks` now counts 21.
- 2026-10-20 — **Spillover modifier (v0.2 D-4; full_game_specs §1.7, act2_text §10, build_depth_spec §3.3 row 22).** `DA_Mod_Spillover_CodeFallback` (`Mod.Spillover`, not in the starting pool: integration unlocks it from the OutfallWin Discovery), any weapon (no caps), matrix row asserted. Data: `URHModifierDef::SpilloverShareAdd` → `FRHBulletSpec::SpilloverShare` (1.0 = all of the overkill). Behaviour (`URHBulletSubsystem::ApplySpillover`, inside `ResolveHitOutcomes`, after `ApplyHits`): a hit that removed HP (`GetLastHitDealt` > 0) and left its enemy dead sends `(spec damage − HP removed) × share` to the nearest **live** enemy within 420 uu, through `IRHStatusTarget::DamageEnemy` (× the target's Shock; a death joins the next kill buffer). Effect damage makes no hit event, so a jump never chains. **Approximation:** the victim's crit and Shock are not visible after `ApplyHits`, so the spec damage stands in for what the hit would have dealt, and a jump never carries more than the hit's base damage. The target is picked with `QueryEnemies`, which checks Alive, and not with `FindNearestEnemy`: `URHEnemySubsystem::FindNearest` returns this step's corpses, which are still in the hash, so it found the victim itself at distance 0 (statuses' file, reported to them). Visual: a pale-aqua arc (`AddMinorBolt`) from the kill to the target, at most 24 per step (player-side hue). The share and the 420 uu range are **placeholders; tuning is GATED**. Counted in `GetBehaviourStats().Spills / SpillDamage`. Text: `Mod.Spillover.Name/.Desc` EN per act2_text; zh 溢流 / 击杀时，多余的伤害会跳到最近的敌人身上。 (reviewed by loc-zh 2026-10-20). Tests: `RogueHero.Modifiers.Sim.Spillover` (the nearest enemy takes exactly the overkill, a farther one is untouched, no jump without the modifier or without a kill); `Modifiers.Fallbacks` now counts 22.
- 2026-10-20 — **Cap.Continuous (build_depth_spec §3.2 / §3.3, planning's note "swap to Cap.Continuous whenever you next touch the caps").** New tag `Cap.Continuous` ("a stream, not discrete shots"), carried by the Spiral pattern (Spiral Projector, and so its evolution). Burst Capacitor's BlockedCaps go from `Mine Weapon.SpiralProjector` to `Mine Continuous`, which is the spec's wording. The matrix is unchanged (row 13 `YYYYYYYY--`, still asserted by `RogueHero.Modifiers.Compatibility`). The weapon-tag form of `BlockedCaps` stays supported but no modifier uses it now.
- 2026-10-20 — **statuses after merging main (env's Outfall water, branches):**
  - **Merge:** enemy movement keeps env's current (`GetCurrentAt`) and then applies Glacier's obstacle push.
  - **Slack Water** now reads env's `Environment/RHOutfallWater.h` (`URHOutfallWater::Get`, `ChannelAt || IsFlooded`). Swap point: env's `IsWater`, which adds the pools, once it is on main.
  - **Perfect Sight** (balance's Radiance crit capstone): `ApplyHits` asks `RHBranchHooks::TakeSureCrit(Effects, WeaponSlot)` after the crit roll. The roll is always drawn, so seeds do not shift.
  - **Unity fix:** `RHEffect_Blessings.cpp`'s file-local `ConsumePeriods` is renamed `BlessConsumePeriods`. It was ambiguous against `RHBranchFx::ConsumePeriods` (pulled in by a file-scope `using` in `RHBranchEffects.cpp`) once the unity grouping changed.
- 2026-10-20 — **Duo reactions: ricochet triggers + the D-37 imbue clause.** Weapons' `FRHRicochetEvent` / `OnRicochets` (main 065edd4) reaches reactions through `URHReactionDriver`. **Live Rail:** a ricochet off a Shocked enemy gets its bounce back (`URHBulletSubsystem::RefundBounce`) and arcs to the nearest other enemy. **Black Ice:** a ricochet off a Frozen enemy (Glide: or a Chilled one) does not use a bounce (refund). **Afterglow:** "a ricochet crits" = a bounce off an enemy that took a crit within 0.1 s (crits dispatch before ricochets each step), at any speed; Blind 0.3 s. Ricochet fires use the same 1 s per-enemy cooldown. **D-37:** a Keeper also counts when it is imbued (`URHEffectManager::GetImbuedGods`, any held blessing source such as a Legendary), not only with a run-state blessing or a duo. Tests: `RogueHero.Reactions.RicochetTriggers`, `.D37ImbueCounts`.
- 2026-10-20 — **L16 transformation hand UI (build_depth_spec §1.2, D-31; build_depth_text §1.3).** The level-up screen shows `Transform.Hand.Title` ("Choose a transformation") when the hand holds a Transform or Fusion card. The sealed 4th card cannot be chosen from the screen either: `URHLevelUpScreen::Choose` ignores it, so the hand stays open (before this the screen would broadcast, then close). It reveals quietly (`RHCardReveal::TierFor` Common, no burst) and settles at 55 % opacity (`URHOfferCardButton::RestOpacity`; the deal-in had reset it to 1). Transformations and fusions get the Legendary reveal. The `Transform.Refund` toast plays on `URHRunState::OnModifiersRefunded`, one line per refunded modifier (`URHDiscoveryToast::EnqueueText`). Dev: `RH.UI.TransformHand [refund]`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Blessing → weapon behaviour layer, API (scheduler top priority; designer feedback "blessings change how weapons behave, modifiers specialise"; vocabulary aligned with planning's keeper_identity_spec).** `Combat/RHWeaponBehaviour.h`: `ERHWeaponBehaviour` {Seek, Curve, Split, Chain, Spread, Density, Orbit, ConvertMelee, Echo, Charge, VolleyOnDash}, and one data struct `FRHWeaponBehaviourMod` with generic fields (Magnitude, Count, AngleDeg, Range, Time, DamageMult, Scale, Trigger OnHit/OnExpiry, Shape Fan/Ring/Spiral, plus the mod's own RequiredCaps / BlockedCaps; the header documents what each field means per kind). **Grant:** `FRHBlessingRank::WeaponBehaviours` (applied and removed with the rank by `URHEffectManager::ApplyBlessing / RemoveBlessing`), or any effect through `URHEffectManager::SetWeaponBehaviours(Source, Mods) / ClearWeaponBehaviours(Source)`. The manager keeps one list per source, flattens them and bumps `GetWeaponBehaviourVersion()`. **Per weapon:** `RHWeaponBehaviour::Merge` filters by the kind's Cap rule (`GetCapRule`, which mirrors the modifier rules: Seek like Homing, Orbit like Orbit Lock, Split on expiry needs Expiry, Charge skips Continuous, and so on) plus the mod's own filters. It stacks per kind (Magnitude / Count / AngleDeg add, Range / Time largest, DamageMult / Scale multiply) and clamps to per-weapon limits (`Clamp`; for example Split ≤ 8 children, Echo ≤ 3, Spread ≤ +12 rounds). The limits are placeholders, GATED. `URHWeaponInstance` marks itself dirty when the version changes, and `RebuildSpec` passes the merged set to `RHSpecComposition::Compose` (new trailing parameter, applied after the socketed modifiers and before hero stats; `FRHComposedWeapon::Behaviours` keeps it for the timing kinds). Dash starts are exposed as `URHEffectManager::GetDashSerial / GetLastDash` so weapons can react without a Player/ hook. This commit is the API only: the set composes in but changes nothing yet. The behaviours follow, 3–4 per commit. Tests: `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.Api` (caps, stacking, limits, names, compose carries the set) and `.Store` (sources, replace, clear, version).
- 2026-10-20 — **Path transformations on weapons' combat hooks (14b8a08); Outfall zh names.** This closes the two swap-outs logged above.
  - **Glacier:** pillars are also `URHBulletSubsystem::SetEnemyBulletBlockers` circles, set with the enemy obstacles each step. Each absorbed enemy round counts as one hit toward the 10 that shatter a pillar (`GetBlockerHits`, matched to pillars by id).
  - **Collection:** each Shade fires the first weapon's base pattern through `URHWeaponInstance::FireVolleyFrom`, with a 25 % spec that keeps the weapon's motion but drops its riders (`DeriveSpec(..., bKeepMotion)`).
  - **Vector:** each dash fires every weapon's own volley (its spec and modifiers) along the dash line through `FireVolleyFrom`. The single-shot fallback and its `ShotsPerWeapon` / `SpreadDeg` params are gone.
  - **Measured** (Smoke, forced statuses): Collection 127 pattern volleys from 10 Shades; Vector 2 dash volleys.
  - **zh names:** the Outfall Cast-offs follow loc-zh's naming doc (`zh_naming.md` §8) through `zh_overrides.csv`: 浚泵 / 缆工 / 渣炮, replacing 疏浚机 / 放线机 / 抛渣机. Applied with Localize → apply_zh → Localize; `apply_zh --check` is clean.
  - **Known:** a frozen enemy can be nudged off the centre of its pillar by separation. This is cosmetic; it is held until the Keeper identity spec.

Omens roll from the run seed, so the hub now rolls the next run's seed once (`URHMetaSubsystem::GetOrRollNextRunSeed`, save `NextRunSeed`) and previews with `RHOmens::Roll(seed, URHOfferFlow::BuildPool(...), "Weapon.PulseRifle")`, the same inputs `ARHGameMode::StartRunState` uses. `URHPatronSelection::BeginRun` adds `?RHRunSeed=` and consumes the seed, and `ARHGameMode::BeginPlay` reads it (one-line hook; `-RHSeed=` and a daily run still win). Verified: the previewed omen is the run's omen. (2) **Run-start banner** `Omen.Banner` + rules on the toast (a core ticker waits for the roll; an idle toast is collapsed and does not tick). (3) **`FRHRunSummary::IchorMult`** filled from `RHOmens::GetActive(Run)` (1 with omens off). (4) **Run memory:** `FRHRunSummary::Transforms / Blessings` → save `RecentRuns` (newest first, 10 kept) → `URHMetaSubsystem::ApplyRunMemory` at run start (one-line hook in `StartRunState`): `SetRecentTransforms` = transforms of the last 3 runs; `SetRecentBlessings` = blessings taken in each of the last 3 runs with this patron (none until there are 3). (5) **Not yet:** the omen re-roll Discovery (no id, condition or text in the spec; asked the scheduler). Test `RogueHero.Meta.RunMemory`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Descent row on env's arena API (full_game_specs §1.2 ⟨D-1⟩).** The Shrine's Descent row sits under the Quota and steps through the unlocked `URHArenaDef`s (`URHArenaSelection::GetArenas / IsUnlocked / SetSelected / Name`). It is disabled while only Depth1 is open, with `LockedHint` of the first sealed arena beside it. Pad and keyboard navigation run Quota → Descent → BEGIN, skipping disabled rows. `URHRunCounters::BuildSummary` fills `FRHRunSummary::Arena` from `URHArenaSelection::GetRunArenaKey` (Depth1 / Depth2), so arena wins, Heat, the Codex arena entry and the D-5 check use the arena actually played. Verified: Begin with the Outfall travels to `L_Arena_Outfall?…?RHArena=Depth2?RHRunSeed=…`. The omen block moved right of centre to clear the row hints.
- 2026-10-20 — **`PrimaryAssetTypesToScan` for `RHTransform`** (`/Script/RogueHero.RHTransformDef`, `/Game/RH`, AlwaysCook) added to `Config/DefaultGame.ini` at the scheduler's request, so `DA_Transform_*` assets replace `RHTransforms::MakeFallbackDefs` when they exist. `RHOmen` (`URHOmenDef`, `DA_Omen_*`) has no scan line either; not added here (not asked).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon behaviours, batch 1 (spec kinds): Seek, Curve, Split, Chain, plus `RH.Give Behaviour.<Kind>`.** All four live in `RHSpecComposition::Compose`, after the socket fold and before hero stats, and reuse existing sim behaviour, so no new bullet code. **Seek** sets Homing; turn rates add to any Homing Gyro and the larger reach wins. The placeholder is 240°/s / 600 uu, weaker than the Gyro's 360. **Curve** adds `CurveDegPerSec` (the generic velocity rotation the Vortex evolution introduced). **Split** maps onto the existing derived bullets: on hit it is Split Core's children (Fan = its half-arc, Ring = 180°); on expiry, Fan is Spread Wave's arc (2× half-arc) and Ring is Scatter Shell's burst. Spiral = Ring with the children curling at 120°/s (`Split/Scatter.CurveDegPerSec`, one direction per weapon). Counts add to the modifiers'; child damage takes the lower multiplier. **Chain** (pierce → chain): every pierce becomes a bounce, i.e. the weapon's and modifiers' pierce plus Terminal Velocity's extra pierce become Ricochet; infinite pierce counts as 8. It adds Count bounces, clears the Pierce flag and takes the larger RicochetRange. Debug: `RH.Give Behaviour.<Kind> [OnExpiry] [Fan|Ring|Spiral]` grants the placeholder parameters under a `Debug.Behaviour.<n>` source (repeat to stack); `RH.Give Behaviour.Clear` removes them. Tests: `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.Spec` (every trigger × shape, Chain on the Rail Lance) and `.Sim.SeekChain` (a Seek round finds an off-line enemy that a plain round misses; a Chain round bounces to a second enemy).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon behaviours, batch 2: Spread, Density (spec), Echo, Charge (weapon instance).** **Spread** adds Count rounds per volley and AngleDeg of spread (negative tightens), and DamageMult scales each round. It goes through `FRHWeaponStats::Count / SpreadDeg`, so every count-driven pattern (Aimed, Cone, Radial, Homing, Lob, Beam) widens with no per-weapon code. **Density** multiplies fire rate by 1 + Magnitude, damage by DamageMult and size by Scale (the "bullet-hell" stream). **Echo** and **Charge** need two more derived spec ids per weapon (`FRHDerivedSpecIds::Echo / Charge`, allocated in `URHWeaponInstance::Init`). `FRHComposedWeapon::Echo / Charge` are copies of Main with the same links: Echo at its DamageMult, Charge at its DamageMult with size and blast radius × Scale. `URHWeaponInstance::FireVolleyAs(spec, ...)` fires one volley under such an id. **Echo:** after each normal volley, Count repeats Time s apart, re-aimed from the hero's current position. Burst and Chain Trigger volleys do not echo, so effects never multiply each other. **Charge:** every Count-th normal volley is charged; with Count 0, the first volley after Time s of not firing is charged. Counters: `GetEchoVolleys / GetChargedVolleys`. Tests: `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.SpreadDensity` and `.EchoCharge` (one echo per volley at 60 %, one charged volley in four at ×3, idle-charge never fires under steady fire).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon behaviours, batch 3: Orbit, ConvertMelee, VolleyOnDash. The 11 kinds of the layer are complete.** **Orbit** = Orbit Lock's flag and parameters (largest Time / Range wins). **ConvertMelee** adds `FRHComposedWeapon::Pattern`, the def's pattern or MeleeArc. `URHWeaponInstance::FireVolley` switches on it, and so do the composer's motion choice and the bullet-density knob. One arc carries the whole volley (damage × rounds per volley × DamageMult, Count 1), the same count → damage rule as Sweep Beam. Reach = Range, arc = AngleDeg (+20° per pierce, as the Arc Blade), the Arc Blade's angular speed and the Blade look. Target acquisition drops to reach + 80 uu, otherwise a rifle swings at enemies 1 400 uu away. Socketed modifiers a melee arc cannot use (Homing, Orbit Lock, Boomerang, Split Core, Fork, expiry bursts, Ricochet) are refunded: `URHWeaponInstance::RebuildSpec` removes them before composing and pays one Renegotiate each through `URHRunState::RefundModifiers`, as evolutions do (keeper_identity_spec §12, which also sets the cleave at volley × 0.8, the default DamageMult). This was amended the same day: the first cut left them dormant. `GetRefundedByConversion` counts them. `RHWeaponBehaviour::Merge` re-checks the other behaviours against the MeleeArc caps, so Seek, Curve, Split, Chain, Spread and Orbit drop out, while Density, Echo, Charge and VolleyOnDash stay. **VolleyOnDash:** each new dash start (`URHEffectManager::GetDashSerial`) fires Count volleys from points every 90 uu along the dash direction. Each aims at what is nearest its point (else along the dash), uses its own spec copy (`FRHDerivedSpecIds::Dash`, DamageMult) and ignores the cooldown. Dashes before the weapon first ticked never fire. Counters: `GetDashVolleys`. Tests: `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.OrbitConvert` (Scatter Cannon → one arc with the volley's damage, Seek dropped and Echo kept; a converted rifle hits a close enemy in play) and `.VolleyOnDash` (3 volleys per dash, none without the behaviour).
- 2026-10-20 — **Evolution card numbers (tiers' report: "Becomes 0 orbiting blades").** The `{0}` of `Evolve.<Weapon>.Desc` is the transform def's `Text0` param. `RHEvolutions`' static registration now also calls `RHTransforms::RegisterFallbackParams("Transform.<Weapon>", {Text0})` for the seven lines with a number: Railstorm 6 blades, Blade Halo 4, Sweep Beam 1.5 s, Carpet 5 blasts, Cyclone 2 hits/s, Murmuration 6 s, Pulse Wall 3 s. Static init cannot build defs, so the numbers are constants (`RHEvolutions::CardTextNumber`). `CardTextNumberFromDef` derives each one from the evolution def (count, steps + 1, lifetime, wall time, beam length × arc / tip speed, 1 / cooldown). Test `RogueHero.Evolutions.CardText`: a number is registered exactly where the string has `{0}`, and it equals the def's value, so retuning a def without the text fails the test.
- 2026-10-20 — **Elite damage-taken hook for starting loadouts** (tiers' Hunting Kit, "Elites take +15 % damage").
  - `URHEnemySubsystem::SetEliteDamageTakenMult(float)` multiplies `DamageTakenMult` for elites only; bosses are not elites. It is a stored value, not a per-hit lookup.
  - The run owner sets it at run start: `RHLoadouts::GetEliteDamageTakenMult`, 1 without the kit. It is not reset by the enemy subsystem, so every run start must set it.
  - Test `RogueHero.Enemies.EliteDamageTakenMult`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon behaviours: StatusApply, Push, Pull (scheduler D-47, keeper_identity_spec §12).** The kinds are appended to `ERHWeaponBehaviour`, and `FRHWeaponBehaviourMod` gains `StatusTag`. **StatusApply** applies StatusTag with chance Magnitude, on any weapon. A round carries one status: a patron element (Divine Conduit) or fusion element set earlier keeps the slot, the same tag adds chance (clamped to 1), and among behaviours the first source's tag wins. Derived bullets inherit it. **Push** adds to `Knockback` (any weapon, like Heavy Slug). **Pull** adds to `BackwashPull` (Projectile or Lob, like Backwash, because the pull needs the shot's direction). **Clash rule:** the larger magnitude wins outright and a tie goes to Pull. It applies in `Merge` between the behaviours and again in Compose on the round's totals, so it now also covers the modifiers. **Changed:** Heavy Slug (600) + Backwash (700) used to apply both; the round now only pulls. Placeholders: StatusApply 25 % Chill, Push 500, Pull 600; limits chance ≤ 1, push / pull ≤ 1 500 uu/s. Pierce and Field stay modifier-like and Hunters belong to statuses (scheduler). `RH.Give Behaviour.StatusApply Status.Burn` picks the status. Test: `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.StatusPushPull`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Blessing behaviour data hook (keeper_identity_spec §9 step 3; data split agreed with balance).** `RHBlessings::MakeFallbackDefs` now calls `RHBlessingBehaviourData::Fill(Def)` on every factory def. **Balance owns** `Build/Blessings/RHBlessingBehaviourData.cpp`: one table, Blessing tag → per-rank `FRHWeaponBehaviourMod` lists, where each rank holds its total like the rank stat mods. All numbers are balance's and GATED. Weapons owns the engine side and the per-weapon `Clamp`; a capstone that needs more asks weapons. The table is committed empty. It is a factory fallback (TDD §17), and the swap-out is to author `WeaponBehaviours` on the DA_Blessing assets and delete the table. Test `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.BlessingRank`: rank behaviours are applied with the blessing id as source, a rank-up replaces them, they are removed with the blessing, and every filled entry names a kind.
- 2026-10-20 — **Per-weapon spawn cap (keeper_identity_spec §12 / §9 step 1).** `URHBulletSubsystem::SpawnInternal` takes one token from the spawning spec's weapon slot budget. Budgets refill at `RH.Bullets.WeaponSpawnCap` per second (400, GATED; ≤ 0 = off) and hold at most 0.5 s worth (minimum 64), starting full. Main volleys and every derived bullet count (Echo, Burst, Split, Chain, Scatter, Wave, zones, Hunters through FireVolleyFrom), so the product of the multipliers is bounded per weapon. A spawn over budget is **dropped** (`BStats.SpawnCapped`), never recycled; the global 6 000 cap with oldest-first recycling still applies on top. Slotless bullets (stress `RH.Bullets`, enemy fire) are exempt. `SpawnInternal` can now return INDEX_NONE, and `Spawn` / `SpawnExtra` / deferred spawns handle it. The three tests that flood one weapon to test the global cap (`Modifiers.CapStorm`, `.CapStormPatterns`, `Fusions.Cap`) switch the per-weapon cap off with `SetWeaponSpawnCapOverride(0)`. **Perf note:** the `RH.GiveExtra` harness weapons are capped too, so a stress profile that relies on them spawns fewer bullets; set the cvar to 0 for budget measurements. Test `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.SpawnCap` (a 1 000-round burst gives 200 and 800 capped, about 400 per second sustained, slotless bullets are free, 0 = off).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon behaviour capability fallbacks (keeper_identity_spec §2 "every flag means something on every weapon"; §9 step 1).** When a kind's cap rule does not fit a weapon, `RHWeaponBehaviour::Merge` keeps it as a **fallback** entry when `GetFallback(kind, caps)` has one (`FRHWeaponBehaviourSet::HasFallback`; `Has` stays native-only). Implemented: **Seek** on a homing weapon adds turn rate. **Split** on melee or mine makes every 3rd volley also throw a ring of Count children from the hero (the Split derived spec at its DamageMult; `GetSplitRings`). **Orbit** on an orbiter adds 1 drone or blade. **ConvertMelee** on melee adds 25 % reach, and on mine or orbit 25 % area. **Chain** on melee adds 20 % swing speed. **Pull** on melee pulls toward the hero (sweep hits now queue tugs), and on mines toward the blast. ConvertMelee's re-check also turns projectile kinds into their melee fallbacks where one exists (Split → ring, Chain → swing speed, Pull → toward the hero). **Not yet:** Seek on melee or mine ("+ damage vs the target class") waits for statuses' Seek target classes (§9 step 5), and Split on orbiters has no activation to hook (no volleys). Every fallback number is a placeholder, GATED. Test `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.Fallbacks`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Starting loadouts (`full_game_specs.md` §3, `act2_text.md` §12.1).** `URHLoadoutDef` (primary type `RHLoadout`, `Build/RHLoadoutDef.h`; fallback `DA_Loadout_<Weapon>_CodeFallback`, 6 loadouts with the spec numbers [GATED]): Tag `Loadout.<Weapon>` (`Config/Tags/RHLoadouts.ini`), start weapon, stat mods (source `Loadout`), an optional Reclaimer-own trait effect, banned weapons and Params. **Selection:** `URHLoadoutSelection` (GameInstance subsystem; meta-ui draws the Shrine row). A loadout is unlocked by default (Standard) or when its Tag is in the profile's `UnlockedContent`, which its Discovery's `Unlocks` puts there. It travels as `?RHLoadout=<Weapon>` (`GetRunOption`); `ForWorld` reads it, else the selection, else Standard. **Run start** (`ARHGameMode`): start weapon at L1, stat mods, trait effect, banned weapons into the offer context (Reaper: Ring Emitter / Spiral Projector never offered), and the omen's Spare Fitting never doubles the start weapon. A loadout never adds Favor or picks a Keeper, and the offer system is otherwise unchanged. **Muscle Memory Form (decision):** a non-Standard loadout's start weapon is the Form weapon; Standard keeps "the first weapon taken from a card". **Traits implemented here:** Breacher's dash knockback (`URHEffect_Loadout_DashKnockback`, r 220, 900 uu/s) and Reaper's kill heal (`URHEffect_Loadout_KillHeal`, 0.5 HP, cap 5 HP/s). **Hooks other owners read (neutral with Standard; inert until they call them):** `RHLoadouts::GetExtraPierce` (Lancer +1; weapons' spec composition), `GetDroneBlockInterval` (Escort 2 s; weapons / bullets), `GetEliteDamageTakenMult` (Hunter ×1.15; enemies' `DamageTakenMult`). The rig socket layout (Art §5, visual) is art's. **Discoveries** (integration's per §12.1; no Recollection, they open a rig): ScatterKills500 now also opens `Loadout.ScatterCannon`; new DronesWin / RailLanceWin (run end: won + `Stat.Run.Owned.<Weapon>`) and SeekerElites30 (mid-run: `Stat.Kills.Elite.<Weapon>` ≥ 30); Steam rows added. Meta tests updated for the 3 rig Discoveries (counts, the win-requiring set, loadout ids not being registry content). **Open:** `ArcBladeOnly10` (Reaper) cannot be met as written, because every run starts with another weapon and weapons are never dropped. It is not implemented until planning rules, so Reaper stays locked. Tests: `RogueHero.Loadouts.{Defs,Offers,Discoveries}`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Outfall run flow (`full_game_specs.md` §1.2, D-1: a separate run chosen at the Shrine).** The pieces already on main are the Shrine arena selector (`URHArenaSelection`, hub), travel with `?RHArena=` (`URHPatronSelection::BeginRun`), the arena's map / timeline / environment (env), and per-arena story counters (meta-ui). Integration wired the gaps: **run end** now records the arena: `URHRunCounters::BuildSummary` sets `FRHRunSummary::Arena` from `URHArenaSelection::GetRunArenaKey`. Until now it was never set, so a won Outfall run counted as a Breach win, and Act III and per-arena Heat could not trigger. **Travel** also carries the loadout (`?RHLoadout=`). The Shrine's patron, Heat and arena already rode on the URL. **Run again** on the summary reopens the arena with the run's own URL options (`URHRunSummaryScreen::RunAgainOptions`: patron, Heat, arena, loadout, daily; the engine's `Name=` dropped). Before, it reopened the map bare and lost every Shrine choice. The run pool is built for the run's arena (Slack Water, `Depth2`). The env continuation hook (`bHoldRunEndForTravel`, alternative B "Descend") stays off, as D-1 decided. **Open, Shrine side (hub / meta-ui):** Heat is per arena in the save (`ArenaHeat`), but `URHHeatSelection::MaxSelectableFor` still uses the global `HighestHeatWon`. Test `RogueHero.RunFlow.RunAgainOptions`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Scheduler decision: Reaper's unlock is `ArcBladeKills500` ("Destroy 500 enemies with the Arc Blade in one run", mid-run, mirrors Breacher's ScatterKills500).** It supersedes `ArcBladeOnly10` ("survive 10:00 with the Arc Blade as your only weapon"), which could not be met: every run starts with another weapon, weapons are never dropped, and the only Arc Blade start is the Reaper loadout it unlocks. Planning updates `full_game_specs.md` §3 / `act2_text.md` §12.1 to match; the ST_Discoveries rows and the Steam row (`ACH_LOADOUT_BLADE`) are in.
- 2026-10-20 — **Heat is per arena at the Shrine (fix, Hub/).** `URHHeatSelection::GetMaxSelectable` read meta's global `GetMaxSelectableHeat` (the save's `HighestHeatWon`), so a Breach Heat opened the same level in the Outfall. It now uses `MaxSelectableForArena(Save, Arena)`: for the Shrine's selected arena (env's `URHArenaSelection`, the Breach by default), 0 before that arena's first win, else its own best Heat won + 1 (`RHStory::GetArenaWins / GetArenaHeat`). The Breach also honours the save's `bFirstWin` for saves without `ArenaWins`. `GetSelected` clamps meta's saved choice to that maximum, so switching to the Outfall never carries a Breach Heat into it. Meta's global getters are unchanged (other readers). Test: `RogueHero.Hub.Heat.PerArena`.
- 2026-10-20 — **v0.2 "Done when" acceptance: E2E test + `LoopCheck -FromSave Breach1`.** **Test** `RogueHero.E2E.V02.DoneWhen` (real game instance, shipping run end). A fresh save has the Outfall locked and is in Act I. After one Breach win through `ARHGameMode::EndRun`, `URHArenaSelection::IsUnlocked(Depth2)` is true without force and the arena can be selected. The act is II, `RHKeeperBids::IsUnlocked` is true in an Outfall run world, and the layer audit behind `RH.Codex.ShowLayer` reaches max layer 3. An Outfall run world (`?RHArena=Depth2`) then wins: `ArenaWins(Depth2)` = 1, the Outfall's Heat opens at 1 (per arena), and the Breach still has one win. **LoopCheck `-FromSave Breach1`** (with `-Arena Outfall`): a seed launch earns one real Breach win (Shrine Begin, then `RH.EndRun win` through the shipping run end; `RH.Story.Win` alone writes only the story side and leaves the audit at layer 2). It then checks `RH.Hub arena Outfall` without force, `RH.Story` act II and the `RH.Codex.ShowLayer` current-save max layer 3. The runs choose the Outfall unforced, and the verdict is the seed checks plus an Outfall win. With `-Driver Assist` the flow, not the difficulty, is tested: at 21:30 the Floodgate's HP drops to 2 % (`RH.BossOutfall.HP`) and the run's damage finishes it through the shipping boss-win path. First pass: PASS (seed checks ok, the Outfall won at 21.3 min, the Floodgate defeated after 80.6 s).

  - Each `Transform.<Path>` registers `Text0`, the card's {0}, derived from the param that drives its behaviour, so the card and the behaviour cannot drift apart: Storm `Jumps` (4), Forge `Life` (4 s), Tide `Life` (3 s), Shade `Damage` × 100 (25 %), Radiance `LanceDamage` × 100 (40 %).

- 2026-10-20 — **Hunters, API** (keeper_identity_spec §2 `Hunter`, §3.4, §12.1 rule 4; D-47: statuses extends the Shade minion system).
  - **Defs:** `URHHunterDef` (`Build/Hunters/`, data only, primary type `RHHunter`, `DA_Hunter_<Path>_<Kind>`) holds:
    - the trigger: Marked kill / every N kills / manual;
    - count, life (≤ 0 = permanent) and MaxPermanent;
    - damage × and weapon slot (whose base pattern it fires);
    - target class (Marked / Nearest / Elite), range, stand-off, speed, leash, colour.
  - **Fallbacks:** `MakeFallback` until the assets exist:
    - `Hunter.Shade.Temporary` (Dividend: 6 s, 25 %, hunts Marked);
    - `Hunter.Shade.Permanent` (Arrears: permanent, max 3, manual; Arrears' owner counts the Souls and calls `Spawn`).
    - The tags are structural: "Collectors" is a fusion's fiction name, which RogueHero.Text.FictionNotKeys rejects as a key.
  - **`URHHunterSubsystem`:**
    - SoA, ≤ `RHHunters::MaxAlive` (6) per player. A new temporary hunter replaces the oldest temporary one; a permanent one is refused at the cap or at its def's MaxPermanent.
    - `SetMods(Source, FRHHunterMods)` folds amplifiers: life adds, damage multiplies, seek takes the largest, heal on kill adds.
    - `Step` runs once per sim time.
  - **`URHEffect_Hunters`:** a blessing names it as its `EffectClass`, and the defs come from their `GrantedBy` (e.g. `Blessing.Shade.Dividend`), so there is no code per card. Losing the effect clears its hunters.
  - Behaviour (triggers, hunting, `FireVolleyFrom` volleys, drawing) is the next commit.
  - Tests `RogueHero.Hunters.{Defs, Cap, Grant}`.
  - Note: `RogueHero.E2E.PIE.HubToRunToSummary` failed once in a full run ("Contracts apply to the next run"), then passed alone twice and in a full re-run (412/412). It is order-dependent and flaky; not caused by this change.
- 2026-10-20 — **Hunters, behaviour** (keeper_identity_spec §3.4 Collectors, §5.1, §12.1 rule 4).
  - **Triggers:** `URHEffect_Hunters` raises hunters from its granted defs: a Marked death raises one where the enemy fell (Dividend), or one every N kills.
  - **Hunting:** each hunter hunts its def's target class (Marked / Elite) within Range, falling back to the nearest enemy. It closes to StandOff, never strays past the Leash from the hero, and circles the hero when idle.
  - **Volleys:** the def's weapon fires its base pattern from the hunter through `URHWeaponInstance::FireVolleyFrom`, at the weapon's interval. A derived spec keeps the pattern's motion and look at DamageMult × mods, and drops the modifier riders (split / linger / scatter / wave / ricochet / boomerang / orbit / fusion), because the spec says "base pattern". With `SeekTurnDeg` (Hound × Collectors) the rounds home.
  - **Kill credit / Escrow:** a target that dies within 0.4 s of a hunter's volley at it is that hunter's kill, and heals `HealOnKill`. Interpretation: kill events carry no shooter, so this is an approximation.
  - **Look:** a violet veil card, a dark hood that turns to face its prey, and a bright eye. Low (≤ 130 uu) and cool, so telegraphs stay on top.
  - **Data and debug:** `DA_Hunter_Shade_Temporary` / `DA_Hunter_Shade_Permanent` are baked (`Tools\BakeEnemyData.bat`, `/Game/RH/Data/Hunters`). `RH.Hunters [spawn N Temporary|Permanent]` raises hunters at the hero.
  - **Measured** (Smoke, 6 hunters, 20 Marked, 120 enemies): 76 volleys and 22 credited kills in about 9 s; Marked deaths raised 15 more (the cap held at 6).
  - **Open:**
    - The Dividend / Arrears blessing defs (balance) must set `EffectClass = URHEffect_Hunters`.
    - Arrears' owner counts the Souls and calls `URHHunterSubsystem::Spawn` with the Permanent def.
    - Debt Collectors / Escrow / Hound × Collectors call `SetMods`.
    - The 400/s per-weapon spawn cap (§12.1 rule 3) is weapons'.
  - Test `RogueHero.Hunters.Hunt`.

- 2026-10-20 — **Keeper identity offer rules O1–O6 (`keeper_identity_spec.md` §6, step 7; D-40, D-46).** Every GDD §5.6 rule holds (tests `RogueHero.Offers.*`). Archetypes are step 5's `Branch.*` table (`RHBranches`): key = Entry role, amplifier = Core, capstone = Capstone. `RHOfferArchetypes` reads them. **O1 (D-46, GATED, off by default):** `bBlessingFirstSlot` makes the guaranteed slot pick a blessing 70 % of the time before L8, measured at 87 % blessing with it on vs 57 % off, card 1 always from the guaranteed set. `bCommitNeedsOwnedArchetype` requires, after L8, at least one pair blessing from an owned archetype, on top of the ≥ 2 of 5 rule. **O2:** momentum 0.4 → 0.5 (spec number), other archetypes × 0.8 after 2 cards (unchanged). **O3 (live):** an amplifier is offered only once its archetype's key is owned; capstones need 2 (E2). **O4 (live):** a modifier that amplifies an owned archetype × 2, one that declares `AmplifiesTags` but hits none × 0.7. **Deviation:** modifiers with no amplify data stay × 1 until step 6 authors `URHModifierDef::AmplifiesTags`, so the rule doesn't devalue every modifier before the data exists. `AppendModifiers` copies the field once it exists (compile-time). **O5 (live):** a new weapon that fits an owned archetype × 1.5. **Fallback:** the fit list is spec §3's "Best weapons", a C++ table keyed `<Path>.<Archetype>` in `RHOfferArchetypes` (Shade's Ledger keyed `Economy`, designation-safe) until the archetype data asset carries it. It's inert until balance registers the archetype tags (step 3). **O6 / D-40:** the starter pool is exactly the archetype keys (Entry roles, default-unlocked). The test follows the table (18 entries today, 20 keys after step 3). **Run seed:** `ARHGameMode` reads `?RHRunSeed=` (the Shrine's pre-rolled seed for the omen); a daily run overrides it. **Not done here:** the power-split ablation in `RH.Stats` (spec step 7 telemetry) waits for the scheduler's call on whether it's balance's sim tool or this. Tests `RogueHero.Offers.Identity.{StarterPool,KeysAndModifiers,BlessingFirstSlot,OwnedArchetypeAfterL8}`.

The tier toast (`URHEffectManager`) picks `RHStoryLink::ActVariantKey(…, RHStoryLink::GetAct)`, so from story Act 2 the `.Act2` row wins when it exists; the god def's own TierLine is used otherwise. **Layer audit:** `RHMeta::AuditLayers` treats ST_Keepers `.Act2` rows as Act II lines: gated before the first win, reachable or readable after it. Before, every ST_Keepers row counted as reachable in any run, which flagged the two Layer 3 Tier3.Act2 lines as pre-win leaks. That is one targeted edit in meta-ui's file. The Meta audit counts are updated (+6 layered rows; 12 Layer ≥ 3 rows after a win). Test `RogueHero.Story.ActVariantKeys`.

Both win milestones fire in `ARHGameMode::EndRun` before meta evaluates Discoveries. The recorder lines in `URHRunTelemetry` are balance's (bid_offered / bid_taken / binding_called), and the counters are meta-ui's. Tests in `RogueHero.OfferFlow.KeeperBids.Flow` and `RogueHero.RunFlow.Milestones`.

The pit's water is its own flow sheet (`MI_Env_WaterFlow_Pit`, darker), and it whirls while the drain pulls: `TickWater` turns its streak direction at up to 90°/s at the spec's 300 uu/s pull, eased in.

- 2026-10-20 — **Omen re-roll Discovery (build_depth_spec E3; scheduler decision 2026-10-20, option a).** `Discovery.OmenReroll` "Second Reading", condition **Win 3 runs**, checked at run end; no Recollection (Narrative §12 ships 15) and nothing in `UnlockedContent`, since the Shrine reads `Save.Discoveries`. **Deviation (mechanism):** Discovery conditions are per-run counters, so lifetime wins are a new counter `RHCounters::RunsWonTotal` (`Stat.Meta.RunsWon` = `RunsWon` + this win). `RHMeta::ApplyRunEnd` adds it only to the copy of the summary the Discovery check sees, so the Ledger-stone never sums it. **Shrine:** the omen box gains "Re-read the terms" (`Hub.Omen.Reroll`, the Reroll key or a click; not focusable, like the level-up tools). It shows when the Discovery is held and is usable **once per run**. `URHMetaSubsystem::RereadOmen` sets the save's `NextRunOmenRerolls` = 1 on the same pre-rolled seed. The preview becomes `RHOmens::Roll(seed, pool, start, 1)`, and the box grows upward 42 px while the action shows. `URHPatronSelection::BeginRun` appends `?RHOmenRerolls=1`, and `ConsumeNextRunSeed` clears it. **Pending (integration's files):** `RHOmens::RollForRun` needs a `Rerolls` param, and `ARHGameMode::StartRunState` has to pass `ParseOption(OptionsString, "RHOmenRerolls")`. Until both land, a re-read run keeps the un-re-read omen; the swap-out point is those two lines. `URHDiscoveryDef::RequiresWin` counts `RunsWonTotal`, so the pre-win audit leaves the re-roll out, and the Steam row is `ACH_OMEN_REROLL`. Dev: `RH.UI.Grant <DiscoveryId>` adds a Discovery to the save. Verified off-screen: the Shrine showed Bounty, R re-read it to Open Terms, and the action hid. Test `RogueHero.Meta.OmenReroll`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Shrine "Rig" row (starting loadout, act2_text §12.1, on integration's `URHLoadoutSelection`).** Under the Descent row: `Hub.Loadout` + the rig's `DisplayName`. Left/right steps through the fitted rigs only, wrapping, like the Descent. The row is disabled while only the Standard Kit is fitted, and its right-hand hint is then `URHLoadoutSelection::LockedHint` of the first locked rig ("Not fitted yet: <Discovery>"). A line under the row shows `GetTraitLine` · `GetDrawbackLine` (new `Hub.Loadout.Line` "{0} · {1}"; trait only when there is no drawback). Stepping the rig re-previews the omen, since the run rolls with the rig's start weapon; the omen preview now uses `GetSelected()->GetStartWeaponId()`. `BeginRun` already appends `GetRunOption()`. **Layout deviation (PL.23):** three option rows do not fit above the foot, so the detail column starts at 580 (was 604) and runs to the foot, and the Select/Move footer prompts move under BEGIN, right-aligned. Pad navigation: Shrine → Quota → Descent → Rig → BEGIN, skipping disabled rows (`GetEnabledOptionRows`). `RH.UI.Grant` also takes content ids (`Loadout.RailLance`). Verified off-screen: Lance Kit selected → `?RHLoadout=RailLance` → "Run: loadout Loadout.RailLance (start Weapon.RailLance)", and the run's omen equals the preview (Quiet Keepers).
- 2026-10-20 — **The 15 Act II Discoveries (act2_text §2, full_game_specs §1.7), meta-side.** `URHDiscoveryDef` fallbacks keyed by condition; 30 `ST_Discoveries` EN rows (zh from loc-zh); Steam rows `ACH_*`. Unlocks: OutfallWin → `Mod.Spillover`, DredgeSurfaced50 → `Mod.Backwash` (both added to the content registry as locked modifiers, so the unlock reaches the pool), StillWater → `Transform.Tide.SlackWater` (scheduler decision; gating it needs `bUnlockedByDefault = false` on the def and `IsInRunPool` honouring save unlocks for non-registry ids, requested from tiers/integration; until then Slack Water stays open in the Outfall). Recollections are wired in the next commit (RecollectionKey None until then). **Counters** (`RHCounters`, `.Max` names are best-of on the Ledger-stone): from `URHRunCounters::BuildSummary` — `Stat.Run.MaxHeat` and `Stat.Run.In.<Arena>` (also in `SyncCoreCounters`), `Stat.Path.MaxTier`, `Stat.Path.MaxPathsAtTier1` (`URHRunState::GetFavorTier`), `Stat.Bid.Taken` / `Stat.Bid.Called` (`GetBidsTaken` / `GetBidsCalled`), `Stat.Run.NoIntercession` (`URHUltimateComponent::GetCounters().Casts == 0`), `Stat.Run.Loadout.Fitted` (`RHLoadouts::GetActive` ≠ Standard). From `URHRunCounters::TickAct2` (public reads only, no other owner's file touched): `Stat.Offer.Refused.Blessing` (`URHOfferFlow::OnOfferResolved` "banish" on a blessing card), `Stat.Outfall.FlushSurvived` (a `URHOutfallWater` surge front crosses the hero's radius while the hero is in that channel), `Stat.Outfall.PumpsIntactP1` (the Floodgate brain leaves `Pumps` with `IsPumpAlive` ×3), `Stat.Outfall.MaxStillTide2Seconds` (sim time, hero speed < 5 uu/s, Tide tier ≥ 2, Depth2). `Stat.Kills.Boss.Form<N>` from `URHMetaSubsystem::NotifyBossFormKilled` (the Warden reports every kill). **Deviation (DredgeSurfaced50):** counted as lifetime Lurker kills — a submerged Dredge is untargetable, so a kill is a surfaced kill; if area damage can kill one underwater, enemies should add a surfaced flag to the kill. **Meta-derived counters** (`RHMeta::AddMetaCounters`, added to a copy of the summary before every Discovery check, never summed): `Stat.Meta.RunsWon`, `Stat.Meta.FacetsRead` (`Codex.Facet.*` seen; `MarkCodexSeen` runs a check outside runs for ReadFacets3), and `Stat.Life.<x>` = Ledger-stone total + this run for lifetime conditions (DredgeSurfaced50, Refuse20). `RequiresWin` also counts `Stat.Kills.Boss.Form*` (a Warden kill is the win). Test `RogueHero.Meta.Act2Discoveries`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Duo reactions re-read with the behaviour flags; duo blessings re-aimed at archetype keys (keeper_identity_spec §5.2, §9 step 8, hub part).** Triggers: **Split / Echo / Hunter children** need no reaction code — `RHSpecComposition::MakeDerived` keeps the parent's `StatusTag` / `ApplyPatronStatus`, Echo and Hunters fire the weapon spec, so their statuses reach the status triggers like a parent round's. **A Slam is a Forge hit:** a hit whose spec sweeps (`ERHBulletMotion::Sweep` — MeleeArc, ConvertMelee cleaves, Slam rings, so also Arc Blade while Forge is held) is a *Forge contact* = Burn; **a Pull landing is Chill contact:** `BackwashPull > 0` (the `Pull` kind and Backwash) or an inward radial knock (Vortex). Gravity Mine pull ticks do not count (not a Pull behaviour). A contact fires StatusMeets reactions at once **either way** (a cleave on a Shocked enemy is Plasma) and stands in for the status for `URHReactionSubsystem::ContactWindow` = 1 s [GATED] in every "enemy has X" check (crits, dashes, later statuses); a status death within 100 uu of a Forge contact of the same step counts as Burning (death events carry no enemy index). Flare on a contact crit without Burn detonates `BurnLeft`. Hit specs resolve through `URHBulletSubsystem::GetSpec` (tests: `SetSpecLookupForTest`). **Duo re-aim:** `URHReactionDef::DuoKeys` names one archetype key per Keeper (StormForge LiveWire + Bellows, StormTide LiveWire + Undertow, StormShade ForkedPath + Lien, StormRadiance Thunderclap + PerfectSight, StormQuicksilver ForkedPath + BlinkStrike, ForgeTide Bellows + ColdFront, ForgeShade Kindling + Lien, ForgeRadiance Kindling + PerfectSight, ForgeQuicksilver Bellows + BlinkStrike, TideShade Undertow + Lien, TideRadiance Undertow + PerfectSight, TideQuicksilver ColdFront + Slipstream, ShadeRadiance Lien + PerfectSight, ShadeQuicksilver Lien + BlinkStrike, RadianceQuicksilver PerfectSight + Slipstream). With the duo owned, each owned key's own behaviour mods are granted again under `Blessing.Duo.<Pair>.Keys` at `DuoKeyAmp` 0.25 of Magnitude / Count / AngleDeg (Charge's "every Nth" Count untouched) and `DuoKeyDamage` ×1.15 [GATED] — the duo amplifies what the key does, never grants a new flag. Effect-only keys (Brittle, High Water, Halo, Fleet…) add nothing until balance gives them behaviours; until balance's step 3 fills `RHBlessingBehaviourData`, no key has behaviours, so the re-aim is live code with no effect yet. Re-derived on blessing changes and whenever `GetWeaponBehaviourVersion` moves (written only when the mods change). Cards: each duo `.Desc` gains "Boosts <archetype> and <archetype>." (archetype names from keeper_identity_text §1; four descs shortened to stay ≤ 90 EN / ≤ 40 zh; provisional zh from zh_naming §9). Spec §5.2 points the duo text at keeper_identity_text §6, which holds the transformation reworks instead — written here by hub. Tests `RogueHero.Reactions.FlagContacts`, `RogueHero.Reactions.DuoAmplifiesKeys`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Loadout rig traits on the weapon side (full_game_specs §3, integration's `RHLoadouts`).** **Lancer:** `URHWeaponInstance::RebuildSpec` adds `RHLoadouts::GetExtraPierce` (1 with the Lance Kit) to the extra pierce passed to Compose, next to Terminal Velocity's, so every weapon gets +1, and with Behaviour Chain it becomes a bounce. **Escort:** each Orbital Drone keeps a timer; when it is ready, the drone removes the first enemy bullet within its size + 24 uu (`URHBulletSubsystem::BlockEnemyBulletNear`, counted in `GetEnemyBulletStats().Blocked`, with the Glacier block flash), then waits `GetDroneBlockInterval` (2 s). This does not use `SetEnemyBulletBlockers`, because statuses' Glacier pillars overwrite that list every step. Counter `GetDroneBlocks`. Test `RogueHero.Weapons.LoadoutRigs` (Lancer +1 pierce; Escort drones block at most one round per drone per 2 s; the Standard kit blocks nothing).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon slot damage factors and "Load" (keeper_identity_spec §4.3, §9 step 2, D-42).** Each weapon computes `RawDps` at RebuildSpec (Main damage × rounds per volley × burst / fire interval, before the factor) and is ranked against its hero's weapons (ties go to the lower slot). Its factor comes from `RH.Weapons.SlotFactors` ("1,0.7,0.55,0.45", GATED; empty = all 1) and multiplies every spec's damage. `URHWeaponInstance::Tick` re-composes a weapon whose rank moved (a new weapon or a level-up), so the ranking settles within a step. Weapons without a hero owner (tests, the `RH.GiveExtra` harness) keep ×1. API for meta-ui's build-screen readout: `RHWeaponLoad::GetLoad(Hero->GetWeapons())` (1 weapon 1.0, 3 weapons 2.25, 4 weapons 2.7) and `URHWeaponInstance::GetSlotFactor`. **Level track reshape (pattern over damage, ~+8 % DPS per level):** the mechanism already exists (`AddLevel` steps count, size and rate), and the per-weapon level numbers are balance's GATED data. This commit does not change them. Test `RogueHero.Weapons.SlotFactors`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Modifier amplification on owned archetypes, the rule half of keeper_identity_spec §9 step 6 (D-43).** New `URHModifierDef::AmplifiesTags` (`Branch.<Path>.<Archetype>`; the data is balance's). `RHSpecComposition::Compose` takes the owned archetypes (new trailing parameter) and composes each modifier whose AmplifiesTags names one at `RH.Modifiers.AmplifyFactor` (1.75, GATED). Only gains scale: a multiplier above 1 becomes 1 + (m − 1) × 1.75, adds are × 1.75 and counts are rounded × 1.75. Drawbacks (multipliers below 1) and behaviour parameters stay. `FRHComposedWeapon::AmplifiedModifiers` counts them. `URHEffectManager::GetOwnedArchetypes` holds the branches whose key the hero owns and is rebuilt on every blessing apply, remove or reset; a change bumps the weapon-behaviour version so weapons recompose. **Stand-in (swap-out point `RebuildOwnedArchetypes`):** "key" = RHBranches role Entry, until balance's re-author (§9 step 3) adds a Key role. The modifier flattening numbers (§4.2) and the offer weight ×2 (§6, integration) are not in this commit. Test `RogueHero.Modifiers.Amplify` (Heavy Slug ×1.6 → ×2.05 with its fire-rate drawback unchanged, Pierce Rounds +2 → +4, knockback × 1.75, and the effect-manager set follows the key).
- 2026-10-20 — **Intercessions, keeper_identity_spec §9 step 4 (owner moved to weapons), part 1: Thunderhead rework + Time Dilation extension.** `URHUltimate::GrantWeaponBehaviours / ClearWeaponBehaviours` grant weapon behaviours under the source "Ult.<Path>" for the active window. `Advance` and `Finish` clear them, so a stopped or finished ultimate never leaks a grant. **Thunderhead (§3.1):** the strikes stay, and for the 6 s every round Splits into 3 (Split, fan half-arc 35°, children ×0.6) and each child arcs once (Chain +1 bounce, 320 uu; children inherit the bounce). The grant ends with the strikes while the scorch lingers. **Time Dilation (§3.6):** the slow-mo and dash reset stay, and every dash during it fires 3 volleys along the dash line at ×0.6 (VolleyOnDash). Every number is a `Param()` placeholder, GATED. The new `Ult.<Path>.Desc` lines (keeper_identity_text §4) are loc-zh's (step 10); Quicksilver's new line takes only the duration as {0}, so `GetDescArgs` changes when that text lands. Tests: `RogueHero.Ultimates.Storm` and `.Quicksilver` now also check that the grant is present while active and gone after.
- 2026-10-20 — **Intercessions part 2: Forge Nova and Closing reworked (keeper_identity_spec §3.2 / §3.4), plus the new descriptions.** **Forge Nova:** 5 s; every 0.5 s a fire slam ring of radius 600 × Area bursts from the hero's current position. It hits everything inside once (40), knocks it outward (700 uu/s through PushEnemy) and Burns it (20 dps, 3 s). The look is a Forge-amber flame ring racing out in 0.2 s, a flash and a small char ring, never red. The expanding front and the burning ground are gone. **Closing:** 6 s; every 0.5 s every enemy on screen is Marked (so newcomers are caught too), with the Mark lasting past the close, and the hero's weapons get Seek (90°/s, 1 200 uu) while it is open. At 6 s, Marked on-screen enemies below 30 % HP are executed (`IRHStatusTarget::GetEnemyHealthFraction` / `ExecuteEnemy`; a boss follows statuses' execute rules). Executed enemies stream violet motes to the hero. The gem pull, the drain pulses and the heal are gone. **Text:** `Ult.{Storm,Forge,Shade,Quicksilver}.Desc` now use keeper_identity_text §4 EN, each with {0} = duration (Time Dilation's `GetDescArgs` follows). The zh lines are drafts in `zh_overrides.csv` for loc-zh to review. Tidal Wave and Sun Lance follow in part 3. All numbers are `Param()` placeholders, GATED. Tests: `RogueHero.Ultimates.Forge` (10 slams, once each inside 600, nothing beyond, Burn and push on every hit) and `.Shade` (on-screen Mark including a newcomer, Seek granted then cleared, the weak executed, the healthy and off-screen spared).
- 2026-10-20 — **Intercessions part 3: Tidal Wave reworked (D-45) and Sun Lance extended (keeper_identity_spec §3.3 / §3.5). The six Intercessions of §9 step 4 are done.** **Tidal Wave** keeps its name and its fate role (the Reclaimer's second Intercession, `SecondIntercession` test unchanged). At the cast, every enemy on screen gets a slot on a line 420 uu in front of the hero, toward the densest side: rows of 32 at 55 uu, ordered along the line so nobody crosses, with extra rows stacked behind. Each enemy is hit (40) and pulled with `PushEnemy(impulse = 10 × distance to its slot)`, since knock decays at 10/s, so it travels about v/10. At 0.4 s every pulled enemy still alive is Frozen for 3 s (`ApplyStatus(Status.Freeze, 1, 3)`). Bosses take a quarter of the pull and refuse the Freeze, per statuses' rules. The water wall now rolls in from beyond the line to it during the pull, then collapses, and rime marks the frozen. Hook names agreed with statuses: only existing `IRHStatusTarget` calls are used; their role-mass for Pull/Push (§9 step 5) goes inside `PushEnemy`, so Tidal Wave inherits it. **Sun Lance:** a 0.5 s charge first (the rig lamp stays white and an aiming thread lies down the line; the WindUp test accepts it). The screen flash moves to the release, so the lockout test now checks it after the charge. Beam ticks always crit (× hero CritDamage). **Nova deviation:** the overkill amount is not visible after the kill resolves in the enemy sim, so an enemy the last tick hit that has died since bursts for one tick's crit damage within 220 uu × Area (`NovaShare`). Swap-out point: an absolute HP query on `IRHStatusTarget`. Text: `Ult.Tide.Desc` and `Ult.Radiance.Desc` now use keeper_identity_text §4 (Tide {0} = Freeze seconds, Radiance {0} = beam seconds), with zh drafts for loc-zh. All numbers are `Param()` placeholders, GATED. Tests: `RogueHero.Ultimates.Tide` (slots for every on-screen enemy only, each pull aimed at its slot at 10 × the distance, the Freeze after the pull for 3 s) and `.Radiance` (nothing during the charge, crit ticks, one nova per kill, beside the kill).
- 2026-10-20 — **Power split, D-43 (scheduler decision (a)+(b)).** **The release gate is balance's offline ablation** (`Tools/Balance/power_split.py` → `Docs/Balance/power_split.md`, L8 / L16 / T20 rows). It replays a bot run's picks into fresh arenas with each source removed, so it cannot run inside a live run. **Added here: a live per-hit-tagged power split, labelled "indicative, not the gate"**, for playtest logs and quick checks. `ERHDamageSource` {Weapon, Modifier, Blessing, Transform, Reaction, Other} lives in `Debug/RHRunStats.h`; `RHPowerSplit::Note(World, Source, Damage)` tags damage. `URHRunStats` keeps cumulative shares and the L8 / L16 / 20:00 snapshots. The `RH.Stats` line reads "power split (indicative, not the gate) now … | L8 … | L16 … | 20:00 …", and the run-stats CSV gets six `power_*` columns at the end. **Tagged now:** everything the effect manager deals, classed by the dispatching effect's source (Blessing / Tier / Imbue → Blessing, Transform / Fusion → Transform, the Reactions driver → Reaction, Loadout → Other), with `FDamageSourceScope` for explicit tagging. **Not tagged yet:** bullet hits (weapons' bullet subsystem; the hit events carry no damage). Until weapons calls `Note` per balance's tag list, the live line under-counts Weapon / Modifier. **Known limit:** per-hit tagging misattributes multiplicative sources (a +24 % damage blessing reads as weapon damage), which is why it is not the gate. The E2E HubToRunToSummary now runs with `rh.Omens 0`, because a run-start omen (Glass Edge) changes the Max HP the Contracts check asserts. Test `RogueHero.Stats.PowerSplit`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Power split tag rules (balance) and Slack Water's unlock.** Balance's rule for the live line is to tag by who *created* the damage instance, never by who amplified it. `ERHDamageSource` now documents it per source and adds **Ultimate**, which, like Other, is reported as a share of all damage and kept out of the Weapon..Reaction denominator. The CSV columns are `power_ultimate_of_all` / `power_other_of_all`. `Branch.*` effects → Blessing; `Ult*` / `Intercession*` → Ultimate. Bullet origin bytes (Modifier / Blessing / Transform children), DoT ticks by their applier, executes counted as HP removed, and the explicit Reaction scope belong to weapons / statuses / hub per balance's list. **Slack Water** (`Transform.Tide.SlackWater`) is no longer open by default: meta-ui's StillWater Discovery opens it (scheduler decision). `URHOfferFlow::IsInRunPool` now honours the save / Contracts unlock set for content outside the registry (transformations).
- 2026-10-20 — **Keeper identity step 5 (1/3): push / pull with mass by role, execute rules** (keeper_identity_spec §9 steps 4-5; statuses, enemy side).
  - **Mass by role:**
    - `FRHEnemyStats::Mass` (0 = by role, `RHEnemy::RoleMass`): Scrapwing 1, Courser 1.2, Tallyman / Linesman 1.5, Dredge 2, Breaker 2.5, Bulwark 3, planted Pylon / Slagger 6, bosses ≥ 4. The old flat quarter push on bosses is now their mass.
    - Every knock divides by it: `PushEnemy`, bullet Knockback, and the new helpers.
    - **Interpretation:** planted roles are heavy, not immovable, so Tide's pull can still drag a far Pylon in.
  - **New `IRHStatusTarget` helpers** (non-pure; exact overrides in `URHEnemySubsystem`):
    - `PullEnemyToward(e, Point, Distance)` / `PushEnemyFrom(e, Point, Distance)`. The enemy travels Distance / mass and never past the point.
    - Measured: the sim's knock decays 10/s *before* it integrates at 60 Hz, so a knock v travels v / 12, not v / 10.
  - **Execute rules:**
    - `TryExecute(e, Threshold, bRequireMark)` (Closing 30 %, Final Notice, Audit 25 %, Shade T3).
    - `IsEnemyElite` / `IsEnemyBoss`.
    - `ExecuteEnemy` refuses bosses, as GDD §3.5 requires; before this, its default `DamageEnemy(1e9)` would have killed a Marked boss hull.
  - **Bug fixed:** `URHEnemySubsystem` never overrode `GetEnemyHealthFraction` (it always read 1), so effect-side execute checks never fired. Shade T3 executed only through the subsystem's own Mark rule.
  - **Frozen enemies still integrate knock in flight.** Halting zeroes only their own walk, which Tidal Wave's pull-then-freeze relies on.
  - `RogueHero.Enemies.PushEnemy` now pushes its Pylon 6 × harder (mass 6) and expects v / 12.
  - Tests `RogueHero.Enemies.Control.{MassAndPull, Execute}`.
- 2026-10-20 — **Keeper identity step 5 (2/3): freeze rules** (keeper_identity_spec §3.3 Tide Frost, §9 step 5).
  - **API:** `URHEnemySubsystem::SetFreezeRules(Source, FRHFreezeRules)` / `ClearFreezeRules(Source)`, folded across sources:
    - `ChillStacksToFreeze`: the lowest wins (Brittle 2; `FRHEnemyStatuses::StacksToFreeze`).
    - `FrozenDamageTakenMult`: multiplies (Brittle 1.3).
    - `HoldUntilHitCap`: the largest wins (Deep Reserve 8).
    - `bFrozenBlock` (Breakwater amplifier).
  - **Freeze-until-hit:**
    - Each new Freeze (from Chill stacks or a direct Freeze) is held, re-armed every status tick, at most Cap at once. Past the cap, the oldest thaws on its normal clock.
    - A later hit on the enemy, from a bullet or effect damage, breaks the hold and thaws it at once. The hit that froze it never counts.
    - Clearing the rule releases every hold.
  - **Frozen-as-obstacle:** with `bFrozenBlock`, a walking enemy is pushed to the rim of any Frozen enemy it overlaps, after the soft separation. The spatial-hash query runs only while the rule is on.
  - **Owners:** the blessing effects (Brittle, Breakwater, Deep Reserve; balance's keeper cards) call `SetFreezeRules` with their own id on activate and `ClearFreezeRules` on deactivate.
  - Test `RogueHero.Enemies.Control.FreezeRules`.

Each Act II Discovery now carries its RecollectionKey, so there are 30 Recollections (Act III's "≥ 20 read" draws from these). **Audit fix:** `RHMeta::AuditLayers` applies the Recollection Layer gate (`RHStory::IsLayerOpen`) to Discovery Recollections as the Codex list already does. Otherwise FourPaths and Refuse20 (Layer 3, no win needed) would count as pre-win leaks in the audit, though the Codex never showed them. `RH.Codex.ShowLayer 3` on the Act II save: max reachable layer 3; fresh and pre-win worst case: max 2, 0 leaks (PASS). **Noted (not changed):** like the v0.1 ones, the two Layer 2 Act II Recollections are listed locked in the Codex from a fresh save, so Act I shows the Dredge and sluice conditions before the Outfall opens. If they should wait for Act II, the Codex list needs an Act flag on the Discovery; that is a design call.
