It is honoured only when `RHStoryLink::IsRefusalUnlocked` (both fates, meta-ui's `RHStory`) and the arena is `Depth1`; dev `rh.Refusal.Force 1`, `-RHForceRefusal` or `rh.Story.Refusal 1`.

    - Win when the curve is at 0 and `NumAlive() == 0`. Fail-safe at 24:00 is `URHEnemySubsystem::KillAll` + win. **Swap point:** the "optic dies, it sinks" visual is owed by enemies/art.

Collecting one sockets a random modifier into the first weapon with a free socket; the modifier must be in the run pool (`URHOfferFlow::IsInRunPool`), compatible (`RHModCompat`) and not already fitted, and the pick uses the run's Combat stream. - 2026-10-20 — **Intercessions on statuses' enemy hooks (§9 step 5 landed).** Tidal Wave pulls each enemy with `IRHStatusTarget::PullEnemyToward(e, slot, distance)`, which moves it distance ÷ role mass and never past the slot. This replaces the 10 × distance impulse, which became wrong once statuses put role mass into knock and the sim was found to travel v / 12. Forge Nova's slams use `PushEnemyFrom(e, hero, 60 uu)` (the `SlamPushDistance` param). Closing executes through `TryExecute(e, 0.3, bRequireMark)`, which checks HP and Mark and never takes a boss. Tests are unchanged apart from the Tide pull factor (the interface default is a 12 × knock).
- 2026-10-20 — **Niagara step 4 A/B, prepared (it waits for the scheduler's GPU-quiet window).** `Tools\NiagaraAB.ps1` runs unattended (~12–15 min) on L_StressTest (TDD §14: 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets, hero firing) at 1920×1080 windowed. For each tier (Epic, Medium) it runs four times in ABBA order (ISM, Niagara, Niagara, ISM) so drift and shader warm-up cancel. Each run sets `RH.Scalability <tier>,RH.Vfx.Niagara <0|1>`, profiles 900 frames after 20 s (`-RHStressProfile / -RHStressWarmup / -RHStressQuit`), and is killed only by its own PID on a 6-min timeout. It writes `Saved\Profiling\NiagaraAB_<stamp>.md` with per-run frame / game / render / GPU medians and p95, and the decision: Niagara becomes the default only if its frame and GPU medians are within +0.1 ms of ISM on every tier. A dry run (Medium, 30 frames, busy machine) checked the mechanics: the tier applied, NS_RH_CombatVfx loaded and the report was written. Its numbers are noise and are not a measurement. `RH.Vfx.Niagara` stays 0 until the real run.

  - **`Status.Slow`** (new `ERHStatusKind::Slow`, tag in `Config/Tags/RHStatuses.ini`, `ERHStatusBits::Slow` = 1 << 6), for the Tide trait Cold Hands:
    - Magnitude is the slow per stack (default 0.08). Each application while slowed adds a stack, up to `MaxSlowStacks` 3 (24 %), refreshing the timer (default 1.5 s). The total is capped at 60 %.
    - It multiplies move speed after Chill and Blind. Bosses take it, because a slow is not a stop.
    - No look of its own (cool, minor). `GetStatusStacks(Status.Slow)` reads the stacks.
  - **Power split (`RHPowerSplit::Note`, indicative):**
    - Burn DoT ticks go to whoever applied the Burn: a spec-borne status (`FRHBulletSpec::StatusTag`: Divine Conduit) is Modifier, anything else (imbue, blessings, tiers) is Blessing.
    - **Interpretation:** a weapon-behaviour `StatusApply` granted by a blessing is also spec-borne, so it is counted as Modifier until the spec says where its status came from.
    - Executes (`ExecuteEnemy`, the Mark rule) note the HP they remove as Blessing.
  - Tests `RogueHero.Enemies.Control.{Slow, PowerSplitTags}`.

- 2026-10-20 — **The Sump, structure and API (Arena.Depth3; v03_refusal_spec §1).**

  **`Environment/URHSumpFloor`** is the seeding floor's one state, with the same pattern as `URHOutfallWater`.
  - **Lanes:** six conveyor lanes, 450 uu wide. Set A runs at bearing 0° and set B at 60°, each at offsets −1 600 / 0 / +1 600. Alternate lanes run opposite ways at 200 uu/s; the belt speed is [GATED].
  - **Pylon sites:** one per lane, at the lane's upstream end inside the bounds (`GetPylonSite`).
  - **Housing:** r 600 at the centre, sealed (`SetHousingOpen`).
  - **Readers:** `GetBeltAt` / `LaneAt` / `IsOnBelt`.
  - **Writers:** `ReverseLane` / `ReverseAll` (1 s telegraph), `SetSpeedMultiplier`.
  - **Arena event:** the timeline's `LineReversal` reverses every lane at ×2 for the event's duration, then reverses back.
  - **Stepping and riders:** `SimStep` steps it. The hero (not while dashing), enemies and gems ride the belts; enemy fire does not.
  - **Tests:** `RogueHero.Sump.*`.

  **Map and data.**
  - `L_Arena_Sump` is empty, and `DA_Arena_Depth3` uses `URHArenaBuilder_Sump`. The `MapArenas` table has the row, and `World.Sump` reads "The Sump" / 沉井.
  - **Unlock:** `URHArenaSelection::IsUnlocked` opens Depth3 after an Outfall win in Act III (`RHStory`).

  **Builder** (`ARHArenaEnvironment::BuildSump`, `RHArenaSump.cpp`).
  - **Floor:** a Founder-stone hall cut by the lanes.
  - **Lanes:** a sunk metal bed and flush rails on each. The belts render with `M_Env_Belt` once it exists; until then they use the metal material.
  - **Pylons:** Founder drum stacks with a pale Ichor crown (#EFE6D2).
  - **Housing:** the sealed housing ring, an iris, and six conduit rings.
  - **Frame:** the Keep's wall and backdrop.
  - **Still to come:** art passes to the art book, and the frozen-dusk sky (spec §4).

  **Fix in the same commit:** the six `Ult.*.Desc` zh overrides were stale against main's reworked Intercession EN. They are replaced by the Keeper identity rows (the same text weapons applied).

- 2026-10-20 — **Integration's two keeper-identity hooks on the weapon side.** (1) **Split chance:** `Combat/RHWeaponBehaviour.h` defines `RH_SPLIT_CHANCE_SUPPORTED 1`; Behaviour Split's `Magnitude` is a per-hit chance for on-hit splits, with 0 meaning every hit. Chances from several sources combine as 1 − Π(1 − p), and any every-hit source wins. A socketed Split Core always splits. Compose writes `FRHBulletSpec::SplitChance`, and the three on-hit split sites (projectile hit, melee sweep hit, blast) roll `RollSplit`, a hash of step, bullet slot and enemy. That roll is deterministic and draws from no stream, so other rolls keep their sequence. Expiry fans and rings always fire. (2) **Storm T2:** while `RHTierEffects::SplitChildrenAreShots` is true (read once per step), each split child queues an `FRHShotEvent` (slot, split spec, impact point) for on-fire effects. Test `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.SplitChance` (compose, stacking, the Split Core override, about 25 % of hits split in the sim).
- 2026-10-20 — **Weapon behaviour visual signatures (playtest 6 item 1: the designer did not notice weapons changing; Art §3).** Every signature is two layers: a thin near-white core inside a wider stroke or flash in the patron Keeper's imbue accent (player cyan before a patron), so the cool core and Keeper tail follow Art §3.2 and nothing is warm or red. Signatures are capped at 48 per sim step (`URHBulletSubsystem::AddSignatureStreak / AddSignatureFlash`, through the combat VFX layers, so Niagara-ready). Only blessing behaviours draw them; `FRHBulletSpec::BehaviourFx` marks Split, Seek and Chain. **Split:** a fan of short strokes along each child's path at the split. **Seek:** a faint lock thread from the round to the target it acquires. **Chain:** an arc from the bounce to the next enemy. **Charge:** a heavy muzzle burst and a short lance along the aim. **Echo:** a ghost muzzle flash. **VolleyOnDash:** bursts strung along the dash line. **ConvertMelee:** the cleave's arc traced at its reach. **Split fallback ring:** radial strokes. Test `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.Signatures` (spec bits; a plain rifle draws none, and Split, Charge, Echo and ConvertMelee draw theirs). The in-game look check waits for the scheduler's GPU-free window (no game launches during the Shipping package).
- 2026-10-20 — **Niagara step 4 measured: keep ISM as the default (`RH.Vfx.Niagara` stays 0).** `Tools\NiagaraAB.ps1` ran in the scheduler's GPU-quiet window on build 3161cb5 (RTX-class dev machine, 1920×1080 windowed, L_StressTest with 400 enemies and 5 000 bullets, 900 frames after 20 s, ABBA order). Medians: **Epic:** ISM frame 6.23 / GPU 5.73 ms vs Niagara 6.16 / 5.68 ms (no slower). **Medium:** ISM 5.36 / 2.54 ms vs Niagara 5.50 / 2.59 ms (frame +0.14 ms, past the +0.1 ms bar). Per the plan, Niagara becomes the default only if it is no slower on every tier, so ISM stays. Both paths sit far inside TDD §14 (game thread ≤ 8 ms, GPU ≤ 12 ms), and the Niagara path stays available behind the cvar. Revisit when the VFX families grow (more layers favour Niagara's GPU-side cost) or on the 3060 tier. Report: `Saved\Profiling\NiagaraAB_20261020_223110.md`.

The code (`Source/RogueHero/Boss/Sump/`) follows the Floodgate's Boss/Outfall pattern: `URHBossSumpSubsystem`, a world-free `FRHBossSumpBrain`, and `URHBossSumpDef` / `DA_Enemy_Boss_Sump` with the named fallback `MakeSumpFallback`.       - Radiance, LUMEN → Turret;
      - Tide, CISTERN → Bruiser;
      - Shade, LEDGER → Support.

The Cast-offs come from statuses' `DA_Enemy_<Role>` at the run's HP multiplier, and `SeededAliveCap` 60 holds the field readable.

  - **The win:** it raises `URHBossSubsystem::OnBossDefeatedNative` and `URHMetaSubsystem::NotifyArenaBossKilled(Depth3)` (`Event.ArenaBoss.Depth3`), the trigger for `Codex.Root.Entry`. The entry's def is meta's.

  - **Ichor rings:** every `IchorInterval` 2.8 s a band `IchorBand` 320 wide is marked by its two red edge lines for `IchorTelegraph` 1.2 s. The outer line sweeps out from the inner one as the telegraph closes. The band then becomes a pale Ichor pool (#EFE6D2, between thin red edges) for `IchorPoolTime` 4 s.
    - In a live pool the hero moves at ×`IchorSlowMult` 0.55 (`ARHHero::ApplySlow`) and takes `IchorDamage` 5 every 0.5 s.
    - "Expanding" is read as each ring lying `IchorStep` 480 further out than the last, from the housing rim to `IchorMaxRadius` 2900, then again from the rim. Pools already flooded finish their time across a phase change.
  - **Danmaku:** the housing fires staggered ring pairs from its rim, reusing the Floodgate's public `FRHBossOutfallBrain::AlignedLaneAngle` / `FireRing` (read-only use of balance's code). A slot points at the hero, with a 3-slot lane beside it, and the second ring's lane is on the other side. The wind-up arcs tighten on the rim with the lane left dark.
  - **Conveyors:** they reverse every `BeltReverseInterval` 8 s through `URHSumpFloor::ReverseAll(1 s)`, and the floor draws its red chevron flash.

- 2026-10-20 — **The Font P3 "Opened"** (spec §1.5).
  - **The housing opens:** the brain's `bOpenHousing` drives `URHSumpFloor::SetHousingOpen(true)`, and the housing stays open after the kill.
  - **The six conduits:** they light in turn in Path order (Storm, Forge, Quicksilver, Radiance, Tide, Shade). Each beat is `KeeperBeat` 2.4 s: `KeeperWindUp` 0.8 s of red telegraph showing the pattern's shape, then one short pattern fired from that conduit's mouth on the rim, as the Warden's Form III. The patterns are local to the Font (`FRHBossSumpBrain::FireConduit`), not the Warden's `FireKeeper`, because the Font is stationary and fires from six mouths. Each one obeys the still-hero rule: Storm forks, a Quicksilver triple burst re-aimed each time, Radiance spokes, a Tide wave and a Shade ring closing on the hero's spot all have a shot on a still hero, and Forge drops embers, the first on the hero's spot. The housing takes ×`ConduitDamageMult` 0.6 while the beats play, so all six play for a typical build.
  - **The finale:** a 2-arm spiral turns out of the open housing, ring pairs come on `RingIntervalOpened` 3.4 s, and the arena edge closes in. The edge is a red hazard ring round the housing, 2900 → 1500, that pulses through a 2 s warning; outside it the hero takes 8 every 0.5 s (as the Warden's P3).
  - **The kill** is the WIN.
  - **Tests and runs:** RogueHero.BossSump.Opened covers it. A scripted in-world run on L_Arena_Sump went 20:00 spawn → Seeding → all six destroyed → Overflow → Opened → kill → `Event.ArenaBoss.Depth3` + run won. `RH.BossSump.Status` reports every counter.

Signatures stay shape-led (fan, arc, ring) around the cool near-white core, and the imbue tails themselves are unchanged. Re-check with meta-ui's tool on the next capture.
- 2026-10-20 — **Behaviour Chain applies DamageMult per bounce (balance, identity re-key: Live Wire "arcs for 60 %").** Compose writes `FRHBulletSpec::BounceDamageMult` from the merged Chain DamageMult (sources multiply; clamped 0.1–1). At the projectile hit site the bullet sim sets the new `FRHHitEvent::DamageScale` = mult ^ bounces made before the hit, so the first hit is at full damage and each bounce is weaker than the one before. **Enemy side pending (statuses' file):** `URHEnemySubsystem::ApplyHits` must multiply its raw damage by `H.DamageScale` (one factor on `const float Raw = S.Damage * ...`). Until it does, the factor is carried but not applied. Test `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.ChainDamage` (compose factor; hit events carry 0.6^bounces).
- 2026-10-20 — **Player-fire Keeper colours made colour-blind safe (meta-ui's CVD report; scheduler: ΔE ≥ 20 in all three vision modes, within Art §3.2).** New `RHGods::GetCombatColour` returns the Keeper colour for player fire in the world; UI (HUD, cards, rig light) keeps the A1 hexes from `GetColour`. Computed with the same method as meta-ui (Machado 2009 full severity, CIE ΔE76 vs the enemy muzzle #FF7A3A; my numbers reproduce theirs exactly). **Forge** keeps its amber 32° hue at a higher lightness: #FFC17A (was #FF9F3A). ΔE normal 38.6 / protan 21.5 / deutan 20.4 / tritan 41.3, was 21.5 / 16.2 / 8.5 / 20.5. No hue between 28° and 38° reaches 20 at the old lightness, so lightness is the lever, and the darker alternative #995200 fails the 3:1 floor rule. **Quicksilver** lime is lifted to #B3FB60 (was #A8F25A): ΔE 20.4 deutan (was 17.2) and above 39 elsewhere, a barely visible change. These colours apply to the imbue tail and accent (`RHBlessingImbue` fallbacks), the behaviour signatures (`SignatureTint`, which also blends warm accents further toward pale gold-white), the lime Dart / BladeDrone looks and the Burn ground glow. Never on the head, which stays the near-white core. Enemy magenta stays clear (min ΔE 33). Tests: `Imbue.Data` now checks the combat colour; `TracerDim` checks the Dart's new hex.

This is a pierce grant path, not a change to D-47 (Pierce stays a modifier on blessings; the kind serves effects and Intercessions). Hound grants Pierce 2 alongside its Seek. **`FRHWeaponBehaviourMod::Generations`** (Split only, 1–2, largest source wins): with 2, the Split children carry SplitOnHit at the same count, fan and chance, linked to a new derived spec `FRHDerivedSpecIds::Split2` / `FRHComposedWeapon::Split2` (children of the children: × SplitDamageMult again, half lifetime, never splitting further). - 2026-10-21 — **Settings → Export logs (v10_polish_spec D-29).** One button zips `Saved/Playtests` (recursive), the newest `Saved/Crashes/<crash>` folder and the live `Logs/RogueHero.log` (read shared after `GLog->Flush()`) into `Desktop\RogueHero_logs_<yyyy-mm-dd_HHMM>.zip` (falls back to `Saved/` if there is no Desktop), and shows the path. Local only: no network, no upload (designer infra decision).
  - **Deviation:** the zip is written by a small hand-written writer (`RHLogExport::FZipWriter`: PKZIP 2.0 local headers, central directory, UTF-8 names, CRC-32), deflating through the engine's `FCompression` zlib with the header and Adler trailer stripped, or storing a file when deflate does not shrink it. The engine's zip writer lives in the Developer `FileUtilities` module, which a shipping game can't link and Build.cs isn't ours to change. **Swap-out point:** `RHLogExport::Export` if a runtime zip module is ever added.
  - **Test:** RogueHero.UI.LogExport (CRC check value, structure, a real export, then the zip is deleted).

  - Events are in `URHOfferFlow`:
    - a pick of a Keeper's card (blessing, Legendary, fusion, transformation, the L8 choice) +1;

    - a Binding debt called −3.

- 2026-10-21 — **Keeper personalities step 2: Bid temperaments (keeper_personality_spec §2, D-56), integration.** They replace the uniform Term roll. Numbers are `URHOfferTuning` "Temperament" [GATED].
  - **Who bids** (`URHOfferGenerator::SelectBidder`, no RNG):
    - below `TideLowHPFraction` (30 %) only Tide, as a non-patron;
    - else the favor race, vetoed by temperament: Forge below 0 doesn't bid, Radiance at −2 stops;
    - Storm at ≤ −1 may bid from `StormEarlyBidLevel` (L3), Binding only (`URHOfferFlow::TryInsertBid`).
  - **Terms** (`RollTemperamentTerms`): the preferred Term at 70 %, else one of the others.
    - Storm and Radiance prefer Binding, Shade Levy, Forge / Tide / Quicksilver Collateral.
    - `FRHOfferCard::BidTermScale` scales the Levy and Collateral values at `ApplyBidTerms`: Tide Collateral ×0.5; Shade's Levy ×(1 + 0.25·min(2, −D)), so 20 % → 30 % at −2.
    - Tide at +2 has no Term. Forge at +2 adds one more rarity step.
    - Radiance sets `BidDebtRanks` (1, the debt-call's exact cost) for the card to print — meta-ui's frame.
    - Quicksilver sets `BidTimeout` 4 s. `URHOfferFlow::ExpireTimedBid` replaces the Bid with a plain card after 4 s real time; the core ticker runs it because the hand pauses the world.
  - **Interpretation:** "Collateral at ½ value" rounds to whole rerolls, so at today's Collateral value 1 Tide's half rounds back to 1 until balance raises the term value.
  - The Shade off-ledger retaliation (Binding + Levy) still overrides the temperament.
  - Test `RogueHero.Personality.BidTemperaments`.

- 2026-10-21 — **The last two Intercession variant TODOs are wired to balance's RHBranchHooks (0008e01).** **Quicksilver · Momentum:** in Begin it calls `RHBranchHooks::SetSpeedScaleMult(Owner, "Ult.Quicksilver.Momentum", Param SpeedScaleMult = 2)`, and `ClearSpeedScaleMult` when the window ends (Tick) or the ultimate is destroyed (BeginDestroy). So every SpeedScale reads double: Fleet, Drafting, and Quicksilver T3 once tiers reads `GetSpeedScaleMult`. **Shade · Economy:** in Begin it calls `RHBranchHooks::Payday(Owner, Param PaydayMult = 3, "Ult.Shade.Economy")`, an instant payday from the current Debt; nothing happens without the Interest key or Debt. **Temporary deviation (swap-out point: when balance's claude/jovial-satoshi-0eb5d9 merges):** 0008e01 depends on balance's unmerged step-3 effect passes, so it was not cherry-picked. Both calls compile behind balance's `#if defined(RH_BRANCH_HOOKS_SPEEDSCALE)` and switch on automatically when that branch merges. Until then the two variants keep their old effects (×2 move speed; 40 % execute). I compiled the guarded code against 0008e01's exact signatures with the macro forced on: only the expected unresolved links, no compile errors. The text describes the merged behaviour (`Ult.Shade.Economy.Desc`, `Ult.Quicksilver.Momentum.Desc` + zh drafts). Tests: `Ultimates.Variants.Shade` / `.Quicksilver` check the variant params. The hook calls themselves need a world, so they are checked in play after the merge (the detached ult rig has none).
- 2026-10-21 — **QA v0.3 Done-when + Sump probe tooling.** **E2E** `RogueHero.E2E.V03.DoneWhen` (real game instance, shipping run end). The seed: a Breach win and an Outfall win through `ARHGameMode::EndRun`, the six facet entries and 20 `Codex.Recollection.*` keys through `URHMetaSubsystem::MarkCodexSeen` (D-5's Act III gate). Then it asserts:
  - Act III;
  - the Sump unlocked and selectable without force;
  - the Root entry read puts Shade off the books;
  - a Sump win returns Tide and unlocks the Refusal;
  - the Shrine takes the Refusal entry;
  - the Refusal win (integration's detached start) has no patron, plays the ending (`Story.EndingPlayed`, Act 4), and a later win is the silent beat;
  - the per-act layer audit (Layer 4 only from Act III) and the pre-win audit.

  The RHE2EV03 hooks `GetAct` and `ShrineListsRefusal` are filled from public API in the test file, unless an owner fills them. The three Shrine-widget hooks stay with meta-ui; their state is checked in LoopCheck instead. A held Refusal win plays the ending, not the summary, so those tests expect `Ending: no RH player controller` headless, not `summary not shown`.

  **`RH.Hub refusal [on|off]`** steps the Descent row (`URHHubScreen::StepArena`) to "The Breach · Refusal" the way a player does. It logs the Shrine's state: Quota row hidden, portraits locked, Rig row live. `RH.Hub arena` also takes `Sump`.

  **`Tools/LoopCheck.ps1 -FromSave Outfall1`** (Assist):
  - Seed: `RH.Story.Win Depth1/Depth2`, `RH.Story.Set 3`, the Root entry read. Checks: Sump selectable unforced, Act III, `ACT AUDIT PASS` + `PRE-WIN AUDIT PASS`.
  - Then 3 runs: the Sump (Font to 2 % at 21:00), the Refusal via `RH.Hub refusal on`, and the Refusal again.
  - PASS = Sump won; Tide returned + Refusal unlocked; the Shrine's Refusal state; `?RHRunMode=Refusal` on the run URL; the ending full once, then silent; Act epilogue after.

  **`-Arena Sump`**: the Sump forced (probe), with a Font column and `DeathBy` (Enemies' "damage by source in the last 10 s" line). It prints `LOOPCHECK: PROBE` (data only). Offer audit: card 1 / two-of-five / owned-archetype bind only while a legal card is left (keys-only pools run dry after L20).

  - **What it does:** `URHPrimaryLayout` listens to Slate's application-activation change and to `FCoreDelegates::ApplicationWillDeactivateDelegate`. Losing focus during a run opens the pause screen through `ARHPlayerController::TogglePause`'s own gate: never over another menu (hub, level-up), never after the run ends. An open pause stays open on refocus.

- 2026-10-21 — **Weapon-behaviour perf pass (TDD §14), measured in the scheduler's quiet window: within budget, no fix needed.** `Tools\PerfBehaviours.ps1` (9ab33a5) runs L_StressTest `-RHStressMix=2` (all six roles, 400 enemies + 5 000 bullets held), 1920×1080 off-screen, 900 frames after 20 s, ABBA per tier. Loadout in both arms: Pulse Rifle, Scatter Cannon, Seeker Swarm, Spiral Projector, all L8. **Heavy** adds Split + Echo + Chain + Density on every weapon (`RH.Give Behaviour.*`) and a Storm · Tempest Intercession kept live (`RH.Ult.Loop`, 3 casts per run; two Split generations). Build e68a0e2 + 9ab33a5, RTX 4080 + i9-13900KF, one idle foreign editor. Game thread / GPU medians, ms: **Epic base 5.82 / 3.58, heavy 5.78–6.76 / 3.54–3.56; Medium base 5.75–6.12 / 1.96–2.11, heavy 5.54–6.01 / 1.84–2.01.** The first Epic base run read 8.19 GPU (cold shaders) and is discarded. **Heavy ≈ base on both tiers**, with game thread ≤ 6.8 ms and GPU ≤ 3.6 ms against the 8 / 12 ms budget. The behaviour layer, its signatures and the Intercession add no measurable cost. **Bullet cap:** heavy peaks at the 6 000 cap (base ~5 300). 20–234 player bullets per run are recycled for enemy fire (bounded by MaxEnemyBullets, as designed). The per-weapon spawn cap (`RH.Bullets.WeaponSpawnCap` 400/s) refuses ~190–230 k child spawns per run (base ~1.6 k), so the cap and the spawn cap together hold the population; this is not a fix item. **Watch:** the game thread rose from 3.5–4.9 ms (earlier entries, one Pulse Rifle) to ~6 ms with four L8 weapons in *both* arms. Game-thread p95 reached 8.9 (heavy) and 9.9 ms (base) in single runs, so it is loadout/sim load, not behaviours. It is still under budget at the median, but is the next place to look if a 4070 / 3060 pass is tight. An earlier contended attempt (other sessions' bot runs, GPU 90 %+) read heavy GPU at about 2× base on Medium; the quiet run shows that was contention. Not measured on a 4070-class GPU. Report: `Saved\Profiling\PerfBehaviours_20261021_010412.md`.

- 2026-10-21 — **Hero damage attribution** (scheduler: hub's Sump probe died at 15:07 with no enemy damage recorded).
  - **Cause:** enemy bullets (Turret / Flare Pylon shots, the Warden's bullets) and the arena bosses' own damage called `ARHHero::ReceiveContactDamage` directly. The enemy subsystem's 10 s source log (`HurtHero` → "Enemies: hero died …") never saw them, and that report only ran when its own hit was the killing blow. In the probe the killing hits were Turret shots, so no line was printed at all.
    - A repro on L_Arena_Sump at 15:00 without the bot logged "shots Turret=74 contact Enemy.Swarm=27". The old line showed only "contact Enemy.Swarm=39".
  - **Fix:**
    - `ReceiveContactDamage(Damage, FName Source = NAME_None)` is the one sink. The hero keeps the last 10 s of HP lost by source (`GetRecentHits`) and logs "Hero died. HP lost in the last 10 s by source (after armor): …; killing blow: …" on every death, whoever dealt the last hit. Untagged hits show as "unattributed".
    - Callers now pass names:
      - the enemy subsystem uses its existing keys (`HeroHitName`);
      - enemy bullets use "shots Turret / Boss / other" (`FRHEnemyBulletStepResult::MaxSource`: a minimal hook in weapons' bullet subsystem);
      - the Font uses "Boss.Sump hazards".
    - The Warden's and the Floodgate's direct calls are balance's to tag; until then they show as "unattributed".
- 2026-10-21 — **Damage taken by source in RH.Stats and the RunStats CSV** (scheduler; balance's GDD §3.5 damage-taken target, by cause).
  - `URHRunStats::RecordDamage(Taken, MaxHP, FName Source = NAME_None)`: the hero passes its `ReceiveContactDamage` source.
  - `FRHRunMetrics::DamageTakenBySourcePerMin`: % max HP per minute over the stats window, largest first.
  - RH.Stats prints it under "dmg taken/min" as "by source /min (%maxHP): …".
  - The CSV appends one quoted column at the end, `dmg_taken_by_source_pct_maxhp_per_min` = "source=value;…", so existing readers keep their indices. Run totals are in `GetRunDamageTakenBySource()`.
  - The hero's death line records each hit's size after armor, not clamped to the HP that was left.
  - A Sump repro at 15:00 with the bot showed shots Turret 62.6, contact Swarm 56.2, contact Tank 37.4, Support / Vampiric drains / Siphon 12.5 each (% max HP / min).

  - **Fixed-step mode** (`RHHeroSim::IsFixedStep()`: on whenever RH.Bot drives, or `rh.Sim.FixedHero 1`). Everything below runs once per sim step at its start, from `ARHGameMode::SimStep`:
    - the bot (`URHBot::Drive`);
    - hero movement / dash / dash charges / regen (`ARHHero::StepMovement`);
    - the Intercession component (`Advance`);
    - `ARHGameMode::OnFixedSimStep` (a hook for other frame-accumulator systems);
    - the Breath-cache queue and optional-hand rails (`StepOptionalHands`).
  - **Normal play is unchanged:** per-frame movement, smooth at any refresh rate. The fixed step is never used for a human player, so a 144 Hz screen never judders.
  - **Rails:** `URHOfferFlow::Now()` uses the run's sim clock under auto-pick, real time otherwise.
  - **Live golden** (`Tools/LiveGolden.ps1`, `rh.Sim.FreezeAt`, `rh.Sim.DigestEvery`, `RH.LiveDigest`):
    - Two seeded runs are now byte-identical in every decision for about 200 s of sim (before: they parted at the first chest, ~L9).
    - They still drift by about one kill around 205 s from a source not yet found. Checked and cleared: bot input, hero movement, the Intercession, rails / caches, global RNG, camera, render-time LODs, threads.
    - **Interim:** `live_bot_4242.txt` is a **diagnostic, not a gate**. Swap point: the last per-frame sim source.
    - Also found: the Floodgate and Font brains seed from world time (`Brain.Reset(..., GetTimeSeconds()*1000)`, balance). Boss fights stay non-reproducible until they seed from the run seed.

The top sources were Turret shots 62.6, Swarm contact 56.2 and Tank contact 37.4. Numbers alone cannot reach the target without defanging the threats.

It now holds the build, favor / tiers, patron / second Keeper, commitments / transforms, tools / lock / skips / banished ids, Bids / levies / binding, loadout / Form, Disposition and Reserve picks. `BuildOfferContext` moved with it; it reads the shared level / queue / omen from its run.

  - **Events:** `URHPlayerRunState` has its own `OnBlessingChanged` / `OnFavorChanged` / `OnFavorTierChanged`. `URHRunState`'s delegates of the same name fire for player 0 only, so every existing listener (HUD, audio, telemetry, player 0's manager) is unchanged.
  - **Managers:** player 0's effect manager is the world subsystem, bound exactly as before. `URHPlayerRunState::GetOrCreateEffects` gives player 1+ a manager of its own (`URHEffectManager::BindPlayer(Run, Index, Stats)`). It is bound to that player's events and reads that player's blessings, favor and patron (`GetPlayerState`). `URHEffectManager::GetForPlayer(World, i)` finds it.
  - **Players:** `URHRunState::AddPlayer(Patron)` (max 2; the rig and tests until S8's join flow).
  - **Still player-0-only until S3:** the run's other events (transform taken, Bids, binding called, Disposition) and the effects' own `GetRunState()` reads.

  - **Level-up queue:** the pending queue is per player (`URHPlayerRunState::EnqueueLevelUp` / `PopPendingLevelUp`, with its own lock spending and its own `OnLevelUpPending`). `URHRunState::QueueLevelUp` hands every level-up to every player (D-18 shared XP) and still fires the run's `OnLevelUpPending` for player 0. The run's queue getters forward to player 0.
  - **Offer flow:** `URHOfferFlow::Init(..., PlayerIndex)` deals for that player. Its build, tools, Bids, Disposition, lock and skips go to `GetPlayer()`; the shared streams / clock / mode / level stay on the run. Player 0's flow listens on the run, as before.

- 2026-10-21 — **The Font: no stalls** (hub's Sump probe on 1255d2a: 3 of 6 runs stalled at the Font until sim minute 49).
  - **Causes:**
    1. `URHBossSubsystem::GetArenaBossTarget` only knew the Floodgate. RH.Bot had no approach point at the Font and kited at 2–3 k.
    2. **Run 3 (P1, every nozzle alive):** planted Turrets and hanging Supports by the housing filled `SeededAliveCap`, so seeding stopped and kills fell to 1–2 / min.
    3. **Runs 4–5 (P2, every nozzle dead):** P2 has no adds by design, and the ring bullets (7 s) died around 2.7 k, short of a hero at the edge. A human who keeps away could stall it the same way.
    4. The Font's tick ran at most 8 sim steps per frame and dropped the rest, so it fell behind at the probes' RH.TimeScale 5.
  - **Fixes:**
    - **Engage point:** `FRHBossSumpBrain::EngagePoint` / `URHBossSumpSubsystem::GetTarget` gives the live nozzle nearest the hero in Seeding, else the housing rim toward the hero, `EngageStandOff` 380 outside the hull. `GetArenaBossTarget` falls through to it after the Floodgate (a 3-line hook in balance's file).
    - **Seed cap:** only seeds within `SeedCapRange` 2 200 of the hero count toward `SeededAliveCap`.
    - **Stall pressure:** no effective damage for `StallTime` 20 s turns on stall pressure. In Seeding, the housing fires the dodge-lane ring pairs until someone engages; it's a skill check, not a phase skip.
    - **Bullet reach:** Font bullets live `BulletLife` 10 s, about 3 600 uu from the rim, so the whole arena is reachable.
    - **Tick cap:** 32 steps per frame.
    - **Probe after the fix** (LoopCheck -Arena Sump, 3 runs, steered bot): both runs that reached the Font won it in 54.6 s and 68.0 s; run 1 died at 15:42, before the Font. Run 2 had every nozzle dead early with only 31 seeded (the old stall case); it stalled 20 s in P3, the pressure came on and the bot re-engaged.
    - **Log heartbeat:** "BossSump: t …, phase, HP, seeded (alive, near the hero), hero distance[, stall pressure]" every 30 s.
  - **Numbers GATED. Test:** RogueHero.BossSump.AntiStall.
- 2026-10-21 — **Environment kit meshes were imported inside-out; fixed (env).** `create_environment.to_obj_axes` mirrored Y for the OBJ importer without reversing each polygon's vertex order, so a mirror flipped every face of every procedural kit mesh (33: `SM_Env_*` from `create_environment.py` and `create_outfall_kit.py`). Back-face culling then drew the insides: from the -62° gameplay camera the floor showed the slabs' *bottom* faces (the joints looked like thin lines), but from low or diagonal cameras every joint read as a black trench with unlit walls, and wall blocks, pilasters, cornices and Vigil's houses rendered dark and flat. Found by an A/B against `/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube` (no trenches) and an unlit capture (each slab a sunken tray); flipping the OBJ normals alone did nothing (the importer rebuilds them from the winding). **Fix:** `to_obj_axes` reverses each polygon; all 33 meshes re-imported with their current materials (no material or texture rebuilt). **Readability:** the real slab tops are lit, so the gameplay-view floor is brighter: Breach p50 0.0425 → 0.0915, p99 0.056 → 0.124 (off-screen, `Tools/env_contrast.py`); the Sump p50 ×1.26. Both stay under the A1 lit-stone ceiling `RHRender::LitStoneLuminance` 0.20 that the Keeper-hue 3:1 and the telegraph margins are computed against, so no colour rule moves; the old inside-out floor was darker than intended. New baselines for `env_contrast.py`: the post-fix captures.

Routing: keeper_identity_spec §9.1.

  - **Font:** `RHUIStyle::Font` / `CompositeFont()` use the Body family again: Noto Sans SC for Latin and CJK. It reverts e328f3c.
  - **Line box:** every Noto CJK face (Sans and Serif) is a transient root-held `UFontFace` with an ascend / descend override of 960 / −256 font units (`AscendOverriddenValue` 15 and `DescendOverriddenValue` −4, in units of 64). That makes the line 1.22 em, against Noto's own 1.45 em and Roboto's 1.17 em, with Roboto's baseline ratio (0.79). The box still clears the ideographic box (880 / −120).
  - **Layouts that grow:** Codex tiles take a minimum height and grow, like Contracts.
  - **Layouts that fit, never clip:** the hub's detail column and the whole run summary sit in a down-only `ScaleBox`. They shrink only when the content would run past the screen (taller metrics, zh, 150 % text, many Recollections).
  - **Verified:** 10 screens × 1080 / 1280 × 800 × en / zh-Hans, with no clipping and no tofu. Captures are in the scheduler's notes, `ui_g9/`. RogueHero.UI.FontCoverage is green.

A later Refusal win now starts on the silent beat from the first frame; before, the first frames of the full sequence and its shot-1 cue played.
- 2026-10-22 — **Claude×Codex cowork tool (`Tools/cowork/`, user-approved; tooling, no game code).**
  - **What it is:** a Python tool the user runs from their own terminal. Per job from `Tools/cowork/queue.json`:
    1. Claude writes a brief from the job's cited spec sections and files.
    2. `git worktree add -b dev/codex-<id>` is cut off `main`.
    3. `codex exec -C <wt> -s workspace-write -c sandbox_workspace_write.network_access=false --json -o …`, brief on stdin. The bypass flags are refused in code.
    4. The tool commits the round and runs merge_verify's gates in the worktree: `Build.bat` with 0 warnings, `BuildNonUnity.bat -Base main`, and for text changes `apply_zh` → `Localize.bat` → `apply_zh --check`; then `TextAudit.py` and the `RogueHero` suite.
    5. Claude reviews the diff and gates against the docs with a structured JSON verdict. At most 2 fix rounds via Claude-written fix briefs.
    6. Report in `Saved/Cowork/<id>.md` plus a line in `Saved/Cowork/log.md`.
  - **What it never does:** merge, push, rebase or reset (refused in its git wrapper). `main`'s hash is checked before and after every job. The scheduler merges.
  - **Claude calls:**
    - The official `anthropic` SDK and Messages API only (no Claude Code / `claude -p` / Agent SDK), so the user's API credits pay.
    - Model `claude-opus-5-5`, adaptive thinking, explicit effort, streaming, `fallbacks: "default"`.
    - The key comes only from `ROGUEHERO_ANTHROPIC_KEY`, passed explicitly; `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is never read or set.
    - The role + `CLAUDE.md` + `AGENTS.md` + TDD §3/§12 prefix is cached with a 1-hour TTL.
  - **Cost control:**
    - A per-job $ and token cap; each call is pre-checked against its worst case.
    - `--max-jobs` per run; per-call usage and an estimated $ in the report.
    - `--dry-run` (briefs only) and `--mock` (no spend, dry runs only).
  - **Guards:** one job at a time. The tool refuses to start, and pauses before editor-launching gates, while an `UnrealEditor*` / `RogueHero*` process runs. Files outside `allowed_files` fail the round, except gate-regenerated `Content/Localization/Game/`. Job context over 400 k chars or a diff over 300 k stops the job (never truncated).
  - **`AGENTS.md` rewritten** as the coding agents' copy of `CLAUDE.md`: the current non-negotiables, the reading order, docs win, and the cowork job rules. The previous file was a stale v0.1 copy.
  - **Tests:** `python -m unittest discover -s Tools/cowork/tests` covers queue parsing, dry run, cap aborts, gate-failure retry, the 2-fix-round stop, scope violations, `main` untouched (a real throwaway repo), the GPU guard, sandboxed codex argv, the real client's request shape against a fake stream, and key handling. 19 tests, mock client only; no real API or Codex call was made building it.
  - **Seed queue:** 3 `trial` jobs from keyword_notes_spec §1/§3 and intercession_variants_spec §3 (kw retrofit of `ST_Modifiers` / `ST_Ultimates`, the Keywords test's unmarked-name warning). Not run.

Repeat (held) presses never confirm. SpaceBar is swallowed on this screen; any press, including a rejected confirm or SpaceBar, skips the reveal. Reroll / Hold / Refuse are unchanged.

- 2026-10-22 — **Card text contrast (playtest 1c; Art §3 / §6, GDD §9).** `RHCardSlab::StoneLook` and `RHCardTech::FinishLook` expose the existing albedo / grain / inlay / specular tables; named surface multipliers are used by the unchanged bake. `AreaTone` samples alpha-covered sRGB bake pixels, converts them back to linear and measures the extrema inside the same `RHCardTextAreas::Layout` rectangles used by `SetCard`. The resolver and `RogueHero.UI.CardStyle.TextContrast` therefore share the actual bake constants, not estimated face colours. Reclaimer currently uses the same static slab as Stone at each rarity; its plaque remains Bone and it carries no Keeper mark.
  - **Rule:** every text role must clear **4.5:1 against both extrema**, including keywords (the `RulesBase` ink) and the Keeper-coloured Bid / warning line. Prefer the existing ink, then a passing dark / light ink; if neither passes, use a palette-tinted local veil at **alpha ≤ 0.6**, compositing each extreme with `lerp(tone, tint, alpha)`. Each soft rounded veil is confined to its own text block, never a full-card plate. The opaque plaque keeps its Keeper / kind fill; its near-black ink is darker to clear Shade as well. Keeper Bid ink retains its hue through lightness adjustment; A1 Forge #FF9F3A (about 31° in sRGB, rounded to 32° in Art §3) is the warm boundary. The rim / silhouette threshold stays 3:1.
  - **Scope of the range:** static face only. Animated sweep / glint / rim light, channel glow, pulses and motes are transient overlays and are excluded. Reveal timing, geometry, sigils, card-family art and D-31's sealed 0.55 opacity are unchanged.
  - **Derived reference ranges**, rounded linear luminance, from the pure C++ bake in the standalone CPU check (30-unit Bid layout, ordinary plaque row). Runtime and automation always recompute from the bake; these numbers are documentation, not inputs. Rules and keyword share a rectangle; warning and no-line layouts are also enumerated by automation.

- 2026-10-22 - **Boss fair lanes (playtest #5 follow-up, GDD sections 3.5/6; Art section 3.3).** Phase-3 spiral skips only the rotating lane sector: width = minimum hero diameters * 2 * hero radius + 2 * bullet radius + margin = 292 uu; W = 2 * asin(clamp(width / (2 * measure distance), 0, 1)) = 0.491602 rad (28.1667 deg) at 600 uu (zero for nonpositive distance). Lane rotation is 0.25 of spiral angular velocity (23.75 deg/s); it starts deterministically at SpiralAngle + PI / max(1, SpiralArms), midway between arms, with no RNG draw. Storm: 5 forks / 64 deg, 16 deg step, adjacent 167 uu becomes a 32 deg / 330.8 uu gap by removing the centre fork, 25 -> 20 shots. Tide: 26 slots / 150 deg, 6 deg step, adjacent 62.8 uu becomes a 36 deg / 370.8 uu gap by removing 5 central slots, 26 -> 21 shots. Both fans centre the lane on locked aim, superseding playtest-3 "one item always on the aim" for Storm/Tide; FanOffset restores its original half-step formula without the aim-index zero special case. LaneReport retains errors for Warden (Boss.*) rows and warnings for Outfall/Sump; the pre-fix LaneReport run verified that its 40 uu row radius matches the hero collision radius (no mismatch). Spiral centre reuses the existing warm/red projected AddStrip; per-item Storm/Tide telegraphs skip the same FanLane items. Speeds, arms, cadence, timing, HP, damage and RNG order are unchanged; emitted plus lane-skipped counts retain the old spiral cadence. Swap-out point: GATED Tuning.Lanes values (rotation fraction 0.25, margin 20 uu, hero radius 40 uu alongside the existing width/distance). Round-3 correction (2026-10-22): actual failures were FanSector "Even half-step offsets" (float rounding; tests now use 1e-5 tolerance), Rings "spiral rate 172 shots in 9.5 s" (assertion now includes lane-skipped shots), and all three Boss.FormI/II/III Phase3 Spiral report rows (0 uu versus the 240 uu clear-width rule, with 716/644/780 invalid sparse snapshots). The report already samples emitted StepSpiral shots; its endpoint-chord formula shrank nearly full-circle clear arcs to zero and rejected bands with fewer than two shots. Only Boss spiral snapshot measurement now credits the clear diameter contained in an arc wider than pi, including zero/one-shot bands; other pattern measurements and Outfall/Sump warnings are unchanged. Fan centre-to-centre chords are compared against 292 uu without subtracting bullet radius twice. Zero-sector behaviour is retained: FireKeeper ignores the false FanLane return and emits the full fan without a warning when the sector is zero. Readability follow-up (Art section 3.3): the existing spiral AddStrip spans hull radius 340 to measure distance 600, only 260 uu by default, and can vanish at/below the hull radius; no geometry or length changes in this job. Verification: TextAudit passes with zero violations; Build.bat and BuildNonUnity.bat were attempted but UBT startup is blocked by access to user-profile trace/config files outside the workspace. The full RogueHero suite ran against the existing, unrebuilt DLL: 523 tests, 5 failures (the three baseline Boss failures plus E2E.PIE.AbandonToHub and UI.LogExport); this cannot verify the source edits. A rebuilt full-suite result remains required; no green-gate claim.

- 2026-10-22 - **Paused offer-card fidelity (PL.21/PL.22, Art section 12 rules 1/3).** `RHCardSlab::TexScale` is **4** (1080x1904 tablets); tone sampling, veils and face bakes retain that shared scale. Stone now has lit chamfers, V-cut border/window grooves with bronze/gold inlay, material-specific grain/pits and worn arrises; polished rules and recessed slots retain low-amplitude grain. Tech adds engraved seams/collar seats, ten countersunk cross-head screws and a second glass edge/reflection/lip shadow, with five-unit, 2%-contrast scanlines; circuit routing and panel darkening remain. Rules and body use **`RHUIStyle::TypeSpec(ERHType::Rule13).Size` (13 before accessibility scaling)** as the card floor: their ScaleBoxes use `None`, never `DownOnly`. Slate measures wrapped full-size text first; `RHCardTextAreas::Layout` grows body/rules upward in 32-design-unit cache buckets (0..128), reclaiming window height and moving plaque/name/body together inside the unchanged card size, with Bid/warning text measured separately. The same lifted face geometry and grown rectangles feed texture upload, veils and contrast selection; CPU face pixels are shared between upload and tone sampling, once per cache key. Windows key new/upgrade motifs by **`FRHOfferCard::Id`**, not `TargetWeaponId` or display text: ten shipped firing patterns, a separate upgrade-chevron lane, and named `GenericTracer` for unknown ids; non-weapon windows do not acquire per-content keys. Final sRGB packing minimally projects saturated boundary pixels into the existing 30.7-275 degree UI hue interval. **Measured one-time CPU generation:** six base slabs/steles 1277.027 ms, three base frames 1037.611 ms, 54 windows (20 weapon/upgrade, two unknown-id fallbacks, 30 Keeper-kind/hue combinations, one modifier and one Reserve fallback) 4925.074 ms; base inventory 7239.711 ms. All 24 additional lifted slab/frame variants cost 8611.794 ms; enumerated 87-bake inventory totals 15851.505 ms, generated lazily, never per frame. These are standalone MSVC `/O2` measurements of the generator source with math/container shims, **excluding UObject creation, mip upload and rendered RHI cost**: UBT stalled before compilation in this sandbox, so engine timings/full build/non-unity gates and the at-least-500-test suite remain for cowork. Direct compilation of the six changed translation units against UE headers was warning-free; CPU surface/motif checks passed (minimum pairwise weapon pixel difference 17.393%, upgrades >=1.479%); the standalone default/grown contrast matrix passed 27,000 cases (minimum 4.500988:1, maximum veil alpha 0.19), and TextAudit passed. These source-level checks do not replace the UE automation suite. - 2026-10-21 — **R16 (Furnace): Burn ticks faster inside player fields** (keeper_identity_spec §3.7).
  - **The rule:** `URHEnemySubsystem::SetBurnFieldRate(Source, Rate)` (the largest across sources wins; Rate ≤ 1 removes the source) makes a burning enemy that touches a player field tick its Burn Rate × as often for the same duration. At 1.25 that is 25 % more Burn damage per burn.
  - **Fields** are the Lingering Field zones: a minimal read-only hook `URHBulletSubsystem::GatherPlayerFields` in weapons' file, gathered only while a rate is on. `FRHEnemyStatuses::Tick(i, Dt, BurnRate)` takes the rate.
  - **Wiring:** balance's Furnace card sets the rate (1.25, GATED) under its blessing id, and adds its burning Field to the fields if it is not a zone bullet. `BurnFieldTicks` counts uses.
  - **Test:** RogueHero.Status.BurnFieldRate.
- 2026-10-21 — **R19 Whirlpool crush, R20 Breakwater herding and crush** (keeper_identity_spec §3.7). One mechanism, one commit.
  - **Arming:** `IRHStatusTarget::NoteKnockHit(Enemy, HitDamage, bPull)` (non-pure; the enemy subsystem implements it) arms a crush for `CrushWindow` 0.6 s, at the active fraction × the hit's damage.
  - **Rules:** `URHEnemySubsystem::SetCrushRules(Source, FRHCrushRules)` / `ClearCrushRules`, with `PullCrushFraction` 0.15 and `PushCrushFraction` 0.3 (GATED). The largest wins; `bHerding` ORs.
  - **The crush:** while the knock still carries the enemy (≥ 120 uu/s), touching something in front of it (an enemy, Frozen or boss included, an arena wall or an obstacle) crushes once.
    - A pull crush (Whirlpool) hurts only the pulled enemy.
    - A push crush (Breakwater) hurts the pushed enemy and the enemy it hits (crush into the boss).
    - Crush counts as Blessing damage in the power split; `CrushHits` / `CrushDamage` count it.
  - **Herding:** the pushes noted since the last step leave as one wave front (their mean direction and speed), applied before movement.
  - **Callers:** weapons' Backwash pull (`PendingPulls`) and balance's Breakwater / Cold Front pushes call `NoteKnockHit` next to their push. balance's cards set the rules. Cold Front every 3rd volley is balance's card number.
  - **Test:** RogueHero.Enemies.Control.Crush.
- 2026-10-21 — **R24 Collectors hunters** (keeper_identity_spec §3.7).
  - **Weapon:** hunters fire the owner's highest-level weapon (`bHighestLevelWeapon`; ties go to the lower slot). Before, it was the first weapon.
  - **Modifiers:** they inherit its modifier riders (`bInheritModifiers`): split, linger, scatter, wave, ricochet, boomerang. Orbits are never copied. The 6 000 cap and oldest-first recycle hold.
  - **Damage:** 40 % (Collectors' temporary hunters, `DA_Hunter_Shade_Temporary`) and 60 % (Arrears' permanent ones, `DA_Hunter_Shade_Permanent`, updated). Both GATED.
  - **Elite kills:** every elite kill raises one temporary hunter where it fell (`bEliteKillRaises`). That's the new `URHEnemySubsystem::OnEliteKilled(Pos)`, which `URHEffect_Hunters` binds while active; counted in `EliteTriggers`.
  - **Card text:** the Dividend card text params (25 %) are balance's to update.
  - **Tests:** RogueHero.Hunters.Defs, Grant.
- 2026-10-21 — **Archetype pass R7 (keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-61): the ×1.75 synergy guard.** `RHModAmplify` (`Build/RHModifierDef.h`), applied in `RHSpecComposition::Compose`. The D-43 archetype synergy still multiplies a modifier's gains by `RH.Modifiers.AmplifyFactor`, with two exceptions. It **never** touches crit chance (`CritChanceAdd`). It never touches a damage multiplier whose own gain is above **+35 %**: `DamageMult` > 1.35 or `CritMultAdd` > 0.35 keeps its unamplified value. Exactly +35 % is still amplified; +20 % becomes +35 %. Effect on today's data: **Heavy Slug** (+60 %) is no longer amplified (was ×2.05, now ×1.6), and **Crit Lens** (+15 % chance, +50 % crit damage) is unchanged under synergy. Counts, adds (Pierce, knockback, ...) and multipliers ≤ +35 % amplify as before. `RogueHero.Modifiers.Amplify` was updated (Heavy Slug ×1.6, Crit Lens chance and damage unamplified, a +20 % multiplier → +35 %). SoloParity is unchanged (512/512).
- 2026-10-21 — **Archetype pass R10 (keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-63, Storm Tempest): converging fans.**
  - **New field:** `FRHWeaponBehaviourMod::bConverge` (Split only; any source turns it on).
  - **Compose:** sets `FRHBulletSpec::bSplitConverge` on the splitting spec, and `ConvergeTurnDegPerSec` (cvar `RH.Bullets.SplitConvergeTurn`, 720 °/s, GATED) on the children (and on the second generation).
  - **On a hit on an elite or a boss** (`IsElite` / `IsBossType`), the children are deferred with that enemy as their target (`FDeferredSpawn::Target`, set as `HomingTarget` at spawn), and the enemy is **not** put into their hit history, so they can hit it again.
  - **Steering:** a non-homing child steers only to that given target (`SteerHoming`'s converge branch). It never picks another target and stops steering when the target dies.
  - **Hits on anything else fan out exactly as before.** The mechanic only matters where an elite or boss is in the line of fire (D-58: no fit multiplier). The same path covers melee splits.
  - **Data:** balance sets `bConverge` on the Tempest key's Split grant (R1's files).
  - **Test:** `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.SplitConverge` covers the compose flags, more hits on a lone elite with converge, and an identical hit count on a swarm enemy.
  - SoloParity is unchanged (513/513).
- 2026-10-21 — **Archetype pass R12 (Storm Arc Grid) + R31 (Quicksilver Rebound), keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-63: the ground return / bounce-back, one mechanism.**
  - **Data:** `FRHWeaponBehaviourMod::bGroundReturn` and `ReturnDamageMult` (Chain only, default 0.5, GATED; any source turns it on and the lowest mult wins) become `FRHBulletSpec::GroundReturnMult` in Compose.
  - **Behaviour:** when a Chain bounce finds no other target in range and the hit enemy is alive, the bounce is spent on that same enemy. The bullet is placed one step short of it with `StateGroundReturn` set and is not added to the hit history, so the next step's hit is the return, at `DamageScale × GroundReturnMult` (on top of the per-bounce `BounceDamageMult`). With other targets in range, bounces chain exactly as before.
  - **Signature:** a short ground-bounce streak when the Chain signature is on. New counter `FRHBulletBehaviourStats::GroundReturns`.
  - **Data owner:** balance sets `bGroundReturn` on the Arc Grid and Rebound Chain grants (R1's files).
  - **Test:** `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.ChainGroundReturn` covers the compose mult, and checks that a lone target is hit again by returning bounces with no returns when the flag is off.
  - SoloParity is unchanged (514/514).
- 2026-10-21 — **Archetype pass R13 (Storm Downpour, keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-63): Echo is a delayed strike on the impact point, not a repeat of the volley.** This is a global change to the Echo behaviour kind: every Echo source now works this way, including balance's Momentum capstone "Echo once".
  - **Removed:** `URHWeaponInstance` no longer re-fires volleys (`EchoLeft` / `EchoVolleys` / `GetEchoVolleys` removed).
  - **Compose:** Main (and the Charge / Dash / Chain-proc copies) carries `EchoSpec` / `EchoCount` / `EchoDelay` = the Echo mod's Count (1–4) and Time. `MakeDefault` Time is now **0.4 s** (was 0.25), DamageMult 0.6.
  - **The Echo spec is a strike** (`bEchoStrike`): Main's hit standing still. Speed 0, sweeps become a plain hit, no ricochet / boomerang / homing / orbit / ground return, and it never echoes again.
  - **Bullets:** every impact calls `DeferEcho`: a projectile hit, a shell or mine blast (`Detonate`) and a melee hit. It defers Count strikes on that spot, Time·k later.
  - **A straight strike** is inert and not drawn until its delay runs out. It then makes one collision pass with a ghost flash and expires, so its end-of-flight spawns (Lingering Field, Scatter, ...) happen again.
  - **A shell strike** lands where it stands and blasts again; an echoed mine arms again.
  - **Result:**
    - blasts and fields double their area-time;
    - a round whose target stood still hits it again;
    - a target that died or moved is missed (empty ground);
    - the same number applies on every weapon (D-58).
  - **Counter:** `FRHBulletBehaviourStats::EchoStrikes`.
  - **Tests:** `WeaponBehaviour.EchoCharge` now checks strikes per impact; the new `WeaponBehaviour.EchoStrike` checks one strike on the spot 0.4 s after the hit.
  - SoloParity is unchanged (515/515).
- 2026-10-21 — **Archetype pass R14 (Forge Hammer, keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-63): cleaves apply every socketed modifier's on-hit effect.** New behaviour kind **`CleaveOnHit`** (appended; caps Melee, so native arcs such as the Arc Blade qualify; `Merge` also lets a weapon that `ConvertMelee` turns into an arc take it). It replaces Masterwork's ×1.5; balance grants it from Masterwork and removes the ×1.5 in R15.
  - **On-hit modifiers:** `RHModCompat::HasOnHitEffect` = Split Core, Ricochet, Backwash (`SplitAdd` / `RicochetAdd` / `BackwashPullAdd`). With the kind held, `RHModCompat::IsCompatible(Caps, Tag, Mod, bCleaveOnHit)` accepts them on a melee arc.
  - **Converted weapons:** `URHWeaponInstance` no longer refunds them when a weapon is converted, and the ConvertMelee compose keeps `SplitOnHit` / `Ricochet`.
  - **On a cleave hit:**
    - Split Core splits off the cleaved enemy (the existing melee split);
    - Backwash pulls (the existing melee pull);
    - Ricochet sends a bolt (new derived spec `FRHDerivedSpecIds::CleaveBounce` / `FRHComposedWeapon::CleaveBounce`) from the cleaved enemy to the next one, which bounces on with the remaining count. The arc itself never ricochets.
  - **Counter:** `FRHBulletBehaviourStats::CleaveBounces`.
  - **⚑ Follow-up (tiers, offer generator):** offering Split Core / Ricochet / Backwash to a *native* melee weapon while the build holds CleaveOnHit needs the generator to call the new `IsCompatible` overload with the build's CleaveOnHit. Until then a native Arc Blade only gets them if they were socketed before, and a converted weapon keeps the ones it had.
  - **Test:** `RogueHero.WeaponBehaviour.CleaveOnHit` covers compat with and without the kind, Homing still refused, the converted compose with and without, and cleave bounces in play on an Arc Blade.
  - SoloParity is unchanged (516/516).
- 2026-10-21 — **Archetype pass R6 (keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-61): Split recursion only from a capstone or an ultimate, max 2 generations.** Scheduler decision (option a; planning recorded it in D-61, 0bcb7c1): Intercession grants count like capstones, so Thunderhead · Tempest keeps its two generations for its window. `RHWeaponBehaviour::Merge` now runs the guard on every grant first. A Split mod's `Generations` is kept (clamped to 1–2) only if `RHWeaponBehaviour::MayRecurse(Source)`: the source is an ultimate (`Ult.<Path>`, stamped by `URHUltimate::GrantWeaponBehaviours`) or a branch **Capstone** card id (`RHBranches::GetCards()`, role Capstone; blessing grants are stamped with the blessing id). Every other source is forced to 1. The guard reads the stamped source, so no data flag is needed and balance's capstone data works as is. The Prism "lances never crit-split again" rule (R26) is a separate mechanism (balance). Tests: `WeaponBehaviour.PierceAndGenerations` now grants generation 2 from `Ult.Storm`. A non-capstone blessing source is held to 1 generation; a capstone card id and `Ult.Storm` may recurse. SoloParity is unchanged (516/516).
- 2026-10-22 — **`Blessing.Tide.Undertow.Desc` EN departs from keeper_identity_text §8: "{0} m toward the impact" becomes "{0} m in" to fit `RogueHero.UI.CardRules.EveryKindEn`'s 140-character limit (reported failing at 169–171).** Rules and args are unchanged. The spec row is not edited in this change because the spec is outside the allowed files; its owner (balance) should update it.
- 2026-10-22 — **Undertow EN rules text deviation:** `Blessing.Tide.Undertow` EN rules text (`Blessing.Tide.Undertow.Desc`) deviates from `Docs/Design/keeper_identity_text.md` §8: "Hits pull enemies {0} m toward the impact and Chill them. Enemies pulled into each other take crush damage." The new text is: `Hits pull enemies {0} m in and <kw id="Chill">Chill</kw> them; colliding enemies are crushed.` The card's rules area also shows the Tide imbue line under the rule (`Imbue.CardJoin`), so the §8 wording rendered 154–156 chars, over the 140-char card text limit. The rule is unchanged: pull {0} m, Chill, crush damage when pulled enemies collide.

- 2026-10-23 - **Radiance second-Keeper layers (I1, D-65/D-66).** Implemented Radiance.Storm / Forge / Tide / Shade / Quicksilver through the existing post-Begin `ApplyLayerEffect` dispatch, gated by the L8 second-Keeper commitment. `DA_IntercessionLayer_Radiance_*_CodeFallback` owns the GATED, second-Path-prefixed parameters and `LayerBeamDamageMult` budget; balance tuning remains D-66 work. Storm branches from every fan beam, Forge stores capped strips at release and Lance re-aims for 6 s, Tide freezes only beam-strip victims on damage ticks, once per cast, Shade stores the same two-segment geometry for damage and drawing (Solar nova positions follow it), and Quicksilver extends the live beam on new dash serials up to its data cap. Hits and statuses retain the normal ultimate/effect-manager route. Five `RogueHero.Ultimates.RadianceLayers.*` tests cover L8 gates and output, including Prism, Solar and Lance. No other patron layer, enemy file, string table or golden was changed. Build, non-unity and runtime gates are deferred to cowork; the sandbox cannot run them.
- 2026-10-23 - **Radiance.Tide timing interpretation (intercession_variants_spec S2.1).** "At its end" means **when the beam ends**, once, paralleling Stored Sky's "at the end" and Freeze Frame's "when the slow-mo ends". All alive Frozen enemies in the view rectangle are eligible, including externally Frozen enemies, up to `TideShatterMax`. Uses Tide.Quicksilver's existing optional `ShatterEnemy(int32)` probe semantics, with normal budgeted `Hit(TideShatterDamage)` when that enemy-side rule is absent; no shared callable shatter rule exists in the current interface.
- 2026-10-23 - **Radiance.Quicksilver origin and Radiance.Shade elite query.** The hero-following origin was already base Sun Lance behavior; keeper_identity_spec S3.5 does not anchor it at the cast point. Base behavior stays intact. Extension affects damage, Solar, rendering and active duration, while Lance keeps its original `Duration / Lances` re-aim slices. Shade uses the existing `IRHStatusTarget::IsEnemyElite(int32)` query implemented by `URHEnemySubsystem`; no elite-query probe or enemy-side change is needed.
- 2026-10-23 - **Radiance.Tide optional shatter ownership (I1 fix round 1).** Enemies owns adding `URHEnemySubsystem::ShatterEnemy(int32)`, the optional function probed by the layer and its fallback-damage test. Until it exists, the layer retains its budgeted `Hit()` fallback. Shade uses the existing `IsEnemyElite(int32)` interface without a probe; no enemy file is changed.
- 2026-10-23 - **Radiance round 3 gate and Cold Light correction (intercession_variants_spec S1.3/S2.1).** Saved/Logs/RogueHero.log reports five failures in `RogueHero.Ultimates.Gating.LayerTable` at RHIntercessionGatingTests.cpp:159: "other patron layers remain unauthored". That empty-Params expectation is superseded for Radiance by its five implemented L8 layers. The Radiance branch now requires mechanic data, a beam budget strictly between 0 and 1, second-Path-prefixed mechanic keys, finite values and no Radiance variant-key collisions; every other check remains intact. `RogueHero.Ultimates.RadianceLayers.Tide` now requires Solar nova victims outside the strip to take damage without Freeze; its end-shatter count changes from three to two (the beam victim and the externally Frozen in-view victim), retaining the beam Freeze, shatter damage, timing and offscreen checks. Death and Solar novas no longer apply Freeze; their damage and end-of-beam shatter behavior are unchanged. Layer budget data and no-layer defaults are unchanged; no golden re-record. Runtime/build gates remain for cowork per the sandbox brief.

- 2026-10-23 - **Quicksilver second-Keeper layers (I1, intercession_variants_spec S1.3/S2.1, D-65/D-66).** Storm / Forge / Tide / Shade / Radiance use the existing post-Begin layer dispatch and GATED code-fallback Params in `LayerDefs()`, including `LayerVolleyDamageMult` for dash volleys and Momentum pressure waves. Dash paths are sampled per tick over `LayerDashSeconds` (0.25 s), with `LayerMaxDashes` and `LayerMaxSegments` bounding retained geometry; Radiance hits each enemy once per recorded dash, even at shared segment endpoints. Tide Freeze lasts `GetActiveDuration() - Age + TideFreezeMargin`; only dash-touched, still-alive, still-Frozen members shatter through `Hit()`. Phantom firing uses equipped `URHWeaponInstance::TickPhantomFire`, independent per-afterimage cadence and copied/remapped derived bullet specs scaled by `ShadePhantomDamageMult`; sweeps, orbits and returns use the fixed afterimage origin, with no spawned Actors. Orbiter shots use per-drone cadence at that origin; contact-only orbiter copies emit stationary contact zones at their orbit positions and contact cadence without altering the hero drone renderer. Phantom hits retain the ordinary bullet/weapon attribution and reaction route, and are **not** added to the ultimate `DamageDealt`. No enemy-side query or requires-probe was needed; no Enemies, Player, EffectManager, string-table or golden edits. Five layer tests cover pre-L8, matching and other second Keepers across base, Vector, Momentum and Rebound. Fix round 1 (2026-10-23) chooses spec-lifetime option (a), release: each private phantom spec graph is retired on slow-mo end, Finish/End, ResetLayer and BeginDestroy (Tide lifecycle pattern). The bullet subsystem reuses released slots only at a later simulation-step boundary after all live bullets, deferred spawns and retained hit events stop referencing any graph member; whole-graph retention protects children and cycles (TDD S4/S5). Phantom firing skips weapons whose specs need rebuilding, leaving the hero normal update responsible for RebuildSpec. Added two-cast/multi-dash reclamation coverage (natural end and early Finish), live-reference retention and dirty-weapon coverage; the existing gating-test change only adds Quicksilver data/key assertions. The mixed EName/FName range-for now uses an explicit FName array; audited the listed initializers/includes for non-unity compilation, with no file-scope using namespace. Builds, automation and text gates are deferred to cowork as required. Fix round 3 changes PhantomLifetime to use a fresh ultimate per cast because a finished ultimate is not restartable; the replaced instance is finished before reassignment and fixture teardown finishes the current instance, with all original assertions intact.
- 2026-10-23 — **D-70 keyword text completion (keyword_notes_spec §2):** added Crush, Herd, SunCharge and Resonance Name/Def rows and glossary entries (58 terms); re-keyed Momentum to MomentumStack, retaining display "Momentum"; redefined Echo as a delayed strike on the same impact point (D-63). Marked Crush / Herd in 3 offer-card strings (Undertow, Riptide, Cold Front), first occurrence with matching EN/zh ids and at most 3 marks; Cold Front uses the mechanic word 驱赶. Drafted Herd.Def, SunCharge.Name/Def, Resonance.Name/Def, Echo.Def and the Cold Front zh wording need loc-zh review. Crush.Def retains the spec EN (pushed/pulled enemies hurt each other on collision) and the job-provided zh (includes walls and ice) verbatim: scope mismatch pending planning / loc-zh reconciliation. Resonance is display-only; no gameplay numbers changed. kw_markup lacks Crush/Herd patterns, so they were supplied in memory with matching restricted to ST_Blessings; no tool file changed. apply_zh only updates existing PO translations, so affected PO keys/sources were synchronized before applying overrides; engine localization compile, builds and automation are deferred to cowork under the sandbox brief; no SoloParity golden re-record.
- 2026-10-23 — **D-57 package reading (scheduler decision, job r1a4-packages-per-spec).** keeper_identity_spec §3.7 D-57 "key + 2 amplifiers + capstone" reads as "key + the amplifiers its §3 row lists + capstone": Storm and Tide archetypes have 1 amplifier, Forge / Shade / Radiance / Quicksilver have 2. `RogueHero.Offers.ArchetypePackages` asserts the per-archetype S3 count; `Tools/Balance/archetype_bands.py` accepts 1 or 2 distinct amplifiers; RHBranches unchanged.

- 2026-10-23 - **Co-op S5 (v04_coop_spec S2/S3):** director rings use the living heroes' GetPos2D centroid with unchanged radius and angle logic; a one-hero branch keeps the original position read, and no living heroes falls back to Primary. URHRunTuning.Coop{Cap,Rate,HP}MultPerExtraPlayer defaults are 1.5 / 1.5 / 1.6, GATED pending S12 two-bot balance runs. Factors use 1 + (MultPerExtraPlayer - 1) * max(0, NumHeroes - 1), counting joined heroes, not only living heroes, so a fallen partner does not reduce pressure. The rounded cap is clamped to the existing enemy-subsystem capacity (512), retaining the existing timeline ceiling and bullet cap (6000). Regular spawn rate and drop-in cadence use the rate factor; director spawn HP (regular spawns, elite waves, non-boss timeline events and their escorts, drop-ins and SpawnRing) uses the HP factor without changing damage or boss HP paths. Non-boss event spawns are director spawns under S5. The boss-time branch returns before consuming HPMul; boss subsystems and their adds retain unscaled HPMult. Fix round (2026-10-23): the gate log showed the bare-world fixture discovered zero joined heroes; no shared S1/S4 two-pawn fixture exists. Centroid and radius tests now call the same pure position/living-mask selection as the runtime adapter, with exact coordinates and the IsDead living predicate. Missing primary/stats reset all factors to one with scaling inactive; solo returns before tuning or multiplication. Solo skips scaling arithmetic instead of multiplying by 1; RNG draws/reseeds and the SoloParity golden are unchanged.

ModuleDescriptor.cpp confirms Runtime and RuntimeNoCommandlet load in Shipping game targets (the latter excludes commandlets); Editor does not. Explicitly enabled XInputDevice, GameInputWindows and GameInput for Win64 in the project. RawInput remains disabled because it is deprecated and its generic USB keys require device mappings outside the existing gamepad IMCs; WinDualShock remains disabled because its Build.cs requires LibScePad, and a recursive engine search found no LibScePad SDK/library. GameInputWindowsLibrary's ThirdParty/GameInput.h and Binaries/x64/GameInput.lib are present; local C:/Windows/System32/GameInputRedist.dll is 3.5.270.0. DefaultInput.ini now configures [GameInputPlatformSettings_Windows GameInputPlatformSettings]: bProcessController=True, bSpecialDevicesRequireExplicitDeviceConfiguration=True, bProcessGamepad=False, bProcessKeyboard=False, bProcessMouse=False. GameInputDeveloperSettings.cpp disables controller processing by default on Windows; its header explicitly warns that bProcessGamepad=True alongside XInput duplicates events, so XInput retains GameInputKindGamepad ownership and Windows retains KBM. **Incomplete non-XInput support:** GameInputDeviceProcessor_Controller.cpp only emits mapped keys with a VID/PID DeviceConfigurations entry; without one it only logs axes/buttons. No profiles are supplied in this engine's GameInput Config directory or project. DualSense/DualShock, Switch Pro and generic device report layouts/VID-PID mappings in Config/DefaultInput.ini are **not verified, not changed**: source/config searches cannot establish physical device layouts, and running hardware probes is outside this job. Do not claim these pads work from plugin enablement alone (CLAUDE.md #10, TDD section 11); completion requires verified device profiles, without changing the IA/IMC bindings. (2) CommonInputBaseTypes.h and PlatformSettingsManager.cpp, plus UObject per-object config section construction, establish [CommonInputPlatformSettings_Windows CommonInputPlatformSettings] in DefaultGame.ini; added bSupportsGamepad=True and DefaultGamepadName=Generic (CommonInputBaseTypes.cpp's GamepadGeneric). The requested InputData is a property of [/Script/CommonInput.CommonInputSettings], not the platform class. Swap-out: point CommonInputSettings.InputData at an authored subclass when one exists. CommonButtonAcceptKeyHandling=TriggerClick and both virtual-pointer/virtual-accept cvars remain unchanged. (3) Searched all Source/RogueHero for UE_BUILD_SHIPPING, !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING, WITH_EDITOR, nogamepad and command-line reads, FNavigationConfig/SetNavigationConfig, SetInputMode, IInputDeviceModule/IInputInterface: no input-setup Shipping/editor guard, nogamepad reader, navigation override or custom device interface found; the sole SetInputMode is GameOnly in BeginPlay. RHPrimaryLayout's unattended/RenderOffscreen switch only suppresses focus-loss pause. Build.cs has no non-Shipping-only module dependencies; DefaultInput.ini and DefaultEngine.ini had no pad/joystick disabling setting. No disabling condition found in the read-only Source/RogueHero.Target.cs or Tools/Package.bat; no edits needed there. Steam OSS remains enabled with SteamDevAppId=480 and bRelaunchInSteam=false; neither project Platform code nor OnlineSubsystemSteam source calls SteamInput/SteamController. External Steam client interception/virtual-pad emulation for App 480 cannot be verified without a Steam/hardware launch; **not verified, not changed**, no evidence warrants changing [OnlineSubsystemSteam] (TDD section 11, CLAUDE.md #10). (4) URHInputConfig::Build creates all IA_* and IMC_Gameplay_KBM/Pad/Menu objects with NewObject, the existing Week-1 fallback, not asset-path loads; Content/RH has no IA_/IMC_ assets. No cook change because there are no input assets to cook; /Game/RH remains AlwaysCook. BeginPlay adds both gameplay contexts together and SetupInputComponent binds IA_Pause without Shipping guards; Pad includes Gamepad_Left2D, D-pad and gamepad buttons. Added RogueHero.Input.Packaged.Plugins (IPluginManager enablement for all three plugins) and .Mappings (contexts, gamepad keys and pad pause); Build.cs adds only Projects for these tests. ShipCheck now validates UBT TargetReceipt.cs's JSON Plugins/BuildPlugins against a Win64 Shipping game receipt, SHA256-matches its executable build product to the staged executable, requires the staged GameInput MSI, fails missing/mismatched evidence, and exposes -ChecksOnly.
