- 2026-10-24 - **Co-op S8 local players and input (v04_coop_spec S3/S4).** P1 retains KBM or pad 1; P2 joins on an unused pad with A/Start and leaves with View/Back (Gamepad_Special_Left). CreateLocalPlayer/RemoveLocalPlayer are hub-only; join retains the Act II gate required by v04_coop_spec S1 (unlock after the first Breach win), using the fix-round item 5 exception; local players persist on the game instance across arena travel. Windows uses PrimaryUserSharesKeyboardAndFirstGamepad: Config/DefaultInput.ini [InputPlatformSettings_Windows InputPlatformSettings] input.DeviceMappingPolicy=1. Verified against UE 5.8 GenericPlatformInputDeviceMapper.cpp GetDeviceMappingPolicyFromConfig: GConfig->GetInt reads that exact section/key from GInputIni; it is not a console variable. InputDeviceMappingPolicy.h assigns value 1; RemapControllerIdToPlatformUserAndDevice preserves controller 0 on the primary user and subsequent controller IDs on their users. The existing setting is retained because the engine explicitly reads it, with bOffsetPlayerGamepadIds=False and bUseSplitscreen=False (D-23). RHGameViewportClient extends the already-enabled CommonUI viewport to handle joins before routing to a local player. P1 opens the hub only through main's ARHHubGameMode::BeginPlay next-tick path; the added controller BeginPlay and hub PostLogin calls serve local index 1 only. OpenHub tracks initialization per controller, preventing another hub push or recovery line even when the recovery screen covers the hub; P2 still only initializes its empty root and camera. Each controller adds its own existing code-fallback mapping contexts (host KBM + Pad; guest Pad) and owns a URHPrimaryLayout. Both owned roots use the full shared viewport: UE LayoutPlayers assigns P2 a zero scene rectangle with split-screen disabled, so AddToPlayerScreen would hide P2's hand. Widget ownership and user focus remain per controller; S9 replaces the overlapping full-size hands with mirrored halves. ARHGameMode::OfferFlows[2] / GetOfferFlow(i) retain GetOfferFlow() as player 0. Runtime registration and test-only AddHeroForTest share player 1 initialization and Init(..., 1), which binds that player's OnLevelUpPending; player 0 retains its run delegate and call order. The existing flow still used shared Offers/Bids streams for both indices: player 1 now owns independently seeded flow streams, with no draw or reseed of host/shared streams. The cited coop-s1-s2-plan is absent from Docs; following the brief and TwoBuilds pattern, P2 starts with Arc Blade L1 via the temporary SecondPlayerStarterWeaponFallback (Weapon.ArcBlade), until S11 / the DA_ data supplies a per-player loadout; existing loadout selection is host-wide and URHRunTuning has no guest starter, the host's chosen patron, default run tools/favor and the starter offer pool, with no host meta bonuses, run memory, loadout, omen or Muscle Memory. S11 replaces that provisional guest initialization with profile selection. Co-op hands still pause; both Tick entry and the mid-frame step guard stop simulation while either OfferFlows[0] or non-null OfferFlows[1] awaits a pick, including UI-less paths, with the solo null-flow branch unchanged. Pause requests across both controllers must drain before unpausing (temporary, swap-out S7). Refusal remains solo per this job, deferring v04_coop_spec S6: the hub rejects a Refusal launch while P2 is joined, runtime refuses its second pawn, and StartRefusalDetached never creates flow 1. Added a headless two-hero test for distinct full hands, unchanged host build/queue/cards/stream positions after a guest pick, legacy accessor parity, registration idempotence and Refusal isolation. Fix round 1 (2026-10-24): renamed the viewport World local and the controller RemovePlayer Player parameter to avoid inherited-member shadowing. Audited RHEffectManagerLocal: its only symbols are GEventOrder and SubIndex and all three uses outside the namespace are already qualified; ApplyOwned already declares P = GetPlayerState() and guards it before use. Builds, non-unity, >=500 automation tests, SoloParity and localization gates are deferred to cowork as required; the solo golden is unmodified. Fix round 2 (2026-10-24): Saved/Logs/RogueHero.log:6024 identifies the sole RuntimeTwoHeroes failure as "two registered heroes" (expected 2, got 1), not empty hands. The bare world never runs the host controller's PostInitializeComponents registration; the fixture now calls World->AddController before possession and asserts host discovery. Both flows use RHTwoBuildsTest::Pool with the existing test seed 13 via the optional test-only AddHeroForTest TestPool parameter, forwarded through RegisterSecondHero to the same Init(..., 1) path; all previous assertions remain intact. QueueLevelUp already enqueues Players in order, including P2, before broadcasting the legacy run delegate, so no runtime enqueue or player-0 seed/stream/start-order change was needed. RegisterSecondHero checks GetBoundHero/GetHero before binding effects or initializing the ultimate, preserving BeginPlay initialization and supplying it for headless heroes. No navigation visibility change: RHLevelUpScreen binds pad shoulders (IA_UI_Prev/Next) to MoveCursor and direct SetUserFocus, and handles A in NativeOnPreviewKeyDown; these do not use Slate directional hit-test navigation. P2 stays HitTestInvisible so its entire layout passes the shared mouse through to P1. Optional Refusal-launch feedback is skipped. Build/non-unity, automation/SoloParity and physical pad verification remain for cowork under the job restriction; the solo golden is untouched.

- 2026-10-26 - **docs cleanup X5:** Compared with main, `Docs/02_TechnicalDesign.md` §17 removes 29 duplicate entries, retaining their first occurrences as the authoritative records: 2026-10-18 spawn director and adaptive unity; 2026-10-19 end-to-end QA, environment GPU/readability measurement, Shipping checks, Heat 1-4, Muscle Memory stage 3, swarm finishing-window TTK, player wear stages, Heat 0/3 LoopCheck and player rig hues; 2026-10-20 Depth2 M2 map/water/kit/arena selector (two duplicate copies), status-hook duo reactions, batched ricochet events, accessibility, Acts II-III story state, second Intercession, bid Lien banking, Act II Codex wiring, Act II hub, per-arena wins, structural arena keys, ricochet-trigger duo reactions, L16 hand UI, blessing-to-weapon behaviour API, patron traits, Path tier reworks and Keeper identity UI. `Docs/Playtests/NEXT_playtest.md` is deleted without a replacement file; its reference in `Docs/Playtests/2026-10-19_human_2.md` under "What the tester reported" becomes "answers to the then-current playtest request". Entire documents move with archived (done) status: `Docs/Art/g14_enemy_models_plan.md` to `Docs/Archive/Art/g14_enemy_models_plan.md`, `Docs/Art/artbook_v0.3_gaps.md` to `Docs/Archive/Art/artbook_v0.3_gaps.md`, and `Docs/Design/coop_evaluation.md` to `Docs/Archive/Design/coop_evaluation.md`; the archived gap list's G9 row replaces its partial status with completion evidence linked to `Docs/02_TechnicalDesign.md` §17's 2026-10-21 "G9: P24 body type, en + zh" entry. Archive references replace the old paths in `Docs/03_Roadmap.md`'s v0.1 additions and full-game specs pointer, `Docs/05_ArtDirection.md` §12.1's gap-list pointer, `Docs/Design/full_game_specs.md` §7 and `Docs/Design/v04_coop_spec.md`'s introduction; the archived gap list's source link targets `Docs/05_ArtDirection.md` §12, and the archived co-op evaluation's opening decision link targets `Docs/Design/full_game_specs.md`. `Docs/01_GameDesign.md` §5.4 removes the pre-redesign tier table, bullet-imbue paragraph/table and original five-blessings paragraph, replacing the obsolete introductory note with a link to `Docs/Design/keeper_identity_spec.md` §3 and removing the dangling Branches sentence about the deleted paragraph; §5.5 replaces the struck-through L16 Legendary fallback sentence with a link to `Docs/Design/build_depth_spec.md` §1 and removes the superseded label from the retained L16 transformation paragraph, whose rule text is unchanged. `Docs/Design/keeper_identity_spec.md` §5.4 replaces its superseded note and fit-table text with a link to §3.7. `Docs/Localization/zh_glossary.md` §9 replaces the first-pass naming history, table and tier-line note with a link to `Docs/Localization/zh_naming.md`; `Docs/Balance/sims.md` §0b and §0c replace their obsolete baseline bodies with links to §0; `Docs/03_Roadmap.md` v0.3 row 6 replaces its superseded cross-Keeper reaction text with a link to `Docs/Design/build_depth_spec.md` §4. `README.md`'s Key decisions replaces 20-minute survival and a boss at 20:00 with 15-minute survival and a boss at 15:00, matching `Docs/01_GameDesign.md` §7. No game rule, number or scope changes.

- 2026-10-27 — **Steam Deck tier, suspend / resume, hub version hash, Shipping without `RH.*` commands (v10_polish_spec §4 Steam Input / Deck, Deck perf tier, Build hygiene; §5 Deck tier).**
  - **Deck preset.** `ERHScalabilityTier::Deck` is a fifth graphics preset, not an engine level. Its data is `[RH_Deck DeviceProfile]` in `DefaultDeviceProfiles.ini` (also launchable with `-dp=RH_Deck`); `DefaultScalability.ini` documents it. The `sg.*` lines set the groups: GI 0 and reflections 0 (Lumen off; **sky light, not SSGI**), the rest 1, `sg.ResolutionQuality=60` (TSR from 60 %). The other lines are held at `ECVF_SetByGameOverride` while the preset is on and restored when any other tier is applied: `r.Shadow.Virtual.Enable=0` (shadow maps; the project setting would otherwise outrank scalability), `r.VolumetricCloud=0`, `rh.FX.DensityLODBias=1`. The arena rig (`RHEnvTiers::For(4)`) uses Low's sky and holds the weather at overcast ("overcast weather only"); the rolled plan and telemetry are unchanged. Bullet cap unchanged.
  - **Impact-FX density LOD one step earlier.** There was no cvar (the PL.18 thresholds were constants). New `rh.FX.DensityLODBias` (default 0 = the art book ladder, dense > 20 kills/s, crowd > 60). At 1, crowd takes the dense rung (> 20) and dense starts at half of it (> 10). Visual only; the sim and the damage-number aggregation (its own 20 kills/s rule) never read it, so goldens are untouched.
  - **Settings.** Graphics steps Deck · Low · Medium · High · Epic (`RHScalability::PresetOrder`); `ST_UI Settings.Graphics.Deck` "Steam Deck" en + zh-Hans. A pick is saved in GameUserSettings.ini `[RHScalability] Preset`.
  - **Auto-select.** Detection is the existing `URHPlatformSubsystem::IsSteamDeck()`: env `SteamDeck=1` (set by the Deck's Steam client) or `-SteamDeck`. Not `ISteamUtils::IsSteamRunningOnSteamDeck` (the platform layer makes no Steamworks SDK calls), and no 1280×800 heuristic (a desktop window of that size is not a Deck). At start-up (not in the editor) the Deck preset applies when the player saved it, or on a Deck when no preset was ever saved; the first auto-select is saved, so a later pick in Settings wins. Tests: `RogueHero.Core.Scalability.Deck`, `RogueHero.UI.Shared.GraphicsStep` (Low −1 is now Deck), `RogueHero.ImpactFX.DensityLODBias`.
  - **Not measured:** no Deck hardware, so the ≥ 40 fps / 30 fps floor is unverified. **Watch:** 60 % is under the 67 % telegraph floor measured at 1080p (`DefaultScalability.ini`, Art §3); at 800p it is the spec's number and must be re-checked on the Deck. Swap-out point: `sg.ResolutionQuality` in `RH_Deck`.
  - **Suspend / resume, co-op.** The focus-loss pause (2026-10-21) now opens one pause in local co-op: only the first local player's layout opens it, and none while any player's pause is open (the pause screen pauses the world for both). It never unpauses by itself. Not exercised with two pads in this pass (no co-op test fixture with controllers).
  - **Version.** `RogueHero.Build.cs` reads the checkout's `.git` (worktrees and packed refs included, no git process) and writes `Intermediate/RHBuildInfo/RHBuildGitHash.h` only when the hash changes; HEAD and the branch ref are UBT external dependencies, so a commit re-runs the rules and recompiles one file (`Core/RHBuildInfo.cpp`). The hub corner shows `v<ProjectVersion> · <id>`: the stamped `[RHBuildInfo] BuildId` if set, else the short git hash, else the version only. Hub only. Test: `RogueHero.Core.BuildInfo`.
  - **`RH.*` commands compiled out of Shipping.** All 146 `RH.*` console command registrations (static `FAutoConsoleCommand*` objects and the `RH_CMD` macro calls) now sit in `#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING` (36 files; 11 were guarded already). Their handler functions still compile (C4505 is off in UE). `Tools/check_shipping_commands.py` fails on any `RH.*` command outside a Shipping-off guard (`!UE_BUILD_SHIPPING`, `WITH_EDITOR`, `WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS`), including project macros that wrap a registration; it self-tests on each run. `Tools/Balance/RunTests.ps1` runs it (a failure counts as one failed test) and `Tools/RCGate.ps1` runs it before the Shipping compile. **Deviation:** console *variables* named `RH.*` / `rh.*` stay in Shipping, because shipped code reads them (e.g. `RH.Bullets.WeaponSpawnCap`, `rh.UI.PauseOnFocusLoss`). The Shipping build has no console, so they are not player-reachable. **Open follow-up (QA tool owner):** `Tools/ShipCheck.ps1` bot runs drive a *Shipping* package through `-RHExec` with `RH.Offer.AutoPick`, `RH.Later`, `RH.Hub` and `RH.TimeScale`. Those commands no longer exist in Shipping, so on a Shipping package its runs stall at the first level-up (its trace reports "not a console command"). The fix is to run the bot runs on a `Tools\Package.bat Test` package (UE's Test configuration keeps `RH.*`; `UE_BUILD_SHIPPING` is 0 there) and keep the Shipping package for the receipt and plugin checks (`-ChecksOnly`). That needs ShipCheck's receipt and exe paths to accept Test, and is not done in this change.
  - **Legibility.** `RogueHero.Platform.DeckMinTextPt` (pure) runs every P24 step and every direct size from 1 to 64 through `RHUIStyle::DeckFloor`, the Deck UI scale and the engine DPI curve at 1280×800, at the 100 % text size. The smallest is ≥ 9 pt (`URHPlatformSubsystem::MinDeckTextPt`; 9 pt = 12 px at 96 DPI).

- 2026-10-27 - **ShipCheck bot runs on a non-Shipping package; Shipping keeps the receipt, plugin and "no RH commands" checks** (closes the open follow-up in the 2026-10-27 Deck tier / Shipping without `RH.*` entry; v10_polish_spec §4 Build hygiene).
  - **Deviation from the follow-up's plan:** the bot runs were to use a `Tools\Package.bat Test` package. The installed UE 5.8.3 at `E:\UE5\UE_5.8` refuses it: UBT stops with "Targets cannot be built in the Test configuration with this engine distribution" (an installed engine ships no Test engine libraries). The bot runs therefore use a **Development** package (`UE_BUILD_SHIPPING` 0, so every `RH.*` command is present). ShipCheck still prefers a Test package when one exists (a source-built engine), so nothing changes when `UE_ROOT` becomes one. Swap-out point: the `@("Test", "Development")` auto-detect list in `Tools/ShipCheck.ps1`.
  - **`Tools\Package.bat`:** Shipping still archives to `Saved\Packages\Windows`; any other configuration now archives to `Saved\Packages\<Config>\Windows`, so a bot package never overwrites the Shipping one (before, Development shared the Shipping folder).
  - **`Tools/ShipCheck.ps1`:** `-Package` (Shipping, default `Saved\Packages\Windows`) gets the existing receipt / gamepad-plugin / input-asset checks, through one receipt function that now takes the configuration, plus a new **no RH commands** check: one off-screen hub launch whose `-RHExec` trace must call `RH.Offer.AutoPick`, `RH.Later`, `RH.Hub` and `RH.TimeScale` "not a console command" (only this launch's time-stamped lines count, since the Shipping trace in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\RogueHero\Saved` is machine-wide). New `-BotPackage` (default: the first of `Saved\Packages\Test\Windows`, `Saved\Packages\Development\Windows` that exists): its configuration is read from the staged `RogueHero*.exe`, a Shipping package is refused, and its executable must match its own build receipt by SHA256 (`RogueHero-Win64-Test.target` / `RogueHero.target`). The runs read the CSV, trace and crashes from `<bot package>\RogueHero\Saved` (a non-Shipping package is not "installed") and still scan the Shipping crash folder. `-ChecksOnly` runs the Shipping checks only. Existing output lines and the `SHIPCHECK: PASS (<n> runs)` / `FAIL` verdict are unchanged; added lines are `SHIPCHECK: PASS|FAIL no RH commands - ...`, `SHIPCHECK: FAIL bot package - ...`, `SHIPCHECK: SKIP bot runs - ...` (exit 2) and an informational `bot package: <Config>, <exe>`.
  - **Measured (this worktree, main 165301cd):** Shipping package OK; Test package refused as above; Development package OK. `ShipCheck.ps1 -Runs 1`: gamepad plugins PASS, no RH commands PASS, `run 1: patron Storm, run end after 94 s (sim 413.77 s)`, hero level 10, 13 offers shown and answered by `RH.Offer.AutoPick worst` (decline), `SHIPCHECK: PASS (1 runs)`.

- 2026-10-27 - **Vigil hub refined to the art book PL.23 / PL.24 panel language (look only; no gameplay change).**
  - **Layout at 1920 x 1080 (ApplyLayout scales it; 16:10 / 1280 x 800 keeps the narrow and short rules):** header (64, 44) with the place label in both languages; Ichor top right; six Shrine portraits 200 x 250, 18 apart, from (64, 168), one status line each; the detail block at (64, 604), 760 wide; the menu column at right 64 / top 168 as 320-wide P24 panels 12 apart (Shrine, Contracts, Codex, Ledger-stone, Settings); BEGIN bottom right (90 over the edge) with the language + version footer under it (60). **Not drawn in PL.23, placed here:** the Quota / Descent / Rig rows (P24 option panels, hints beside them) anchored bottom left under the detail block and fitted to their height; the omen panel between them and BEGIN on the footer line; the Vigil remarks and rumour banner in the right column under the menu, fitted down to BEGIN.
  - **P24 states in code:** `URHTextButton::SetPanelStyle` / `URHOptionButton::SetPanelStyle` (default 55 % ink + 1 px bone 10 %; focused 78 % ink + bone edge + a soft glow in the chosen Keeper's colour; disabled = locked: dashed bone 25 % edge, the label at 55 % bone and the value in bone on the 55 % ink fill, never faded as a whole so the value keeps >= 4.5:1 over the backdrop; Shrine portraits keep PL.24's 45 %). `RHUIStyle::GlowBrush` (a 9-slice transient Gaussian soft glow, drawn centred behind the panel, fading over Outset px: 44 for portraits, 24 for panels) and `RHUIStyle::MakeDashedEdge` (tiled 4-on-4-off transient textures) are the swap-out points for style assets. `URHNoFocusRing` wraps the hub tree and answers `OnQueryShowFocus` = false, so Slate's thin keyboard-focus rectangle no longer doubles the P24 focused state (focus and navigation unchanged). The settings screen keeps its old look (opt-in only).
  - **Deviations:** (1) PL.23's Shrine menu panel is the current screen: drawn in the focused style, not a focus stop (the pad reaches the Keepers directly); a click returns focus to the chosen Keeper. (2) Locked greyscale uses Slate's disabled draw effect on the shrine image (desaturates and darkens) instead of a material; swap-out point: a desaturate UI material. (3) BEGIN's line reads "· accept the terms of the {Keeper}" (`ST_World Hub.Begin.Terms`; zh "· 接受{0}的条件") because the honorifics make an English possessive ("Keeper of Storms's") ungrammatical. (4) Other-language captions ("CONTRACTS" beside 契约, 契约 beside Contracts, the honorific pair in the detail) come from the string tables at runtime: the English source string in zh-Hans, the compiled zh-Hans `.locres` in English (`RHUIStyle::OtherCultureText`); no duplicate rows. (5) PL.23's footer archive number is the build id / git hash (the existing hub version string); the run archive number stays on the Ichor recovery line and only after a run has been filed (no placeholder). (6) The footer keeps the Select / Move device glyphs before the language line. (7) The EN title renders in Marcellus SC (the Inscription family's Latin face); Noto Serif SC carries the zh title, as PL.24's family table says. (8) The live sigil stays at the foot of each portrait (Art §12 PL.07: sigils animate at the Shrine), though PL.23 does not draw it.
  - **Verification:** `Tools/HubShots.ps1` (new) boots L_Hub per culture / resolution in its own save slot (`-RHSaveSlot`), seeds a locked Tide through `RH.Story.*` for the locked cases, and saves `Saved/Cowork/hub_menu/<case>.png`.
